人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

Ross D. King 1英国剑桥大学化学工程与生物技术系。 2瑞典哥德堡查尔姆斯理工大学计算机科学与工程系。Ross D. King 1Department of Chemical Engineering and Biotechnology, University of Cambridge, Cambridge, UK. 2Department of Computer Science and Engineering, Chalmers University of Technology, Gothenburg, Sweden.

摘要Abstract

我们对人工智能科学家的过去和未来进行了调查：能够实现科学自动化的机器。人工智能科学家可以提出假设，推断其结果，设计和执行实验，解释其结果，并修正其信念。这样的系统不是放大的预测程序或对话助手。它们是综合的科学代理，与文献、形式知识、数学模型、模拟、数据分析系统和物理实验室相连。We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are not enlarged prediction programs or conversational assistants. They are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.

亚当是第一台通过假设形成和物理实验的循环做出新科学发现的机器。 Eve 建立了现代自动驾驶实验室的架构。基础模型、自主代理和实验室机器人现在使得构建比亚当或夏娃更通用的系统成为可能。Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern selfdriving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.

中心问题不再是科学的各个组成部分是否可以自动化。他们可以。问题在于整合。人工智能科学家必须将神经学习与逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、模拟、实验设计、机器人技术和正式科学记录结合起来。The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.

人工智能科学家有潜力改变科学：让科学更快、更便宜、更系统、更可重复。人工智能科学家可以研究对于人类科学而言过于复杂的系统，并使数千名人工智能科学家能够共同解决单一问题。他们还可以集中科学力量，自动出错，并将科学推理直接与危险的身体行为联系起来。因此，它们的开发必须包括严格的评估、完整的来源、问责制、安全性和治理。AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems. They could also concentrate scientific power, automate error and connect scientific reasoning directly to dangerous physical actions. Their development must therefore include rigorous evaluation, complete provenance, accountability, safety and governance.

诺贝尔图灵挑战赛的目标是到 2050 年开发出能够实现诺贝尔级别发现自动化的人工智能系统。进展比计划提前。当我们成功时，它将创造一种新的科学形式并改变世界。The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

一、简介1. Introduction

科学方法在历史上只起源过一次，即17世纪的欧洲。它改变了世界。得益于基于科学的技术，数十亿人现在过着比过去国王更好的生活：他们可以获得更好的食物、医疗保健、卫生设施、通讯、教育和旅行机会。然而，为了应对二十一世纪世界面临的严峻挑战——包括气候变化、粮食不安全、抗菌素耐药性、癌症和未来的流行病——科学和技术需要大幅提高生产力。实现科学生产力所需增长的唯一可行方法是应用人工智能（AI）。The scientific method originated only once in history, in seventeenth-century Europe. It transformed the world. Thanks to technologies based on science, billions of people now live better lives than kings did in the past: they have access to better food, medical care, sanitation, communication, education and opportunities for travel. However, to meet the formidable challenges facing the twenty-first-century world—including climate change, food insecurity, antimicrobial resistance, cancer and future pandemics—science and technology need to become substantially more productive. The only feasible way to achieve the required increase in scientific productivity is through the application of Artificial Intelligence (AI).

自动推理的想法很古老（Boden，2006）。它的最终形态是AI。现代人工智能的诞生通常与阿兰·图灵的开创性论文“计算机器与智能”相关（图灵，The idea of automating reasoning is ancient (Boden, 2006). Its ultimate form is AI. The birth of modern AI is generally associated with Alan Turing’s seminal paper ‘Computing Machinery and Intelligence’ (Turing,

1

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

1950 年）和 1956 年达特茅斯夏季人工智能研究项目（McCarthy 等人，1955 年；Boden，2006 年）。人工智能可以被定义为智能体的研究和构建，这些智能体感知环境并采取旨在最大程度地实现目标的可能性的行动（Russell & Norvig，2021）。将此定义应用到科学中，人工智能科学家是一种智能代理，通过实验观察现实世界并形成模型来预测和解释观察到的内容。1950) and the Dartmouth Summer Research Project on Artificial Intelligence in 1956 (McCarthy et al., 1955; Boden, 2006). AI may be defined as the study and construction of intelligent agents that perceive their environments and take actions designed to maximise the probability of achieving their goals (Russell & Norvig, 2021). Applying this definition to science, an AI Scientist is an intelligent agent that observes the real world through experiments and forms models that predict and explain what is observed.

科学特别适合人工智能的应用（Kitano，2021）。科学是抽象的，就像国际象棋和围棋游戏一样，人工智能系统现在可以击败最优秀的人类棋手。科学问题的范围也受到限制。这很重要，因为人工智能系统传统上很难理解问题的更广泛背景，并确定哪些知识是相关的：“卷心菜和国王”问题（Boden，1977）。处理特定科学问题的人工智能系统主要需要了解直接的科学背景；它不需要蔬菜、政治和世界其他方面的全面知识。Science is particularly well suited to the application of AI (Kitano, 2021). Science is abstract, like the games of chess and Go, in which AI systems can now defeat the best human players. Scientific problems are also restricted in scope. This matters because AI systems traditionally struggled to understand the wider context of a problem and to determine what knowledge is relevant: the ‘Cabbages and Kings’ problem (Boden, 1977). An AI system working on a particular scientific problem needs principally to understand the direct scientific context; it does not need comprehensive knowledge of vegetables, politics and every other aspect of the world.

人工智能对科学的另一个显着优势是自然是诚实的。如果人类或人工智能科学家进行科学实验，物理世界不会在策略上试图欺骗科学家。这与人工智能在商业、政治、网络安全和战争中的许多应用有着根本的不同，在这些应用中，智能代理可能会主动寻求相互误导。自然可能难以理解，实验观察可能充满噪音，科学模型可能不完整，但底层世界并不是对抗性主体。Another significant advantage of AI for science is that nature is honest. If a human or an AI Scientist performs a scientific experiment, the physical world is not strategically attempting to deceive the scientist. This differs fundamentally from many applications of AI in business, politics, cybersecurity and warfare, where intelligent agents may actively seek to mislead one another. Nature may be difficult to understand, experimental observations may be noisy, and scientific models may be incomplete, but the underlying world is not an adversarial agent.

人工智能系统已经在科学的特定组成部分拥有超人的科学力量。他们可以搜索和处理超出人类能力的大量信息，在连接到证明系统时执行形式验证的推论，维护大型假设空间上的概率分布，分析巨大的数据集，重复运行模拟并无疲劳地操作。他们的能力与人类科学家的能力是互补的，人类科学家在物理基础、灵活性、默契实验理解、社会智力、道德责任以及识别重要问题而不仅仅是易于处理的问题方面仍然具有优势。AI systems already possess superhuman scientific powers in specific components of science. They can search and process quantities of information beyond human capacity, execute formally verified deductions when connected to proof systems, maintain probability distributions over large hypothesis spaces, analyse enormous datasets, run simulations repeatedly and operate without fatigue. Their capabilities are complementary to those of human scientists, who remain superior in physical grounding, flexibility, tacit experimental understanding, social intelligence, moral responsibility and the identification of questions that are important rather than merely tractable.

最近的成功已使人工智能成为科学的通用技术。深度学习系统可以预测蛋白质结构和分子相互作用，识别天文和物理数据中的模式，生成候选分子和材料，模拟计算成本高昂的模拟，分析生物医学图像并从科学文献中提取结构化信息（LeCun et al., 2015; Jumper et al., 2021; Merchant et al., 2023; Lam et al., 2023; Abramson et al., 2024; Dagdelen等人，2024）。大型语言模型 (LLM) 可以讨论科学概念、编写和调试软件、调用外部工具、搜索文档、分析数据并生成候选假设。代理系统可以将这些功能组合成多步骤工作流程，规划行动、使用工具、评估中间结果并修改计划（Boiko et al., 2023; Bran et al., 2024; Lu et al., 2024; Gottweis et al., 2026; Ghareeb et al., 2026）。Recent successes have established AI as a general-purpose technology for science. Deep learning systems can predict protein structures and molecular interactions, identify patterns in astronomical and physical data, generate candidate molecules and materials, emulate computationally expensive simulations, analyse biomedical images and extract structured information from the scientific literature (LeCun et al., 2015; Jumper et al., 2021; Merchant et al., 2023; Lam et al., 2023; Abramson et al., 2024; Dagdelen et al., 2024). Large Language Models (LLMs) can discuss scientific concepts, write and debug software, invoke external tools, search documents, analyse data and generate candidate hypotheses. Agentic systems can combine such capabilities into multi-step workflows that plan actions, use tools, evaluate intermediate results and revise their plans (Boiko et al., 2023; Bran et al., 2024; Lu et al., 2024; Gottweis et al., 2026; Ghareeb et al., 2026).

这些进步使得有必要区分科学人工智能系统的几个相关类别。These advances make it necessary to distinguish several related classes of scientific AI system.

人工智能科学助理在人类的密切指导下支持一项或多项科学活动。例子包括文献检索、数据分析、代码生成、预测、图像解释和手稿准备系统。An AI scientific assistant supports one or more scientific activities under close human direction. Examples include systems for literature search, data analysis, code generation, prediction, image interpretation and manuscript preparation.

代理科学系统规划和执行多步骤计算研究工作流程。这样的系统可以产生想法、搜索文献、编写和运行代码、分析结果并准备科学报告，但它不需要与世界进行物理交互。An agentic scientific system plans and executes multi-step computational research workflows. Such a system may generate ideas, search the literature, write and run code, analyse results and prepare scientific reports, but it need not interact physically with the world.

自动驾驶实验室在定义的实验领域内自主选择、执行和分析物理实验。其主要重点是闭环优化或实验空间的探索。A self-driving laboratory autonomously selects, executes and analyses physical experiments within a defined experimental domain. Its principal emphasis is the closed-loop optimisation or exploration of experimental space.

2

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

人工智能科学家将科学知识、假设生成、实验设计、实验的物理或计算执行、结果解释以及科学假设或理论的迭代修订整合在一起，并具有特定程度的自主权。An AI Scientist integrates scientific knowledge, hypothesis generation, experimental design, the physical or computational execution of experiments, interpretation of results and iterative revision of scientific hypotheses or theories, with a specified degree of autonomy.

这些类别有重叠，但它们不是同义词。预测模型并不是人工智能科学家，仅仅因为它的预测具有科学价值。撰写研究论文的法学硕士不一定是人工智能科学家。同样，只有当实验选择和解释集成到闭合反馈环中时，实验室自动化才能成为自动驾驶实验室。These categories overlap, but they are not synonymous. A predictive model is not an AI Scientist merely because its predictions are scientifically valuable. An LLM that writes a research paper is not necessarily an AI Scientist. Similarly, laboratory automation becomes a self-driving laboratory only when experimental selection and interpretation are integrated into a closed feedback loop.

The central thesis of this article is that the components required to construct increasingly capable AI Scientists are now converging. These components include formal scientific knowledge, foundation models, automated reasoning, probabilistic belief states, hypothesis generation, simulation, experimental-design algorithms, agentic software, laboratory robotics, machine-readable protocols, data analysis and provenance. Their integration will enable the progressive automation of the scientific method itself.

2. The History of Discovery Science

The application of AI to science has traditionally been termed ‘discovery science’ or ‘machine discovery’. Its founding masterwork was the DENDRAL/Meta-DENDRAL project, developed at Stanford University during the 1960s and 1970s (Buchanan et al., 1969; Lindsay et al., 1980). The project was conceived and led by Joshua Lederberg, a Nobel laureate in Physiology or Medicine, and involved a remarkable interdisciplinary team that included Edward Feigenbaum, later a recipient of the ACM A. M. Turing Award; Carl Djerassi, one of the inventors of the oral contraceptive pill; and Bruce Buchanan.

The application domain of DENDRAL was analytical chemistry, specifically the inference of molecular structures from mass-spectrometric and related chemical data. DENDRAL was the world’s first expert system. It demonstrated that a computer could achieve expert-level performance in a specialised scientific domain by combining formalised domain knowledge with heuristic search. Its success established the central expert-system insight that intelligence depends not only on general inference mechanisms, but also on extensive, explicit and carefully structured knowledge about a particular field.

Figure 1 Discovery Science Timeline.

3

The Past and Future of AI Scientists

Meta-DENDRAL extended this work by learning rules for interpreting mass spectra from examples. It was therefore also one of the first important machine-learning systems and one of the earliest demonstrations that a machine could infer scientifically meaningful rules from empirical data. The DENDRAL programme established several themes that remain central to AI for science: the formal representation of scientific knowledge, the use of search to explore large spaces of hypotheses, the integration of data with background knowledge, and the production of interpretable scientific conclusions.

Another key early achievement in discovery science was the BACON project developed by Patrick Langley, Gary Bradshaw, Jan Żytkow and Herbert Simon (Langley et al., 1987). BACON used machine learning to generate equations that fitted numerical data and could rediscover forms of well-known scientific laws. This task would now be termed equation discovery or symbolic regression. The BACON systems demonstrated that apparently creative aspects of scientific reasoning could be represented as computational search guided by heuristics such as identifying constant ratios, products and differences among measured quantities.

The importance of BACON was not that it rediscovered scientific laws, but that it provided a computational account of part of the scientific discovery process. It showed that the formation of mathematical laws could, at least in restricted domains, be decomposed into explicit operations that a machine could perform. Contemporary systems for symbolic regression, equation discovery and automated model identification continue this research tradition, although they now make use of substantially greater computing power, improved search methods, deep learning and richer representations of physical knowledge.

Other important early discovery systems included AM and EURISKO, developed by Douglas Lenat (Lenat, 1977, 1983), and systems such as CYRANO for conceptual scientific discovery. These projects explored the generation of concepts, heuristics and conjectures rather than only the fitting of predictive models. They addressed a central challenge that remains unresolved: how a machine can transform its own representational language and invent concepts that were not explicitly specified by its designers.

自动化数学中出现了一个相关的历史传统。逻辑理论家证明计算机可以证明数学定理（Newell et al., 1957），而随后的自动化定理证明者和证明助手则日益机械化演绎推理。数学不同于经验科学，因为数学主张是通过证明而不是通过物理实验确立的。然而，自动化数学为与人工智能科学家直接相关的形式表示、搜索、演绎和验证提供了基本方法。A related historical tradition arose in automated mathematics. The Logic Theorist demonstrated that computers could prove mathematical theorems (Newell et al., 1957), while subsequent automated theorem provers and proof assistants increasingly mechanised deductive reasoning. Mathematics differs from empirical science because mathematical claims are established by proof rather than by physical experiment. Yet automated mathematics provided essential methods for formal representation, search, deduction and verification that are directly relevant to AI Scientists.

基于文献的发现构成了机器辅助科学假设生成的另一条重要途径。唐·斯旺森（Don Swanson）表明，通过连接以前独立的科学文献可以产生新的假设。他的经典著作将有关鱼油、血液粘度和雷诺综合征的证据联系起来，尽管没有直接联系这两个终点概念的出版物（Swanson，1986）。基于文献的发现表明，新的科学假设可能已经隐含在已发表文献的分布式结构中，但对于个别科学家来说仍然不可见，因为没有人可以阅读和整合所有相关论文。Literature-based discovery constituted another important route to machine-assisted scientific hypothesis generation. Don Swanson showed that novel hypotheses could be generated by connecting previously separate bodies of scientific literature. His classic work linked evidence concerning fish oil, blood viscosity and Raynaud’s syndrome, despite the absence of publications directly connecting the two endpoint concepts (Swanson, 1986). Literature-based discovery established that new scientific hypotheses may already be implicit in the distributed structure of the published literature, but remain invisible to individual scientists because no human can read and integrate all relevant papers.

从早期专家系统严格的、自上而下的符号逻辑到现代深度学习的多层神经架构的转变是由 20 世纪 90 年代和 2000 年代历史中期向统计生物信息学和化学信息学的关键转变所介导的。在化学领域，定量构效关系（QSAR）模型的成熟超越了手动设计的 DENDRAL 启发法，引入了严格的统计回归和经典机器学习，包括支持向量机和随机森林，以将高维分子描述符映射到化学和生物活性（Kubinyi，1993；Cherkasov 等，2014）。The transition from the rigid, top-down symbolic logic of early expert systems to the multi-layered neural architectures of modern deep learning was mediated by a crucial mid-history shift towards statistical bioinformatics and cheminformatics during the 1990s and 2000s. In chemistry, moving beyond the manually engineered heuristics of DENDRAL, the maturation of Quantitative Structure-Activity Relationship (QSAR) models introduced rigorous statistical regression and classical machine learning, including support-vector machines and random forests, to map high-dimensional molecular descriptors to chemical and biological activity (Kubinyi, 1993; Cherkasov et al., 2014).

机器学习也在计算生物学和药物设计中得到了应用。早期工作使用归纳学习从氨基酸序列预测蛋白质二级结构，后来得到发展Machine learning also became established in computational biology and drug design. Early work used inductive learning to predict protein secondary structure from amino-acid sequence and later developed

4

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

针对同一问题的可理解的机器诱导规则（King，1987；King & Sternberg，1990）。然后使用归纳逻辑编程来学习甲氧苄啶类似物与二氢叶酸还原酶结合的关系结构-活性模型，说明明确的化学背景知识如何限制药物设计假设（King 等人，1992）。comprehensible machine-induced rules for the same problem (King, 1987; King & Sternberg, 1990). Inductive logic programming was then used to learn relational structure–activity models for trimethoprim analogues binding to dihydrofolate reductase, illustrating how explicit chemical background knowledge could constrain drug-design hypotheses (King et al., 1992).

与此同时，高通量测序革命产生的大量基因组数据需要能够解析随机生物序列的概率框架。隐马尔可夫模型 (HMM) 成为这个时代的权威数学架构，推动了结构基因发现、通过 Profile-HMM 进行蛋白质家族分类（Krogh 等，1994；Eddy，1998）以及 BLAST 等序列比对算法的统计验证（Altschul 等，1990）方面的突破。这些领域不再依赖明确的逻辑谓词来定义生物系统，而是转变为利用大型经验数据集上的连续概率分布来模拟进化空间（Durbin 等，1998）。通过将计算范式从硬编码的演绎规则转变为数据驱动的经验归纳，这场统计革命建立了存储库、数学框架和多序列比对工作流程，直接实现了 AlphaFold 等当代结构生物学的突破。Concurrently, the deluge of genomic data generated by the high-throughput sequencing revolution necessitated probabilistic frameworks capable of parsing stochastic biological sequences. Hidden Markov Models (HMMs) emerged as the definitive mathematical architecture for this era, driving breakthroughs in structural gene finding, the classification of protein families via profile-HMMs (Krogh et al., 1994; Eddy, 1998), and the statistical validation of sequence alignment algorithms like BLAST (Altschul et al., 1990). Rather than relying on explicit logical predicates to define biological systems, these fields transitioned to leveraging continuous probability distributions over large empirical datasets to model evolutionary space (Durbin et al., 1998). By shifting the computational paradigm from hard-coded deductive rules to datadriven empirical induction, this statistical revolution established the repositories, mathematical frameworks, and multiple sequence alignment workflows that directly enabled contemporary structural biology breakthroughs like AlphaFold.

Jan Żytkow 及其同事首次使用人工智能系统直接控制实验室设备（Żytkow 等人，1990）。这是一个重要的概念转变。早期的发现系统主要基于现有数据进行操作。一旦人工智能系统能够控制科学仪器，机器不仅可以分析观察结果，还可以干预物理世界并获取新的观察结果以区分相互竞争的假设。The first use of an AI system to directly control laboratory equipment was made by Jan Żytkow and colleagues (Żytkow et al., 1990). This was an important conceptual transition. Earlier discovery systems operated principally on existing data. Once an AI system could control scientific instruments, it became possible for the machine not only to analyse observations, but also to intervene in the physical world and acquire new observations selected to discriminate among competing hypotheses.

在 2010 年代和 2020 年代，自主实验在化学、材料科学、生物学和工程学领域迅速扩展。机器人技术、云计算、主动学习、标准化实验室界面和机器可读协议的进步使得将人工智能系统与物理实验连接起来变得越来越实用。法学硕士和基础模型随后添加了自然语言界面、软件生成、工具使用和代理规划。这些发展将在第 3 节中进行回顾。它们的历史意义在于两个传统的融合：亚当建立的以假设为主导的机器发现，以及夏娃建立的数据驱动的闭环实验。真正的人工智能科学家最终必须将科学知识和假设与对世界的干预、这些干预产生的观察结果以及信仰的理性改变联系起来。During the 2010s and 2020s, autonomous experimentation expanded rapidly across chemistry, materials science, biology and engineering. Advances in robotics, cloud computing, active learning, standardised laboratory interfaces and machine-readable protocols made it increasingly practical to connect AI systems to physical experiments. LLMs and foundation models subsequently added natural-language interfaces, software generation, tool use and agentic planning. These developments are reviewed in Section 3. Their historical significance lies in the convergence of two traditions: hypothesis-led machine discovery, established by Adam, and data-driven closed-loop experimentation, established by Eve. Genuine AI Scientists must ultimately connect scientific knowledge and hypotheses to interventions in the world, observations generated by those interventions and rational changes in belief.

2.1.亚当与夏娃2.1. Adam & Eve

机器人科学家项目提出了科学方法自动化的核心思想。第一位机器人科学家 Adam 被设计用于自动化酿酒酵母的功能基因组学研究（King 等人，2004 年；King 等人，2009 年）。 Adam 用逻辑表示背景生物学知识和相互竞争的假设，选择实验来区分这些假设，使用实验室机器人实际执行实验，解释结果数据并修改分配给假设的概率。The Robot Scientist project proposed the core idea of automating the scientific method. The first Robot Scientist, Adam, was designed to automate functional-genomics research in the yeast Saccharomyces cerevisiae (King et al., 2004; King et al., 2009). Adam represented background biological knowledge and competing hypotheses in logic, selected experiments to discriminate among those hypotheses, physically executed the experiments using laboratory robotics, interpreted the resulting data and revised the probabilities assigned to the hypotheses.

Adam 是第一台通过假设形成、实验选择、物理实验和解释循环自主发现新科学知识的机器。因此，亚当直接导致了人工智能科学家的现代概念：机器能够自动化科学方法的大部分内容，而不仅仅是执行孤立的预测或优化任务。Adam was the first machine to autonomously discover novel scientific knowledge through cycles of hypothesis formation, experiment selection, physical experimentation and interpretation. Adam therefore led directly to the modern conception of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating substantial parts of the scientific method, rather than merely performing isolated prediction or optimisation tasks.

第二位机器人科学家 Eve 将相同的一般理念应用于早期药物发现（Williams 等，2015）。 Eve 集成了化合物筛选、检测自动化、机器学习、主动学习和迭代实验选择。而 Adam 主要是围绕明确的组织起来的The second Robot Scientist, Eve, applied the same general philosophy to early-stage drug discovery (Williams et al., 2015). Eve integrated compound screening, assay automation, machine learning, active learning and iterative experimental selection. Whereas Adam was organised primarily around explicit

5

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

与相互竞争的生物学假设相比，Eve 更加强调闭环实验优化以及化学和生物空间的有效探索。competing biological hypotheses, Eve placed greater emphasis on closed-loop experimental optimisation and the efficient exploration of chemical and biological spaces.

因此，Eve 直接通向了现代自动驾驶实验室领域。自动驾驶实验室将自动化实验与机器学习或优化相结合，以便一组实验的结果决定接下来进行哪些实验。系统重复执行预测、实验选择、物理执行、测量、学习和更新选择的循环。该术语后来变得广泛，特别是在化学和材料科学领域，但基本架构是由 Eve 建立的。Eve therefore led directly to the modern field of self-driving laboratories. A self-driving laboratory combines automated experimentation with machine learning or optimisation so that the results of one set of experiments determine which experiments are conducted next. The system repeatedly executes a cycle of prediction, experiment selection, physical execution, measurement, learning and renewed selection. The terminology became widespread later, particularly in chemistry and materials science, but the essential architecture was established by Eve.

这种历史区别仍然很重要。 Adam 是人工智能科学家的主要概念祖先，因为它自动化了以假设为主导的科学推理。 Eve 是自动驾驶实验室的主要概念祖先，因为它展示了自主、数据驱动、闭环实验搜索。当代系统越来越多地结合这两种传统：源自人工智能科学家血统的明确或基于语言的科学推理，以及源自自动驾驶实验室血统的高效闭环实验。This historical distinction remains important. Adam is the primary conceptual ancestor of AI Scientists because it automated hypothesis-led scientific reasoning. Eve is the primary conceptual ancestor of selfdriving laboratories because it demonstrated autonomous, data-driven, closed-loop experimental search. Contemporary systems increasingly combine these two traditions: explicit or language-based scientific reasoning derived from the AI Scientist lineage, and efficient closed-loop experimentation derived from the self-driving-laboratory lineage.

图 2 原始科学自动化图（King et al., 2004）。Figure 2 The Original Automation of Science diagram from (King et al., 2004).

3. 科学人工智能的现状3. The Current State of AI for Science

人工智能已经融入现代科学中。它预测结构、提出分子、编写代码、阅读论文和选择实验。然而大多数系统会自动执行一项任务。边界不是另一个预测因素。正是将这些力量整合到进行科学的主体中。AI is already embedded in modern science. It predicts structures, proposes molecules, writes code, reads papers and selects experiments. Yet most systems automate one task. The frontier is not another predictor. It is the integration of these powers into agents that conduct science.

机器学习的商业成功得益于数十年来对大规模捕获、通信和存储数据的基础设施的投资。科学领域也出现了类似的过程（Hey et al., 2009）。现代仪器、高通量实验、电子健康记录、成像设施、基因组测序程序、天文观测站和物理模拟产生的大量数据无法仅由人类进行分析。数据、元数据、本体、软件和可执行分析方法现在构成了科学人工智能的重要基础。The commercial success of machine learning was made possible by decades of investment in infrastructure for capturing, communicating and storing data at large scale. A parallel process has occurred in science (Hey et al., 2009). Modern instruments, high-throughput experiments, electronic health records, imaging facilities, genome-sequencing programmes, astronomical observatories and physical simulations produce quantities of data that cannot feasibly be analysed by humans alone. Data, metadata, ontologies, software and executable analysis methods now form an essential foundation for scientific AI.

目前的情况可以分为四个重叠的类别：预测科学人工智能、基于法学硕士的代理科学系统、自动驾驶实验室和综合人工智能科学家。The present landscape can be organised into four overlapping classes: predictive scientific AI, LLM-based and agentic scientific systems, self-driving laboratories and integrated AI Scientists.

6

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

3.1.预测科学人工智能3.1. Predictive scientific AI

人工智能在科学领域最明显的成功涉及预测。深度神经网络可以从大量经验数据中学习复杂的函数，并且通常无需使用科学界传统上青睐的显式符号理论即可做出准确的预测。The most visible successes of AI for science involve prediction. Deep neural networks can learn complex functions from large quantities of empirical data and often make accurate predictions without using the explicit symbolic theories traditionally favoured in science.

最突出的例子是 AlphaFold 2，它在根据氨基酸序列预测蛋白质结构方面取得了质的进步（Jumper 等人，2021）。相关的 AlphaFold 蛋白质结构数据库为超过 2 亿种蛋白质提供了预测结构。 AlphaFold 3 将方法扩展到蛋白质、核酸、小分子、离子和修饰残基的联合结构和相互作用（Abramson 等人，2024）。The most prominent example is AlphaFold 2, which achieved a qualitative advance in predicting protein structure from amino-acid sequence (Jumper et al., 2021). The associated AlphaFold Protein Structure Database made predicted structures available for more than 200 million proteins. AlphaFold 3 extended the approach to the joint structures and interactions of proteins, nucleic acids, small molecules, ions and modified residues (Abramson et al., 2024).

类似的方法用于分子生成、蛋白质设计、天气预报、材料性能预测、医学成像、量子化学和替代建模。科学基础模型寻求从大型且异构的科学数据集中学习一般表示，并使这些表示适应多个下游任务。Comparable methods are used for molecular generation, protein design, weather forecasting, materialsproperty prediction, medical imaging, quantum chemistry and surrogate modelling. Scientific foundation models seek to learn general representations from large and heterogeneous scientific datasets and adapt those representations to multiple downstream tasks.

这些系统拥有令人印象深刻的科学能力，但仅靠预测并不是科学自主性。预测模型通常给出一个人类定义的问题、输入和输出的固定表示、人类选择的训练数据以及外部指定的成功衡量标准。它不一定确定应该提出哪个科学问题、制定解释性假设、设计决定性的实验或修改其对某个领域的概念表示。Such systems possess impressive scientific capabilities, but prediction alone is not scientific autonomy. A predictive model is generally given a human-defined problem, a fixed representation of inputs and outputs, training data selected by humans and an externally specified measure of success. It does not necessarily identify which scientific question should be asked, formulate explanatory hypotheses, design decisive experiments or revise its conceptual representation of a domain.

3.2.大型语言模型和代理科学系统3.2. Large Language Models and agentic scientific systems

法学硕士扩大了可以自动化的科学任务的范围。他们可以总结文献、解释概念、生成和调试代码、在自然语言和形式语言之间进行翻译、提出实验、解释表格和图形以及传达结果。他们的一般语言能力使他们能够充当人类科学家、科学软件、数据库和实验室设备之间的接口。LLMs have expanded the range of scientific tasks that can be automated. They can summarise literature, explain concepts, generate and debug code, translate between natural and formal languages, propose experiments, interpret tables and figures and communicate results. Their general linguistic competence allows them to act as interfaces among human scientists, scientific software, databases and laboratory equipment.

重要的发展不是孤立的语言生成，而是集成到代理系统中。代理科学系统可以将目标分解为子任务、搜索信息、选择和调用工具、编写和执行软件、检查中间输出、识别错误并更改其计划。架构通常将工作划分为规划、文献搜索、编码、分析、批评和沟通等专门组件。The important development is not language generation in isolation, but integration into agentic systems. An agentic scientific system can decompose a goal into subtasks, search for information, select and invoke tools, write and execute software, inspect intermediate outputs, identify errors and alter its plan. Architectures commonly divide work among specialised components for planning, literature search, coding, analysis, criticism and communication.

Coscientist 将法学硕士与互联网和文档搜索、代码执行和实验自动化相结合，以设计和执行化学任务（Boiko 等人，2023）。 ChemCrow 将法学硕士与专业化学工具联系起来（Bran 等人，2024）。人工智能科学家自动化了从想法生成到代码执行、分析、手稿制作和模拟同行评审的计算工作流程（Lu et al., 2024）。联合科学家使用多种智能体来生成、辩论、排序和完善生物医学假设（Gottweis 等人，2026）。 Robin 集成了文献搜索和数据分析代理来生成假设、提出实验、解释生物数据并更新其假设（Ghareeb 等人，2026）。Coscientist combined LLMs with internet and documentation search, code execution and experimental automation to design and perform chemistry tasks (Boiko et al., 2023). ChemCrow connected an LLM to specialist chemical tools (Bran et al., 2024). The AI Scientist automated a computational workflow from idea generation through code execution, analysis, manuscript production and simulated peer review (Lu et al., 2024). Co-Scientist used multiple agents to generate, debate, rank and refine biomedical hypotheses (Gottweis et al., 2026). Robin integrated literature-search and data-analysis agents to generate hypotheses, propose experiments, interpret biological data and update its hypotheses (Ghareeb et al., 2026).

这些都是实质性的进步。然而，大多数仍然依赖于人类定义的目标、策划的数据、既定的工具和受限的环境。它们可以产生错误的陈述、捏造的参考文献、无效的推理以及看似合理但实验上不受支持的假设。研究工作流程的计算完成本身并不是经验科学的完全自动化。These are substantial advances. Yet most remain dependent on human-defined objectives, curated data, established tools and restricted environments. They can generate false statements, invented references, invalid reasoning and plausible but experimentally unsupported hypotheses. Computational completion of a research workflow is not by itself the complete automation of empirical science.

7

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

虽然此类系统通过自动化端到端计算研究工作流程（从执行到手稿生成）展示了令人印象深刻的工程集成，但它们也强调了过程自动化和真正概念发现之间的关键界限。通过利用大型语言模型迭代现有的机器学习管道，该框架成功简化了研究任务的执行和报告。然而，生成的见解在很大程度上仍然是增量的，并且受到初始代码环境的严格限制。这强调了一个重要的区别：自动化交付结构化学术成果并不等同于真正的科学洞察力，真正的科学洞察力最终需要本体论创新、基本异常的识别以及超越现有文献的范式转变。While such systems demonstrate impressive engineering integration by automating the end-to-end computational research workflow—from execution to manuscript generation—they also highlight a critical boundary between process automation and genuine conceptual discovery. By utilising large language models to iterate on existing machine learning pipelines, the framework successfully streamlines the execution and reporting of research tasks. However, the insights generated remain largely incremental and tightly bounded by the initial code environment. This underscores a vital distinction: automating the delivery of structured academic artifacts is not synonymous with true scientific insight, which ultimately demands ontological innovation, the identification of fundamental anomalies, and paradigm shifts that look beyond the existing literature.

3.3.自动驾驶实验室3.3. Self-driving laboratories

自动驾驶实验室将自动化实验设备与算法相结合，使用累积的结果来选择后续实验。他们的典型目标是有效地搜索实验空间、优化一个或多个目标或学习将实验条件与结果相关的预测模型。Self-driving laboratories integrate automated experimental equipment with algorithms that use accumulated results to select subsequent experiments. Their typical objective is to search an experimental space efficiently, optimise one or more objectives or learn a predictive model relating experimental conditions to outcomes.

他们的基本循环是：Their basic cycle is:

  • 代表当前的知识状态；Represent the current state of knowledge;

  • 选择下一个或多个实验；Select the next experiment or experiments;

  • 将选择翻译成可执行协议；Translate the selection into an executable protocol;

  • 使用实验室自动化执行协议；Execute the protocol using laboratory automation;

  • 收集并分析结果数据；Collect and analyse the resulting data;

  • 更新模型或假设分布；Update the model or hypothesis distribution;

  • 重复循环。Repeat the cycle.

Eve 建立了这种药物发现架构。随后的自动驾驶实验室已将相关原理应用于化学合成、催化剂优化、材料发现、配方、蛋白质工程和工艺开发。贝叶斯优化、主动学习、进化搜索和多目标优化通常用于选择实验。Eve established this architecture for drug discovery. Subsequent self-driving laboratories have applied related principles to chemical synthesis, catalyst optimisation, materials discovery, formulation, protein engineering and process development. Bayesian optimisation, active learning, evolutionary search and multi-objective optimisation are commonly used to choose experiments.

A-Lab 结合了计算预测、从文献中提取的知识、主动学习、机器人技术和自动化无机材料合成表征（Szymanski 等人，2023）。 SAMPLE 执行了蛋白质设计、构建和测试的重复闭环循环（Rapp 等人，2024）。 Fast-Cat 绘制了催化剂的多目标 Pareto 前沿（Bennett 等人，2024），而自动化流动平台则联合优化了反应产率、工艺强化和规模化（Slattery 等人，2024）。A-Lab combined computational predictions, knowledge extracted from the literature, active learning, robotics and automated characterisation for autonomous inorganic-materials synthesis (Szymanski et al., 2023). SAMPLE performed repeated closed-loop cycles of protein design, construction and testing (Rapp et al., 2024). Fast-Cat mapped multi-objective Pareto fronts for catalysts (Bennett et al., 2024), while automated flow platforms have jointly optimised reaction yield, process intensification and scale-up (Slattery et al., 2024).

现代自动驾驶实验室越来越多地使用 LLM 代理作为实验室软件、文档和协议的接口。这减少了所需的特定任务软件的数量，但提高了可靠性标准：错误的文本陈述是不可取的，而对物理实验室的错误命令可能会损坏设备、浪费稀有材料或造成安全隐患。Modern self-driving laboratories increasingly use LLM agents as interfaces to laboratory software, documentation and protocols. This reduces the amount of task-specific software required, but raises the standard of reliability: a false textual statement is undesirable, whereas an incorrect command to a physical laboratory can damage equipment, waste scarce materials or create a safety hazard.

3.4.综合人工智能科学家3.4. Integrated AI Scientists

人工智能科学家比自动驾驶实验室更笼统。自动驾驶实验室可以有效地优化目标，而无需生成或评估明确的科学假设。人工智能科学家应该使用背景知识来解释观察结果，提出假设，得出可通过实验测试的结果，选择区分替代方案的实验，执行这些实验，解释其结果并修正其科学信念。AI Scientists are more general than self-driving laboratories. A self-driving laboratory may efficiently optimise an objective without generating or evaluating explicit scientific hypotheses. An AI Scientist should use background knowledge to explain observations, originate hypotheses, derive experimentally testable consequences, select experiments that discriminate among alternatives, execute those experiments, interpret their results and revise its scientific beliefs.

8

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

亚当是第一个以物理具体形式实例化这一概念的系统。目前法学硕士、知识图、神经符号人工智能、因果推理、概率编程和自动化实验方面的进展创造了比 Adam 更广泛的科学能力的系统的前景。Adam was the first system to instantiate this conception in a physically embodied form. Current advances in LLMs, knowledge graphs, neuro-symbolic AI, causal inference, probabilistic programming and automated experimentation create the prospect of systems with much broader scientific competence than Adam.

目前还没有任何系统能够与一流人类科学家的广度、深度和开放式自主权相媲美。目前的系统通常在人类选择的领域内运行。他们的工具、材料、假设语言、目标函数和允许的行为在很大程度上是预先确定的。他们很少发明新的实验技术、重建其概念基础或独立决定哪些科学问题是重要的。No existing system yet matches the breadth, depth and open-ended autonomy of a first-rate human scientist. Present systems usually operate within domains chosen by humans. Their instruments, materials, hypothesis languages, objective functions and permissible actions are largely predetermined. They rarely invent new experimental technologies, reconstruct their conceptual foundations or decide independently which scientific problems are important.

尽管如此，自主科学的主要组成部分都表现出了强大的能力。基础模型获得广泛的科学表征；法学硕士使用工具并协调工作流程；形式语言和知识图表示明确的信息；推理者得出结果并检查一致性；学习系统生成模型和假设；主动学习选择信息丰富的实验；机器人执行协议；代理商分析结果并修改计划。挑战日益成为一体化的挑战。The major components of autonomous science have nevertheless each demonstrated substantial capability. Foundation models acquire broad scientific representations; LLMs use tools and coordinate workflows; formal languages and knowledge graphs represent explicit information; reasoners derive consequences and check consistency; learning systems generate models and hypotheses; active learning selects informative experiments; robots execute protocols; and agents analyse results and revise plans. The challenge is increasingly one of integration.

表 1 中的区别是功能性的，而不是促销性的。我们应该为集成了科学循环大部分内容的系统保留“人工智能科学家”这个术语。一个有用的预测者并不一定是科学家。The distinctions in Table 1 are functional rather than promotional. We should reserve the term AI Scientist for systems that integrate substantial parts of the scientific loop. A useful predictor is not automatically a scientist.

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

4. AI科学家的架构和路线图4. Architecture and Roadmap for AI Scientists

人工智能科学家必须关闭科学循环。它必须从知识到问题，从问题到假设，从假设到实验，从观察回到修正的知识。该循环中的任何中断都会使系统返回到工具状态。An AI Scientist must close the scientific loop. It must move from knowledge to questions, from questions to hypotheses, from hypotheses to experiments, and from observations back to revised knowledge. Any break in that loop returns the system to the status of a tool.

图3Figure 3将人工智能科学家展示为交互模块的联合体，而不是单个整体模型。Shows an AI Scientist as a federation of interacting modules rather than a single monolithic model.

主要组成部分是：The principal components are:

  • 包含正式知识、概率、模型、数据、协议和出处的科学记忆；A scientific memory containing formal knowledge, probabilities, models, data, protocols and provenance;

  • 用于检索、阅读、摘录和引文检查的文献界面；A literature interface for retrieval, reading, extraction and citation checking;

  • 问题和假设生成器；A question and hypothesis generator;

  • 形式逻辑、数学和概率推理者；Formal logical, mathematical and probabilistic reasoners;

  • 仿真和模型执行环境；A simulation and model-execution environment;

  • 实验设计和投资组合分配系统；An experimental-design and portfolio-allocation system;

  • 与库存和设备功能相关的协议编译器；A protocol compiler linked to inventories and equipment capabilities;

  • 实验室控制和观察接口；Laboratory-control and observational interfaces;

  • 数据质量、统计分析和解释模块；Data-quality, statistical-analysis and interpretation modules;

10

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

  • 敌对批评者和独立核查机构；Adversarial critics and independent verification agents;

  • 安全和权限管理者；A safety and permissions governor;

  • 记录每一个重大决定和行动的来源系统；A provenance system recording every material decision and action;

  • 人类科学家通过其设定目标、检查推理和干预的界面。Interfaces through which human scientists set objectives, inspect reasoning and intervene.

该循环中的每个箭头都是潜在的故障点。一个假设可能在语言上是合理的，但在科学上却是不连贯的。模拟可能会实现错误的模型。对于仪器来说，有效的实验设计可能会被错误地编译。机器人可能会发出预期的命令，但物理上会失败。统计分析可能在数学上是正确的，但回答了错误的问题。一个完整的人工智能科学家必须明确地表示和检查这些区别。Every arrow in this loop is a potential failure point. A hypothesis may be linguistically plausible but scientifically incoherent. A simulation may implement the wrong model. A valid experimental design may be compiled incorrectly for an instrument. A robot may issue the intended command but fail physically. A statistical analysis may be mathematically correct while answering the wrong question. A complete AI Scientist must represent and check these distinctions explicitly.

科学记忆不应该是被动的档案。它应该包含对竞争假设和模型的概率信念状态。每一个新的观察结果都应该与其实验和分析的来源联系起来，并且应该更新相关的信念。系统应该保留分歧和负面结果，而不是用单个当前答案覆盖它们。The scientific memory should not be a passive archive. It should contain a probabilistic belief state over competing hypotheses and models. Every new observation should be linked to its experimental and analytical provenance and should update the relevant beliefs. The system should preserve disagreements and negative results rather than overwrite them with a single current answer.

该架构应该是神经象征性的和概率性的。神经模型提供感知、灵活的语言理解、表示学习和生成搜索。符号系统提供明确的语义、约束、演绎和证明。概率论代表了不确定性和理性信念的修正。数学和可执行程序表达定量模型。机器人和仪器将这些表征与自然联系起来。The architecture should be neuro-symbolic and probabilistic. Neural models provide perception, flexible language understanding, representation learning and generative search. Symbolic systems provide explicit semantics, constraints, deduction and proof. Probability theory represents uncertainty and rational belief revision. Mathematics and executable programs express quantitative models. Robotics and instruments connect these representations to nature.

没有单一的代表是足够的。纯粹的符号系统无法手动获取所有相关的科学知识。纯粹的神经系统无法保证逻辑有效性、校准的不确定性或忠实的出处。纯粹的统计系统可以预测而不需要解释。一个完整的人工智能科学家必须根据科学任务选择和组合表示。No single representation is sufficient. A purely symbolic system cannot acquire all relevant scientific knowledge manually. A purely neural system cannot guarantee logical validity, calibrated uncertainty or faithful provenance. A purely statistical system may predict without explaining. A complete AI Scientist must select and combine representations according to the scientific task.

4.1.一个自治框架4.1. One autonomy framework

应使用一个自治框架来描述进展，而不是几个重叠的阶段系统。我们采用六个级别，编号为0到5。级别描述了人类对科学过程的干预程度；它们本身并不衡量智力或科学价值。Progress should be described using one autonomy framework, not several overlapping stage systems. We adopt six levels, numbered 0 to 5. The levels describe the degree of human intervention in the scientific process; they do not by themselves measure intelligence or scientific merit.

自主性必须与能力分开报告：系统的性能如何；普遍性：其运作的领域和表现形式的范围；规模：协调的假设、代理、实验和实验室的数量；可靠性：错误的频率和严重程度；科学意义：发现的重要性；风险：失败或误用的潜在后果。Autonomy must be reported separately from capability: how well the system performs; generality: the range of domains and representations in which it operates; scale: the number of hypotheses, agents, experiments and laboratories coordinated; reliability: the frequency and severity of error; scientific significance: the importance of the discoveries; and risk: the potential consequences of failure or misuse.

3 级系统可能会做出重要的发现，而 5 级系统可能会产生琐碎或不正确的工作。相反，出于安全或法律原因，功能强大的系统可能会在密切监督下运行。A Level 3 system may make an important discovery, while a Level 5 system may produce trivial or incorrect work. Conversely, a highly capable system may be operated under close supervision for reasons of safety or law.

4.2.里程碑4.2. Milestones

近期进展主要来自于使有界科学系统变得可靠。第一个要求是从文献中可靠地提取主张、证据和协议，以及将自然语言与模型、数据和实验连接起来的形式表示。这些表示必须支持竞争假设和前瞻性假设生成的校准不确定性，其质量由实验而不是修辞的合理性决定。Near-term progress will come principally from making bounded scientific systems dependable. The first requirement is reliable extraction of claims, evidence and protocols from the literature, together with formal representations that connect natural language to models, data and experiments. Those representations must support calibrated uncertainty over competing hypotheses and prospective hypothesis generation whose quality is determined by experiment rather than rhetorical plausibility.

第二组里程碑涉及物理执行。协议生成必须经过正式检查，了解库存和设备，并受到安全规则的约束。自主实验室必须诊断实验失败并从中恢复，而测量和分析管道则保留不确定性和完整的来源。结果应在实验室和实施中独立再现，而不仅仅是在同一平台上重复。A second group of milestones concerns physical execution. Protocol generation must be formally checked, aware of inventory and equipment, and constrained by safety rules. Autonomous laboratories must diagnose and recover from experimental failures, while measurement and analysis pipelines preserve uncertainty and complete provenance. Results should be reproduced independently across laboratories and implementations, not merely repeated on the same platform.

进展还需要衡量新颖性、正确性、自主性和科学意义的基准，以及实验室和计算资源的可互操作网络。向更高自主权的过渡将需要的不仅仅是更长的代理工作流程：系统必须能够改变自己的问题表示，发明新的实验行为，识别现有仪器何时不足，以及区分失败的理论和失败的实验。Progress will also require benchmarks that measure novelty, correctness, autonomy and scientific significance, and interoperable networks of laboratories and computational resources. The transition to higher autonomy will demand more than longer agentic workflows: systems must be able to change their own problem representations, invent new experimental actions, recognise when an existing instrument is inadequate, and distinguish a failed theory from a failed experiment.

4.3.评估作为架构的一部分4.3. Evaluation as part of the architecture

评估不能推迟到部署之后。每个模块都应该公开有关其能力、不确定性和操作限制的可衡量的声明，并且完整的人工智能科学家应该识别出问题何时超出其验证范围，并请求人工协助或独立验证。Evaluation cannot be postponed until after deployment. Each module should expose measurable claims about its competence, uncertainty and operating limits, and the complete AI Scientist should recognise when a problem lies outside its validated domain and request human assistance or independent verification.

每一项科学主张都应该带有机器可读的证据记录。该记录应确定正在评估的假设或模型、使用的证据、进行的推论和模拟、选择的实验和选择它们的原因、获得的观察结果、分析Every scientific claim should carry a machine-readable evidential record. That record should identify the hypothesis or model under assessment, the evidence used, the deductions and simulations performed, the experiments selected and the reasons for selecting them, the observations obtained, the analyses

12

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

应用，以及由此产生的系统信念状态的变化。它还应该区分人类和机器的贡献，并说明仍然存在的不确定性。applied, and the resulting changes to the system’s belief state. It should also distinguish human and machine contributions and state the uncertainty that remains.

该架构为本文的其余部分提供了组织框架。第 5 节和第 6 节涉及科学表述和文献；第 7 节讨论假设的生成；第 8 节讨论演绎和模拟；第 9 节和第 10 节涉及观察、实验、分析和信念修正；第 11 节涉及评估；第 12 至 14 节讨论协作、治理和未来。This architecture provides the organising framework for the remainder of the article. Sections 5 and 6 address scientific representation and literature; Section 7 addresses hypothesis generation; Section 8 addresses deduction and simulation; Sections 9 and 10 address observation, experimentation, analysis and belief revision; Section 11 addresses evaluation; and Sections 12–14 address collaboration, governance and the future.

5. 科学知识和模型的表示5. Representations for Scientific Knowledge and Models

科学家只能发现其语言能够代表的内容。表示的选择决定了可以提出哪些假设、可以进行哪些推论以及可以设计哪些实验。因此，人工智能科学家需要具有表现力、可执行性和精确性的语言。A scientist can discover only what its language enables it to represent. The choice of representation determines which hypotheses can be stated, which deductions can be made and which experiments can be designed. AI Scientists therefore require languages that are expressive, executable and precise.

我们区分形式语言和自然语言，例如英语。大多数人类科学都是使用两者的混合来进行的。自然语言用于激发问题、描述观察结果、交流论点和解释意义。形式语言用于表达数学模型、统计假设、化学结构、逻辑关系、计算机程序和实验数据。We distinguish formal languages and natural languages such as English. Most human science is conducted using a mixture of the two. Natural language is used to motivate problems, describe observations, communicate arguments and explain significance. Formal languages are used to express mathematical models, statistical assumptions, chemical structures, logical relations, computer programs and experimental data.

形式语言在科学中受到青睐，因为只有它们才能提供科学知识的无限制交换、计算集成和可再现性所需的语义清晰度。自然语言语句可能不明确、不完整、依赖于上下文或内部不一致。相比之下，定义良好的形式语言指定有效语句的语法，并为这些语句提供显式语义。这允许检查科学主张的一致性，与其他主张进行演绎相关，并在适当的情况下自动验证。Formal languages are to be preferred in science because only they provide the semantic clarity required for the unrestricted exchange, computational integration and reproducibility of scientific knowledge. Natural-language statements can be ambiguous, incomplete, context-dependent or internally inconsistent. By contrast, a well-defined formal language specifies the syntax of valid statements and gives those statements an explicit semantics. This permits scientific claims to be checked for consistency, related deductively to other claims and, in suitable cases, verified automatically.

用于表达科学知识的最容易理解的形式语言是形式逻辑、概率论、数学，以及最常见的计算机程序或图灵机。这些语言是互补的。逻辑特别适合表达实体、关系、假设和演绎结果。概率表达不确定性和信念程度。数学表达数量结构和科学定律。计算机程序可以表示任意复杂的可执行模型和过程。The best-understood formal languages for expressing scientific knowledge are formal logic, probability theory, mathematics and, most generally, computer programs or Turing machines. These languages are complementary. Logic is particularly suitable for expressing entities, relations, hypotheses and deductive consequences. Probabilities express uncertainty and degrees of belief. Mathematics expresses quantitative structures and scientific laws. Computer programs can represent arbitrarily complex executable models and procedures.

人工智能科学家通过溯因或归纳推理形成假设，并辅以概率推理、类比、搜索以及新概念和谓词的发明。因此，用于表示假设的语言的表达能力决定了人工智能科学家可以探索的科学思想的空间。AI Scientists form hypotheses through abductive or inductive inference, supplemented by probabilistic reasoning, analogy, search and the invention of new concepts and predicates. The expressive power of the language used to represent hypotheses therefore determines the space of scientific ideas that an AI Scientist can explore.

5.1.逻辑、本体和可执行知识5.1. Logic, Ontologies and Executable Knowledge

形式逻辑由亚里士多德大约 2,400 年前发明/发现，从一开始就被用来表达科学知识（Kneale & Kneale，1962）。亚里士多德是物理学和生物学以及形式逻辑的创始人。知识的逻辑表示随后成为经院哲学和现代分析哲学发展的核心。Formal logic was invented/discovered by Aristotle approximately 2,400 years ago and was used from the beginning to express scientific knowledge (Kneale & Kneale, 1962). Aristotle was a founder of both physics and biology, as well as of formal logic. The logical representation of knowledge subsequently became central to scholastic philosophy and to the development of modern analytical philosophy.

使用逻辑来表示科学知识历来受到科学哲学家的青睐。正如图尔明所观察到的，大多数分析科学哲学都基于这样的假设：自然科学的知识内容可以表示为具有明确逻辑结构的形式命题系统（图尔明，1953）。逻辑的优点是易于理解，The use of logic to represent scientific knowledge has traditionally been favoured by philosophers of science. As Toulmin observed, most analytical philosophy of science was based on the assumption that the intellectual content of a natural science could be represented as a formal propositional system possessing a definite logical structure (Toulmin, 1953). Logic has the advantages of being comprehensible,

13

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

支持严格的推理，并为结论如何从前提得出提供透明的解释。supporting rigorous inference and providing transparent explanations of how conclusions follow from premises.

逻辑研究催生了计算机科学。现代计算机源于对数学形式化和确定哪些逻辑和数学运算可以机械执行的尝试（Hilbert & Ackermann，1928；Gödel，1931；Church，1936；Turing，1936）。逻辑仍然是知识计算表示、数据库理论、编程语言语义、软件验证和自动推理的基础。The study of logic gave birth to computer science. The modern computer emerged from attempts to formalise mathematics and determine which logical and mathematical operations could be executed mechanically (Hilbert & Ackermann, 1928; Gödel, 1931; Church, 1936; Turing, 1936). Logic remains fundamental to the computational representation of knowledge, database theory, programminglanguage semantics, software verification and automated reasoning.

5.1.1.科学知识的形式化5.1.1. The formalisation of scientific knowledge

科学知识的形式化在技术上变得越来越必要。每个科学领域都面临着越来越多的出版物、数据、模型、协议和软件。在许多领域，这种增长已成为洪水般的增长。因此，科学越来越依赖计算机来存储、检索、整合、分析和交流信息。The formalisation of scientific knowledge is becoming increasingly technologically necessary. Every area of science faces a growing volume of publications, data, models, protocols and software. In many fields, this growth has become a deluge. Science therefore depends increasingly on computers to store, retrieve, integrate, analyse and communicate information.

计算机的全部功能——其本身是数学形式化的结果——只有当它们所运行的知识被形式化时才能被充分利用。计算机可以在自然语言文本中搜索统计相关的字符串，但除非相关实体、关系、假设和含义足够明确，否则它无法可靠地推理科学知识。The full power of computers—which themselves originated as a consequence of the formalisation of mathematics—can be exploited only when the knowledge upon which they operate is formalised. A computer can search natural-language text for statistically associated strings, but it cannot reliably reason over scientific knowledge unless the relevant entities, relations, assumptions and meanings have been made sufficiently explicit.

这一要求推动了语义网的发展：通过标准的开发，网络上的信息可以被赋予机器可解释的含义，而不是仅表示为供人类读者使用的文档（Berners-Lee 等，2001）。包括资源描述框架（RDF）、网络本体语言、规则语言、查询语言和起源模型在内的技术为形式化知识的发布、链接和推理提供了基础设施的元素。This requirement motivated the Semantic Web: the development of standards through which information on the web could be given machine-interpretable meaning rather than being represented only as documents intended for human readers (Berners-Lee et al., 2001). Technologies including the Resource Description Framework (RDF), the Web Ontology Language, rule languages, query languages and provenance models provide elements of an infrastructure for publishing, linking and reasoning over formalised knowledge.

电子科学和公平原则的基础是一个相关的目标：论文、数据、元数据、软件、工作流程和实验方法应该是可查找的、可访问的、可互操作的和可重用的（Wilkinson 等人，2016）。对于人工智能科学家来说，互操作性需要的不仅仅是常见的文件格式。它需要就数据表示什么、如何获得测量结果、做出什么假设以及结论如何与证据相关等问题达成明确一致。A related objective underlies e-Science and the FAIR principles: papers, data, metadata, software, workflows and experimental methods should be findable, accessible, interoperable and reusable (Wilkinson et al., 2016). For AI Scientists, interoperability requires more than common file formats. It requires explicit agreement about what the data denote, how measurements were obtained, what assumptions were made and how conclusions are related to evidence.

5.1.2.本体论5.1.2. Ontologies

科学知识形式化的一个关键步骤是定义一个明确的本体论：对领域中存在的内容以及理解它的类别和关系的描述。逻辑通常用于指定本体。A key step in formalising scientific knowledge is to define an explicit ontology: an account of what exists in the domain and of the categories and relations through which it is understood. Logic is generally used to specify ontologies.

我们遵循广泛接受的本体论定义，即“概念化的明确规范”（Gruber，1993）。更具体地说，本体由概念、它们的层次结构、它们之间的关系以及形式化它们的定义和允许的关系的公理组成（Mizoguchi，2004）。We follow the widely accepted definition of an ontology as an “explicit specification of a conceptualisation” (Gruber, 1993). More compositionally, an ontology consists of concepts, their hierarchical organisation, relations among them and axioms that formalise their definitions and permitted relations (Mizoguchi, 2004).

本体不仅仅提供受控词汇。它定义了术语的预期含义并限制了它们的组合方式。例如，本体论可以将实验过程与其物理输入和输出区分开来，将假设与其所表达的命题区分开来，将观察与由此推断出的结论区分开来。如果科学记录要支持可靠的机器推理，这种区别是必不可少的。An ontology does not merely provide a controlled vocabulary. It defines the intended meanings of terms and constrains how they may be combined. For example, an ontology may distinguish an experimental process from its physical inputs and outputs, a hypothesis from the proposition it expresses, and an observation from the conclusion inferred from it. Such distinctions are essential if scientific records are to support reliable machine reasoning.

14

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

本体越来越多地用于组织和交换科学知识。它们被开发来描述基因、蛋白质、疾病、化学物质、表型、科学假设、实验设计、实验室行为、机器学习研究和研究起源。 EXPO 本体提供了科学实验的一般形式表示（Soldatova & King，2006），而 EXACT 则形式化了实验协议中涉及的操作（Soldatova 等，2008）。这些本体论通过明确实验的目标、结构、执行和解释来支撑机器人科学家系统。Ontologies are increasingly used to organise and exchange scientific knowledge. They have been developed to describe genes, proteins, diseases, chemicals, phenotypes, scientific hypotheses, experimental designs, laboratory actions, machine-learning studies and research provenance. The EXPO ontology provided a general formal representation of scientific experiments (Soldatova & King, 2006), while EXACT formalised the actions involved in experimental protocols (Soldatova et al., 2008). These ontologies underpinned the Robot Scientist systems by making the aims, structure, execution and interpretation of experiments explicit.

后来的标准扩大了这种语义基础设施。生物医学研究本体代表了跨学科生物医学研究的规划、执行和报告（Bandrowski 等，2016）。 W3C PROV-O 为实体、活动、代理和来源关系提供了通用本体（Lebo 等人，2013），而 RO-Crate 将数据、软件、工作流程及其元数据打包为连接的、机器可读的研究对象（Soiland-Reyes 等人，2022）。 LabOP 将相关原理应用于可执行的生物协议（Bartley 等人，2023）。这些资源共同展示了从孤立的领域词汇到完整科学过程的可互操作表示的转变。Later standards broadened this semantic infrastructure. The Ontology for Biomedical Investigations represents the planning, execution and reporting of biomedical investigations across disciplines (Bandrowski et al., 2016). W3C PROV-O provides a general ontology for entities, activities, agents and provenance relations (Lebo et al., 2013), while RO-Crate packages data, software, workflows and their metadata as connected, machine-readable research objects (Soiland-Reyes et al., 2022). LabOP applies related principles to executable biological protocols (Bartley et al., 2023). Together, these resources show a movement from isolated domain vocabularies towards interoperable representations of complete scientific processes.

本体论对于未来的人工智能科学家至关重要，因为科学术语无法孤立地进行可靠解释。它们的含义取决于与其他概念的关系、对其使用的限制以及产生权利要求的上下文。本体提供了关联自然语言表达、数据库标识符、形式假设、实验行为和观察所需的语义支架。Ontologies will be essential to future AI Scientists because scientific terms cannot be interpreted reliably in isolation. Their meanings depend on relations to other concepts, constraints on their use and the context in which a claim was produced. Ontologies provide the semantic scaffolding required to relate natural-language expressions, database identifiers, formal hypotheses, experimental actions and observations.

5.1.3.专家系统和知识获取瓶颈5.1.3. Expert systems and the knowledge-acquisition bottleneck

早期的人工智能系统主要基于逻辑推理（Boden，2006）。随着专家系统的出现，这种情况发生了变化，专家系统将一般推理机制与专门领域的正式声明和操作知识结合起来。Early AI systems were based principally on logical inference (Boden, 2006). This changed with the advent of expert systems, which combined general inference mechanisms with formalised declarative and operational knowledge about specialised domains.

专家系统在 20 世纪 80 年代主导了人工智能。他们的巨大优势在于他们的知识是明确和形式化的，他们的推理可以被检查，他们的结论可以被查询和检查。如果编码的事实和推理规则正确并且推理引擎健全，专家系统就不会产生幻觉。他们的结论是通过明确的推理步骤从可识别的知识体系中得出的。 （布坎南和肖特利夫，1984）。Expert systems dominated AI during the 1980s. Their great strengths were that their knowledge was explicit and formalised, their reasoning could be inspected, and their conclusions could be queried and checked. Provided that the encoded facts and inference rules were correct and the inference engine was sound, expert systems did not hallucinate. Their conclusions followed from an identifiable body of knowledge through explicit inference steps. (Buchanan & Shortliffe, 1984).

专家系统程序受到知识获取瓶颈的限制。知识工程——人类专家所拥有的知识的提取和形式化表达——被证明是困难、昂贵和缓慢的。人类专家常常无法清楚地表达出他们判断背后的隐性知识。即使知识被阐明，手动将其转换为正式规则和本体也需要领域专家和知识工程师之间的广泛协作。 （费根鲍姆，1977）。The expert-system programme was limited by the knowledge-acquisition bottleneck. Knowledge engineering—the extraction and formal representation of knowledge possessed by human experts— proved difficult, expensive and slow. Human experts frequently could not articulate the tacit knowledge underlying their judgements. Even when the knowledge was articulated, converting it manually into formal rules and ontologies required extensive collaboration between domain experts and knowledge engineers. (Feigenbaum, 1977).

大多数明确的人类知识都包含在书面自然语言文本中。直到最近，提取此类文本中表达的知识并将其转化为形式表示在大规模计算上仍然很困难。这阻碍了足够全面的专家系统的构建和维护。Most explicit human knowledge is contained in written natural-language texts. Until recently, extracting the knowledge expressed in such texts and translating it into formal representations was computationally intractable at scale. This prevented the construction and maintenance of sufficiently comprehensive expert systems.

5.1.4.知识图谱5.1.4. Knowledge graphs

表示大量形式化知识的最常用的当代结构是知识图。知识图将实体表示为节点，将关系表示为边，以及属性、类型、标识符、出处和模式级约束。它可以集成The most commonly used contemporary structures for representing large bodies of formalised knowledge are knowledge graphs. A knowledge graph represents entities as nodes and relations as edges, together with attributes, types, identifiers, provenance and schema-level constraints. It can integrate

15

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

通过连接引用相同实体的记录并根据共享本体来组织它们来构建异构数据集。heterogeneous datasets by connecting records that refer to the same entities and by organising them according to a shared ontology.

知识图中信息的含义由其本体或模式定义。这指定了可能出现的实体的类型、可能连接它们的关系以及对这些关系的约束。然后，推理器可以得出额外的知识，识别不一致之处或根据更一般的概念对实体进行分类。The meaning of the information in a knowledge graph is defined by its ontology or schema. This specifies the types of entities that may occur, the relations that may connect them and constraints on those relations. A reasoner may then derive additional knowledge, identify inconsistencies or classify entities under more general concepts.

知识图广泛应用于搜索引擎、问答系统、推荐系统、生物医学和自治系统。大型商业图包含数十亿个实体，这些实体通过更多的关系连接起来（Noy 等人，2019）。科学知识图包括描述基因、蛋白质、疾病、化学品、药物、材料、出版物和实验证据的资源。Knowledge graphs are widely used in search engines, question-answering systems, recommender systems, biomedicine and autonomous systems. Large commercial graphs contain billions of entities connected by still larger numbers of relations (Noy et al., 2019). Scientific knowledge graphs include resources describing genes, proteins, diseases, chemicals, drugs, materials, publications and experimental evidence.

图结构提供了实际效率、可扩展性和易于集成性，但它也限制了可以直接表达的语义细节和推理形式。大多数大型知识图将知识表示为二元关系或主谓宾三元组。然而，科学主张通常涉及量化、否定、模态、不确定性、时间条件、实验背景和关系之间的关系。因此，当科学假设的语义无法充分简化为三元组的集合时，需要更丰富的逻辑表示。The graph structure provides practical efficiency, scalability and ease of integration, but it also limits the semantic detail and forms of inference that can be expressed directly. Most large knowledge graphs represent knowledge as binary relations or subject–predicate–object triples. Scientific claims, however, commonly involve quantification, negation, modality, uncertainty, temporal conditions, experimental context and relations among relations. Richer logical representations are therefore required when the semantics of a scientific hypothesis cannot be reduced adequately to a collection of triples.

知识图和法学硕士具有互补的优势。知识图包含明确的、可检查和可寻址的语句，但不完整且构建成本昂贵。法学硕士包含广泛的隐性知识，可以解释不同的语言表达，但它们的知识是通过数值参数分布的，无法直接检查或信任。未来的人工智能科学家将把法学硕士的灵活性与本体和知识图的显式语义和可验证性结合起来。Knowledge graphs and LLMs have complementary strengths. Knowledge graphs contain explicit, inspectable and addressable statements, but are incomplete and expensive to construct. LLMs contain broad implicit knowledge and can interpret diverse linguistic expressions, but their knowledge is distributed through numerical parameters and cannot be inspected or trusted directly. Future AI Scientists will combine the flexibility of LLMs with the explicit semantics and verifiability of ontologies and knowledge graphs.

5.1.5。逻辑编程和作为知识的程序5.1.5. Logic programming and programs as knowledge

最常见的知识形式化表示是计算机程序或图灵机。程序可以代表可执行的科学模型、模拟、实验协议、数据分析过程或生成预测的理论。The most general possible formal representation of knowledge is a computer program or Turing machine. A program can represent an executable scientific model, a simulation, an experimental protocol, a dataanalysis procedure or a theory that generates predictions.

逻辑编程是一种编程范式，其中逻辑既充当知识的声明性表示又充当可执行语言。 Kowalski 的表述将一组子句的逻辑含义与用于执行它们的控制策略分开，建立了算法可以理解为逻辑加控制的原则（Kowalski，1974）。随后系统地开发了 Prolog 和相关语言的语义和操作基础（Lloyd，1987）。Logic programming is a programming paradigm in which logic serves both as a declarative representation of knowledge and as an executable language. Kowalski’s formulation separated the logical meaning of a set of clauses from the control strategy used to execute them, establishing the principle that an algorithm can be understood as logic plus control (Kowalski, 1974). The semantics and operational foundations of Prolog and related languages were subsequently developed systematically (Lloyd, 1987).

这种双重角色对人工智能科学家来说很有吸引力。相同的表示可以陈述背景知识、表达假设、得出预测和解释结论。变量和关系允许对结构化对象进行一般性陈述；约束可以对单位、类型和允许的实验状态进行编码；证明痕迹可以将结论与明确的前提联系起来。由于声明性内容与执行是分离的，因此替代搜索或推理引擎可以对相同的科学知识进行操作。This dual role is attractive for AI Scientists. The same representation can state background knowledge, express hypotheses, derive predictions and explain conclusions. Variables and relations permit general statements about structured objects; constraints can encode units, types and admissible experimental states; and proof traces can connect a conclusion to explicit premises. Because the declarative content is separable from execution, alternative search or inference engines can operate over the same scientific knowledge.

归纳逻辑编程通过从观察和背景知识中学习逻辑程序来扩展范式（Muggleton，1991）。因此，它统一了知识表示、机器学习和可执行模型，并支持递归关系、可解释规则和发明谓词的学习。现代 ILP 系统使用元级搜索、约束和神经引导来减少巨大的假设空间，但可扩展性、噪声和谓词发明仍然是中心研究问题（Cropper 等人，2022）。Inductive logic programming extends the paradigm by learning logic programs from observations and background knowledge (Muggleton, 1991). It therefore unifies knowledge representation, machine learning and executable models, and supports the learning of recursive relations, interpretable rules and invented predicates. Modern ILP systems use meta-level search, constraints and neural guidance to reduce the immense hypothesis space, but scalability, noise and predicate invention remain central research problems (Cropper et al., 2022).

16

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

5.1.6。法学硕士和专家系统的回归5.1.6. LLMs and the return of expert systems

法学硕士改变了正式科学知识的前景。它们在从自然语言科学文本中提取实体、关系、事件、测量和其他结构化信息方面表现非常出色。他们可以将不同的语言表达映射到通用模式上，提出本体类和关系，将文本翻译成逻辑或程序表示，并为以前未命名的概念生成候选定义。LLMs transform the prospects for formal scientific knowledge. They perform remarkably well at extracting entities, relations, events, measurements and other structured information from natural-language scientific text. They can map varied linguistic expressions onto common schemas, propose ontology classes and relations, translate text into logical or programmatic representations and generate candidate definitions for previously unnamed concepts.

材料科学的实验表明，语言模型可以从科学论文中提取复杂的结构化记录，包括材料、掺杂剂、物相、形态、性质和应用之间的关系（Dagdelen 等人，2024）。基于 LLM 的系统还可以构建知识图，而不需要将完整的模式放置在提示中，并且可以自动归纳和规范化部分模式（Zhang & Soh，2024）。但科学提取仍然不完善，特别是在论文包含复杂的表格、图形、隐含条件或特定领域约定的情况下。因此，形式化之后必须进行检查和验证。Experiments in materials science have shown that language models can extract complex structured records from scientific papers, including relations among materials, dopants, phases, morphologies, properties and applications (Dagdelen et al., 2024). LLM-based systems can also construct knowledge graphs without requiring the complete schema to be placed in the prompt and can induce and canonicalise parts of the schema automatically (Zhang & Soh, 2024). But scientific extraction remains imperfect, especially where papers contain complex tables, figures, implicit conditions or domain-specific conventions. Formalisation must therefore be followed by checking and verification.

由此产生的架构扭转了经典专家系统的主要弱点。现在可以通过使用法学硕士将自然语言科学文献转化为形式表示来克服知识获取瓶颈。然后可以使用本体、类型系统、逻辑推理器、数据库、证明助手和主要证据的链接来检查所提出的表示。The resulting architecture reverses the principal weakness of classical expert systems. The knowledgeacquisition bottleneck can now be overcome by using LLMs to translate the natural-language scientific literature into formal representations. The proposed representations can then be checked using ontologies, type systems, logical reasoners, databases, proof assistants and links to primary evidence.

适当的管道是：The appropriate pipeline is:

  • 检索相关科学文献和数据；Retrieve the relevant scientific documents and data;

  • 使用法学硕士来识别权利要求、实体、关系、数量、条件和出处；Use an LLM to identify claims, entities, relations, quantities, conditions and provenance;

  • 将这些转化为正式的表述；Translate these into a formal representation;

  • 将表示与已建立的本体和标识符保持一致；Align the representation with established ontologies and identifiers;

  • 检查其语法、类型约束和逻辑一致性；Check its syntax, type constraints and logical consistency;

  • 将每个正式主张与其提取的证据联系起来；Connect each formal claim to the evidence from which it was extracted;

  • 使用健全的形式推理机测试推论；Test deductions using a sound formal reasoner;

  • 识别不确定性、矛盾和缺失信息；Identify uncertainty, contradictions and missing information;

  • 将未解决的索赔提交给人工审查或实验测试；和Submit unresolved claims to human review or experimental testing; and

  • 将经过验证的主张添加到持久的正式科学知识库中。Add verified claims to a persistent formal scientific knowledge base.

一旦知识被形式化，就可以检查其正确性和真实性。逻辑结果可以机械地验证。可以识别矛盾。索赔可以追溯到他们的证据。实验观察可用于增加或减少分配给正式假设的概率。因此，现在可以大规模生成经过认证的科学知识。法学硕士提供提取和创建形式表示所需的灵活接口；符号系统提供了法学硕士所缺乏的语义规则、验证和透明度。Once knowledge has been formalised, it can be checked for correctness and truth. Logical consequences can be verified mechanically. Contradictions can be identified. Claims can be traced to their evidence. Experimental observations can be used to increase or decrease the probability assigned to formal hypotheses. Thus, certified scientific knowledge can now be generated at scale. LLMs provide the flexible interface required to extract and create formal representations; symbolic systems provide the semantic discipline, verification and transparency that LLMs alone lack.

该组合为具有可信智能输出的新一代专家系统奠定了基础。与经典专家系统不同，它们的知识库可以根据科学文献自动构建和更新。与没有基础的法学硕士不同，他们的结论可以受到显性知识的约束，可以通过健全的推理引擎进行检查并追溯到证据。The combination provides the basis for a new generation of expert systems with trusted intelligent output. Unlike classical expert systems, their knowledge bases can be constructed and updated automatically from the scientific literature. Unlike ungrounded LLMs, their conclusions can be constrained by explicit knowledge, checked by sound inference engines and traced to evidence.

科学文献的形式化将创造未来人工智能科学家所需的基础知识。它将不仅使他们能够检索类似于查询的文本，而且还能了解已提出的主张、支持这些主张的证据、它们与其他主张的逻辑关系如何以及哪些实验将最有效地解决剩余的不确定性。The formalisation of the scientific literature will create the foundational knowledge required by future AI Scientists. It will allow them not merely to retrieve text that resembles a query, but to understand what claims have been made, what evidence supports them, how they relate logically to other claims and which experiments would most efficiently resolve the remaining uncertainty.

17

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

5.2.概率5.2. Probabilities

在 1850 年的一封信中，詹姆斯·克拉克·麦克斯韦 (James Clerk Maxwell) 提出了著名且正确的说法：“这个世界的真正逻辑是概率计算”（Maxwell，1850；Harman，1990）。科学知识是临时的、概率性的。因此，既定的科学知识和新假设之间不存在明显的认知区分。区别在于程度不同：既定知识由假设和理论组成，科学界对这些假设和理论赋予了很高的概率，因为它们得到了广泛而多样的证据的支持。In an 1850 letter, James Clerk Maxwell famously and correctly stated that “the true logic for this world is the calculus of probabilities” (Maxwell, 1850; Harman, 1990). Scientific knowledge is provisional and probabilistic. There is therefore no sharp epistemic division between established scientific knowledge and new hypotheses. The distinction is one of degree: established knowledge consists of hypotheses and theories to which the scientific community assigns high probabilities because they are supported by extensive and varied evidence.

自动化科学的一个核心思想是假设可能具有与其正确性相关的概率。人工智能科学家通常不应该简单地将假设表示为真或假。它应该代表其对假设的相信程度、与其参数相关的不确定性、证据的可靠性以及相关替代解释的概率。A core idea in automating science is that hypotheses may have probabilities associated with their correctness. An AI Scientist should not normally represent a hypothesis simply as true or false. It should represent its degree of belief in the hypothesis, the uncertainty associated with its parameters, the reliability of the evidence and the probabilities of relevant alternative explanations.

5.2.1.贝叶斯更新5.2.1. Bayesian updating

贝叶斯定理提供了根据新证据修改分配给假设的概率的基本规则。Bayes’ theorem provides the fundamental rule for revising the probability assigned to a hypothesis in the light of new evidence.

当假设做出的预测得到实验证实时，如果观察结果在该假设下比在其替代方案下更有可能出现，则该假设的概率就会增加。相反，当预测未得到证实时，如果在假设下失败的可能性低于在竞争假设下失败的可能性，则假设的概率会降低。When a prediction made by a hypothesis is experimentally confirmed, the probability of that hypothesis increases when the observation was more probable under the hypothesis than under its alternatives. Conversely, when a prediction is not confirmed, the probability of the hypothesis decreases when the failure was less probable under the hypothesis than under competing hypotheses.

这对人工智能科学家有直接影响。人工智能科学家应该选择在竞争假设下其可能结果具有非常不同概率的实验。每个假设都同样准确地预测的实验提供的信息很少，即使它们的结果与所有假设都一致。This has direct implications for AI Scientists. An AI Scientist should select experiments whose possible outcomes have very different probabilities under competing hypotheses. Experiments that are predicted equally well by every hypothesis provide little information, even if their results agree with all of them.

5.2.2.波普尔和概率确认5.2.2. Popper and probabilistic confirmation

一些科学哲学家反对将概率分配给假设，最著名的是卡尔波普尔（Popper，1972）。波普尔认为，科学理论不能通过归纳来证实，而应该通过试图证伪来判断。Some philosophers of science have argued against assigning probabilities to hypotheses, most notably Karl Popper (Popper, 1972). Popper argued that scientific theories cannot be confirmed inductively and should instead be judged through attempted falsification.

波普尔错了。科学证据通常会增加或减少对假设的理性信心，而不会最终证明或最终证伪它。几个世纪以来，人们也知道假设的先验概率是不同的。高度具体且独立动机的理论不应获得与仅为适应现有观察而设计的任意且过于复杂的结构相同的先验概率。Popper was wrong. Scientific evidence commonly increases or decreases rational confidence in a hypothesis without either conclusively proving or conclusively falsifying it. It has also been known for centuries that the prior probabilities of hypotheses differ. A highly specific and independently motivated theory should not receive the same prior probability as an arbitrary and excessively complicated construction designed only to fit existing observations.

在经验科学中，证伪本身很少具有逻辑决定性。理论与观察之间的明显冲突可能是由于不正确的辅助假设、仪器故障、不适当的统计模型、实验污染或数据处理错误造成的。因此，理性的科学推理需要对全部证据的替代解释进行概率比较。Falsification is itself rarely logically decisive in empirical science. An apparent conflict between a theory and an observation may result from an incorrect auxiliary assumption, an instrument failure, an inappropriate statistical model, experimental contamination or an error in data processing. Rational scientific inference therefore requires probabilistic comparison among alternative explanations of the complete body of evidence.

波普尔的观点是正确的，即冒险的预测和严格的测试具有科学价值。贝叶斯理论解释了原因。当某个观察结果在该假设下很有可能但在其竞争对手下不太可能时，则对该假设提供了强有力的支持。因此，成功的风险预测需要很大的贝叶斯因子。Popper was correct that risky predictions and severe tests are scientifically valuable. Bayesian theory explains why. An observation gives strong support to a hypothesis when it was highly probable under that hypothesis but improbable under its competitors. A successful risky prediction therefore has a large Bayes factor.

18

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

5.2.3.奥卡姆剃刀5.2.3. Ockham’s razor

更简单的假设更有可能的启发式被称为奥卡姆剃刀，以中世纪经院哲学家奥卡姆的威廉命名。贝叶斯理论为这一原理提供了几个理论依据（Jefferys & Berger，1992；MacKay，1992）。The heuristic that simpler hypotheses are more probable is known as Ockham’s razor, after the medieval scholastic philosopher William of Ockham. Bayesian theory provides several theoretical justifications for this principle (Jefferys & Berger, 1992; MacKay, 1992).

灵活的模型可以适合许多可能的数据集，包括实际观察到的数据集。但因为它的概率分布在大范围的可能观察中，所以它不需要为发生的特定证据分配高概率。更简单的模型做出的预测范围更窄，因此可以为观察到的数据分配更大的概率。A flexible model may fit many possible datasets, including the one actually observed. But because its probability is spread across a large range of possible observations, it need not assign high probability to the particular evidence that occurred. A simpler model makes a narrower range of predictions and may therefore assign greater probability to the observed data.

因此，贝叶斯推理不仅仅奖励拟合现有数据的模型。它平衡拟合与参数空间的体积和模型允许的预测。这从原则上解释了为什么更简单的模型可能比更灵活且拟合度稍好的模型更受青睐。Bayesian inference therefore does not merely reward a model for fitting existing data. It balances fit against the volume of parameter space and predictions that the model permits. This provides a principled explanation of why a simpler model may be preferred over a more flexible model with a marginally better fit.

然而，简单性取决于表示语言。在一个坐标系中简单的方程在另一种坐标系中可能会很复杂。因此，分配给假设的先验反映了科学背景知识、代表性选择和关于哪些结构是自然的假设。Simplicity is nevertheless dependent on the language of representation. An equation that is simple in one coordinate system may be complicated in another. The prior assigned to a hypothesis therefore reflects scientific background knowledge, representational choices and assumptions about which structures are natural.

5.2.4.贝叶斯模型平均和解释选择5.2.4. Bayesian model averaging and Explanation Selection

贝叶斯理论为科学知识的概率分配提供了严格的数学基础（Berger，1985；Jaynes，2003）。当多个互斥模型 {𝑀!, … , 𝑀"} 仍然合理时，标准贝叶斯最优预测方法是贝叶斯模型平均 (BMA)，其中预测按每个模型的后验概率进行加权 (Hoeting et al., 1999)：Bayesian theory provides a mathematically rigorous foundation for assigning probabilities to scientific knowledge (Berger, 1985; Jaynes, 2003). When multiple mutually exclusive models {𝑀!, … , 𝑀"} remain plausible, the standard Bayesian optimal approach to prediction is Bayesian Model Averaging (BMA), where predictions are weighted by each model’s posterior probability (Hoeting et al., 1999):

贝叶斯模型平均保留了不确定性，并避免了与选择单个“最佳”模型相关的过度自信，但其解释取决于假设空间。在 M 封闭分析中，假设候选模型之一是数据生成模型；在 M-open 分析中，每个候选人都可能被错误指定。然后，后验概率可以集中于最接近数据生成过程的模型，而无需表明该模型实际上是正确的。对于错误指定下的纯粹预测目标，通过交叉验证堆叠预测分布可以优于传统的后验模型概率。Bayesian Model Averaging preserves uncertainty and avoids the overconfidence associated with selecting a single “best” model, but its interpretation depends on the hypothesis space. In an M-closed analysis one of the candidate models is assumed to be the data-generating model; in an M-open analysis every candidate may be misspecified. Posterior probability can then concentrate on the model closest to the data-generating process without showing that the model is literally true. For purely predictive objectives under misspecification, stacking predictive distributions by cross-validation can outperform conventional posterior model probabilities.

科学发现通常是 M-open 的：可用的模型是临时近似值，而不是包含事实的详尽列表。因此，人工智能科学家应该通过后验预测检查、差异模型和异常检测来保留绝对模型不足的证据，而不是将相对后验偏好解释为确认所选模型是充分的。Scientific discovery is generally M-open: the available models are provisional approximations rather than an exhaustive list containing the truth. An AI Scientist should therefore preserve evidence of absolute model inadequacy through posterior predictive checks, discrepancy models and anomaly detection, rather than interpreting relative posterior preference as confirmation that the selected model is adequate.

然而，经验科学需要明确的、可解释的理论，而不仅仅是预测混合或黑盒集成平均值。为了解决预测平均和解释选择之间的紧张关系，未来的人工智能科学家可能会整合最小消息长度（MML）的原则（Wallace＆Boulton，1968）。 MML 是一种信息理论框架，通过将模型评估为数据压缩器来操作奥卡姆剃刀。 MML 不是在所有参数上维持无定形、不可解释的混合分布，而是通过最小化两部分消息的总长度来发现单一的最佳解释（或一组简洁、离散的不同假设）：However, empirical science requires explicit, interpretable theories rather than just predictive blending or black-box ensemble averages. To resolve the tension between predictive averaging and explanation selection, future AI Scientists may integrate the principle of Minimal Message Length (MML) (Wallace & Boulton, 1968). MML is an information-theoretic framework that operationalizes Ockham’s razor by evaluating models as data compressors. Instead of maintaining an amorphous, uninterpretable mixture distribution over all parameters, MML seeks to discover the single best explanation (or a succinct, discrete set of distinct hypotheses) by minimizing the total length of a two-part message:

19

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

𝐼(𝑀, 𝐸) = 𝐼(𝑀) + 𝐼(𝐸∣𝑀)

其中𝐼(𝑀)是陈述模型结构框架和参数所需的二进制代码长度，𝐼(𝐸∣𝑀)是对该模型下的实验证据进行编码所需的代码长度。where 𝐼(𝑀) is the binary code length required to state the model’s structural framework and parameters, and 𝐼(𝐸∣𝑀) is the code length required to encode the experimental evidence under that model.

最小消息长度解决了一个不同但互补的目标。通过将假设和无法解释的数据视为两部分代码，它可以平衡解释性拟合与陈述模型所需的复杂性。它并不使 M-开假设空间变得完整，但它为简洁、可检查的解释提供了原则性的搜索标准。因此，人工智能科学家可以使用堆栈进行稳健预测，同时使用 MML 引导的程序搜索来识别紧凑的候选法则以进行后续测试。Minimal Message Length addresses a different but complementary objective. By treating a hypothesis and the unexplained data as a two-part code, it balances explanatory fit against the complexity required to state the model. It does not make an M-open hypothesis space complete, but it provides a principled search criterion for concise, inspectable explanations. An AI Scientist could therefore use stacking for robust prediction while using MML-guided program search to identify compact candidate laws for subsequent testing.

5.2.5。图形和关系概率模型5.2.5. Graphical and relational probabilistic models

概率论与定义结构的结合可实现图形模型，包括贝叶斯网络、马尔可夫随机场、因子图和动态贝叶斯网络（Pearl，1988；Koller & Friedman，2009）。这些模型将变量表示为节点，将概率依赖性表示为边或因子。The combination of probability theory with defined structure enables graphical models, including Bayesian networks, Markov random fields, factor graphs and dynamic Bayesian networks (Pearl, 1988; Koller & Friedman, 2009). These models represent variables as nodes and probabilistic dependencies as edges or factors.

图形模型提供了联合概率分布的紧凑表示，并使条件独立假设变得明确。它们的结构支持有效的概率推理，并且可以使复杂的模型更容易理解。动态图形模型可以描述随时间变化的系统，而分层模型可以表示在多个尺度上运行的科学过程。Graphical models provide a compact representation of joint probability distributions and make conditional-independence assumptions explicit.
