Their structure supports efficient probabilistic inference and can make complex models more comprehensible. Dynamic graphical models can describe systems that change through time, while hierarchical models can represent scientific processes operating at multiple scales.

传统的图形模型通常是命题性的：必须单独指定变量及其关系。概率与一阶谓词逻辑的集成允许将概率分配给涉及对象、关系和量化的一般逻辑结构。Conventional graphical models are generally propositional: the variables and their relationships must be specified individually. The integration of probability with first-order predicate logic permits probabilities to be assigned to general logical structures involving objects, relations and quantification.

当概率表示与关系学习相结合时，该领域被称为统计关系学习（Getoor & Taskar，2007；De Raedt 等人，2008）。相关形式包括概率逻辑程序、马尔可夫逻辑网络、贝叶斯逻辑程序和概率归纳逻辑程序。When probabilistic representations are combined with relational learning, the field is termed statistical relational learning (Getoor & Taskar, 2007; De Raedt et al., 2008). Related formalisms include probabilistic logic programs, Markov logic networks, Bayesian logic programs and probabilistic inductive logic programming.

统计关系学习与科学尤其相关，因为大多数科学知识都是相关的。分子包含通过键连接的原子；基因调节其他基因；生物体参与生态系统；患者接受治疗；实验将材料、行动、条件、测量和结论联系起来。这些结构不能自然地表示为独立的特征向量。Statistical relational learning is especially relevant to science because most scientific knowledge is relational. Molecules contain atoms connected by bonds; genes regulate other genes; organisms participate in ecosystems; patients receive treatments; and experiments connect materials, actions, conditions, measurements and conclusions. These structures cannot be represented naturally as independent feature vectors.

未来的人工智能科学家将需要结合显式实体和关系的概率关系表示；逻辑背景知识；事实和规则的不确定性；替代模型结构的不确定性；时间和因果关系；出处和实验背景；以及从证据中学习新关系和规则的能力。Future AI Scientists will require probabilistic relational representations that combine explicit entities and relations; logical background knowledge; uncertainty in facts and rules; uncertainty about alternative model structures; temporal and causal relations; provenance and experimental context; and the ability to learn new relations and rules from evidence.

5.2.6。因果推理5.2.6. Causal reasoning

与概率推理密切相关的是因果推理（Pearl，1988，2009；Spirtes et al.，2000）。标准统计分析和模式识别根据变量之间的关联和从数据中学到的相关性得出结论。当科学问题具有因果性而不仅仅是关联性时，这些是不够的。Closely connected to probabilistic reasoning is causal reasoning (Pearl, 1988, 2009; Spirtes et al., 2000). Standard statistical analysis and pattern recognition draw conclusions from associations among variables and correlations learned from data. These are insufficient when scientific questions are causal rather than merely associative.

20

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

我们区分观察两个变量相关并确定改变一个变量将改变另一个变量。预测关系可能源于直接因果关系、反向因果关系、共同原因、选择偏差或机会。We distinguish observing that two variables are associated and determining that changing one variable will change the other. A predictive relationship may arise from direct causation, reverse causation, a common cause, selection bias or chance.

因果模型不仅代表概率分布，还代表干预措施的后果。 Pearl 的 𝑑𝑜 运算符将观察 𝑋= 𝑥 与干预设置 𝑑𝑜(𝑋= 𝑥) 区分开来。这种区别是实验科学的核心。实验是对世界的干预。他们的目的不仅仅是收集额外的观察结果，而是打破现有的因果依赖性，并揭示当选定的变量被故意改变时系统如何响应。Causal models represent not only probability distributions but also the consequences of interventions. Pearl’s 𝑑𝑜-operator distinguishes observing 𝑋= 𝑥 from intervening to set 𝑑𝑜(𝑋= 𝑥). This distinction is central to experimental science. Experiments are interventions in the world. Their purpose is not simply to collect additional observations, but to break existing causal dependencies and reveal how systems respond when selected variables are deliberately changed.

do-演算提供了确定何时可以从观察数据和干预数据的组合中识别因果效应的规则（Pearl，2009）。结构因果模型还支持反事实推理：在与实际执行的干预不同的干预下，特定系统会发生什么？并非每个表述都需要因果充分性，但未测量的常见原因可能会妨碍识别，除非通过测量的协变量、有效的工具、实验干预或明确的潜变量假设来解决它们。因此，在复杂的生物系统中，隐藏的混杂因素是一个核心限制，而不是一个次要的技术例外。The do-calculus provides rules for determining when causal effects can be identified from combinations of observational and interventional data (Pearl, 2009). Structural causal models also support counterfactual reasoning: what would have happened to a particular system under an intervention different from the one actually performed? Causal sufficiency is not required in every formulation, but unmeasured common causes can prevent identification unless they are addressed through measured covariates, valid instruments, experimental interventions or explicit latent-variable assumptions. In complex biological systems, hidden confounding is therefore a central limitation rather than a minor technical exception.

因果关系的哲学基础仍然存在争议。然而因果推理对于科学来说是不可或缺的。在物理学中，因果关系传统上与时间之箭有关——原因不能影响过去——而与光速有关——因果影响不能传播到光锥之外。The philosophical foundations of causality remain disputed. Yet causal reasoning is indispensable to science. In physics, causality is traditionally connected to the arrow of time—causes cannot affect the past—and to the speed of light—causal influence cannot propagate outside the light cone.

未来的人工智能科学家必须学习因果模型，识别哪些因果关系是不确定的，设计区分替代因果结构的干预措施，并根据实验结果修改这些结构。最近关于因果发现基础模型的工作可能会提高因果结构推理的速度和通用性，但因果可识别性将继续依赖于假设、先验知识和干预。Future AI Scientists must learn causal models, identify which causal relations are uncertain, design interventions that discriminate among alternative causal structures and revise those structures in response to experimental results. Recent work on foundation models for causal discovery may improve the speed and generality of causal-structure inference, but causal identifiability will continue to depend on assumptions, prior knowledge and interventions.

5.2.7.概率规划5.2.7. Probabilistic programming

概率编程语言 (PPL) 将领域知识编码为可执行的生成模型，指定观察结果如何从潜在变量和随机过程中产生。推理通过从观察到的效果估计可能的隐藏原因来逆转这一过程。 PPL 将通用编程语言的表达能力与内置或可编程推理相结合，使得系统地构建、修改和比较丰富的概率模型变得更加容易。Probabilistic Programming Languages (PPLs) encode domain knowledge as executable generative models that specify how observations arise from latent variables and stochastic processes. Inference reverses this process by estimating probable hidden causes from observed effects. PPLs combine the expressive power of general programming languages with built-in or programmable inference, making it easier to construct, modify and compare rich probabilistic models systematically.

Church 证明了通用概率语言如何使用一小组语言结构来表示复杂的生成过程（Goodman 等，2008）。后来的系统——包括 Stan、Pyro、Turing 和 Gen——提供了建模灵活性、自动推理、可微性和计算效率的不同组合（Carpenter 等人，2017 年；Bingham 等人，2019 年；CusumanoTowner 等人，2019 年）。Church demonstrated how a universal probabilistic language could represent complex generative processes using a small set of language constructs (Goodman et al., 2008). Later systems—including Stan, Pyro, Turing and Gen—have provided different combinations of modelling flexibility, automatic inference, differentiability and computational efficiency (Carpenter et al., 2017; Bingham et al., 2019; CusumanoTowner et al., 2019).

概率程序是生成模型：它们将不可观察的原因与可观察的数据联系起来。它们可以代表分层科学理论、随机动力系统、测量过程、缺失数据、潜在变量和实验不确定性。Probabilistic programs are generative models: they relate unobservable causes to observable data. They can represent hierarchical scientific theories, stochastic dynamical systems, measurement processes, missing data, latent variables and experimental uncertainty.

因此，PPL 是人工智能科学家的自然语言。它们使科学理论能够表达为可执行的概率模型。然而，在自主人工智能科学家中部署 PPL 可能会引发严重的可扩展性问题： MCMC 难以处理：精确马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗 (MCMC) 无法扩展到现代发现科学的高维数据和深层架构；变分偏差：变分推理 (VI) 通过将积分视为优化来进行扩展，但引入了结构PPLs are therefore a natural language for AI Scientists. They enable scientific theories to be expressed as executable probabilistic models. However, deploying PPLs within autonomous AI Scientists potentially triggers severe scalability problems: MCMC Intractability: Exact Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) fails to scale to the high-dimensional data and deep architectures of modern discovery science; Variational Bias: Variational Inference (VI) scales by treating integration as optimization, but introduces structural

21

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

系统性地低估后验方差的偏差，存在病理性过度自信假设的风险。biases that systematically underestimate posterior variance, risking pathologically overconfident hypotheses.

人工智能科学家可以使用 PPL 将假设视为流动代码，从而使其能够生成和修改代表物理机制的概率程序；使用缩放混合推理从噪声观测中推断隐藏参数；计算边际可能性来评估结构模型的适应性；模拟反事实场景来预测干预结果；最大化实验选择的预期信息增益，同时最小化实验和计算成本。An AI Scientist could use PPLs to treat hypotheses as fluid code, allowing it to generate and modify probabilistic programs representing physical mechanisms; infer hidden parameters from noisy observations using scaled, hybrid inference; compute marginal likelihoods to evaluate structural model fitness; simulate counterfactual scenarios to predict intervention outcomes; and maximize expected information gain for experiment selection while minimizing experimental and computational costs.

将概率编程与因果结构、神经网络和实验室自动化相结合，形成了人工智能科学家的自我校正计算核心。Integrating probabilistic programming with causal structure, neural networks, and lab automation forms the self-correcting computational core of the AI Scientist.

5.3.数学和方程5.3. Mathematics and Equations

十七世纪科学革命的一个关键支柱是认识到数学，尤其是微积分，是表达科学定律的最佳语言。直到二十世纪，许多最伟大的科学家也是伟大的数学家，牛顿可以说是最伟大的科学家和最伟大的数学家。卡尔·弗里德里希·高斯将数学描述为“科学女王”。A key pillar of the seventeenth-century scientific revolution was the recognition that mathematics, especially calculus, is the best language in which to express scientific laws. Until the twentieth century, many of the greatest scientists were also great mathematicians, Newton being arguably both the greatest scientist and the greatest mathematician. Carl Friedrich Gauss described mathematics as “the queen of the sciences”.

数学仍然是表达定量科学知识的标准语言。所有人类科学家都或多或少地接受过数学训练。科学理论通常通过方程、不等式、几何结构、概率分布、优化问题和算法来表达。Mathematics remains the standard language for expressing quantitative scientific knowledge. All human scientists are trained in mathematics to a greater or lesser extent. Scientific theories are commonly expressed through equations, inequalities, geometric structures, probability distributions, optimisation problems and algorithms.

数学在科学中的有效性部分源于其精确性和压缩能力。一小组方程可以总结大量的观察结果，并对以前从未检查过的情况进行预测。数学理论也支持演绎：一旦指定了模型及其假设，就可以导出和检查它们的结果。The effectiveness of mathematics in science arises partly from its precision and compressive power. A small set of equations can summarise an enormous number of observations and generate predictions for circumstances never previously examined. Mathematical theories also support deduction: once a model and its assumptions have been specified, their consequences can be derived and checked.

5.3.1.方程发现5.3.1. Equation discovery

从 BACON 开始，机器科学发现的许多研究都试图使用数学作为假设和理论的语言（Langley 等，1987）。实验或观察数据被提供给人工智能系统，该系统在候选方程空间中搜索适合数据的表达式。Beginning with BACON, much research in machine scientific discovery has sought to use mathematics as the language of hypotheses and theories (Langley et al., 1987). Experimental or observational data are supplied to an AI system, which searches a space of candidate equations for expressions that fit the data.

这项任务与系统识别的工程学科密切相关，其中动力系统的数学模型是根据测量的输入和输出推断出来的（Ljung，1999）。在传统的系统识别中，模型的一般形式通常由人选择，并根据数据估计参数。自动方程发现旨在推断函数形式及其参数。This task is closely related to the engineering discipline of system identification, in which mathematical models of dynamical systems are inferred from measured inputs and outputs (Ljung, 1999). In conventional system identification, the general form of the model is often selected by a human and the parameters are estimated from data. Automated equation discovery seeks to infer the functional form as well as its parameters.

有两个目标很重要。第一个是现象学方程的发现：总结观察规律并预测未来数据的数学关系，而不必解释潜在的机制。Two objectives matter. The first is the discovery of phenomenological equations: mathematical relationships that summarise regularities in observations and predict future data without necessarily explaining the underlying mechanism.

第二个是机械方程的发现：与实体、过程和因果结构相关的数学表达式，解释了观察到的规律性发生的原因。The second is the discovery of mechanistic equations: mathematical expressions connected to entities, processes and causal structures that explain why the observed regularity occurs.

即使在理解其更深入的解释之前，现象学关系也可能具有科学价值。巴尔默的氢谱线波长公式早于量子力学。开普勒定律早于牛顿力学。历史一再表明，无法解释的数字关系可以为更深层次的理论提供线索。Phenomenological relations can be scientifically valuable even before their deeper explanation is understood. Balmer’s formula for the wavelengths of hydrogen spectral lines preceded quantum mechanics. Kepler’s laws preceded Newtonian mechanics. History repeatedly shows that unexplained numerical relationships can provide clues leading to deeper theories.

22

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

5.3.2.符号回归5.3.2. Symbolic regression

当直接在符号空间中搜索方程时，该任务称为符号回归。与普通回归不同，符号回归不采用固定的函数形式。它搜索变量、常量和数学运算符的组合。When the search for an equation is performed directly in a symbolic space, the task is termed symbolic regression. Unlike ordinary regression, symbolic regression does not assume a fixed functional form. It searches over combinations of variables, constants and mathematical operators.

遗传编程是符号回归最成功的通用方法之一（Koza，1992）。候选方程被表示为表达式树，并通过突变、重组和选择而演化。它们的适应性通常反映了预测误差和表达复杂性的组合。Genetic programming has been one of the most successful general approaches to symbolic regression (Koza, 1992). Candidate equations are represented as expression trees and are evolved through mutation, recombination and selection. Their fitness generally reflects a combination of predictive error and expression complexity.

Sašo Džeroski 及其同事通过拉格朗日 (LAGRANGE) 对方程发现做出了基础性贡献，拉格朗日将机器发现从静态关系扩展到描述动力系统的代数和微分方程系统，并通过后来基于语法的方法将先验科学知识、简约性和量纲一致性纳入方程搜索中 (Džeroski & Todorovski, 1993; Brence et al., 2021, 2023; Mežnar et al., 2023）。 Stefan Kramer 及其同事最近将符号回归置于从方程发现到自主发现系统的更广泛进展中，并引入了 Science-Gym，这是一个基准，智能体在推断控制方程之前必须设计实验并收集自己的数据（Kramer 等，2026；Cerrato 等，2026）。Sašo Džeroski and colleagues made foundational contributions to equation discovery through LAGRANGE, which extended machine discovery from static relations to systems of algebraic and differential equations describing dynamical systems, and through later grammar-based methods that incorporate prior scientific knowledge, parsimony and dimensional consistency into the search over equations (Džeroski & Todorovski, 1993; Brence et al., 2021, 2023; Mežnar et al., 2023). Stefan Kramer and colleagues have recently situated symbolic regression within the wider progression from equation discovery to autonomous discovery systems and introduced Science-Gym, a benchmark in which agents must design experiments and collect their own data before inferring the governing equations (Kramer et al., 2026; Cerrato et al., 2026).

施密特和利普森将符号回归与动力系统的实验观察相结合，并从运动数据中恢复了守恒关系和其他数学规律（施密特和利普森，2009）。他们的工作表明，自动搜索可以从物理系统的测量中识别紧凑的数学结构。Schmidt and Lipson integrated symbolic regression with experimental observations of dynamical systems and recovered conservation relations and other mathematical regularities from motion data (Schmidt & Lipson, 2009). Their work demonstrated that automated search could identify compact mathematical structures from measurements of physical systems.

非线性动力学的稀疏识别 (SINDy) 假设控制动力学仅包含从较大的候选函数库中提取的少量项。然后使用稀疏回归来识别活跃术语（Brunton 等人，2016）。这提供了奥卡姆剃刀的计算实现：更喜欢能够解释观察到的动力学的最小术语集。Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics, or SINDy, assumes that the governing dynamics contain only a small number of terms drawn from a larger library of candidate functions. Sparse regression is then used to identify the active terms (Brunton et al., 2016). This provides a computational implementation of Ockham’s razor: prefer the smallest set of terms capable of explaining the observed dynamics.

困难在于系统只能在适当的坐标系中拥有简单的数学描述。冠军等人。将深度自动编码器与稀疏方程发现相结合，以学习简化的坐标系及其内部的控制动力学（Champion 等人，2019）。神经网络学习表示，而符号稀疏回归则识别方程。A difficulty is that a system may possess a simple mathematical description only in an appropriate coordinate system. Champion et al. combined deep autoencoders with sparse equation discovery to learn both a reduced coordinate system and the governing dynamics within it (Champion et al., 2019). The neural network learns the representation, while symbolic sparse regression identifies the equations.

AI Feynman 结合了维度分析、神经网络、对称性检测、可分离性和符号搜索，从数据中恢复方程（Udrescu & Tegmark，2020）。通过在进行详尽的符号搜索之前识别数学结构，系统大大减少了有效假设空间。AI Feynman combined dimensional analysis, neural networks, symmetry detection, separability and symbolic search to recover equations from data (Udrescu & Tegmark, 2020). By identifying mathematical structure before conducting an exhaustive symbolic search, the system substantially reduced the effective hypothesis space.

这些方法表明神经方法和符号方法是互补的。神经网络在学习表示和逼近复杂函数方面非常有效。符号方法可以有效地产生简洁、明确和数学上可解释的假设。These methods demonstrate that neural and symbolic approaches are complementary. Neural networks are effective at learning representations and approximating complex functions. Symbolic methods are effective at producing concise, explicit and mathematically interpretable hypotheses.

5.3.3.将数据与科学理论相结合5.3.3. Integrating data with scientific theory

传统符号回归的一个主要限制是它搜索适合数据的方程，而不是与既定科学知识一致的方程。许多数学上不同的表达式可能同样适合小型或嘈杂的数据集，而只有少数表达式与相关背景理论兼容。A central limitation of conventional symbolic regression is that it searches for equations that fit data, rather than equations that are consistent with established scientific knowledge. Many mathematically different expressions may fit a small or noisy dataset equally well, while only a few are compatible with the relevant background theory.

23

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

AI-笛卡尔将符号回归与逻辑演绎相结合（Cornelio et al., 2023）。根据代表现有科学知识的公理评估从数据生成的候选方程。该系统可以证明候选值可以从背景理论中推导出来，证明它与该理论不一致，或者量化它与可推导结果的距离。AI-Descartes combines symbolic regression with logical deduction (Cornelio et al., 2023). Candidate equations generated from data are assessed against axioms representing existing scientific knowledge. The system can prove that a candidate is derivable from the background theory, demonstrate that it is inconsistent with that theory or quantify how far it is from a derivable result.

AI-Hilbert 通过发现同时与实验数据和公理背景知识一致的多项式定律，进一步发展了这一想法（Cory-Wright 等人，2024）。该方法使用数学优化和真实代数几何的证书来推导并正式验证候选定律。它还可以识别背景假设体内不一致的元素。AI-Hilbert develops this idea further by discovering polynomial laws that are simultaneously consistent with experimental data and axiomatic background knowledge (Cory-Wright et al., 2024). The approach uses mathematical optimisation and certificates from real algebraic geometry to derive and formally verify candidate laws. It can also identify inconsistent elements within a body of background assumptions.

AI-笛卡尔和 AI-希尔伯特例证了未来人工智能科学家所需的混合推理类型。经验数据限制了哪些方程是合理的；背景理论约束哪些是有意义的；形式推导确定所提出的方程是否符合所述假设。AI-Descartes and AI-Hilbert exemplify the type of hybrid reasoning required by future AI Scientists. Empirical data constrain which equations are plausible; background theory constrains which are meaningful; and formal deduction determines whether the proposed equations follow from the stated assumptions.

5.3.4.用于方程发现的大型语言模型5.3.4. Large Language Models for equation discovery

法学硕士为方程发现提供了一种新机制，因为它们包含科学概念、数学符号和已知方程形式的广泛隐性知识。法学硕士可以根据科学背景提出方程结构，而不是盲目地搜索表达式树。LLMs provide a new mechanism for equation discovery because they contain extensive implicit knowledge of scientific concepts, mathematical notation and known equation forms. Rather than searching blindly through expression trees, an LLM can propose equation structures informed by the scientific context.

LLM-SR 将候选方程表示为程序，并将 LLM 生成的假设与进化搜索和数值参数优化相结合（Shojaee 等人，2025a）。法学硕士提供科学先验并提出候选项目结构，而对数据的明确评估则提供了闭环反馈循环。LLM-SR represents candidate equations as programs and combines LLM-generated hypotheses with evolutionary search and numerical parameter optimisation (Shojaee et al., 2025a). The LLM supplies scientific priors and proposes candidate program structures, while explicit evaluation against data provides a closed feedback loop.

这种方法可能允许比传统表达式树更丰富的数学表示。候选模型可能包含条件语句、递归、数值求解器或对其他函数的调用。因此，将方程视为程序将方程发现与作为可执行计算的科学知识的更一般表示联系起来。This approach potentially permits richer mathematical representations than conventional expression trees. A candidate model may contain conditional statements, recursion, numerical solvers or calls to other functions. Treating equations as programs therefore connects equation discovery with the more general representation of scientific knowledge as executable computation.

但法学硕士发现方程的问题还远未得到解决。标准基准通常包含可能出现在模型训练数据中的著名方程，因此很难区分真正的发现和记忆。 LLM-SRBench 引入了旨在减少这种污染的转换和综合问题，并发现即使是最好的系统也只能准确地解决少数方程（Shojaee 等人，2025b）。But equation discovery by LLMs remains far from solved. Standard benchmarks often contain famous equations likely to have appeared in model-training data, making it difficult to distinguish genuine discovery from memorisation. LLM-SRBench introduced transformed and synthetic problems designed to reduce this contamination and found that even the best systems solved only a minority of the equations exactly (Shojaee et al., 2025b).

因此，未来的系统将需要结合基于法学硕士的科学先验；数字和符号搜索；尺寸和对称性约束；实验数据；因果和机械背景知识；形式数学验证；并在真正新颖的系统上进行测试。Future systems will therefore need to combine LLM-based scientific priors; numerical and symbolic search; dimensional and symmetry constraints; experimental data; causal and mechanistic background knowledge; formal mathematical verification; and tests on genuinely novel systems.

5.3.5。深度和普遍性5.3.5. Depth and generality

一个中心问题是所发现方程的深度和普遍性。牛顿运动定律比开普勒定律更深刻、更普遍。量子力学比之前的原子光谱经验公式更加深刻。A central question is the depth and generality of a discovered equation. Newton’s laws of motion are deeper and more general than Kepler’s laws. Quantum mechanics is deeper than the empirical formulae for atomic spectra that preceded it.

数学上精确的关系不一定是解释性的科学理论。足够灵活的算法可能会重现数据集，但无法深入了解负责观察的实体、机制或不变性。A mathematically accurate relationship is not necessarily an explanatory scientific theory. A sufficiently flexible algorithm may reproduce a dataset while providing no insight into the entities, mechanisms or invariances responsible for the observations.

方程的深度可以通过几个相关标准来评估：它解释的现象的范围；它统一了以前单独的法律的数量；变换下的不变性；它的The depth of an equation may be assessed through several related criteria: the range of phenomena it explains; the number of previously separate laws it unifies; its invariance under transformations; its

24

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

与底层机制的联系；其新颖预测的准确性；其假设的简单性；它可以从更基本的原则中推导出来；及其支持成功反事实推理的能力。connection to underlying mechanisms; the accuracy of its novel predictions; the simplicity of its assumptions; its derivability from more fundamental principles; and its capacity to support successful counterfactual reasoning.

这些标准通常不能简化为对保留数据集的预测准确性。未来的人工智能科学家不仅必须寻找适合观察的方程，还要寻找在不同描述级别上运行的数学模型的层次结构。These criteria cannot generally be reduced to predictive accuracy on a held-out dataset. Future AI Scientists must search not merely for equations that fit observations, but for hierarchies of mathematical models operating at different levels of description.

现象学模型可能适合某一尺度的预测，而更基本的模型则解释了为什么这种现象学关系成立。人工智能科学家应该代表这些模型之间的关系，而不是坚持只有一个级别在科学上是合法的。A phenomenological model may be appropriate for prediction at one scale, while a more fundamental model explains why that phenomenological relationship holds. An AI Scientist should represent the relationship between such models rather than insisting that only one level is scientifically legitimate.

5.3.6。数学理解5.3.6. Mathematical understanding

未来的人工智能科学家必须了解用数学表达的现有科学理论并了解如何应用它们。现有的方程发现系统可以观察双摆等经典物理系统，并找到预测其行为各方面的方程。它们与专家物理学家所表现出的一般数学理解相去甚远。Future AI Scientists must understand existing scientific theories expressed in mathematics and understand how to apply them. Existing equation-discovery systems can observe a classical physical system such as a double pendulum and find equations that predict aspects of its behaviour. They remain far from the general mathematical understanding displayed by an expert physicist.

例如，Landau 和 Lifshitz 的《力学》的开头章节介绍了最小作用原理和力学的拉格朗日公式（Landau 和 Lifshitz，1976）。For example, the opening chapters of Landau and Lifshitz’s Mechanics introduce the principle of least action and the Lagrangian formulation of mechanics (Landau & Lifshitz, 1976).

然后邀请读者解决形成双摆系统的拉格朗日量的问题。为了实现这一目标，他们必须：The reader is then invited to solve problems as forming the Lagrangian of a double-pendulum system. To achieve this they must:

  • 确定合适的广义坐标；Identify suitable generalised coordinates;

  • 表达两个质量的位置和速度；Express the positions and velocities of both masses;

  • 计算动能和势能；Calculate the kinetic and potential energies;

  • 形成拉格朗日函数 (𝐿= 𝑇−𝑉)；Form the Lagrangian (𝐿= 𝑇−𝑉);

  • 应用欧拉-拉格朗日方程；和Apply the Euler–Lagrange equations; and

  • 推导耦合运动方程。这项任务需要的不仅仅是将方程拟合到轨迹。它需要将系统视为一般理论框架的实例，选择适当的表示并从第一原理推导出方程。Derive the coupled equations of motion. This task requires more than fitting equations to trajectories. It requires recognising the system as an instance of a general theoretical framework, selecting an appropriate representation and deriving the equations from first principles.

  • 未来的人工智能科学家必须能够朝两个方向发展：Future AI Scientists must be able to move in both directions:

  • 通过归纳和方程发现，从数据到方程；和from data to equations, through induction and equation discovery; and

  • 从理论到预测，通过数学建模和推导。他们还必须确定所发现的方程何时是近似值，确定其应用的条件，并认识到哪些被忽视的项在该范围之外变得重要。from theory to predictions, through mathematical modelling and deduction. They must also identify when a discovered equation is an approximation, determine the conditions under which it applies and recognise which neglected terms become important outside that regime.

5.3.7.人工智能数学家5.3.7. AI mathematicians

未来的人工智能科学家还需要普遍理解和创造数学。为了实现这一目标，他们将与人工智能数学家整合。Future AI Scientists will also need to understand and create mathematics generally. To achieve this, they will be integrated with AI mathematicians.

数学涉及识别模式、制定定义和猜想，然后从公理演绎证明结果命题。计算机的出现部分源于对数学推理形式化和机械化的尝试（Turing，1936）。Mathematics involves identifying patterns, formulating definitions and conjectures, and then proving the resulting propositions deductively from axioms. Computers arose partly from the attempt to formalise and mechanise mathematical reasoning (Turing, 1936).

机器学习非常适合识别模式和提出猜想。戴维斯等人。利用机器学习来指导人类数学直觉，并产生了关于结理论和表示论的新结果（Davies et al., 2021）。Machine learning is well suited to identifying patterns and proposing conjectures. Davies et al. used machine learning to guide human mathematical intuition and produced new results concerning knot theory and representation theory (Davies et al., 2021).

FunSearch 将法学硕士与进化搜索和自动评估器相结合。它发现了上限设置问题的改进结构和在线装箱的新启发式（Romera-FunSearch combined an LLM with an evolutionary search and an automatic evaluator. It discovered improved constructions for the cap-set problem and new heuristics for online bin packing (Romera-

25

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

帕雷德斯等人，2024）。它的重要性在于将生成提案与拒绝无效输出的客观评估器相结合。Paredes et al., 2024). Its importance lies in the integration of generative proposals with an objective evaluator that rejects invalid outputs.

AlphaGeometry 将神经语言模型与符号演绎引擎相结合，解决了 30 个选定的奥林匹克级几何问题中的 25 个（Trinh 等人，2024 年）。它说明了神经符号整合的力量：神经成分指导搜索，而符号成分确保演绎有效性。AlphaGeometry combined a neural language model with a symbolic deduction engine and solved 25 of 30 selected Olympiad-level geometry problems (Trinh et al., 2024). It illustrates the power of neuro-symbolic integration: the neural component guides search, while the symbolic component ensures deductive validity.

AlphaProof 在精益定理证明环境中使用强化学习。它生成由精益内核检查的证明，确保成功的输出在形式上有效。与 AlphaGeometry 2 一起，它在 2024 年竞赛问题上取得了相当于国际数学奥林匹克银牌的成绩（Hubert 等人，2026）。AlphaProof uses reinforcement learning within the Lean theorem-proving environment. It produces proofs that are checked by the Lean kernel, ensuring that successful outputs are formally valid. Together with AlphaGeometry 2, it achieved a score equivalent to an International Mathematical Olympiad silver medal on the 2024 competition problems (Hubert et al., 2026).

这些进展表明人工智能数学在三个互补方面取得了实质性进展：模式识别和猜想生成、数学构造和算法的搜索以及形式化验证的定理证明。These developments show substantial progress in three complementary aspects of AI mathematics: pattern recognition and conjecture generation, search for mathematical constructions and algorithms, and formally verified theorem proving.

形式验证的要求尤其重要。法学硕士生成的非正式数学论证可能包含难以察觉的微妙错误。小型可信验证检查内核接受的证明提供了更强有力的正确性保证。The requirement for formal verification is especially important. An informal mathematical argument generated by an LLM may contain a subtle error that is difficult to detect. A proof accepted by a small trusted proof-checking kernel provides a much stronger guarantee of correctness.

2026 年达到了一个重要的里程碑，内部 OpenAI 推理模型自动反驳了 Erdős 长期以来的平面单位距离猜想。为无限多个 𝑛 构建的模型，至少包含 𝑛 的平面点集A key milestone was reached in 2026, when an internal OpenAI reasoning model autonomously disproved Erdős’s long-standing planar unit-distance conjecture. The model constructed, for infinitely many 𝑛 , planar point sets containing at least 𝑛！%&!%&使用离散几何和代数数论的深层结果之间的意外联系，计算某些固定 𝛿> 0 的单位距离（OpenAI，2026）。随后，包括诺加·阿隆 (Noga Alon) 和蒂姆·高尔斯 (Tim Gowers) 在内的外部数学家小组对原始证明进行了检查、简化和情境化。 Gowers 将这一结果描述为人工智能数学的里程碑，并认为它达到了最强数学期刊所期望的标准（Alon et al., 2026）。这与解决基准问题或奥林匹克问题有本质上的不同：通用人工智能系统自主地解决了一个突出的、长期存在的开放问题，并引入了一个专家界没有注意到的令人惊讶的数学结构。unit distances for some fixed 𝛿> 0, using an unexpected connection between discrete geometry and deep results from algebraic number theory (OpenAI, 2026). The original proof was subsequently checked, simplified and contextualised by an external group of mathematicians including Noga Alon and Tim Gowers. Gowers described the result as a milestone in AI mathematics and judged it to be of the standard expected by the strongest mathematical journals (Alon et al., 2026). This is qualitatively different from solving benchmark or Olympiad problems: a generalpurpose AI system autonomously resolved a prominent, long-standing open problem and introduced a surprising mathematical construction that had escaped the expert community.

5.3.8.人工智能科学家的人工智能数学5.3.8. AI Mathematics within an AI Scientist

人工智能数学家和人工智能科学家并不相同。数学通过证明建立与公理相关的结论。经验科学通过观察和实验建立关于物理世界的临时信念。人工智能数学家可以证明某个结论是从指定的数学模型得出的。仅通过证据无法证明该模型描述了物理世界。这需要实验证据。An AI mathematician and an AI Scientist are not the same. Mathematics establishes conclusions relative to axioms through proof. Empirical science establishes provisional beliefs about the physical world through observation and experiment. An AI mathematician can prove that a conclusion follows from a specified mathematical model. It cannot establish through proof alone that the model describes the physical world. That requires experimental evidence.

未来的人工智能科学家将整合数学和经验推理：提出科学假设；将假设表达为数学模型；得出其可观察的后果；将这些结果与实验数据进行比较；修改模型或其概率；制定模型所需的新数学猜想；并利用AI数学家和证明助手来证明相关结果。Future AI Scientists will integrate mathematical and empirical reasoning: formulate a scientific hypothesis; express the hypothesis as a mathematical model; derive its observable consequences; compare those consequences with experimental data; revise the model or its probability; formulate new mathematical conjectures required by the model; and use AI mathematicians and proof assistants to prove relevant results.

因此，数学证明将成为人工智能科学家的内部工具。它将证明推论、验证算法、分析限制和不变性，并确定所提出的方程是否遵循背景理论。Mathematical proof will therefore become an internal tool of AI Scientists. It will certify deductions, verify algorithms, analyse limits and invariances, and establish whether proposed equations follow from background theories.

与人工智能科学家相比，人工智能数学家的一个问题是，数学哲学不如经验科学哲学清晰。对于数学对象是被发现还是发明、什么构成了数学理解、或者非正式的解释性见解与正式证明之间的关系，目前还没有达成共识。One problem with AI mathematicians, compared with AI Scientists, is that the philosophy of mathematics is less clear than the philosophy of empirical science. There is no consensus on whether mathematical objects are discovered or invented, what constitutes mathematical understanding, or how informal explanatory insight relates to formal proof.

26

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

然而，数学为经过验证的人工智能推理提供了最可靠的领域。证明助手创造了可以机械检查每个推论的环境。这种严格性与实证实验的结合对于人工智能科学家来说至关重要，他们不仅能够拟合数据，而且能够发展深入、普遍和值得信赖的科学理论。Yet mathematics provides the most reliable domain for verified AI reasoning. Proof assistants create environments in which every inference can be checked mechanically. The integration of this rigour with empirical experimentation will be essential to AI Scientists capable not merely of fitting data, but of developing deep, general and trustworthy scientific theories.

5.4.神经网络5.4. Neural Networks

数学、逻辑和概率是传统的科学语言。直到最近，它们还是机器学习模型表达假设的首选语言。随着深度神经网络 (DNN) 的兴起，这种情况发生了变化（LeCun 等人，2015 年；Goodfellow 等人，2016 年）。 DNN 现在负责机器学习在科学中的许多最成功的应用，包括蛋白质结构和分子相互作用预测、蛋白质设计、材料发现、天气预报、医学成像以及用于物理模拟的快速替代模型的构建（Jumper 等人，2021；Merchant 等人，2023；Abramson 等人，2024；Bodnar 等人，2025）。Mathematics, logic and probability are the traditional languages of science. Until recently, they were also the preferred languages in which machine-learning models expressed their hypotheses. This changed with the rise of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) (LeCun et al., 2015; Goodfellow et al., 2016). DNNs are now responsible for many of the most successful applications of machine learning to science, including proteinstructure and molecular-interaction prediction, protein design, materials discovery, weather forecasting, medical imaging and the construction of fast surrogate models for physical simulations (Jumper et al., 2021; Merchant et al., 2023; Abramson et al., 2024; Bodnar et al., 2025).

传统的科学语言是象征性的、明确的。原则上，数学变量、逻辑谓词或概率参数的含义可以独立于其发生的特定计算来表述。因此，可以通过显式规则来检查、交流和操作以符号模型表达的知识。Traditional scientific languages are symbolic and explicit. The meaning of a mathematical variable, logical predicate or probabilistic parameter can, in principle, be stated independently of the particular calculation in which it occurs. The knowledge expressed in a symbolic model can therefore be inspected, communicated and manipulated through explicit rules.

相比之下，DNN 以子符号和隐式形式表达知识。他们的知识分布在大量数值参数（权重、偏差、嵌入和激活值）中，这些参数共同定义了一个复杂的函数。单个参数通常没有简单的科学解释。相同的概念可以分布在许多参数中，而单个参数可以贡献许多不同的概念。In contrast, DNNs express knowledge in a sub-symbolic and implicit form. Their knowledge is distributed across large numbers of numerical parameters—weights, biases, embeddings and activation values— which collectively define a complex function. Individual parameters generally have no simple scientific interpretation. The same concept may be distributed across many parameters, while a single parameter may contribute to many different concepts.

因此，经过训练的 DNN 既是模型，又是可执行语言。它的参数指定从输入到输出的计算转换，但它们通常不提供网络学到的科学规律的明确声明。该网络可以做出高度准确的预测，而无需以人类科学家可以理解的形式揭示这些规律。A trained DNN is therefore both a model and an executable language. Its parameters specify a computational transformation from inputs to outputs, but they do not normally provide an explicit statement of the scientific regularities that the network has learned. The network may make highly accurate predictions without revealing those regularities in a form that a human scientist can understand.

5.4.1.可微表示学习5.4.1. Differentiable representation learning

DNN 的成功基于其对多层可微变换的使用。在训练过程中，网络预测中的错误通过这些层向后传播，允许基于梯度的优化同时改变数百万或数十亿个参数（Rumelhart 等人，1986）。The success of DNNs is based on their use of multiple layers of differentiable transformations. During training, errors in the network’s predictions are propagated backwards through these layers, allowing gradient-based optimisation to alter millions or billions of parameters simultaneously (Rumelhart et al., 1986).

深度网络特别强大，因为它们可以学习表示和预测。早期的机器学习系统通常依赖人类专家来选择信息丰富的描述符或特征。相反，DNN 可以直接从相对较弱的输入中学习表示的层次结构。在计算机视觉中，早期的层可能表示局部边缘和纹理，而后面的层表示形状和对象。在分子科学中，网络可以学习局部化学环境、官能团、三维几何和远程相互作用的表示。Deep networks are particularly powerful because they learn representations as well as predictions. Earlier machine-learning systems generally depended on human experts to select informative descriptors or features. DNNs can instead learn a hierarchy of representations directly from relatively weak inputs. In computer vision, early layers may represent local edges and textures, while later layers represent shapes and objects. In molecular science, a network may learn representations of local chemical environments, functional groups, three-dimensional geometry and long-range interactions.

这个过程称为表征学习。它使 DNN 能够将最初较差的表示（例如图像像素、氨基酸序列或原子坐标列表）转换为更容易进行目标预测的潜在表示。端到端学习共同优化表示和最终的科学任务。This process is termed representation learning. It enables a DNN to transform an initially poor representation—such as image pixels, an amino-acid sequence or a list of atomic coordinates—into a latent representation in which the target prediction is easier to make. End-to-end learning jointly optimises the representation and the final scientific task.

大多数 DNN 的权重都是实数，现代系统经常使用降低的数值精度进行训练或操作。当相位、振荡、The weights of most DNNs are real numbers, and modern systems frequently train or operate using reduced numerical precision. Complex-valued weights may be advantageous where phase, oscillation,

27

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

干涉、旋转或波传播是基础，因为它们共同表示幅度和相位，并且可以为电磁场、量子系统、磁共振数据、声学和信号处理提供自然表示（Trabelsi 等人，2018 年；Lee 等人，2022 年）。然而，复值网络在复数自然产生的领域之外仍然不常见，部分原因是实值软件、硬件和优化方法更加成熟。interference, rotation or wave propagation are fundamental, because they represent amplitude and phase jointly and can provide natural representations for electromagnetic fields, quantum systems, magnetic-resonance data, acoustics and signal processing (Trabelsi et al., 2018; Lee et al., 2022). Complexvalued networks nevertheless remain uncommon outside domains in which complex quantities arise naturally, partly because real-valued software, hardware and optimisation methods are more mature.

5.4.2.架构归纳偏差5.4.2. Architectural inductive biases

DNN 不是单一模型类。不同的网络架构施加不同的归纳偏差：对数据中可能出现的结构的假设。DNNs are not a single model class. Different network architectures impose different inductive biases: assumptions about the structures that are likely to occur in the data.

卷积神经网络编码这样的假设：相似的局部模式可能出现在不同的空间位置。循环网络对顺序处理和记忆进行编码。 Transformer 使用注意力来建模元素之间的交互，而不管它们在序列中的分离情况（Vaswani 等人，2017）。图神经网络（GNN）直接表示实体及其关系，使其特别适合分子、材料、生物网络和物理系统。Convolutional neural networks encode the assumption that similar local patterns may occur at different spatial positions. Recurrent networks encode sequential processing and memory. Transformers use attention to model interactions between elements irrespective of their separation in a sequence (Vaswani et al., 2017). Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) represent entities and their relationships directly, making them particularly suitable for molecules, materials, biological networks and physical systems.

科学网络越来越多地编码已知的物理对称性。如果分子旋转，标量能量应保持不变，而预测的力应随分子旋转。等变神经网络保证其输出在旋转、反射、平移或其他对称操作下正确转换。例如，NequIP 架构将三维欧几里得等变性融入到学习的原子间势中，并通过比许多早期方法少得多的训练示例实现了高精度（Batzner 等人，2022）。Scientific networks increasingly encode known physical symmetries. A scalar energy should remain unchanged if a molecule is rotated, whereas a predicted force should rotate with the molecule. Equivariant neural networks guarantee that their outputs transform correctly under rotations, reflections, translations or other symmetry operations. The NequIP architecture, for example, incorporates threedimensional Euclidean equivariance into learned interatomic potentials and achieves high accuracy with substantially fewer training examples than many earlier approaches (Batzner et al., 2022).

对称性的结合表明符号科学知识和神经学习之间的区别并不是绝对的。已知的科学原理可以纳入 DNN 的结构中，而不仅仅是作为训练示例提供。守恒定律、几何对称性、量纲关系和微分方程可以通过架构、损失函数或可微模拟器进行编码。The incorporation of symmetry demonstrates that the distinction between symbolic scientific knowledge and neural learning is not absolute. Known scientific principles can be incorporated into the structure of a DNN rather than merely supplied as training examples. Conservation laws, geometric symmetries, dimensional relationships and differential equations may be encoded through architecture, loss functions or differentiable simulators.

扩散模型提供了另一个重要的架构。他们学会扭转结构逐渐被噪音破坏的过程。经过训练后，他们可以通过从噪声开始并反复对其进行去噪来生成新样本。扩散模型现在用于生成图像、分子、蛋白质和三维生物分子结构。 AlphaFold 3 使用基于扩散的组件来生成生物分子复合物的坐标（Abramson 等人，2024）。Diffusion models provide another important architecture. They learn to reverse a process in which structure is progressively corrupted by noise. Once trained, they can generate new samples by starting from noise and repeatedly denoising it. Diffusion models are now used to generate images, molecules, proteins and three-dimensional biomolecular structures. AlphaFold 3 uses a diffusion-based component to generate the coordinates of biomolecular complexes (Abramson et al., 2024).

神经算子将 DNN 从有限维向量之间的映射扩展到函数空间之间的映射。它们作为微分方程组解的学习近似非常重要。它们作为替代模型的使用及其局限性将在第 8 节中讨论。Neural operators extend DNNs from mappings between finite-dimensional vectors to mappings between function spaces. They are important as learned approximations to families of differential-equation solutions. Their use as surrogate models, together with their limitations, is discussed in Section 8.

5.4.3.比例和基础模型5.4.3. Scale and foundation models

目前没有其他形式的机器学习能够学习规模与最大的 DNN 相当的模型，或者能够从相当数量的异构数据中有效地学习。 DNN 可以包含数十亿甚至数万亿个可调节参数，并且可以使用文本、图像、序列、结构、模拟和实验测量进行训练。No other form of machine learning is currently capable of learning models comparable in size to the largest DNNs or of learning effectively from comparable quantities of heterogeneous data. DNNs can contain billions or even trillions of adjustable parameters and can be trained using text, images, sequences, structures, simulations and experimental measurements.

我们还不知道这种主导地位是否源自神经网络作为假设语言的内在属性，或者源自投入于神经网络的大量研究工作、数据、专用硬件和财务资源。神经网络的可微性允许使用梯度下降进行有效的优化，而它们的模块化架构自然地映射到高度并行的图形上We do not yet know whether this dominance arises from an intrinsic property of neural networks as a hypothesis language, or from the vastly greater research effort, data, specialised hardware and financial resources devoted to them. The differentiability of neural networks permits efficient optimisation using gradient descent, while their modular architectures map naturally onto highly parallel graphical-

28

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

处理硬件。这些实用特性促成了更大的数据集、更大的模型和更大的计算量不断提高性能的循环。processing hardware. These practical properties have enabled a cycle in which larger datasets, larger models and greater computation repeatedly improve performance.

基础模型经过广泛的数据训练，随后适应多种任务。基础模型不是为每个科学问题开发单独的模型，而是学习领域的一般潜在表示。然后可以对其进行提示、微调或连接到专业模块。A foundation model is trained on a broad body of data and subsequently adapted to multiple tasks. Instead of developing a separate model for every scientific problem, a foundation model learns a general latent representation of a domain. It can then be prompted, fine-tuned or connected to specialist modules.

已经为语言、蛋白质、分子、材料、基因组、天气和更广泛的地球系统开发了科学基础模型。它们的重要性不仅在于规模，还在于它们在任务之间转移知识的潜力。然而，在没有明确结构标签的情况下训练的蛋白质模型仍然可以获得二级结构、残基接触、功能和进化关系的潜在表示。Scientific foundation models have been developed for language, proteins, molecules, materials, genomes, weather and the wider Earth system. Their importance lies not simply in scale, but in their potential to transfer knowledge between tasks. A protein model trained without explicit structural labels can nevertheless acquire latent representations of secondary structure, residue contacts, function and evolutionary relationships.

基础模型还允许生成科学。他们不仅可以预测现有物体的特性，还可以提出满足特定约束的新蛋白质、分子和材料。但生成仍然取决于训练数据的质量和覆盖范围，并且生成的对象需要计算或实验验证。Foundation models also permit generative science. Rather than only predicting properties of existing objects, they can propose new proteins, molecules and materials that satisfy specified constraints. But generation remains dependent on the quality and coverage of the training data, and generated objects require computational or experimental validation.

5.4.4. AlphaFold 和蛋白质基础模型5.4.4. AlphaFold and protein foundation models

机器学习在科学领域最引人注目的应用是 AlphaFold 2 的开发，用于根据氨基酸序列预测蛋白质的三维结构（Jumper 等人，2021）。The highest-profile application of machine learning to science has been the development of AlphaFold 2 for predicting the three-dimensional structures of proteins from their amino-acid sequences (Jumper et al., 2021).

Anfinsen 的工作表明，在适当的条件下，蛋白质采用其天然结构所需的信息是在其氨基酸序列中编码的（Anfinsen，1973）。因此，研究人员花了几十年的时间试图学习从序列到结构的映射。早期的机器学习工作集中于中间任务，例如预测二级结构，首先通过归纳学习，然后通过可理解的机器诱导规则（King，1987；King＆Sternberg，1990）。Anfinsen’s work established that, under appropriate conditions, the information required for a protein to adopt its native structure is encoded in its amino-acid sequence (Anfinsen, 1973). Researchers therefore spent decades attempting to learn the mapping from sequence to structure. Early machine-learning work concentrated on intermediate tasks such as predicting secondary structure, first through inductive learning and subsequently through comprehensible machine-induced rules (King, 1987; King & Sternberg, 1990).

进步是由三个相互促进的发展推动的：可用计算能力的巨大增加；蛋白质序列和结构数据的巨大增加；以及能够表示更复杂函数的学习算法的开发。Progress was driven by three mutually reinforcing developments: an enormous increase in available computing power; an enormous increase in protein sequence and structural data; and the development of learning algorithms capable of representing much more complex functions.

蛋白质结构预测系统的有效性通过结构预测批判性评估（CASP）进行客观评估，这是一项两年一次的盲实验，参与者预测尚未公开发布的结构（Moult 等，1995）。在 2020 年的 CASP14 中，AlphaFold 2 的准确度大大超过了之前的系统，标志着蛋白质结构预测向前迈出了质的一步。The effectiveness of protein-structure-prediction systems is evaluated objectively through the Critical Assessment of Structure Prediction (CASP), a biennial blind experiment in which participants predict structures that have not yet been publicly released (Moult et al., 1995). In CASP14 in 2020, AlphaFold 2 achieved a level of accuracy substantially beyond that of previous systems, marking a qualitative step forward in protein-structure prediction.

AlphaFold 2 将注意力、几何推理、进化信息和迭代细化结合在高度复杂的端到端网络中。它没有明确地从蛋白质折叠的紧凑符号理论中推导出蛋白质结构。相反，它学习从序列和进化信息到空间结构的强大隐式映射。尽管其运作的各个组成部分已被了解，但仍无法对网络为何运行良好进行完整的机械解释。AlphaFold 2 combines attention, geometric reasoning, evolutionary information and iterative refinement in a highly complex end-to-end network. It does not explicitly derive a protein structure from a compact symbolic theory of protein folding. Instead, it learns a powerful implicit mapping from sequence and evolutionary information to spatial structure. Although components of its operation are understood, a complete mechanistic explanation of why the network works so well remains unavailable.

AlphaFold 3 将任务从预测蛋白质结构扩展到预测蛋白质、核酸、小分子、离子和修饰残基的联合结构和相互作用（Abramson 等人，2024）。这很重要，因为生物功能通常取决于分子相互作用而不是孤立的蛋白质结构。AlphaFold 3 broadened the task from predicting protein structures to predicting the joint structures and interactions of proteins, nucleic acids, small molecules, ions and modified residues (Abramson et al., 2024). This matters because biological function generally depends on molecular interaction rather than on isolated protein structure.

29

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

蛋白质语言模型提供了一种补充方法。他们将氨基酸序列视为进化产生的语言。当对数亿个蛋白质序列进行训练时，结构和功能信息会在其潜在表示中出现。Protein language models provide a complementary approach. They treat amino-acid sequences as a language generated by evolution. When trained on hundreds of millions of protein sequences, structural and functional information emerges within their latent representations.

ESMFold 证明，原子级蛋白质结构可以直接从大型蛋白质语言模型的表示中推断出来，而不需要为每个预测构建昂贵的多序列比对 (Lin et al., 2023)。该模型能够预测超过 6 亿个宏基因组蛋白的结构。ESMFold demonstrated that atomic-level protein structures could be inferred directly from the representations of a large protein language model, without requiring the expensive construction of a multiple-sequence alignment for every prediction (Lin et al., 2023). The model enabled the prediction of structures for more than 600 million metagenomic proteins.

ESM3 跨序列、结构和功能扩展了蛋白质语言模型。它生成了一种功能性荧光蛋白，与最接近的已知荧光蛋白只有 58% 的序列同一性，这种差异程度估计相当于 5 亿多年的自然进化（Hayes 等人，2025 年）。这表明神经网络不仅可以在已知的自然实例中进行插值：它们还可以在序列空间的偏远区域生成功能性生物结构。ESM3 extended protein language modelling across sequence, structure and function. It generated a functional fluorescent protein with only 58% sequence identity to the nearest known fluorescent proteins—a degree of divergence estimated to correspond to more than 500 million years of natural evolution (Hayes et al., 2025). This demonstrates that neural networks can do more than interpolate among known natural examples: they can generate functional biological structures in remote regions of sequence space.

5.4.5。跨科学领域的神经网络5.4.5. Neural networks across the sciences

DNN 在科学领域的成功远远超出了结构生物学的范围。在材料科学中，GNoME 使用图神经网络和大规模主动学习来预测无机晶体的稳定性。该系统识别了 220 万个候选稳定结构，其中包括更新的热力学稳定性凸包上的 381,000 个结构，代表了先前已知的计算目录的数量级扩展（Merchant 等人，2023）。The success of DNNs in science extends far beyond structural biology. In materials science, GNoME used graph neural networks and large-scale active learning to predict the stability of inorganic crystals. The system identified 2.2 million candidate stable structures, including 381,000 structures on an updated convex hull of thermodynamic stability, representing an order-of-magnitude expansion of the previously known computational catalogue (Merchant et al., 2023).

在天气预报方面，Pangu-Weather 和 GraphCast 证明，根据历史大气再分析数据训练的深度神经网络在许多中期预报措施上可以等于或超过领先的确定性数值系统，同时生成预报的速度要快得多（Bi 等人，2023 年；Lam 等人，2023 年）。In weather forecasting, Pangu-Weather and GraphCast demonstrated that deep neural networks trained on historical atmospheric reanalysis data could equal or exceed leading deterministic numerical systems on many medium-range forecasting measures while producing forecasts much more rapidly (Bi et al., 2023; Lam et al., 2023).

奥罗拉（Aurora）将这种方法扩展为地球系统的基础模型。它接受了超过一百万小时的不同地球物理数据的预训练，随后适应了天气、空气污染、海浪和热带气旋预报任务（Bodnar 等人，2025）。Aurora extended this approach into a foundation model for the Earth system. It was pretrained on more than one million hours of diverse geophysical data and subsequently adapted to weather, air-pollution, ocean-wave and tropical-cyclone forecasting tasks (Bodnar et al., 2025).

这些模型不需要完全取代数学物理。 NeuralGCM 将大规模大气动力学的可微数值求解器与未解决的物理过程的学习神经表示相结合。它可以进行天气和气候模拟，同时保留明确的动力核心（Kochkov 等人，2024）。这种混合模型表明，最有效的科学系统通常会将神经学习与已建立的数学理论相结合，而不是在它们之间专门进行选择。These models need not replace mathematical physics completely. NeuralGCM combines a differentiable numerical solver for large-scale atmospheric dynamics with learned neural representations of unresolved physical processes. It can produce weather and climate simulations while retaining an explicit dynamical core (Kochkov et al., 2024). Such hybrid models suggest that the most effective scientific systems will often integrate neural learning with established mathematical theories rather than choosing exclusively between them.

DNN 也越来越多地用作学习原子间势、量子力学计算的替代模型、流体和气候模拟的模拟器、分子结构的生成器和逆向设计系统的组件。它们的速度允许评估大量候选假设或设计，而仅使用直接物理模拟是不可行的。DNNs are also increasingly used as learned interatomic potentials, surrogate models for quantummechanical calculations, emulators of fluid and climate simulations, generators of molecular structures and components of inverse-design systems. Their speed permits quantities of candidate hypotheses or designs to be evaluated that would be infeasible using direct physical simulation alone.

5.4.6。神经网络和科学解释5.4.6. Neural networks and scientific explanation

DNN 的成功重新开启了关于科学中观察到的实体与理论实体之间关系的长达一个世纪的逻辑实证主义争论（Carnap，1974）。逻辑实证主义者强调可观察的关系，并对无法直接观察的实体的形而上学承诺持怀疑态度。然而，科学实践传统上是通过不可观测的理论实体来表述的，包括场、波函数、基因、夸克和电磁四势。The success of DNNs reopens the century-old logical-positivist debate about the relationship between observed and theoretical entities in science (Carnap, 1974). Logical positivists emphasised observable relationships and were suspicious of metaphysical commitments to entities that could not be observed directly. Scientific practice, however, has traditionally been formulated through unobservable theoretical entities, including fields, wavefunctions, genes, quarks and the electromagnetic four-potential.

30

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

DNN 可能会显得很工具主义。它可以学习从观察到预测的直接映射，而不代表底层实体和机制的明确理论。如果一个网络准确地预测了实验结果，即使科学家无法解释它是如何实现这一预测的，它也可能是有用的。A DNN can appear strongly instrumentalist. It may learn a direct mapping from observations to predictions without representing an explicit theory of the underlying entities and mechanisms. If a network accurately predicts the result of an experiment, it may be useful even when scientists cannot explain how it reaches that prediction.

这就提出了一个基本问题：成功的预测是否构成科学理解？对于深入的科学理解来说，预测是必要的，但还不够。模型可以准确地进行预测，因为它已经识别出真正的因果结构，因为它捕获了稳定但无法解释的相关性，或者因为它利用了训练数据中的偶然规律。当模型仅根据从与其训练示例相同的分布中提取的数据进行评估时，这些可能性可能无法区分。This raises a fundamental question: does successful prediction constitute scientific understanding? Prediction is necessary but not sufficient for deep scientific understanding. A model may predict accurately because it has identified a genuine causal structure, because it has captured a stable but unexplained correlation, or because it has exploited an accidental regularity in its training data. These possibilities may be indistinguishable when the model is evaluated only on data drawn from the same distribution as its training examples.

DNN 特别容易受到虚假相关性的影响；分布转移；隐藏的数据泄露；校准不良；对抗性或异常输入；超出训练数据范围的故障；以及缺乏明确的因果解释。DNNs are particularly vulnerable to spurious correlations; distribution shift; hidden data leakage; poor calibration; adversarial or anomalous inputs; failures outside the range of the training data; and the absence of explicit causal interpretation.

与此同时，DNN 使预测与理论之间的简单区别变得复杂。从数据中学习的潜在表示可能会对未明确提供的具有科学意义的实体和结构进行编码。蛋白质语言模型从序列中获取结构和功能的表示。等变网络获取与物理几何一致的张量表示。神经算子学习动力学定律族的近似值。At the same time, DNNs complicate the simple distinction between prediction and theory. Latent representations learned from data may encode scientifically meaningful entities and structures that were not supplied explicitly. Protein language models acquire representations of structure and function from sequences. Equivariant networks acquire tensorial representations consistent with physical geometry. Neural operators learn approximations to families of dynamical laws.

困难在于这些表示通常是隐含的。科学家必须通过训练后分析网络来推断它们的含义。机械可解释性旨在对神经网络进行逆向工程并识别内部表示的算法、特征和概念。但解释具有数十亿参数的网络仍然是一个未解决的问题。The difficulty is that these representations are usually implicit. Scientists must infer their meaning by analysing the network after training. Mechanistic interpretability seeks to reverse-engineer neural networks and identify the algorithms, features and concepts represented internally. But interpreting networks with billions of parameters remains an unsolved problem.

5.4.7.人工智能科学家中的神经网络5.4.7. Neural networks within AI Scientists

未来的人工智能科学家将需要 DNN，但它们不能仅仅基于 DNN。 DNN 提供卓越的感知和模式识别能力；从原始数据中学习表示；分析图像、光谱、序列和传感器输出；逼近复杂函数和模拟；在自然语言和形式语言之间进行翻译；识别异常；并提出假设和实验行动。它们的缺点与形式方法的优点是互补的。神经网络自然不会提供明确的语义、有效的逻辑证明、校准概率、因果保证或透明的科学解释。Future AI Scientists will require DNNs, but they cannot be based on DNNs alone. DNNs provide exceptional capabilities for perception and pattern recognition; learning representations from raw data; analysing images, spectra, sequences and sensor outputs; approximating complex functions and simulations; translating between natural and formal languages; recognising anomalies; and proposing hypotheses and experimental actions. Their weaknesses are complementary to the strengths of formal methods. Neural networks do not naturally provide explicit semantics, valid logical proofs, calibrated probabilities, causal guarantees or transparent scientific explanations.

因此，最强大的未来架构可能是神经符号和概率的。 DNN 可以生成候选表示、假设、分子或实验方案。符号系统可以检查逻辑一致性并得出结果。概率系统可以表示不确定性并比较替代方案。因果模型可以确定哪种干预措施会区分相互竞争的解释。机器人可以执行选定的实验，所得观察结果可用于更新显式知识库和神经模型。The most powerful future architecture will therefore likely be neuro-symbolic and probabilistic. A DNN may generate a candidate representation, hypothesis, molecule or experimental protocol. A symbolic system can check logical consistency and derive consequences. A probabilistic system can represent uncertainty and compare alternatives. A causal model can determine which intervention would discriminate among competing explanations. A robot can execute the selected experiment, and the resulting observations can be used to update both the explicit knowledge base and the neural models.

在这样的人工智能科学家中，神经网络将构成灵活的、高容量的模式学习组件。逻辑、数学、概率和程序将提供明确的含义、验证和解释。Within such an AI Scientist, neural networks will constitute the flexible, high-capacity, pattern-learning component. Logic, mathematics, probability and programs will provide explicit meaning, verification and explanation.

核心的科学机会不是在符号表示和神经表示之间进行选择。就是将它们整合起来。 DNN 可以以前所未有的规模发现数据中有用的隐式结构。形式语言可以将这些结构转化为可以理解、检查、交流和实验测试的假设。The central scientific opportunity is not to choose between symbolic and neural representations. It is to integrate them. DNNs can discover useful implicit structures in data at unprecedented scale. Formal languages can transform those structures into hypotheses that can be understood, checked, communicated and experimentally tested.

31

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

6. 阅读和形式化科学文献6. Reading and Formalising the Scientific Literature

科学产生的文献比任何人能够阅读的还要多。重要证据分散在论文、图表、表格、补充文件和数据库中。人工智能科学家必须将这些庞大而无序的记录转化为明确的、可测试的知识。Science has produced more literature than any human can read. Important evidence is scattered across papers, figures, tables, supplementary files and databases. An AI Scientist must turn this vast and disorderly record into explicit, testable knowledge.

科学散文通常是不完整的并且依赖于上下文。作者省略了他们期望专业读者提供的假设，对同一实体使用多个名称，并且偶尔对不同实体使用相同的术语。主张可以在论文的一个部分中得到限定，但在其他地方没有限定地重述。实验方法通常包含诸如“过夜”、“在室温下”、“彻底清洗”或“使用标准程序”等表达方式，其含义取决于默会的学科或实验室知识。Scientific prose is often incomplete and context-dependent. Authors omit assumptions that they expect specialist readers to supply, use several names for the same entity and occasionally use the same term for different entities. Claims may be qualified in one section of a paper but restated without qualification elsewhere. Experimental methods commonly contain expressions such as ‘overnight’, ‘at room temperature’, ‘washed thoroughly’ or ‘using the standard procedure’, whose meanings depend on tacit disciplinary or laboratory knowledge.

尽管如此，自然语言仍然是不可或缺的。在合适的正式词汇出现之前，它足以灵活地描述新的观察结果和概念。它使科学家能够解释动机、类比、机制、不确定性和意义。因此，人类科学是通过散文、数学、图表、表格、计算机程序和实验记录的混合来进行的。Natural language nevertheless remains indispensable. It is flexible enough to describe new observations and concepts before a suitable formal vocabulary exists. It enables scientists to explain motivations, analogies, mechanisms, uncertainties and significance. Human science is therefore conducted through a mixture of prose, mathematics, diagrams, tables, computer programs and experimental records.

科学文献非常丰富。书目基础设施现在包含数亿研究成果的记录（Tkaczyk 等人，2026）。人类科学家在一生中只能阅读一小部分相关文献。The scientific literature is vast. Bibliographic infrastructures now contain records for hundreds of millions of research outputs (Tkaczyk et al., 2026). No human scientist can read more than a small fraction of the relevant literature during a lifetime.

这对人类科学造成了根本性的限制。相关发现可能不会被注意到，因为它们是在另一个学科中发表的、使用不熟悉的术语或被隐藏在补充信息中。矛盾的主张可能会持续存在，但不会被视为矛盾。实验可能会不必要地重复，而负面或不确定的结果会从可访问的记录中消失。因此，科学阅读的自动化对于人工智能科学家的发展至关重要。This creates a fundamental limitation on human science. Relevant findings may remain unnoticed because they were published in another discipline, use unfamiliar terminology or are buried in supplementary information. Contradictory claims may persist without being recognised as contradictions. Experiments may be repeated unnecessarily, while negative or inconclusive results disappear from the accessible record. The automation of scientific reading is therefore essential to the development of AI Scientists.

6.1.作为知识体系的科学文献6.1. The scientific literature as a knowledge system

科学论文不仅仅是连续的散文。它是一个连接背景知识、研究问题、方法、观察、分析和结论的结构化论证。不同的部分有不同的认知功能。A scientific paper is not simply a continuous body of prose. It is a structured argument connecting background knowledge, research questions, methods, observations, analyses and conclusions. Different sections have different epistemic functions.

引言描述了公认的背景，确定了知识差距并激发了调查。这些方法描述了如何产生观察结果。结果报告测量和分析。讨论解释了这些结果，将它们与之前的工作联系起来并提出了解释。补充材料可能包含正文中未出现的基本细节、附加分析和负面结果。The introduction describes the accepted background, identifies a gap in knowledge and motivates the investigation. The methods describe how observations were produced. The results report measurements and analyses. The discussion interprets those results, relates them to previous work and proposes explanations. Supplementary materials may contain essential details, additional analyses and negative findings that do not appear in the main text.

人工智能科学家必须区分这些功能。引言中的陈述可以报告普遍接受的观点、总结另一篇论文或仅仅激发当前的研究。讨论中的陈述可能是一种解释，而不是直接观察。将每个句子视为等效的事实断言会破坏论文的大部分认知结构。An AI Scientist must distinguish among these functions. A statement in the introduction may report a generally accepted view, summarise another paper or merely motivate the present study. A statement in the discussion may be an interpretation rather than a direct observation. Treating every sentence as an equivalent factual assertion would destroy much of the epistemic structure of the paper.

科学文献还分布在几个相互关联的对象中：期刊文章和会议论文；预印本和修订版；补充文件；数据集和数据库记录；软件和计算笔记本；协议；更正、表达关注和撤回；以及引用和被引用的论文。The scientific literature is also distributed across several interconnected objects: journal articles and conference papers; preprints and revised versions; supplementary files; datasets and database records; software and computational notebooks; protocols; corrections, expressions of concern and retractions; and citing and cited papers.

因此，科学知识的单位并不是孤立的文件。它是一个由主张、证据、方法、数据、版本和响应组成的网络。阅读文献需要遵循这些关系。The unit of scientific knowledge is therefore not the isolated document. It is a network of claims, evidence, methods, data, versions and responses. Reading the literature requires following these relationships.

32

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

科学主张也必须按时间来解释。一篇论文可能准确地代表了发表时可用的证据，但后来被矛盾或取代。预印本可能与其最终版本有很大不同。一篇论文在被纳入评论、数据库和语言模型训练语料库后可能会被更正或撤回。Scientific claims must also be interpreted temporally. A paper may accurately represent the evidence available when it was published but later be contradicted or superseded. Preprints may differ substantially from their final versions. A paper may be corrected or retracted after it has already been incorporated into reviews, databases and language-model training corpora.

因此，机器可读的科学文献必须保留出处和版本信息。人工智能科学家不仅需要知道所陈述的内容，还需要知道是谁、何地、何时、基于哪些证据以及该主张后来是否被纠正、质疑或复制。A machine-readable scientific literature must therefore preserve provenance and version information. An AI Scientist needs to know not only what was stated, but who stated it, where, when, on the basis of which evidence and whether the claim was later corrected, challenged or replicated.

6.2.文本挖掘6.2. Text mining

在大型语言模型 (LLM) 兴起之前，文本挖掘和自然语言处理系统主导了从科学论文中提取知识的尝试（Feldman & Sanger，2007）。文本挖掘系统可以处理非常大的文档集合并大规模识别指定形式的信息。Before the rise of Large Language Models (LLMs), text-mining and Natural Language Processing systems dominated attempts to extract knowledge from scientific papers (Feldman & Sanger, 2007). Text-mining systems can process very large document collections and identify specified forms of information at scale.

传统的科学文本挖掘通常被组织为管道。文档分为部分、句子和标记。语言工具识别语法结构。命名实体被识别并链接到标准标识符。提取涉及这些实体的关系和事件，并将结果记录存储在数据库或知识图中。Traditional scientific text mining is commonly organised as a pipeline. Documents are divided into sections, sentences and tokens. Linguistic tools identify grammatical structure. Named entities are recognised and linked to standard identifiers. Relations and events involving those entities are extracted, and the resulting records are stored in databases or knowledge graphs.

一个代表性的生物医学任务是基因和蛋白质名称的识别和标准化。系统必须首先识别指代生物实体的单词，然后将每个提及链接到正确的数据库记录。这个看似简单的任务由于对同一实体使用多个名称而变得复杂。不同实体的名称相同；缩写仅在论文中定义一次；物种之间的差异；基因、RNA 和蛋白质名称之间的重叠；用作基因符号的普通英语单词；以及术语随时间的变化。A representative biomedical task is the recognition and normalisation of gene and protein names. The system must first identify the words referring to a biological entity and then link each mention to the correct database record. This apparently simple task is complicated by the use of several names for the same entity; identical names for different entities; abbreviations defined only once in a paper; differences between species; overlap between gene, RNA and protein names; ordinary English words used as gene symbols; and changes in nomenclature over time.

仅实体识别是不够的。系统必须确定论文在已识别实体之间断言的关系。例如，它可能需要区分：Entity recognition alone is insufficient. The system must determine what relationship the paper asserts between the recognised entities. It may need to distinguish, for example, between:

  • 蛋白质 A 激活蛋白质 B。Protein A activates protein B.

  • 蛋白 A 不会激活蛋白 B。Protein A does not activate protein B.

  • 蛋白质 A 可能会激活蛋白质 B。Protein A may activate protein B.

  • 先前的研究报道蛋白 A 激活蛋白 B。Previous studies reported that protein A activates protein B.

  • 我们测试了蛋白质 A 是否激活蛋白质 B，但并未证实。这些陈述包含相似的实体和词汇，但提出了截然不同的科学主张。We tested, but did not confirm, whether protein A activates protein B. These statements contain similar entities and vocabulary but make very different scientific claims.

科学文本挖掘极大地受益于共享注释语料库和基准任务。这些资源允许根据常见示例对系统进行培训和评估。主要任务包括基因和蛋白质识别、化学和疾病识别、关系提取、分子事件提取、引文分类以及实验证据支持的陈述识别。Scientific text mining has benefited greatly from shared annotated corpora and benchmark tasks. These resources allow systems to be trained and evaluated on common examples. Major tasks have included gene and protein recognition, chemical and disease identification, relation extraction, molecular-event extraction, citation classification and the identification of statements supported by experimental evidence.

上下文神经语言模型显着提高了性能。 SciBERT 接受了科学出版物的培训，并适应了多种科学语言任务（Beltagy 等人，2019）。 BioBERT 和 PubMedBERT 接受了生物医学文献的训练，在实体识别、关系提取和生物医学问答方面取得了良好的成果（Lee et al., 2020；Gu et al., 2021）。 scispaCy 等软件使科学 NLP 模型在实际处理流程中可用（Neumann 等人，2019）。Contextual neural language models improved performance substantially. SciBERT was trained on scientific publications and adapted to several scientific-language tasks (Beltagy et al., 2019). BioBERT and PubMedBERT were trained on biomedical literature and achieved strong results in entity recognition, relation extraction and biomedical question answering (Lee et al., 2020; Gu et al., 2021). Software such as scispaCy made scientific NLP models available within practical processing pipelines (Neumann et al., 2019).

传统的文本挖掘系统保留了几个优点。它们的目标输出通常是明确定义的，它们的准确性可以根据带注释的示例来衡量，并且可以检查各个组件。它们的弱点是每个新任务通常需要新的语料库、注释方案和Traditional text-mining systems retain several advantages. Their target outputs are usually defined explicitly, their accuracy can be measured against annotated examples, and individual components can be inspected. Their weakness is that each new task often requires a new corpus, annotation scheme and

33

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

专业管道。错误也在阶段之间传播：未被识别的实体不能参与随后提取的关系。specialised pipeline. Errors also propagate between stages: an entity that is not recognised cannot participate in a subsequently extracted relation.

法学硕士提供了更灵活的选择。同一模型可以根据自然语言指令和示例提取不同类别的信息。但当需要精确的跨度识别、详尽的检索和固定注释策略的一致应用时，专业模型可能仍然优越。因此，基于法学硕士的提取应该补充而不是简单地取代已建立的文本挖掘方法。LLMs provide a more flexible alternative. The same model can extract different classes of information in response to natural-language instructions and examples. But specialist models may remain superior when exact span recognition, exhaustive retrieval and consistent application of a fixed annotation policy are required. LLM-based extraction should therefore complement, rather than simply replace, established text-mining methods.

6.3.文献检索6.3. Searching and retrieving the literature

阅读科学文献的首要要求是找到相关文献。传统的书目检索主要基于单词、短语、主题分类、引文和元数据。现代语义搜索系统使用学习的表示来检索其含义与查询相关的文档，即使它们不包含相同的术语。The first requirement for reading the scientific literature is finding the relevant documents. Traditional bibliographic search is based principally on words, phrases, subject classifications, citations and metadata. Modern semantic search systems use learned representations to retrieve documents whose meaning is related to a query even when they do not contain the same terminology.

科学检索异常困难，因为术语因学科而异并且随着时间的推移而变化。相同的基本概念在分子生物学、医学和化学中可能有不同的描述。相反，同一个术语在不同领域可能具有不相关的含义。Scientific retrieval is unusually difficult because terminology varies between disciplines and changes through time. The same underlying concept may be described differently in molecular biology, medicine and chemistry. Conversely, the same term may have unrelated meanings in different fields.

引文网络提供与文本相似性互补的信息。即使词汇有很大差异，论文也可以引用它扩展、挑战或应用的工作。因此，同引和书目耦合方法可以识别仅从文本中无法明显看出的知识关系。Citation networks provide information that is complementary to textual similarity. A paper may cite the work it extends, challenges or applies even when the vocabulary differs substantially. Co-citation and bibliographic-coupling methods can therefore identify intellectual relationships that are not apparent from the text alone.

检索应该是迭代的。最初的论文揭示了新的术语、作者、方法和引用的作品，进而指导后续的搜索。该系统还应该刻意寻找反对其当前解释的证据，而不是只检索似乎支持它的论文。Retrieval should be iterative. Initial papers reveal new terminology, authors, methods and cited work, which in turn guide subsequent searches. The system should also search deliberately for evidence that opposes its current interpretation rather than retrieving only papers that appear to support it.

目标不是检索尽可能多的论文。它是确定足以代表相关证据、方法、理论立场和分歧的最小文献体。即使对于人工智能科学家来说，阅读所有内容既没有必要，也没有计算效率。The objective is not to retrieve the largest possible number of papers. It is to identify the smallest body of literature sufficient to represent the relevant evidence, methods, theoretical positions and disagreements. Reading everything is neither necessary nor computationally efficient, even for an AI Scientist.

6.4.文献综合6.4. Literature synthesis

检索论文不同于综合其内容。有用的综合必须确定主要主张，确定它们是如何得到支持的，并解释论文之间的相互关系。Retrieving papers is different from synthesising their contents. A useful synthesis must identify the principal claims, determine how they are supported and explain how the papers relate to one another.

法学硕士非常适合总结单个文档，但科学综合需要的不仅仅是流畅的压缩。该系统必须区分共识和重复、独立证据和重复引用同一原始结果、以及直接实验支持和猜测。LLMs are well suited to summarising individual documents, but scientific synthesis requires more than fluent compression. The system must distinguish consensus from repetition, independent evidence from repeated citation of the same original result, and direct experimental support from speculation.

检索增强生成将语言模型连接到外部文档集合（Lewis 等人，2020）。检索相关段落并将其放置在模型的上下文中，从而使生成的答案能够基于可识别的来源，而不是仅仅依赖于隐式存储在模型参数中的信息。Retrieval-Augmented Generation connects a language model to an external document collection (Lewis et al., 2020). Relevant passages are retrieved and placed in the model’s context, allowing the generated answer to be grounded in identifiable sources rather than relying solely on information stored implicitly in the model’s parameters.

OpenScholar 将这种方法应用于科学文献综合。它将检索、重新排名、生成和自我评估与大量开放获取论文相结合，生成明确引用支持来源的答案（Asai 等人，2026）。与不接地气的对话模型相比，此类系统更适合科学用途。OpenScholar applies this approach to scientific literature synthesis. It combines retrieval, reranking, generation and self-evaluation with a large collection of open-access papers, producing answers with explicit citations to supporting sources (Asai et al., 2026). Such systems are much better suited to scientific use than ungrounded conversational models.

34

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

然而，引用的存在并不能保证该引用支持相关的主张。系统可能会检索相关论文，但会歪曲其结论、省略重要的限定条件或在需要主要证据时引用评论。因此，必须在单个声明的层面上检查引用的准确性。Yet the presence of a citation does not guarantee that the citation supports the associated claim. A system may retrieve a relevant paper but misrepresent its conclusion, omit an important qualification or cite a review when the primary evidence is required. Citation accuracy must therefore be checked at the level of individual claims.

值得信赖的文献综述应至少区分以下内容： 直接报告的观察结果；对这些观察结果进行统计分析；因果或机械解释；作者提出的假设；结果独立重现；研究结果仅得到一个实验室的支持；相互矛盾的证据；方法的局限性；以及审查系统本身得出的结论。A trustworthy literature synthesis should distinguish at least the following: directly reported observations; statistical analyses of those observations; causal or mechanistic interpretations; hypotheses proposed by the authors; results reproduced independently; findings supported only by one laboratory; conflicting evidence; methodological limitations; and conclusions generated by the reviewing system itself.

当分析相同的数据集或来自相同的实验队列时，综合还应避免将多篇论文计算为独立证据。证据的独立性比引用的数量更重要。The synthesis should also avoid counting multiple papers as independent evidence when they analyse the same dataset or derive from the same experimental cohort. Independence of evidence matters more than the number of citations.

系统评价提供了一个重要的模型。他们的方法旨在定义搜索标准、记录纳入和排除决定、评估证据质量并使合成可重复。机器学习可以加速文档筛选和信息提取，但标准和决策应保持明确（Marshall & Wallace，2019）。Systematic reviews provide an important model. Their methods aim to define search criteria, record inclusion and exclusion decisions, assess the quality of evidence and make the synthesis reproducible. Machine learning can accelerate document screening and information extraction, but the criteria and decisions should remain explicit (Marshall & Wallace, 2019).

文献综合还可以通过连接以前独立的工作主体来产生新的假设。由于此功能涉及科学思想的创造而不是单独阅读，因此第 7 节讨论基于文献的发现。Literature synthesis can also generate new hypotheses by connecting previously separate bodies of work. Because this function concerns the creation of scientific ideas rather than reading alone, literature-based discovery is treated in Section 7.

6.5.多模式科学文献6.5. Multimodal scientific documents

很大一部分科学信息并不包含在论文的散文中。它出现在表格、图形、方程、化学结构、光谱、图像和补充文件中。A large proportion of scientific information is not contained in the prose of a paper. It appears in tables, figures, equations, chemical structures, spectra, images and supplementary files.

数字通常是实验证据的主要表现形式。他们的标题可能不会描述每一个相关的观察结果，而正文可能只讨论图中的选定部分。表格可能包含从未出现在句子中的数字结果。实验条件可以分布在方法、图例和补充电子表格中。Figures are often the primary presentation of experimental evidence. Their captions may not describe every relevant observation, while the main text may discuss only a selected part of the figure. Tables may contain numerical results that never appear in sentences. Experimental conditions may be distributed across the methods, a figure legend and a supplementary spreadsheet.

因此，阅读科学论文需要多模态解释。系统必须将文本中的陈述与绘制的测量结果联系起来；误差线和统计注释；标签和图例；显微镜或医学图像；分子和蛋白质结构；数学方程；表格值；以及补充方法和数据。Reading a scientific paper therefore requires multimodal interpretation. A system must connect statements in the text to plotted measurements; error bars and statistical annotations; labels and legends; microscopy or medical images; molecular and protein structures; mathematical equations; tabulated values; and supplementary methods and data.

文档布局也传达了意义。标题表明段落的作用；脚注可以限定表格；上标可以将一个值与统计检验联系起来。将论文转换为无差别的文本流会丢弃这种结构。Document layout also conveys meaning. A heading indicates the role of a passage; a footnote may qualify a table; a superscript may connect a value to a statistical test. Converting a paper into an undifferentiated stream of text discards this structure.

科学文献系统应保留提取信息的坐标和文档上下文。一个主张不仅应该链接到一篇论文，还应该链接到支持它的特定句子、表格单元格、图形面板或方程。Scientific literature systems should preserve the coordinates and document context of extracted information. A claim should be linked not only to a paper, but to the particular sentence, table cell, figure panel or equation that supports it.

由于需要整合文本和图形，因此获取完整的文章变得非常重要。摘要不足以解决许多科学问题。他们压缩了作者首选的结论，并经常省略局限性、不成功的结果和详细的方法。The need to integrate text and figures makes access to complete articles important. Abstracts are insufficient for many scientific questions. They compress the authors’ preferred conclusion and frequently omit limitations, unsuccessful results and detailed methods.

35

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

6.6。可靠性、幻觉和出处6.6. Reliability, hallucination and provenance

一个旨在阅读科学的系统必须区分语言的合理性和科学真理。法学硕士经过训练可以生成可能的标记序列。它本质上不限于复制真实的主张，甚至不限于复制其源材料中出现的主张。因此，它可以发明事实、引文、论文和参考文献。A system designed to read science must distinguish linguistic plausibility from scientific truth. An LLM is trained to generate probable sequences of tokens. It is not inherently constrained to reproduce claims that are true or even claims that occurred in its source material. It can therefore invent facts, quotations, papers and references.

卡拉狄加展示了科学语言模型的潜力和危险。它接受了大约 4800 万篇科学论文、参考材料和相关示例的培训，旨在提供科学知识的通用接口（Taylor 等人，2022）。但它也产生了听起来很权威的虚假声明和捏造的参考资料。其公开示威在发布后不久就被撤回。Galactica demonstrated both the potential and the danger of scientific language models. It was trained on approximately 48 million scientific papers, reference materials and related examples, and was intended to provide a general interface to scientific knowledge (Taylor et al., 2022). But it also generated authoritative-sounding false claims and fabricated references. Its public demonstration was withdrawn shortly after release.

失败不仅仅是由于科学训练不足造成的。对更科学的语言进行训练可以使模型在科学话语中更加流利，而不会使每个生成的陈述更加可靠。The failure was not caused simply by insufficient scientific training. Training on more scientific language can make a model more fluent in scientific discourse without making every generated statement more reliable.

因此，科学文献系统需要外部验证。每个实质性陈述都应与已确定的来源相关联，并且应检查来源以确定其是否支持准确的主张。参考文献应该从书目数据库中检索，而不是作为文本自由生成。该记录将使文献分析具有可重复性。另一位科学家或人工智能系统可以检查相同的证据并确定合成是否合理。Scientific literature systems therefore require external verification. Each substantive statement should be connected to an identified source, and the source should be checked to determine whether it supports the precise claim. References should be retrieved from bibliographic databases rather than generated freely as text. This record would make the literature analysis reproducible. Another scientist or AI system could inspect the same evidence and determine whether the synthesis was justified.

该系统还应该区分缺乏证据和缺乏证据。未能检索到支持主张的文件并不能证明此类文件不存在。文献可能难以获取、索引错误、未发表或使用意想不到的术语表达。The system should also distinguish absence of evidence from evidence of absence. Failure to retrieve a paper supporting a claim does not establish that no such paper exists. The literature may be inaccessible, badly indexed, unpublished or expressed using unexpected terminology.

6.7.撰写科学文献6.7. Writing the scientific literature

科学传播有两个方向。人工智能科学家必须阅读人类科学文献，但他们也必须将自己的发现传达给人类和其他机器。法学硕士非常有能力将结构化信息转化为流畅的散文。他们可以起草摘要、方法、结果和讨论；提高语法质量；使写作适应期刊的风格；并在不同语言之间翻译科学文本。尽管不同学科和语言社区的流行程度有所不同，但它们在科学写作中的使用已经广泛且不断增加（Liang et al., 2025）。这创造了提高清晰度和减少语言障碍的机会，但也带来了增加表面上合理且未经充分验证的科学文本数量的风险。Scientific communication moves in two directions. AI Scientists must read the human scientific literature, but they must also communicate their own discoveries to humans and other machines. LLMs are exceptionally capable of transforming structured information into fluent prose. They can draft abstracts, methods, results and discussions; improve grammatical quality; adapt writing to a journal’s style; and translate scientific text between languages. Their use in scientific writing is already widespread and increasing, although prevalence differs among disciplines and linguistic communities (Liang et al., 2025). This creates opportunities to improve clarity and reduce linguistic barriers, but also risks increasing the volume of superficially plausible and poorly verified scientific text.

科学写作不仅仅是文体表达。一篇论文做出了一系列认知承诺。它说明了研究之前已知的内容、所做的事情、观察到的内容以及证据得出的结论。自动撰写的论文必须保留这些区别。Scientific writing is not merely stylistic presentation. A paper makes a sequence of epistemic commitments. It states what was known before the study, what was done, what was observed and what follows from the evidence. An automatically written paper must preserve these distinctions.

机器生成的论文中的每个事实陈述都应可追溯到以下内容之一：已识别的外部出版物、指定的数据集、记录的实验、可执行的分析和正式验证的推论。Every factual statement in a machine-generated paper should be traceable to one of the following: an identified external publication, a specified dataset, a recorded experiment, an executable analysis, and a formally verified deduction.

该系统不能仅仅为了产生更流畅的散文而将不确定性转化为确定性或将关联转化为因果关系。当阴性结果和失败的实验与解释相关时，应予以报告。The system must not transform uncertainty into certainty or association into causation merely to produce smoother prose. Negative results and failed experiments should be reported when they are relevant to the interpretation.

36

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

必须选择引文，因为它们支持特定的主张，而不仅仅是因为它们的标题或摘要看起来相关。该系统应区分主要证据和评论，并在适当的情况下引用主要出版物。Citations must be selected because they support particular claims, not merely because their titles or abstracts appear related. The system should distinguish primary evidence from reviews and cite the primary publication where appropriate.

数据到论文系统演示了多个语言模型代理如何将数据集和分析转换为论文，同时保留数据、代码、结果和结果文本之间的链接（Ifargan 等人，2025）。这种类型的出处是至关重要的。读者必须能够从论文中的句子转向证明该句子合理性的分析或实验。The data-to-paper system demonstrates how several language-model agents can transform datasets and analyses into a paper while preserving links among the data, code, results and resulting text (Ifargan et al., 2025). This type of provenance is essential. A reader must be able to move from a sentence in the paper to the analysis or experiment that justifies it.

未来的人工智能科学家应该产生两个相互关联的科学记录（King et al., 2009）。Future AI Scientists should produce two connected scientific records (King et al., 2009).

第一个应该是人类可读的：解释问题、方法、证据、结论、局限性和意义的传统论文或报告。The first should be human-readable: a conventional paper or report explaining the question, methods, evidence, conclusions, limitations and significance.

第二个应该是机器可读的：声明、证据、协议、数据、分析、不确定性、出处以及与先前文献的关系的结构化记录。The second should be machine-readable: a structured record of the claims, evidence, protocols, data, analyses, uncertainty, provenance and relationships to previous literature.
