人类可读的出版物允许解释、评估和批评。机器可读的记录允许计算检查、集成和重用。The human-readable publication permits explanation, evaluation and criticism. The machine-readable record permits computational checking, integration and reuse.

因此，人工智能科学家不应仅仅撰写有说服力的科学散文。它应该对如何从文献和自己的观察中得出结论进行透明且可验证的说明。An AI Scientist should therefore not merely write persuasive scientific prose. It should produce a transparent and verifiable account of how its conclusions follow from the literature and from its own observations.

6.8。付费墙和“暗数据”问题6.8. Paywalls and the “Dark Data” Problem

人工智能科学家阅读全部人类科学知识的愿景假设这些文献是完全数字化且易于访问的。事实上，大量的科学数据仍然是“黑暗的”——被困在出版商的付费专区后面、隐藏在非数字化的物理实验室笔记本中、在传统软件格式中被掩盖，或者被孤立在专有的公司数据库中。虽然开放获取授权正在改善近期论文的可用性，但历史记录仍然高度分散。对于人工智能科学家来说，要实现真正、公正的文献综合，必须建立法律和技术框架，以允许在不侵犯版权的情况下跨付费语料库进行机器阅读，同时共同努力对大量历史暗数据档案进行数字化和标准化（Heidorn，2008）。The vision of an AI Scientist reading the entirety of human scientific knowledge assumes that this literature is perfectly digitised and accessible. In reality, an enormous volume of scientific data remains “dark”— trapped behind publisher paywalls, hidden in non-digitised physical laboratory notebooks, obscured within legacy software formats, or siloed in proprietary corporate databases. While open-access mandates are improving the availability of recent papers, the historical record remains highly fragmented. For an AI Scientist to achieve true, unbiased literature synthesis, legal and technical frameworks must be established to allow machine-reading across paywalled corpora without violating copyright, alongside concerted efforts to digitise and standardise the vast archives of historical dark data (Heidorn, 2008).

7. 假设生成和科学创造力7. Hypothesis Generation and Scientific Creativity

当观察需要解释或问题需要解决时，科学就开始了。生成可信的文本很容易。产生一个连贯的、可解释的且可通过实验检验的新假设是很困难的。这是AI科学家的创意核心。Science begins when an observation demands an explanation or a problem demands a solution. Generating plausible text is easy. Generating a new hypothesis that is coherent, explanatory and experimentally testable is hard. This is the creative core of an AI Scientist.

科学假设不仅仅是任何可以被检验的句子。它是对科学知识的拟议扩展或修订，用于解释观察结果、预测新现象或识别以前未识别的规律。假设的生成必须与背景知识、不确定性、实验可行性和经验歧视的可能性联系起来。A scientific hypothesis is not simply any sentence that could be tested. It is a proposed extension or revision of scientific knowledge that explains observations, predicts new phenomena or identifies a previously unrecognised regularity. Hypothesis generation must be connected to background knowledge, uncertainty, experimental feasibility and the possibility of empirical discrimination.

假设空间由科学家可用的语言决定。只能改变数值参数的系统只能发现可表达为参数设置的假设。一个能够发明谓词、实体、方程、机制和实验概念的系统可以改变问题本身的表示。因此，开放式科学发现取决于代表性创造力。The hypothesis space is determined by the language available to the scientist. A system that can vary only numerical parameters can discover only hypotheses expressible as parameter settings. A system that can invent predicates, entities, equations, mechanisms and experimental concepts can transform the representation of the problem itself. Open-ended scientific discovery therefore depends on representational creativity.

37

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

7.1.问题提出和问题生成7.1. Problem formulation and question generation

在产生假设之前，必须提出一个科学问题。人类研究通常始于异常现象、实际目标、文献中的空白、模型之间的矛盾或新仪器的可用性。人工智能科学家必须能够识别所有这些。Before a hypothesis can be generated, a scientific problem must be formulated. Human research commonly begins with an anomaly, a practical objective, a gap in the literature, a contradiction among models or the availability of a new instrument. An AI Scientist must be able to identify all of these.

问题生成并不等于产生疑问句。一个有用的科学问题应该涉及尚未解决的不确定性；原则上通过观察、实验或证明来回答；连接到连贯的背景知识体系；对其他信念或应用产生影响；足够具体以指导调查；并且足够重要以证明所需资源的合理性。Question generation is not equivalent to producing interrogative sentences. A useful scientific question should concern an uncertainty that is not already resolved; be answerable in principle by observation, experiment or proof; connect to a coherent body of background knowledge; have consequences for other beliefs or applications; be sufficiently specific to guide investigation; and be sufficiently important to justify the required resources.

系统可以通过定位知识图谱的低置信度区域、出版物之间的矛盾、系统模型差异、数据中无法解释的集群或现有理论预测不佳的测量来生成问题。它还可以从人类目标（例如治愈疾病或减少能源使用）倒退，以确定科学上的不确定性，解决这些不确定性将使进步成为可能。A system can generate questions by locating low-confidence regions of a knowledge graph, contradictions between publications, systematic model discrepancies, unexplained clusters in data, or measurements that existing theories predict poorly. It can also work backwards from human goals, such as curing a disease or reducing energy use, to identify the scientific uncertainties whose resolution would make progress possible.

问题的重要性部分是认知性的，部分是社会性的。人工智能系统可以估计解决问题将如何减少科学不确定性、连接领域或实现进一步的实验。它无法自主确定哪些人类需求应该得到优先考虑。高级别目标必须仍然受到第 13 节中讨论的治理的约束。Question importance is partly epistemic and partly social. An AI system can estimate how resolving a question would reduce scientific uncertainty, connect fields or enable further experiments. It cannot autonomously determine which human needs should receive priority. High-level objectives must remain subject to the governance discussed in Section 13.

7.2.绑架7.2. Abduction

逻辑为描述科学假设提供了丰富的结构。假设形成涉及逻辑推理的两种主要形式：溯因和归纳。溯因推理是对假设的推论，如果该假设成立，则可以解释观察结果。它通常被描述为对最佳解释的推断（Peirce，1931-1958）。侦探小说中，绑架被用于调试程序、诊断疾病、确定实验异常的原因以及侦破谋杀案。Logic provides a rich structure for describing scientific hypotheses. Two principal forms of logical inference are involved in hypothesis formation: abduction and induction. Abduction is the inference of a hypothesis that, if true, would explain an observation. It is often described as inference to the best explanation (Peirce, 1931–1958). Abduction is used when debugging programs, diagnosing diseases, identifying the causes of experimental anomalies and solving murders in detective fiction.

生物医学中的许多假设的形成都是溯因的。例如，假设先前未表征的基因具有特定功能，微生物导致疾病，或者分子途径的抑制解释了药物观察到的效果（King 等人，2004 年，2009 年）。Much hypothesis formation in biomedicine is abductive. Examples include hypothesising that a previously uncharacterised gene has a particular function, that a microorganism causes a disease, or that the inhibition of a molecular pathway explains a drug’s observed effect (King et al., 2004, 2009).

溯因推理在演绎意义上并不健全：解释的存在并不能证明该解释是正确的。多种假设可以解释同一个观察结果。然而，溯因推理提供了一种生成假设的系统方法，随后可以对其进行检验。Abductive reasoning is not sound in the deductive sense: the existence of an explanation does not establish that the explanation is true. Multiple hypotheses may explain the same observation. Yet abduction provides a systematic means of generating hypotheses that may subsequently be tested.

大多数科学溯因计算工作都使用逻辑作为其形式语言。为了使溯因推理具有可操作性，解释的概念通常与演绎蕴涵相关。因此，需要找到一个一致的基础假设，将理论与观察结合起来（即理论∧假设⊧观察）（Flach & Kakas, 2000）Most computational work on abduction for science has used logic as its formal language. To make abduction operational, the concept of explanation is generally related to deductive entailment. Therefore, what is required is to find a consistent ground Hypothesis that together a Theory entail an Observation (i.e. Theory ∧ Hypothesis ⊧ Observation) (Flach & Kakas, 2000)

然后需要额外的标准来在替代解释中进行选择。这些可能包括与背景知识的一致性、先验概率、简单性、解释性覆盖范围、因果合理性以及区分它们所需的实验成本（Flach＆Kakas，2000）。Additional criteria are then needed to choose among alternative explanations. These may include consistency with background knowledge, prior probability, simplicity, explanatory coverage, causal plausibility and the cost of the experiments required to discriminate among them (Flach & Kakas, 2000).

当观察结果令人惊讶时，绑架尤其有价值。人工智能科学家应该将观察结果与其当前模型的预测分布进行比较。一个大的、结构化的差异应该触发对差异的解释，而不是立即拒绝数据或任意扩大误差线。Abduction is especially valuable when observations are surprising. An AI Scientist should compare an observation with the predictive distributions of its current models. A large, structured discrepancy should trigger the generation of explanations for the discrepancy rather than immediate rejection of the data or arbitrary enlargement of error bars.

38

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

最好的解释不一定是语言上最合理的。候选解释应根据先验概率、解释覆盖范围、因果一致性、简单性、与背景知识的兼容性以及区分它们的实验的预期值进行排名。The best explanation need not be the most linguistically plausible. Candidate explanations should be ranked according to prior probability, explanatory coverage, causal coherence, simplicity, compatibility with background knowledge and the expected value of experiments that distinguish them.

7.3.归纳和关系学习7.3. Induction and relational learning

科学的一个关键部分是从具体观察到一般假设的推理。这就是归纳推理。机器学习本质上是自动归纳。A key part of science is reasoning from specific observations to general hypotheses. This is inductive inference. Machine learning is, in essence, automated induction.

大多数传统的机器学习方法使用数据的命题表示。每个示例都由固定的属性元组或向量表示。这些属性是被认为与目标概念相关的描述符或特征。字符串、数组、图像和图表最终可能被编码为属性的数字集合。Most conventional machine-learning methods use a propositional representation of data. Each example is represented by a fixed tuple or vector of attributes. These attributes are descriptors or features believed to be relevant to the target concept. Strings, arrays, images and graphs may ultimately be encoded as numerical collections of attributes.

机器学习方法部分取决于表达假设的目标语言。线性模型将假设表达为加权数学函数。决策树表达嵌套的命题条件。贝叶斯方法表达概率结构。深度神经网络将假设表达为数值变换网络。逻辑机器学习系统将假设表达为规则或逻辑公式。Machine-learning approaches vary partly according to the target language in which their hypotheses are expressed. Linear models express hypotheses as weighted mathematical functions. Decision trees express nested propositional conditions. Bayesian methods express probabilistic structures. Deep neural networks express hypotheses as networks of numerical transformations. Logical machine-learning systems express hypotheses as rules or logical formulae.

归纳法的哲学问题已经被认识了几个世纪。休谟认为，重复观察不能演绎地建立普遍法则（Hume，1748）。罗素使用了人们所熟悉的预期，即未来将与过去相似，以表明如果没有本身归纳性的假设，归纳实践就无法被证明是合理的（Russell，1912）。因此，波普尔将归纳法视为猜想，然后试图反驳，而不是确认逻辑（Popper，1959），而普特南表明，形式确认系统不可避免地依赖于表征和方法论的选择（Putnam，1963）。The philosophical problem of induction has been recognised for centuries. Hume argued that repeated observations cannot deductively establish a universal law (Hume, 1748). Russell used the familiar expectation that the future will resemble the past to show that inductive practice cannot be justified without assumptions that are themselves inductive (Russell, 1912). Popper therefore treated induction as conjecture followed by attempted refutation rather than as a logic of confirmation (Popper, 1959), while Putnam showed that formal confirmation systems inevitably depend on representational and methodological choices (Putnam, 1963).

机器学习在实践中取得的巨大成功并没有消除归纳法的哲学问题。相反，它表明归纳程序可以生成具有强大预测能力的模型。他们的结论仍然是可以推翻的：新的观察结果可能需要修改或拒绝假设。这就是为什么归纳法必须与概率、实验测试和理性信念修正相结合。The enormous practical success of machine learning has not eliminated the philosophical problem of induction. It has shown instead that inductive procedures can generate models with substantial predictive power. Their conclusions remain defeasible: new observations may require a hypothesis to be revised or rejected. This is why induction must be integrated with probabilities, experimental testing and rational belief revision.

7.3.1.一阶逻辑和归纳逻辑编程7.3.1. First-order logic and inductive logic programming

一阶谓词逻辑比命题逻辑更丰富、更普遍。它的开发是为了形式化数学，可以表达实体、属性、关系、变量和量化。因此，它允许表达不能自然地表示为固定属性元组的一般科学陈述。First-order predicate logic is substantially richer and more general than propositional logic. It was developed to formalise mathematics and can express entities, properties, relations, variables and quantification. It therefore permits the expression of general scientific statements that cannot be represented naturally as fixed tuples of attributes.

关系学习和归纳逻辑编程使用一阶逻辑或逻辑程序来表示数据、背景知识和学习假设（Muggleton，1991；De Raedt，2008）。它们的主要优点是可以直接推理结构化和关系对象，并且可以将广泛的背景知识融入学习中。Relational learning and inductive logic programming use first-order logic or logic programs to represent data, background knowledge and learned hypotheses (Muggleton, 1991; De Raedt, 2008). Their principal advantage is that they can reason directly about structured and relational objects and can incorporate extensive background knowledge into learning.

例如，生物医学学习系统可以表示基因、蛋白质、通路、化合物、表型、生物体和实验之间的关系，而不是将每个案例视为孤立的特征向量。背景生物学知识可以限制假设空间并能够从相对较少的示例中进行概括（Orhobor 等人，2020）。For example, a biomedical learning system can represent relations among genes, proteins, pathways, compounds, phenotypes, organisms and experiments rather than treating each case as an isolated feature vector. Background biological knowledge can constrain the hypothesis space and enable generalisation from comparatively small numbers of examples (Orhobor et al., 2020).

39

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

一阶逻辑更强的表达能力使得学习问题变得更加困难，因为假设空间比命题学习的空间大得多。这就产生了对语言偏差、类型约束、背景知识、高效搜索和发明有用的中间谓词的需求。The greater expressive power of first-order logic makes the learning problem more difficult because the hypothesis space is vastly larger than that of propositional learning. This creates the need for language biases, type constraints, background knowledge, efficient search and the invention of useful intermediate predicates.

人类科学家通常使用一阶和高阶关系进行隐式推理和学习。它们对实体类别、实体之间的关系、机制、嵌套结构和一般规律进行推理。未来的人工智能科学家将需要同样的能力。一阶逻辑已广泛应用于演绎人工智能系统，但在现代机器学习中却很少使用。Human scientists routinely reason and learn implicitly using first-order and higher-order relations. They reason about classes of entities, relations between entities, mechanisms, nested structures and general laws. Future AI Scientists will require the same capability. First-order logic has been used extensively in deductive AI systems, but much less commonly in modern machine learning.

关系学习很重要，因为科学假设通常涉及机制和结构，而不是孤立的属性。一个有用的假设可能表明，具有特定结构关系的一类蛋白质在特定条件下调节另一类蛋白质。这样的陈述不能在不失去其科学形式的情况下自然地表示为平面向量。Relational learning is important because scientific hypotheses commonly concern mechanisms and structures rather than isolated attributes. A useful hypothesis may state that a class of proteins with a particular structural relation regulates another class under specified conditions. Such a statement cannot be represented naturally as a flat vector without losing its scientific form.

7.4.概念和谓词发明7.4. Concept and predicate invention

谓词发明是学习问题的初始语言中不存在的新概念或关系的创建。它是归纳逻辑编程和机器科学发现的核心问题之一。Predicate invention is the creation of new concepts or relations that are not present in the initial language of a learning problem. It is one of the central problems of inductive logic programming and of machine scientific discovery.

一个系统可能无法使用其原始词汇来表达一个简单的理论，但一旦发明了适当的中间概念就可以做到这一点。在科学中，许多重大发现正是涉及这个过程：引入新的实体、属性和关系，通过这些新的实体、属性和关系可以更简单地描述观察结果并更深入地解释观察结果。A system may be unable to express a simple theory using its original vocabulary, but able to do so once an appropriate intermediate concept has been invented. In science, many major discoveries involve precisely this process: the introduction of new entities, properties and relations with which observations can be described more simply and explained more deeply.

传统的谓词发明方法在计算上很困难，并且经常生成缺乏清晰语义解释的中间谓词。法学硕士极大地改善了谓词发明的前景，因为他们对自然语言和科学话语的了解使他们能够为数据中识别的潜在概念提出有意义的名称、定义和关系。然而，他们并不能自己解决问题。必须使用归纳逻辑编程、演绎推理和最终实验来测试、完善和验证所提出的谓词。Traditional approaches to predicate invention have been computationally difficult and have often generated intermediate predicates that lack clear semantic interpretations. LLMs substantially improve the prospects for predicate invention because their knowledge of natural language and scientific discourse enables them to propose meaningful names, definitions and relations for latent concepts identified in data. They do not, however, solve the problem by themselves. Proposed predicates must be tested, refined and verified using inductive logic programming, deductive inference and ultimately experiment.

因此，最强大的架构是神经象征性的。法学硕士提出候选谓词和规则；逻辑编程或定理证明系统确定它们在语法上是否有效、一致且足以解释示例；由此产生的证明失败或反例将返回LLM进行修改。最近的工作精确地证明了基于 LLM 的溯因生成与基于 Prolog 的演绎验证的结合，表明形式验证大大提高了发明谓词的成功率（Yu 等人，2026）。The most powerful architecture is therefore neuro-symbolic. An LLM proposes candidate predicates and rules; a logic-programming or theorem-proving system determines whether they are syntactically valid, consistent and sufficient to explain the examples; and the resulting proof failures or counterexamples are returned to the LLM for revision. Recent work has demonstrated precisely this combination of LLM-based abductive generation with Prolog-based deductive verification, showing that formal verification substantially improves the success of invented predicates (Yu et al., 2026).

法学硕士解决谓词发明的主张应该被理解为一个研究计划，而不是一个完整的结果。法学硕士非常有能力提出名称和定义，但提出的概念只有在压缩观察结果、支持正确预测、参与解释并经受实验测试时才具有科学用途。形式验证可以建立相对于所述前提的一致性；它不能确定新的谓词对应于自然界中真正的规律性。The claim that LLMs solve predicate invention should be understood as a research programme rather than a completed result. LLMs are exceptionally capable of proposing names and definitions, but a proposed concept becomes scientifically useful only when it compresses observations, supports correct predictions, participates in explanations and survives experimental testing. Formal verification can establish consistency relative to stated premises; it cannot establish that the new predicate corresponds to a real regularity in nature.

7.5。类比与迁移7.5. Analogy and transfer

许多重要的科学假设都是通过类比形成的。在一个领域中已知的机制在另一个领域中被提出，在一个领域中使用的数学结构被转移到不同的现象，Many important scientific hypotheses are formed by analogy. A mechanism known in one domain is proposed in another, a mathematical structure used in one field is transferred to a different phenomenon,

40

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

或者两个明显不同的系统被识别为同一抽象模式的实例。计算帐户强调关系结构的映射而不是表面属性，以及连接关系从源域到目标域的系统转移（Gentner，1983；Holyoak & Thagard，1995）。or two apparently different systems are recognised as instances of the same abstract pattern. Computational accounts emphasise the mapping of relational structure rather than superficial attributes, and the systematic transfer of connected relations from a source to a target domain (Gentner, 1983; Holyoak & Thagard, 1995).

计算类比需要将关系结构与表面词汇分开的表示。人工智能科学家应该识别实体和关系之间的映射，评估源机制的哪些部分可以转移，并确定类比在哪里失败。最强烈的类比会产生新的可测试的结果，而不仅仅是修辞上的相似性。Computational analogy requires representations that separate relational structure from surface vocabulary. An AI Scientist should identify mappings among entities and relations, assess which parts of the source mechanism can be transferred and determine where the analogy breaks down. The strongest analogies generate new testable consequences rather than merely rhetorical similarity.

基础模型在类比方面很有前景，因为它们可以学习跨多个学科的广泛表示。它们的广度也带来了危险：它们可以在几乎所有概念之间产生表面上有吸引力的联系。因此，候选类比必须受到形式结构、因果合理性和前瞻性检验的约束。Foundation models are promising for analogy because they learn broad representations across many disciplines. Their breadth also creates a danger: they can generate superficially attractive connections among almost any concepts. Candidate analogies must therefore be constrained by formal structure, causal plausibility and prospective tests.

7.6。基于文献的发现7.6. Literature-based discovery

科学文献可能包含从未在任何一篇论文中明确阐述过的知识。联系可能分布在两个或多个使用不同术语并由不同科学界阅读的研究机构中。The scientific literature can contain knowledge that has never been stated explicitly in any one paper. A connection may be distributed across two or more bodies of research that use different terminology and are read by different scientific communities.

斯旺森关于基于文献的发现的工作提供了经典的例子。一份文献将雷诺氏综合症与血液粘度增加和血小板聚集联系起来，而另一份文献将鱼油与血液粘度降低和血小板聚集联系起来。这两篇文献并没有直接联系，但它们的结合提出了这样的假设：鱼油可以使雷诺综合征患者受益（Swanson，1986）。Swanson’s work on literature-based discovery provided the classic example. One body of literature connected Raynaud’s syndrome to increased blood viscosity and platelet aggregation, while another connected fish oil to reduced blood viscosity and platelet aggregation. The two literatures had not been connected directly, but their combination suggested the hypothesis that fish oil could benefit patients with Raynaud’s syndrome (Swanson, 1986).

这种形式的发现特别适合机器，因为它需要搜索和整合比任何人类可以阅读的更多的文献。现代系统可以识别大型集合中的连接实体、路径、机制或数学结构。This form of discovery is particularly suitable for machines because it requires searching and integrating more literature than any human can read. Modern systems can identify connecting entities, pathways, mechanisms or mathematical structures across large collections.

尽管如此，基于文献的发现必须与不受限制的关联区分开来。大型知识网络包含概念之间的大量可能路径，其中大多数在科学上没有意义。Literature-based discovery must nevertheless be distinguished from unrestricted association. Large knowledge networks contain enormous numbers of possible paths between concepts, most of which are scientifically meaningless.

文献可以产生候选联系，但需要实验证据来确定它们是否正确。The literature can generate candidate connections, but experimental evidence is required to establish whether they are correct.

基于文献的发现应该基于主张和证据，而不仅仅是单词共现。仅当知识图的边对应于支持的关系并且组成的路径产生连贯的机制或预测时，知识图的路径才具有科学价值。系统应记录证据是否来自独立实验、对同一数据集的重复分析或推测性讨论。Literature-based discovery should operate over claims and evidence rather than word co-occurrence alone. A path through a knowledge graph is scientifically valuable only when its edges correspond to supported relations and when the composed path yields a coherent mechanism or prediction. The system should record whether the evidence comes from independent experiments, repeated analysis of the same dataset or speculative discussion.

7.7.机制和因果假设7.7. Mechanistic and causal hypotheses

预测关联不一定是科学解释。假设生成应区分： 总结观察到的规律性的描述性假设；预测未观察到的结果的预测假设；指定干预效果的因果假设；识别产生效果的实体和过程的机制假设；统一连接以前独立的法律或领域的假设。A predictive association is not necessarily a scientific explanation. Hypothesis generation should distinguish: descriptive hypotheses that summarise an observed regularity; predictive hypotheses that forecast unobserved outcomes; causal hypotheses that specify effects of intervention; mechanistic hypotheses that identify entities and processes producing the effect; and unifying hypotheses that connect previously separate laws or domains.

41

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

机制假设很困难，因为机制可能涉及未观察到的实体和多个层次的组织。人工智能科学家应该使用因果模型、守恒定律、时空约束、已知的生化或物理能力以及干预数据来限制合理机制的空间。Mechanistic hypotheses are difficult because mechanisms may involve unobserved entities and multiple levels of organisation. An AI Scientist should use causal models, conservation laws, spatial and temporal constraints, known biochemical or physical capabilities and intervention data to restrict the space of plausible mechanisms.

拟议的机制应该产生超出用于构建它的观察结果。它应该解释为什么会出现规律性，确定规律失败的条件并支持反事实的预测。如果几种机制在观察上是等效的，系统应该设计将它们分开的干预措施。A proposed mechanism should generate observations beyond those used to construct it. It should explain why the regularity occurs, identify conditions under which it fails and support counterfactual predictions. Where several mechanisms are observationally equivalent, the system should design interventions that separate them.

7.8。法学硕士和多主体假设生成7.8. LLMs and multi-agent hypothesis generation

法学硕士包含广泛的科学语言隐性知识，可以快速生成假设。多智能体系统可以将过程划分为生成者、批评者、文献检查者、方法论者和实验设计者。 Co-Scientist 和 Robin 提供了迭代假设生成、批评和更新的早期演示（Gottweis 等人，2026；Ghareeb 等人，2026）。LLMs contain extensive implicit knowledge of scientific language and can generate hypotheses rapidly. Multi-agent systems can divide the process among generators, critics, literature checkers, methodologists and experimental designers. Co-Scientist and Robin provide early demonstrations of iterative hypothesis generation, criticism and updating (Gottweis et al., 2026; Ghareeb et al., 2026).

法学硕士的主要优势是广度。他们可以结合遥远的文献，在学科词汇之间进行翻译，并提出用流利的科学语言表达的机制。它们的主要弱点是语言的合理性与真理的联系很弱。模型可能会将已知的想法重述为新颖，产生与主要证据相矛盾的假设或发明没有物理意义的机制。The principal strength of LLMs is breadth. They can combine distant literatures, translate between disciplinary vocabularies and propose mechanisms expressed in fluent scientific language. Their principal weakness is that linguistic plausibility is only weakly connected to truth. A model may restate a known idea as novel, generate a hypothesis contradicted by primary evidence or invent a mechanism with no physical meaning.

因此，可靠的架构应该将生成与接受分开。应鼓励生成系统产生多样化的候选人。独立模块应该： 检索最近的先前工作；检查这个想法是否真正新颖；将提案转化为正式或可执行的表述；测试逻辑和尺寸一致性；估计先前的合理性；得出预测的观察结果；确定决定性的实验；并拒绝无法凭经验区分的候选者。A reliable architecture should therefore separate generation from acceptance. Generative systems should be encouraged to produce diverse candidates. Independent modules should then: retrieve the nearest prior work; check whether the idea is genuinely novel; translate the proposal into a formal or executable representation; test logical and dimensional consistency; estimate prior plausibility; derive predicted observations; identify decisive experiments; and reject candidates that cannot be distinguished empirically.

多样性应该经过精心设计。在类似文献上训练的多个模型可能会重现相同的共识和相同的错误。应该使用独立的表征、先验、数据集和搜索程序来创建真正不同的假设。Diversity should be designed deliberately. Multiple models trained on similar literature may reproduce the same consensus and the same errors. Independent representations, priors, datasets and search procedures should be used to create genuinely different hypotheses.

7.9。评估候选假设7.9. Evaluating candidate hypotheses

许多生成的假设毫无价值，因此选择与生成同样重要。首先应将候选者与文献和系统可访问的训练源进行比较，以确定其是否真正新颖。然后应该测试它与有充分支持的背景知识、解释性覆盖范围、预测特异性、因果和机制合理性、所选表示语言的简单性以及对合理替代假设的稳健性的一致性。Many generated hypotheses are worthless, so selection is as important as generation. A candidate should first be compared with the literature and the system’s accessible training sources to establish whether it is genuinely novel. It should then be tested for consistency with well-supported background knowledge, explanatory coverage, predictive specificity, causal and mechanistic plausibility, simplicity within the chosen representation language, and robustness to reasonable alternative assumptions.

具有科学价值的假设还必须在实验上是可辨别的。该系统应识别在相互竞争的解释下会有所不同的观察结果，并评估这些观察结果是否可以合乎道德、安全且以适当的成本获得。科学重要性和实际可行性与概率不同：保守的假设可能有可能但不重要，而激进的假设可能具有较低的先验概率但具有变革性的后果。因此，投资组合应该保留高概率的增量工作和低概率、高价值的可能性。A scientifically valuable hypothesis must also be experimentally discriminable. The system should identify observations that would differ under competing explanations and assess whether those observations can be obtained ethically, safely and at proportionate cost. Scientific importance and practical feasibility are distinct from probability: a conservative hypothesis may be likely but unimportant, while a radical hypothesis may have a low prior probability but transformative consequences. A portfolio should therefore preserve both high-probability incremental work and lower-probability, high-value possibilities.

新颖性必须进行前瞻性评估。重现训练数据中存在的著名发现是不够的。最强有力的评估记录了整个机器对真正未解决的问题的贡献，然后将所得的假设进行独立的实验或证明。Novelty must be evaluated prospectively. It is not enough to reproduce a famous discovery present in training data. The strongest evaluation records the complete machine contribution to a genuinely unresolved problem and then subjects the resulting hypothesis to independent experiment or proof.

42

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

7.10。从假设到研究计划7.10. From hypothesis to research programme

一项主要假设很少能通过一项实验得到检验。它生成一个研究计划：一系列测量、控制、复制和模型修订。人工智能科学家应该将广泛的假设分解为附属主张，并确定应首先测试哪些依赖关系。A major hypothesis is rarely tested by one experiment. It generates a research programme: a sequence of measurements, controls, replications and model revisions. An AI Scientist should decompose a broad hypothesis into subsidiary claims and determine which dependencies should be tested first.

系统应在每次结果后更新程序。负面结果可能会反驳其中一个组成部分，同时使更广泛的机制保持可行。异常的观察可能会激发一个新的概念。成功的预测可能会增加信心，但也会揭示相互竞争的假设会做出相同的预测。The system should update the programme after each result. A negative outcome may refute one component while leaving the broader mechanism viable. An anomalous observation may motivate a new concept. A successful prediction may increase confidence but also reveal that competing hypotheses make the same prediction.

因此，假设的生成不是一个单一的创造性事件。这是一个迭代过程，其中表征、演绎、实验和信念修正不断重塑可能解释的空间。Hypothesis generation is therefore not a single creative event. It is an iterative process in which representation, deduction, experiment and belief revision continually reshape the space of possible explanations.

8. 推演、模拟与预测8. Deduction, Simulation and Prediction

只有当一个假设具有可检验的后果时，它才变得科学。演绎得出这些结果。模拟将推论扩展到对于解析解来说过于复杂的系统。两者都不能确立真理；两者都告诉我们如果模型正确的话将会发生什么。A hypothesis becomes scientific only when it has consequences that can be tested. Deduction derives those consequences. Simulation extends deduction to systems too complex for analytical solution. Neither establishes truth; both tell us what would follow if the model were right.

简单理论的演绎结果可以通过分析得出。在复杂的系统中，结果很难以封闭的数学形式导出。相反，该理论必须表示为可执行模型，并通过模拟计算其结果。The deductive consequences of simple theories may be derived analytically. In complicated systems, the consequences are too difficult to derive in closed mathematical form. The theory must instead be represented as an executable model and its consequences calculated through simulation.

因此，模拟是现代科学中使用的条件演绎的主要形式之一。计算模拟对从宇宙的大规模结构和地球气候到生物细胞、化学反应和基本粒子的系统进行建模。最抽象的模拟是思想实验，其中在心理上执行简化的模型。Simulation is therefore one of the principal forms of conditional deduction used in modern science. Computational simulations model systems ranging from the large-scale structure of the Universe and the Earth’s climate to biological cells, chemical reactions and elementary particles. The most abstract simulations are thought experiments, in which a simplified model is executed mentally.

模拟并不能确定其输出对于世界来说是真实的。它确定输出大致遵循模型、其参数、初始和边界条件以及数值方法。如果这些假设中的任何一个不正确，则模拟可能在内部有效，同时对自然做出错误的预测。A simulation does not establish that its output is true of the world. It establishes that the output follows, approximately, from the model, its parameters, initial and boundary conditions and numerical method. If any of these assumptions is incorrect, the simulation may be internally valid while making false predictions about nature.

因此，人工智能科学家必须区分推论的逻辑正确性、模拟的数值正确性、科学模型的经验充分性以及模型对所调查情况的适用性。An AI Scientist must therefore distinguish between the logical correctness of the deduction, the numerical correctness of the simulation, the empirical adequacy of the scientific model, and the applicability of the model to the situation under investigation.

8.1.作为可执行假设的科学模型8.1. Scientific models as executable hypotheses

科学模型是世界选定方面的正式表示。它指定了实体、状态、关系和规则来管理所表示的系统的行为方式。模型必然会抽象出真实系统的大部分复杂性。A scientific model is a formal representation of selected aspects of the world. It specifies entities, states, relations and rules governing how the represented system behaves. A model necessarily abstracts away from much of the complexity of the real system.

定量物理模型最广泛使用的语言是微积分，因为微分方程表达了系统如何通过空间和时间连续变化。牛顿力学、电磁学、流体动力学、量子力学和广义相对论都主要通过微分方程来表述。The most widely used language for quantitative physical models is calculus, because differential equations express how systems change continuously through space and time. Newtonian mechanics, electromagnetism, fluid dynamics, quantum mechanics and general relativity are all formulated principally through differential equations.

43

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

不同的科学领域使用适合其可用知识和计算限制的表示。例如，在系统生物学中，详细的微分方程模型通常是不切实际的，因为许多反应机制和动力学常数是未知的。布尔网络可以表示定性调节关系，而通量平衡分析使用线性约束和优化来预测可行的代谢通量，而不需要完整的酶动力学（Kauffman，1969；Orth 等，2010）。Different scientific domains use representations suited to their available knowledge and computational constraints. In systems biology, for example, detailed differential-equation models are often impractical because many reaction mechanisms and kinetic constants are unknown. Boolean networks can represent qualitative regulatory relationships, while flux-balance analysis uses linear constraints and optimisation to predict feasible metabolic fluxes without requiring complete enzyme kinetics (Kauffman, 1969; Orth et al., 2010).

当随机性在科学上很重要时，就需要随机模型。例如，Gillespie 的随机模拟算法可为化学反应系统生成统计上正确的轨迹，其中分子数量很少且确定性浓度方程不充分（Gillespie，1977）。Stochastic models are required when randomness is scientifically important. Gillespie’s stochastic simulation algorithm, for example, generates statistically correct trajectories for chemically reacting systems in which the numbers of molecules are small and deterministic concentration equations are inadequate (Gillespie, 1977).

基于代理的模型将系统表示为交互的个体或组件的集合。当人口层面的行为源于异质的局部相互作用时，例如在生态学、流行病学、经济和社会系统中，它们是有用的（Bonabeau，2002）。Agent-based models represent systems as collections of interacting individuals or components. They are useful when population-level behaviour emerges from heterogeneous local interactions, as in ecology, epidemiology, economics and social systems (Bonabeau, 2002).

当模型生成可与观察结果进行比较的预测时，模型就是可执行的假设。同一模型可能既包含既定的科学知识又包含推测性假设。未来的人工智能科学家必须能够识别哪些组件得到了良好的支持，哪些组件是不确定的，以及哪些观察结果可以区分不同的模型结构。A model is an executable hypothesis when it generates predictions that can be compared with observations. The same model may contain both established scientific knowledge and conjectural assumptions. Future AI Scientists must be able to identify which components are well supported, which are uncertain and which observations could discriminate among alternative model structures.

8.2.正向模拟和预测8.2. Forward simulation and prediction

正向模拟会在自然给出答案之前询问模型预测什么。Forward simulation asks what the model predicts before nature answers.

正演模拟计算模型预测的观测值。让A forward simulation calculates the observations predicted by a model. Let

𝑦= 𝑆(𝑀, 𝜃, 𝑥, 𝑢)

表示模拟器 𝑆 ，其中 𝑀 是模型结构，𝜃 其参数，𝑥 初始条件和边界条件，𝑢 干预或实验输入，𝑦 预测观测值。denote a simulator 𝑆 , where 𝑀 is the model structure, 𝜃 its parameters, 𝑥 the initial and boundary conditions, 𝑢 an intervention or experimental input, and 𝑦 the predicted observations.

正向模拟回答以下形式的问题：Forward simulations answer questions of the form:

如果这个模型、这些参数和这些条件都是正确的，那么应该观察什么？If this model, these parameters and these conditions are correct, what should be observed?

在某些情况下，模型本身正在接受测试。在其他情况下，暂时假设它是正确的，并且模拟用于预测有用的量，例如明天的天气、候选材料的行为、航天器的轨迹或生物系统对干预的响应。In some cases, the model itself is under test. In others, it is provisionally assumed to be correct and the simulation is used to predict useful quantities, such as tomorrow’s weather, the behaviour of a candidate material, the trajectory of a spacecraft or the response of a biological system to an intervention.

模拟还允许反事实推理。一旦指定了模型，科学家就可以询问在尚未发生或不易通过实验产生的条件下会发生什么。这使得仿真对于实验规划、工程设计和风险评估很有价值。Simulations also permit counterfactual reasoning. Once a model has been specified, the scientist can ask what would happen under conditions that have not yet occurred or cannot easily be produced experimentally. This makes simulation valuable for experimental planning, engineering design and risk assessment.

确定性模拟的输出通常表示为单个轨迹或数字。这可能会产生误导，因为输入和模型结构是不确定的。人工智能科学家通常应该通过模拟器传播不确定性，并生成可能结果的分布，而不是单个明显准确的预测。The output of a deterministic simulation is often presented as a single trajectory or number. This can be misleading because the inputs and model structure are uncertain. An AI Scientist should normally propagate uncertainty through the simulator and produce a distribution over possible outcomes rather than a single apparently exact prediction.

8.3.反问题和基于模拟的推理8.3. Inverse problems and simulation-based inference

逆问题的方向相反。给定观测值 𝑦，目标是推断可能生成它们的模型、参数、初始条件或干预措施：The inverse problem runs in the opposite direction. Given observations 𝑦, the objective is to infer the model, parameters, initial conditions or interventions that could have generated them:

44

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

𝑦↦𝑃（𝑀，𝜃，𝑥，𝑢∣𝑦）𝑦↦𝑃(𝑀, 𝜃, 𝑥, 𝑢∣𝑦)

逆问题贯穿整个科学。示例包括从光谱推断分子结构、从断层扫描测量重建物体的内部、从天文观测估计宇宙学参数以及从实验时间序列识别生物模型的参数。Inverse problems occur throughout science. Examples include inferring a molecular structure from a spectrum, reconstructing the interior of an object from tomographic measurements, estimating cosmological parameters from astronomical observations and identifying the parameters of a biological model from experimental time series.

对于简单模型，可以通过分析计算可能性 𝑃(𝑦∣𝑀, 𝜃)。然而，对于许多实际模拟，可以从模型生成观察结果，但不能评估其可能性。那么模拟器就是一个隐式生成模型。For simple models, the likelihood 𝑃(𝑦∣𝑀, 𝜃) can be calculated analytically. For many realistic simulations, however, it is possible to generate observations from the model but not to evaluate their likelihood. The simulator is then an implicit generative model.

基于模拟的推理 (SBI)，也称为无似然推理，通过从重复模拟中学习来解决这个问题。当模拟数据与观测结果相似时，近似贝叶斯计算接受参数值。最近的方法使用神经密度估计、分类器或比率估计来近似后验分布或似然比（Cranmer 等人，2020）。Simulation-Based Inference (SBI), also termed likelihood-free inference, addresses this problem by learning from repeated simulations. Approximate Bayesian Computation accepts parameter values when simulated data resemble the observations. More recent methods use neural density estimation, classifiers or ratio estimation to approximate posterior distributions or likelihood ratios (Cranmer et al., 2020).

SBI 对于人工智能科学家来说很重要，因为许多最现实的科学模型更容易执行而不是反演。人工智能科学家可以在不同的假设下生成大量的模拟世界，并了解哪些假设与观察结果最相符。SBI is important for AI Scientists because many of the most realistic scientific models are easier to execute than to invert. An AI Scientist can generate large numbers of simulated worlds under different hypotheses and learn which hypotheses are most compatible with the observations.

但只有当模拟器包含能够生成真实观察结果的机制时，推理才是可靠的。如果缺少重要过程，推断的参数可能会补偿缺失的机制并获得误导性的值。基于模拟的推理并不能消除模型误差；它使推理以模拟器为条件，而模拟器的充分性仍需测试。But inference is reliable only when the simulator includes the mechanisms capable of generating the real observations. If an important process is absent, the inferred parameters may compensate for the missing mechanism and acquire misleading values. Simulation-based inference does not remove model error; it makes inference conditional on a simulator whose adequacy must still be tested.

8.4.验证、确认和校准8.4. Verification, validation and calibration

科学模拟需要三个不同的活动：验证、确认和校准。Scientific simulation requires three distinct activities: verification, validation and calibration.

验证询问模型的方程或规则是否已正确实现和求解。它通常表达为：我们解方程正确吗？验证包括检查软件正确性、数值收敛性、单位一致性、守恒性质和离散化效果。由于编程错误、算法不稳定或数值近似过于粗糙，模拟可能无法验证。Verification asks whether the equations or rules of the model have been implemented and solved correctly. It is commonly expressed as: Are we solving the equations right? Verification includes checking software correctness, numerical convergence, unit consistency, conservation properties and the effect of discretisation. A simulation may fail verification because of a programming error, an unstable algorithm or a numerical approximation that is too coarse.

验证询问模型是否足够好地代表真实系统以满足其预期用途：我们是否解出了正确的方程？验证需要与独立的实验或观察数据进行比较。一个模型可能在数值上是完美的，但在经验上是不充分的，因为它忽略了一个重要的过程或仅适用于所研究的体系之外。Validation asks whether the model represents the real system sufficiently well for its intended use: Are we solving the right equations? Validation requires comparison with independent experimental or observational data. A model may be numerically perfect but empirically inadequate because it omits an important process or applies only outside the regime being studied.

校准根据观测估计不确定的模型参数。模型可能包含反应速率、材料常数或初始条件，其值无法精确得知。校准会调整这些值，以便模型更好地匹配相关数据。校准不应与验证混淆。即使其底层结构不正确，灵活的模型通常也可以进行校准以适应数据集。因此，验证必须使用在校准过程中不只是吸收的数据。Calibration estimates uncertain model parameters from observations. A model may contain reaction rates, material constants or initial conditions whose values are not known precisely. Calibration adjusts these values so that the model better matches relevant data. Calibration should not be confused with validation. A flexible model can often be calibrated to fit a dataset even when its underlying structure is incorrect. Validation must therefore use data that were not simply absorbed during calibration.

Kennedy 和 O’Hagan 的贝叶斯框架将模型参数的不确定性与模型差异区分开来：模型的最佳可能输出与真实系统之间的系统差异（Kennedy & O’Hagan，2001）。如果没有明确的差异项，参数估计可能会吸收模型的不足，并且看起来比证据所保证的更精确。Kennedy and O’Hagan’s Bayesian framework distinguishes uncertainty in model parameters from model discrepancy: the systematic difference between the best possible output of the model and the real system (Kennedy & O’Hagan, 2001). Without an explicit discrepancy term, parameter estimates may absorb model inadequacy and appear more precise than the evidence warrants.

人工智能科学家应该代表至少四种形式的不确定性：模型结构的不确定性、参数值的不确定性、初始和边界条件的不确定性以及测量和数值近似产生的不确定性。AI Scientists should represent at least four forms of uncertainty: uncertainty in model structure, uncertainty in parameter values, uncertainty in initial and boundary conditions, and uncertainty arising from measurements and numerical approximation.

45

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

他们还应该进行敏感性分析，以确定哪些假设和参数对预测结论影响最大。严重依赖于不确定参数的预测不如在合理参数范围内保持稳定的预测稳健。They should also perform sensitivity analysis to determine which assumptions and parameters have the greatest influence on the predicted conclusion. A prediction that depends critically on an uncertain parameter is less robust than one that remains stable across the plausible parameter range.

8.5。可微分编程8.5. Differentiable programming

科学模拟的一个重要发展是可微分编程。在可微分编程中，数值程序执行的运算被组织起来，以便可以自动计算其输出相对于输入或参数的导数（Baydin 等人，2018；Innes 等人，2019）。An important development in scientific simulation is differentiable programming. In differentiable programming, the operations performed by a numerical program are organised so that derivatives of its outputs with respect to its inputs or parameters can be calculated automatically (Baydin et al., 2018; Innes et al., 2019).

自动微分不同于符号微分和有限差分。符号微分操纵数学表达式。有限差分通过在附近点重复评估函数来估计导数。自动微分将链式法则系统地应用于程序执行的初等运算，并精确地计算导数至数值精度。Automatic differentiation is different from symbolic differentiation and finite differences. Symbolic differentiation manipulates mathematical expressions. Finite differences estimate a derivative by repeatedly evaluating a function at nearby points. Automatic differentiation applies the chain rule systematically to the elementary operations executed by the program and calculates derivatives accurately to numerical precision.

如果模拟器产生预测If a simulator produces a prediction

损失函数 𝐿(𝑦, 𝑦'()) 衡量与观察结果的不一致，自动微分提供and a loss function 𝐿(𝑦, 𝑦'()) measures disagreement with observations, automatic differentiation provides

这些梯度表明参数应如何改变以使模拟与数据更加一致。它们可用于参数估计、最优控制、逆向设计和实验优化。These gradients indicate how the parameters should change to make the simulation agree more closely with the data. They can be used for parameter estimation, optimal control, inverse design and experimental optimisation.

可微分模拟器可以优化从科学参数到预测观测的整个链条。例如，人工智能科学家可以通过化学过程模型进行区分，以确定预期产生所需结果的反应条件，或者通过光学模拟器来设计具有指定属性的结构。Differentiable simulators make it possible to optimise through the complete chain from scientific parameters to predicted observations. For example, an AI Scientist could differentiate through a model of a chemical process to identify reaction conditions expected to produce a desired outcome, or through an optical simulator to design a structure with specified properties.

JAX 和 Zygote 等系统已将自动微分从神经网络扩展到通用数值程序。这使得微分方程求解器、线性代数例程、概率模型和其他科学软件能够参与基于梯度的学习。Systems such as JAX and Zygote have extended automatic differentiation from neural networks to general numerical programs. This allows differential-equation solvers, linear-algebra routines, probabilistic models and other scientific software to participate in gradient-based learning.

对于每个科学过程来说，可微分性并不是自动的。模拟可能包含离散事件、不连续性、不断变化的拓扑、随机选择或混沌动力学。反向模式微分还可能需要大量存储器，因为必须保留或重建中间程序状态。Differentiability is not automatic for every scientific process. Simulations may contain discrete events, discontinuities, changing topologies, stochastic choices or chaotic dynamics. Reverse-mode differentiation can also require large amounts of memory because intermediate program states must be retained or reconstructed.

梯度不是因果解释。它描述了指定模拟器的输出在局部数学扰动下如何变化。如果模型不正确或者参数与物理上可实现的干预措施不对应，则梯度可能没有什么科学意义。A gradient is not a causal explanation. It describes how the output of the specified simulator changes under a local mathematical perturbation. If the model is incorrect or the parameter does not correspond to a physically realisable intervention, the gradient may have little scientific meaning.

8.6。混合科学模型8.6. Hybrid scientific models

纯机械模型和纯数据驱动模型具有互补的优势。机械模型对已建立的知识、守恒定律和因果假设进行编码，但可能不完整或计算成本昂贵。机器学习模型很灵活，可以近似Purely mechanistic models and purely data-driven models have complementary strengths. Mechanistic models encode established knowledge, conservation laws and causal assumptions, but may be incomplete or computationally expensive. Machine-learning models are flexible and can approximate

46

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

未建模的关系，但可能需要大型数据集，并且在其训练分布之外表现不可预测。unmodelled relationships, but may require large datasets and behave unpredictably outside their training distribution.

混合模型结合了这两种方法。已知的物理或生物模型提供了主要结构，而学习的组件则代表未知、未解决或过于昂贵而无法直接计算的过程。Hybrid models combine the two approaches. A known physical or biological model supplies the principal structure, while a learned component represents processes that are unknown, unresolved or too expensive to calculate directly.

通用微分方程提供了这一想法的通用表述。微分方程模型通过可训练函数进行增强，这些函数可以是神经网络、符号表达式或其他参数化组件（Rackauckas 等人，2020）。模型的已知部分强加了科学结构，而学习的部分则捕获了缺失的动态。Universal Differential Equations provide one general formulation of this idea. A differential-equation model is augmented with trainable functions, which may be neural networks, symbolic expressions or other parameterised components (Rackauckas et al., 2020). The known part of the model imposes scientific structure, while the learned component captures missing dynamics.

例如，如果For example, if

然后𝑓then 𝑓+',+代表既定理论，𝑓-++',+ represents the established theory and 𝑓-++',+ 可以从观察中得知。由此产生的模型仍然与科学概念相关，同时使用数据来纠正或完善理论。+',+ can be learned from observations. The resulting model remains connected to scientific concepts while using data to correct or complete the theory.

当完整的第一原理模拟不可行但纯粹的数据驱动模型会忽略太多既定知识时，混合模型尤其有价值。它们还提供了模型发现的自然途径：随后可以通过显式数学表达式对学习的组件进行分析和近似。Hybrid models are especially valuable when a full first-principles simulation is infeasible but a purely datadriven model would ignore too much established knowledge. They also provide a natural route towards model discovery: the learned component can subsequently be analysed and approximated by an explicit mathematical expression.

8.7.替代模型和仿真8.7. Surrogate models and emulation

许多科学模拟的计算成本很高。对气候、湍流、分子动力学或复杂工程系统的高分辨率模拟可能需要数小时、数天或数月的计算。这使得运行模拟器数百万次以进行优化、不确定性分析或实验设计是不切实际的。Many scientific simulations are computationally expensive. A high-resolution simulation of climate, turbulence, molecular dynamics or a complex engineering system may require hours, days or months of computation. This makes it impractical to run the simulator millions of times for optimisation, uncertainty analysis or experimental design.

代理模型是昂贵模拟器的更便宜的近似：A surrogate model is a cheaper approximation to an expensive simulator:

代理接受原始模拟生成的输入输出示例的训练。一旦经过训练，它就可以更快地做出预测。The surrogate is trained on input–output examples generated by the original simulation. Once trained, it can make predictions much more rapidly.

经典的替代方法包括响应面、样条、降阶模型和高斯过程。现代方法使用神经网络、图网络和算子学习方法。Classical surrogate methods include response surfaces, splines, reduced-order models and Gaussian processes. Modern approaches use neural networks, graph networks and operator-learning methods.

神经算子学习函数之间的映射，而不仅仅是固定维向量之间的映射。例如，他们可以学习将初始条件或空间变化系数映射到微分方程解的算子。 DeepONet 和傅里叶神经算子证明，单个学习模型可以在不同的初始条件和参数设置下近似微分方程组解（Lu 等人，2021；Li 等人，2021）。Neural operators learn mappings between functions rather than only mappings between fixeddimensional vectors. They can learn, for example, the operator mapping an initial condition or spatially varying coefficient to the solution of a differential equation. DeepONet and the Fourier Neural Operator demonstrated that a single learned model could approximate families of differential-equation solutions over different initial conditions and parameter settings (Lu et al., 2021; Li et al., 2021).

代理对人工智能科学家来说很有价值，因为它们允许快速探索大空间的模型、参数和实验条件。它们可以支持不确定性传播；敏感性分析；逆问题；贝叶斯优化；实验设计；实时控制；以及大量假设的比较。Surrogates are valuable to AI Scientists because they allow rapid exploration of large spaces of models, parameters and experimental conditions. They can support uncertainty propagation; sensitivity analysis; inverse problems; Bayesian optimisation; experimental design; real-time control; and the comparison of large numbers of hypotheses.

但代理继承了用于训练它的模拟的局限性，并引入了额外的近似误差。它可以在训练域内准确地插值，但在新颖的情况下会失败But a surrogate inherits limitations from the simulations used to train it and introduces additional approximation error. It may interpolate accurately within the training domain but fail under novel

47

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

条件。因此，其不确定性应包括原始模拟器的不确定性和近似产生的不确定性。conditions. Its uncertainty should therefore include both the uncertainty of the original simulator and uncertainty arising from the approximation.

代理不应默默地取代原始模拟器。人工智能科学家应该确定预测何时超出训练分布，并有选择地调用完整的模拟器来检查或改进代理。这创建了一个多保真度架构，其中廉价的近似用于广泛搜索，而昂贵的高保真模拟用于确认。A surrogate should not silently replace the original simulator. An AI Scientist should identify when a prediction lies outside the training distribution and selectively call the full simulator to check or improve the surrogate. This creates a multi-fidelity architecture in which cheap approximations are used for broad search and expensive high-fidelity simulations are used for confirmation.

8.8.多尺度和多物理场仿真8.8. Multiscale and multiphysics simulation

许多科学系统涉及在不同空间和时间尺度上运行的过程。例如，生物有机体取决于分子相互作用、细胞动力学、组织组织和整个有机体生理学。气候模型整合了从微观云形成到全球大气环流的过程。Many scientific systems involve processes operating at different spatial and temporal scales. A biological organism, for example, depends on molecular interactions, cellular dynamics, tissue organisation and whole-organism physiology. A climate model integrates processes ranging from microscopic cloud formation to global atmospheric circulation.

没有单一的建模形式在每个尺度上都是最佳的。因此，未来的人工智能科学家将需要构建和协调多尺度和多物理场模拟。这些可以将量子计算与分子动力学、分子动力学与连续介质力学或细胞模型与组织水平描述联系起来。No single modelling formalism is optimal at every scale. Future AI Scientists will therefore need to construct and coordinate multiscale and multiphysics simulations. These may connect quantum calculations to molecular dynamics, molecular dynamics to continuum mechanics, or cellular models to tissue-level descriptions.

模型之间的接口通常比模型本身更困难。在一种尺度上明确表示的数量必须转换为另一种尺度下的有效参数。错误和不确定性必须通过这些接口传播。The interfaces between models are often more difficult than the models themselves. Quantities represented explicitly at one scale must be translated into effective parameters at another. Errors and uncertainty must be propagated across these interfaces.

人工智能可以帮助识别简化的表示、学习封闭关系并自适应地决定哪里需要高分辨率。但学习耦合必须遵守守恒定律和其他科学约束。当嵌入到较长的模拟中时，高度精确的局部近似仍然可能产生物理上不可能的全局行为。AI can help identify reduced representations, learn closure relationships and decide adaptively where high resolution is required. But learned couplings must respect conservation laws and other scientific constraints. A highly accurate local approximation may still produce physically impossible global behaviour when embedded in a longer simulation.

“模拟智能”一词是为了将科学模拟与机器学习、概率编程、因果推理、优化和自动化编程相集成而提出的（Lavin 等人，2021）。这种整合很重要，因为没有一种方法足以进行自主科学推理。The phrase ‘simulation intelligence’ has been proposed for the integration of scientific simulation with machine learning, probabilistic programming, causal inference, optimisation and automated programming (Lavin et al., 2021). This integration is important because no single method is sufficient for autonomous scientific reasoning.

8.9。数字孪生8.9. Digital twins

数字孪生是特定物理系统的计算模型，可以使用该系统的观察结果反复更新。传统模型可能代表一般类别的对象，而数字孪生则试图代表特定的机器、患者、实验室或环境。A digital twin is a computational model of a particular physical system that is repeatedly updated using observations from that system. Whereas a conventional model may represent a general class of objects, a digital twin seeks to represent a specific machine, patient, laboratory or environment.

循环是：The cycle is:

  • 从物理系统收集观察结果；Collect observations from the physical system;

  • 更新模型的状态和参数；Update the state and parameters of the model;

  • 模拟其未来的行为；Simulate its future behaviour;

  • 确定有用或有风险的干预措施；Identify useful or risky interventions;

  • 应用或建议干预；Apply or recommend an intervention;

  • 吸收由此产生的观察结果。Assimilate the resulting observations.

因此，数字孪生与人工智能科学家密切相关。两者都需要模型、观察、不确定性、干预和迭代信念修正的整合。Digital twins are therefore closely related to AI Scientists. Both require the integration of models, observations, uncertainty, intervention and iterative belief revision.

48

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

数字孪生不应被误认为是完美的数字副本。该模型的充分性取决于可用的测量、假设和预期用途。不同的决策可能需要不同级别的保真度。足以监测的模型可能不足以进行因果干预。A digital twin should not be mistaken for a perfect digital copy. It is a model whose adequacy depends on the available measurements, assumptions and intended use. Different decisions may require different levels of fidelity. A model sufficient for monitoring may be inadequate for causal intervention.

可靠的数字孪生的构建在很大程度上仍然是定制的。扩展它们需要标准表示、可互操作的软件、不确定性量化以及将机械模型与流数据相结合的系统方法（Niederer 等人，2021）。The construction of reliable digital twins remains largely bespoke. Scaling them requires standard representations, interoperable software, uncertainty quantification and systematic methods for combining mechanistic models with streaming data (Niederer et al., 2021).

8.10。 AI 科学家的模拟8.10. Simulation within an AI Scientist

未来的人工智能科学家将把模拟融入整个科学循环中。候选假设必须转化为可执行模型，其预测可以在相关实验条件下得出，并与竞争假设的预测进行比较。系统应识别导致不同结果的假设和参数，传播不确定性，并选择预期能够区分替代方案的实验。Future AI Scientists will incorporate simulation throughout the scientific loop. A candidate hypothesis must be translated into an executable model whose predictions can be derived under relevant experimental conditions and compared with those of competing hypotheses. The system should identify the assumptions and parameters responsible for divergent outcomes, propagate uncertainty, and select experiments expected to distinguish among the alternatives.

实验结束后，人工智能科学家应将观察结果与模拟预测分布进行比较，更新竞争假设的概率，识别系统模型差异，并在必要时修改模型。它通常应该维护多个模型，而不是过早地致力于一个模型：模型平均可以支持预测，同时可以专门选择判别实验来揭示结构差异。After an experiment, the AI Scientist should compare the observations with the simulated predictive distributions, update the probabilities of the competing hypotheses, identify systematic model discrepancies and revise the model where necessary. It should normally maintain several models rather than commit prematurely to one: model averaging may support prediction, while discriminating experiments can be selected specifically to expose structural differences.

每个模拟都应保留为可重现的计算对象。记录应包含方程或源代码、参数值、初始和边界条件、软件和库版本、数值算法和公差、随机数种子、相关硬件、输入数据集和科学结论中使用的输出。Every simulation should be preserved as a reproducible computational object. The record should contain the equations or source code, parameter values, initial and boundary conditions, software and library versions, numerical algorithms and tolerances, random-number seeds, relevant hardware, input datasets and the outputs used in the scientific conclusion.

模拟还提供了一个相对安全且廉价的环境，可以在物理执行之前检查所提出的实验。它可以拒绝不可能的协议、估计预期结果并识别信息丰富的测量结果。然而，它不能揭示模型中不存在的现象。因此，人工智能科学家必须结合使用模拟和物理实验：通过模拟来推导和比较结果，通过实验来确定哪个代表的世界最接近现实。Simulation also provides a comparatively safe and inexpensive environment in which proposed experiments can be checked before physical execution. It can reject impossible protocols, estimate expected outcomes and identify informative measurements. It cannot, however, reveal phenomena absent from the model. AI Scientists must therefore use simulation and physical experimentation together: simulation to derive and compare consequences, and experiment to determine which represented world most closely corresponds to reality.

9. 观察、测量和实验9. Observation, Measurement and Experimentation

大自然是科学假设的最终裁判。观察记录了大自然的行为。实验介入并向大自然提出一个受控的问题。人工智能科学家必须同时做到这两点，他们必须准确地知道测量的内容、测量的方式以及可能出现的问题。Nature is the final judge of a scientific hypothesis. Observation records what nature does. Experiment intervenes and asks nature a controlled question. AI Scientists must do both, and they must know exactly what was measured, how it was measured and what could have gone wrong.

因此，普通人工智能科学家必须支持观察科学和实验科学。它必须决定观察什么、如何测量、哪些干预措施是可能的且合乎道德的，以及结果数据如何与其模型中的理论量相关。A general AI Scientist must therefore support both observational and experimental science. It must decide what to observe, how to measure it, which interventions are possible and ethical, and how the resulting data relate to the theoretical quantities in its models.

9.1.观察、实地科学和自然实验9.1. Observation, field science and natural experiments

观察并不是世界的被动复制。它是通过选择测量地点、时间和方式、使用哪些仪器、如何采样以及记录哪些信号而产生的。因此，观察设计与实验设计一样重要。An observation is not a passive copy of the world. It is produced through choices about where, when and how to measure, which instruments to use, how to sample and which signals to record. Observational design can therefore be as important as experimental design.

49

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

在天文学中，人工智能科学家可以安排望远镜时间、选择波长、协调跨设施的观测并在检测到瞬态事件后调整其策略。在生态学中，它可以结合现场传感器、卫星图像、声学监测和自动驾驶车辆。在流行病学中，它可以利用政策变化或环境变化产生的自然实验。在古生物学中，它可以选择挖掘地点并将不完整的物理痕迹与地质背景结合起来。In astronomy, an AI Scientist may schedule telescope time, choose wavelengths, coordinate observations across facilities and adapt its strategy after detecting a transient event. In ecology, it may combine field sensors, satellite imagery, acoustic monitoring and autonomous vehicles. In epidemiology, it may exploit natural experiments created by policy changes or environmental variation. In palaeontology, it may select excavation sites and integrate incomplete physical traces with geological context.

观测系统必须解决选择效应。望远镜仅检测高于其灵敏度阈值的物体。临床数据库包含进入医疗保健系统的患者。现场传感器仅记录其部署的位置。因此，观察过程必须明确表示。如果没有一个可以观察到但没有观察到的模型，人工智能科学家可能会将测量偏差误认为是自然属性。Observational systems must address selection effects. A telescope detects only objects above its sensitivity threshold. A clinical database contains patients who entered a healthcare system. A field sensor records only the locations in which it was deployed. The observation process must therefore be represented explicitly. Without a model of what could have been observed but was not, the AI Scientist may mistake measurement bias for a property of nature.

有些观察是机会主义的。罕见的天文事件、自然灾害和疫情爆发可能会毫无预警地发生。人工智能系统可以持续监控数据流并触发快速跟进。这是相对于纯人类科学的一个重要优势，只要自动触发被校准并为以后的解释保留足够的背景。Some observations are opportunistic. Rare astronomical events, natural disasters and outbreaks may occur without warning. AI systems can monitor data streams continuously and trigger rapid follow-up. This is an important advantage over human-only science, provided that automated triggers are calibrated and preserve sufficient context for later interpretation.

现场和观测科学还需要物流、机器人和安全。自动驾驶车辆可以在海洋、极地、矿山或太空中收集样本。科学规划者必须考虑导航、能源、通信、天气以及失败行动无法修复的可能性。Field and observational science also require logistics, robotics and safety. Autonomous vehicles may collect samples in oceans, polar regions, mines or space. The scientific planner must consider navigation, energy, communications, weather and the possibility that a failed action cannot be repaired.

9.2.测量、校准和可追溯性9.2. Measurement, calibration and traceability

科学理论涉及数量、实体和关系；仪器产生信号。测量将两者联系起来。人工智能科学家必须表示仪器输出如何取决于感兴趣的基础数量、样本、环境和仪器状态。Scientific theories concern quantities, entities and relations; instruments produce signals. Measurement connects the two. An AI Scientist must represent how an instrument output depends on the underlying quantity of interest, the sample, the environment and the instrument state.

测量模型可以示意性地写为：A measurement model may be written schematically as:

𝑧= 𝑔(𝑞, 𝑠, 𝑒) + 𝜀

其中，𝑧 表示观测信号，𝑞 表示真实量，𝑠 表示仪器状态，𝑒 表示环境，𝜀 表示误差项。测量误差不一定是独立的噪声。仪器会漂移、饱和、具有检测限并对干扰物质做出反应。样品可能会降解。图像分析管道可能会引入系统分割错误。因此，结果可能是精确的但不准确。Here, 𝑧 denotes the observed signal, 𝑞 the true quantity, 𝑠 the instrument state, 𝑒 the environment, and 𝜀 the error term. Measurement error is not necessarily independent noise. Instruments drift, saturate, possess detection limits and respond to interfering substances. Samples may degrade. Image-analysis pipelines may introduce systematic segmentation errors. A result can therefore be precise but inaccurate.

校准将仪器输出与参考标准相关联。可追溯性通过不间断的校准链将测量结果与公认的标准连接起来，并在每个阶段分配不确定性。未来的人工智能科学家应该将校准记录、参考材料和质量控制测量视为证据的一部分，而不是实验室管理。Calibration relates instrument output to reference standards. Traceability connects a measurement through an unbroken chain of calibrations to recognised standards, with uncertainty assigned at each stage. Future AI Scientists should treat calibration records, reference materials and quality-control measurements as part of the evidence, not as laboratory administration.

完整的测量记录应包括： 仪器的标识和配置；校准状态和参考标准；检测和定量限；采样时间和地点；环境条件；样品制备和监管链；原始信号和转换步骤；测量不确定度；已知的干扰和故障模式。人工智能科学家应该根据正在测试的假设来选择测量。方便的测量可能仅与理论量有微弱的相关性。生物标志物、代理变量和替代终点需要明确的理由。该系统应该代表从物理信号到科学结论的推理链。A complete measurement record should include: The identity and configuration of the instrument; Calibration status and reference standards; Detection and quantification limits; Sampling time and location; Environmental conditions; Sample preparation and chain of custody; Raw signals and transformation steps; Measurement uncertainty; and Known interferences and failure modes. The AI Scientist should select measurements according to the hypotheses being tested. A convenient measurement may be only weakly related to the theoretical quantity. Biomarkers, proxy variables and surrogate endpoints require explicit justification. The system should represent the inferential chain from physical signal to scientific conclusion.

重复测量可以减少随机误差，但不能减少系统偏差。因此，使用不同仪器或物理原理进行独立测量尤其有价值。具有不同故障模式的方法之间的一致性比来自一个自动化管道的重复一致性提供了更有力的证据。Repeated measurement can reduce random error but not systematic bias. Independent measurement using different instruments or physical principles is therefore especially valuable. Agreement among methods with distinct failure modes provides stronger evidence than repeated agreement from one automated pipeline.

50

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

9.3.实验设计9.3. Experimental design

实验设计领域涉及构建实验，其结果支持正确且足够精确的结论（Fisher，1935；Cochran & Cox，1957）。科学上有用的实验必须区分所研究变量的影响与测量误差、生物变异和实验条件之间不受控制的差异。The field of experimental design is concerned with constructing experiments whose results support correct and sufficiently precise conclusions (Fisher, 1935; Cochran & Cox, 1957). A scientifically useful experiment must distinguish the effect of the variable under investigation from measurement error, biological variation and uncontrolled differences between experimental conditions.

实验设计指定：实验单位；干预或治疗；测量的结果；积极、消极和程序控制；重复的数量和类型；根据条件分配实验单位；干扰变量的处理；统计分析；以及解释结果的标准。An experimental design specifies: the experimental units; the interventions or treatments; the measured outcomes; positive, negative and procedural controls; the number and type of replicates; the allocation of experimental units to conditions; the treatment of nuisance variables; the statistical analysis; and the criteria by which the result will be interpreted.

典型的例子是新植物肥料的测试。如果经过处理的植物在一种土壤中生长，而未经处理的植物在另一种土壤中生长，则肥料的作用与土壤的作用不能分开。土壤成分是一个混杂变量。有效的实验必须保持其恒定或以受控方式改变它，以允许估计其效果。The classic example is the testing of a new plant fertiliser. If treated plants are grown in one type of soil and untreated plants in another, the effect of the fertiliser cannot be separated from the effect of the soil. Soil composition is a confounding variable. A valid experiment must either hold it constant or vary it in a controlled manner that permits its effect to be estimated.

控制是根本。阴性对照建立了在没有干预的情况下预期的背景反应。阳性对照表明实验系统可以检测到效果。程序或假对照将干预的效果与执行程序本身的效果区分开来。适当的控制取决于实验的因果结构，不能仅从数据的统计形式推断出来。Controls are fundamental. A negative control establishes the background response expected in the absence of the intervention. A positive control establishes that the experimental system can detect an effect. A procedural or sham control distinguishes the effect of the intervention from the effect of performing the procedure itself. The appropriate controls depend on the causal structure of the experiment and cannot be inferred solely from the statistical form of the data.

随机化通常用于防止实验单元分配中的系统偏差。但无限制的随机化并不总是最有效的设计。分块可以确保已知的变异源在不同条件下适当分布。拉丁方设计可以同时控制两个有害变量。因子设计允许在同一实验中研究多个因素及其相互作用。Randomisation is commonly used to prevent systematic bias in the allocation of experimental units. But unrestricted randomisation is not always the most efficient design. Blocking can ensure that known sources of variation are distributed appropriately across conditions. Latin-square designs can control simultaneously for two nuisance variables. Factorial designs allow several factors and their interactions to be investigated within the same experiment.

复制有几个目的。技术重复估计由测量和处理引起的变化。生物或独立产生的重复估计生物系统或实验制剂之间的差异。仅仅重复测量同一样本并不能证明结果可以推广到独立生成的样本。Replication serves several purposes. Technical replicates estimate variation introduced by measurement and handling. Biological or independently produced replicates estimate variation among biological systems or experimental preparations. Merely repeating the measurement of the same sample does not establish that the result generalises to independently generated samples.

盲法可防止对实验条件的预期影响样品处理、测量或解释。自动化实验可以减少某些形式的操作员偏差，但不能消除样本选择、仪器配置、终点定义或结果分析中的偏差。Blinding prevents expectations about the experimental condition from influencing sample handling, measurement or interpretation. Automated experiments can reduce some forms of operator bias, but they do not eliminate bias in the selection of samples, the configuration of instruments, the definition of endpoints or the analysis of results.

统计功效是当特定大小的效应存在时，实验检测到该效应的概率。重复性不足的实验可能无法区分合理的假设，无论其执行的准确性如何。相反，不必要的大型实验会浪费时间、材料和金钱。因此，人工智能科学家应该将功率分析和预期精度纳入实验计划中。Statistical power is the probability that an experiment will detect an effect of a specified size when that effect is present. An experiment with insufficient replication may be incapable of discriminating among plausible hypotheses, regardless of how accurately it is executed. Conversely, unnecessarily large experiments waste time, material and money. AI Scientists should therefore incorporate power analysis and expected precision into experimental planning.

经典的实验设计通常假设完整的设计在进行观察之前就已确定。贝叶斯实验设计通过概率性地处理不确定参数和模型并根据其预期效用选择设计来扩展该框架（Chaloner & Verdinelli，1995）。效用可以反映预测准确性、参数精度、模型之间的区别、熵的减少、经济价值或这些目标的某种组合。Classical experimental design generally assumes that the complete design is fixed before the observations are made. Bayesian experimental design extends this framework by treating uncertain parameters and models probabilistically and selecting designs according to their expected utility (Chaloner & Verdinelli, 1995). The utility may reflect prediction accuracy, parameter precision, discrimination among models, reduction in entropy, economic value or some combination of these objectives.

对于提议的设计 (d)，贝叶斯实验设计选择具有最大预期效用的设计：For a proposed design (d), Bayesian experimental design selects the design with the greatest expected utility:

51

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

𝑑∗ = argmax/𝔼0∼2(0∣/)[𝑈(𝑑, 𝑦)]

其中 𝑦 表示可能的实验结果，𝑈(𝑑, 𝑦) 表示在设计 𝑑 下获得该结果的效用。where 𝑦 denotes a possible experimental outcome and 𝑈(𝑑, 𝑦) denotes the utility of obtaining that outcome under design 𝑑.

这种表述非常适合人工智能科学家，因为它使设计的目标和假设变得明确。它还允许在共同框架内比较实验的成本、风险和预期科学价值。This formulation is well suited to AI Scientists because it makes the objectives and assumptions of the design explicit. It also permits the costs, risks and expected scientific value of experiments to be compared within a common framework.

自适应设计根据早期的观察结果修改实验的后续部分。他们可以更有效地分配资源，但需要小心谨慎。重复检查结果、改变终点或在出现所需结果时停止可能会使传统的统计测试失效。人工智能科学家必须记录所有适应并使用适合自适应设计的推理程序。Adaptive designs modify later parts of an experiment in response to earlier observations. They can allocate resources more efficiently, but they require care. Repeated inspection of results, changing endpoints or stopping when a desired result appears can invalidate conventional statistical tests. An AI Scientist must record all adaptations and use inferential procedures appropriate to the adaptive design.

自动化可以通过确保一致地实施对照、随机化、分组和复制来改进实验设计。但选择有效的设计仍然是一个科学推理问题。完全随机的实验可能会回答一个不重要的问题，测量错误的变量或操纵一个无法区分感兴趣的假设的因素。Automation can improve experimental design by ensuring that controls, randomisation, blocking and replication are implemented consistently. But the choice of a valid design remains a scientific reasoning problem. A perfectly randomised experiment may answer an unimportant question, measure the wrong variable or manipulate a factor that does not distinguish the hypotheses of interest.

9.4.高效的实验选择9.4. Efficient experiment selection

实验科学家面临着时间、金钱、设备、人员和材料的严重限制。科学优先还奖励先于其他研究人员获得正确的结果。因此，一个实验仅仅有效还不够：它还应该被有效地选择。Experimental scientists face severe limitations in time, money, equipment, personnel and materials. Scientific priority also rewards obtaining a correct result before other researchers. It is therefore not sufficient for an experiment to be valid: it should also be selected efficiently.

序贯实验设计使用先前实验的结果来选择每个新实验。这是人工智能科学家实验的自然形式。每个实验周期结束后，系统都会更新其模型或假设概率，并选择预期提供最大后续效益的实验。Sequential experimental design chooses each new experiment using the results of previous experiments. This is the natural form of experimentation for an AI Scientist. After each experimental cycle, the system updates its models or hypothesis probabilities and selects the experiment expected to provide the greatest subsequent benefit.

当代自动驾驶实验室中最广泛使用的算法方法是贝叶斯优化（Shahriari et al., 2016；Frazier, 2018）。贝叶斯优化旨在当每次评估对应于昂贵的实验时优化昂贵的函数。The most widely used algorithmic approach in contemporary self-driving laboratories is Bayesian optimisation (Shahriari et al., 2016; Frazier, 2018). Bayesian optimisation is designed for optimising an expensive function when each evaluation corresponds to a costly experiment.

概率替代模型适合实验观察。该模型预测未测试条件的预期值和不确定性。然后，采集函数通过平衡开发、预期表现良好的测试条件来选择下一个实验；以及模型不确定的探索、测试条件。A probabilistic surrogate model is fitted to the experimental observations. This model predicts both the expected value and the uncertainty of untested conditions. An acquisition function then selects the next experiment by balancing exploitation, testing conditions expected to perform well; and exploration, testing conditions about which the model is uncertain.

常见的获取函数包括预期改进、改进概率、置信上限和信息论标准。现代扩展支持噪声观测、并行批次、多目标、约束、混合离散和连续变量以及不同成本的实验。Common acquisition functions include expected improvement, probability of improvement, upperconfidence bounds and information-theoretic criteria. Modern extensions support noisy observations, parallel batches, multiple objectives, constraints, mixed discrete and continuous variables and experiments with different costs.

当目标是找到最大化或最小化测量量的条件时，贝叶斯优化特别合适：化学反应的产率、催化剂的活性、材料的稳定性或蛋白质的性能。Bayesian optimisation is particularly appropriate when the objective is to find a condition that maximises or minimises a measured quantity: the yield of a chemical reaction, the activity of a catalyst, the stability of a material or the performance of a protein.

但科学发现并不总是一个优化问题。相反，目标可能是学习整个实验空间的准确模型，估计不确定参数或区分相互竞争的假设。在这些情况下，主动学习和贝叶斯最优实验设计通常更合适。But scientific discovery is not always an optimisation problem. The aim may instead be to learn an accurate model of the whole experimental space, estimate uncertain parameters or discriminate among competing hypotheses. In these cases, active learning and Bayesian optimal experimental design are often more appropriate.

主动学习是监督机器学习的一个分支，其中学习系统选择从中学习的示例（Cohn 等人，1996；King 等人，2004）。一个常见的策略是选择一个Active learning is the branch of supervised machine learning in which the learning system selects the examples from which it learns (Cohn et al., 1996; King et al., 2004). A common strategy is to select an

52

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

当前模型最不确定的实验。更一般地，系统可以选择预期最大程度地减少模型参数、预测或竞争假设的不确定性的实验。experiment for which the current model is most uncertain. More generally, the system may choose the experiment expected to produce the greatest reduction in uncertainty over model parameters, predictions or competing hypotheses.

贝叶斯优化和主动学习之间的区别很重要。贝叶斯优化主要寻求找到最优值。主动学习主要旨在改进模型。接近预测最佳值的实验可能对优化有用，但提供的有关实验空间剩余部分的信息很少。相反，在高度不确定的区域进行的实验可能会提供科学信息，但预计不会立即产生有用的结果。The distinction between Bayesian optimisation and active learning is important. Bayesian optimisation seeks principally to locate an optimum. Active learning seeks principally to improve the model. An experiment close to a predicted optimum may be useful for optimisation but provide little information about the remainder of the experimental space. Conversely, an experiment in a highly uncertain region may be scientifically informative without being expected to produce an immediately useful outcome.

最近的工作说明了这种区别的重要性。 BATCHIE 使用贝叶斯主动学习来设计组合药物筛选，其目的是了解反应情况，而不仅仅是确定一种最佳方案。在涉及 206 种药物和 16 种细胞系的前瞻性筛选中，它在观察了大约 4% 的可能实验后产生了有用的预测（Tosh 等人，2025）。Recent work illustrates the importance of this distinction. BATCHIE uses Bayesian active learning to design combination-drug screens whose purpose is to learn the response landscape rather than merely identify one optimum. In a prospective screen involving 206 drugs and 16 cell lines, it produced useful predictions after observing approximately four per cent of the possible experiments (Tosh et al., 2025).

实验选择还可能需要区分不同的模型结构，而不仅仅是估计单个模型内的参数。因此，开发了贝叶斯主动学习方法来选择同时改进参数估计和模型选择的测量，包括在光谱学和生化途径分析中的应用（Nabika 等人，2024 年；Isenberg 等人，2024 年）。Experimental selection can also require discrimination among different model structures rather than merely the estimation of parameters within a single model. Bayesian active-learning methods have therefore been developed to choose measurements that simultaneously improve parameter estimation and model selection, including applications in spectroscopy and biochemical pathway analysis (Nabika et al., 2024; Isenberg et al., 2024).

强化学习 (RL) 也经常与自主实验联系在一起（Angermueller 等人，2020）。在强化学习中，代理与环境交互。在每个时间点，它观察一个状态，选择一个动作，接收奖励并移动到一个新状态。其目标是学习最大化累积奖励的策略。Reinforcement Learning (RL) is also frequently associated with autonomous experimentation (Angermueller et al., 2020). In RL, an agent interacts with an environment. At each time point it observes a state, selects an action, receives a reward and moves to a new state. Its objective is to learn a policy that maximises cumulative reward.

强化学习和科学方法之间的映射并不紧密。应该指定什么为代理、状态和环境并不明显。几种映射是可能的：在物理实验室内运行的实验室机器人系统；在可能的实验空间内起作用的实验选择系统；以及在科学模型空间内起作用的模型改进系统。The mapping between RL and the scientific method is not close. It is not obvious what should be designated as the agent, state and environment. Several mappings are possible: the laboratory robotic system acting within the physical laboratory; the experiment-selection system acting within the space of possible experiments; and the model-improvement system acting within a space of scientific models.

没有一个能够完全掌握科学方法。在许多强化学习应用中，动作成本低廉、反馈迅速且奖励明确指定。科学实验可能需要数小时、数周或数年的时间，消耗稀缺的材料，产生模糊的结果，并产生无法用简单的标量奖励来表示的后果。None captures the scientific method completely. In many RL applications, actions are cheap, feedback is rapid and the reward is clearly specified. Scientific experiments may take hours, weeks or years, consume scarce materials, produce ambiguous results and have consequences that cannot be represented by a simple scalar reward.

尽管如此，强化学习对于较低级别的实验室控制仍然很有价值。它可以优化机器人运动、调度、样品运输、故障恢复或复杂程序的执行。它还可以在可进行足够真实模拟的重复领域中学习实验选择策略。但主动学习提供了与科学方法最接近的标准机器学习类比，因为它的核心关注点是选择能够改进模型的证据。RL can nevertheless be valuable for lower-level laboratory control. It may optimise robotic motion, scheduling, sample transport, fault recovery or the execution of complex procedures. It can also learn experiment-selection policies in repeated domains where sufficiently realistic simulations are available. But active learning provides the closest standard machine-learning analogy to the scientific method because its central concern is the selection of evidence that will improve a model.

人类科学家通常一次只考虑一个或少数假设。机器可以克服这个限制。人工智能科学家可以维持大量相互竞争的假设的概率分布，并根据他们区分这些假设的预期能力来选择实验。Human scientists typically consider only one or a small number of hypotheses at a time. Machines can overcome this limitation. An AI Scientist can maintain a probability distribution over a large set of competing hypotheses and select experiments according to their expected ability to discriminate among them.

假设当前假设集 𝐻、可能的实验 𝑒、直接成本 𝑐(𝑒) 以及一组可能的结果 𝑜。理想的预期成本重现可以写为：Assume a current hypothesis set 𝐻, a possible experiment 𝑒, a direct cost 𝑐(𝑒), and a set of possible outcomes 𝑜. An ideal expected-cost recurrence can be written as:

当不再需要区分其余假设时，𝑉(𝐻) = 0。这里，𝐻6,7 表示观察实验 𝑒 的结果 𝑜 后的假设分布。with 𝑉(𝐻) = 0 when the remaining hypotheses no longer need to be distinguished. Here, 𝐻6,7 denotes the hypothesis distribution after observing outcome 𝑜 from experiment 𝑒.

53

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

它具有决策树的结构：实验形成内部节点，结果形成分支，解决的假设形成叶子。一般来说，构建全局最优的最小成本决策树在计算上是很困难的（Hyafil & Rivest，1976）。因此，实用的人工智能科学家必须使用近似方法，包括贪婪的预期信息增益、熵减少、信息价值、蒙特卡罗树搜索和限制性前瞻。This has the structure of a decision tree: experiments form internal nodes, outcomes form branches and resolved hypotheses form leaves. Constructing the globally optimal minimum-cost decision tree is computationally intractable in general (Hyafil & Rivest, 1976). Practical AI Scientists must therefore use approximations, including greedy expected-information gain, entropy reduction, value of information, Monte Carlo tree search and restricted look-ahead.

最初的机器人科学家证明，智能实验选择可以与人类选择竞争，并且比随机或最便宜优先策略更有效（King 等，2004）。现代系统使用概率模型、主动学习和批次选择将这种方法扩展到更大、更嘈杂的空间。The original Robot Scientist demonstrated that intelligent experiment selection could be competitive with human selection and substantially more efficient than random or cheapest-first strategies (King et al., 2004). Contemporary systems extend this approach to larger and noisier spaces using probabilistic models, active learning and batch selection.

实验的成本不应仅由其货币价格来表示。一个现实的效用函数应该包括执行时间；占用设备的机会成本；样品或试剂短缺；实验失败的概率；安全和环境风险；并行运行实验的可能性；快速确认重要结果的价值；以及所产生的知识的预期科学重要性。The cost of an experiment should not be represented only by its monetary price. A realistic utility function should include execution time; opportunity cost of occupying equipment; scarcity of samples or reagents; probability of experimental failure; safety and environmental risk; the possibility of running experiments in parallel; the value of confirming an important result rapidly; and the expected scientific importance of the knowledge produced.

因此，人工智能科学家不仅应该选择统计上信息最丰富的实验，还应该选择在现有限制下具有最大预期科学价值的实验。An AI Scientist should therefore choose not simply the statistically most informative experiment, but the experiment with the greatest expected scientific value under the available constraints.

9.5。实验方案9.5. Experimental protocols

一旦人类或人工智能科学家选择了一个实验，就必须执行该实验。对执行实验所需操作的有序描述是实验方案。Once a human or AI Scientist has selected an experiment, the experiment must be executed. The ordered description of the actions required to perform an experiment is an experimental protocol.

重复已发表的实验的能力是实验室科学的基础。但许多已发布的协议不完整或含糊不清。它们通常比计算机程序更类似于烹饪食谱（Soldatova 等，2008，2014）。The ability to repeat a published experiment is a foundation of laboratory science. But many published protocols are incomplete or ambiguous. They often resemble cooking recipes more closely than computer programs (Soldatova et al., 2008, 2014).

考虑酵母转化方案中的以下说明：Consider the following instruction from a yeast-transformation protocol:

接种 4 ml 液体 YPAD 或 10 ml SC，并在 30 °C 下摇动孵育过夜。Inoculate 4 ml of liquid YPAD or 10 ml of SC and incubate with shaking overnight at 30 °C.

该指示留下了几个未解答的重要问题。 “接种”是否意味着使用固体板中的单个菌落、特定数量的细胞或先前液体培养物中的一定体积？应使用什么起始密度？ “有震动”是指每分钟 20 转还是每分钟 400 转？ “过夜”是指 12 小时、16 小时还是 24 小时？需要什么容器和工作体积，以及摇床应使用什么轨道直径？This instruction leaves several important questions unanswered. Does ‘inoculate’ mean using a single colony from a solid plate, a defined number of cells or a volume from a previous liquid culture? What starting density should be used? Does ‘with shaking’ mean 20 revolutions per minute or 400 revolutions per minute? Does ‘overnight’ mean 12 hours, 16 hours or 24 hours? What vessel and working volume are required, and what orbital diameter should the shaker use?

不同的解释可以产生不同的生理状态，从而产生不同的实验结果。歧义会引入不受控制的变化并导致不可再现性。Different interpretations can produce different physiological states and therefore different experimental results. Ambiguity introduces uncontrolled variation and contributes to irreproducibility.

因此，越来越需要有效、完整和明确地表示实验方案。这可以通过使用本体、形式语言和可执行计算机程序来实现，而不仅仅是自然语言。There is consequently a growing need to represent experimental protocols efficiently, completely and unambiguously. This can be achieved using ontologies, formal languages and executable computer programs rather than natural language alone.

EXPO 是科学实验的第一个通用本体（Soldatova & King，2006）。 EXACT 是第一个正式表示实验行为的本体（Soldatova 等，2008）。 EXPO 和 EXACT 通过明确实验的目标、结构、材料、动作和结果来支撑机器人科学家系统。EXPO was the first general ontology of scientific experiments (Soldatova & King, 2006). EXACT was the first ontology for formally representing experimental actions (Soldatova et al., 2008). EXPO and EXACT underpinned the Robot Scientist systems by making the goals, structure, materials, actions and results of experiments explicit.

EXACT2 扩展了捕获生物医学协议再现性所需语义的方法，并支持将以自然语言编写的协议翻译为机器可处理的表示形式（Soldatova 等人，2014）。EXACT2 extended the approach to capture the semantics required for biomedical-protocol reproducibility and to support the translation of protocols written in natural language into machine-processable representations (Soldatova et al., 2014).

现代协议系统在几个不同的级别上运行：Modern protocol systems operate at several distinct levels:

54

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

科学语义。 EXPO、EXACT 和 LabOP 等本体描述了实验的目的及其组成部分的科学意义。 LabOP 为生物协议提供了开放的表示形式，可以支持人类可读的程序和自动执行（Bartley 等人，2023）。Scientific semantics.
