Ontologies such as EXPO, EXACT and LabOP describe what an experiment is intended to do and the scientific meaning of its components. LabOP provides an open representation for biological protocols that can support both human-readable procedures and automated execution (Bartley et al., 2023).

可执行协议语言。自动协议将实验室操作表示为结构化指令。化学描述语言 XDL 提供了化学合成过程的独立于硬件的表示，并已用于将化学文献翻译成自动可执行的协议（Steiner 等人，2019；Mehr 等人，2020）。Executable protocol languages. Autoprotocol represents laboratory operations as structured instructions. The Chemical Description Language, XDL, provides a hardware-independent representation of chemical synthesis procedures and has been used to translate the chemical literature into automatically executable protocols (Steiner et al., 2019; Mehr et al., 2020).

硬件抽象。 PyLabRobot 为不同制造商的液体处理器和配件提供了通用的开源 Python 接口，从而减少了为每个机器人系统单独重写相同协议的需要（Wierenga 等人，2023）。Hardware abstraction. PyLabRobot provides a common, open-source Python interface for different manufacturers’ liquid handlers and accessories, reducing the need to rewrite the same protocol separately for each robotic system (Wierenga et al., 2023).

仪器通讯。 SiLA 2 定义了一个面向服务的标准，实验室仪器和软件可以通过该标准公开功能并交换命令和数据（Juchli，2022）。 OPC 统一架构以及实验室和分析设备标​​准提供了源自工业自动化的相关方法。Instrument communication. SiLA 2 defines a service-oriented standard through which laboratory instruments and software can expose capabilities and exchange commands and data (Juchli, 2022). OPC Unified Architecture and the Laboratory and Analytical Device Standard provide related approaches derived from industrial automation.

工作流程编排。 ChemOS、AlabOS 和 IvoryOS 等系统可协调实验规划器、仪器、分析和数据流。 IvoryOS 在六个不同的自动驾驶实验室中展示了一个通用的基于网络的编排界面（Zhang 等人，2025）。Workflow orchestration. Systems such as ChemOS, AlabOS and IvoryOS coordinate experimental planners, instruments, analyses and data flows. IvoryOS demonstrated a common web-based orchestration interface across six different self-driving laboratories (Zhang et al., 2025).

这些层是互补的。设备级通信标准不指定实验的科学目的。高级本体本身并不提供操作特定仪器所需的命令。These layers are complementary. A device-level communication standard does not specify the scientific purpose of an experiment. A high-level ontology does not by itself provide the commands required to operate a particular instrument.

适当的架构类似于编译器。高层次的科学意图逐步转化为： 抽象的实验设计；通过域操作表达的正式协议；为可用设备分配操作的时间表；设备特定命令；受监控的物理执行；以及实际发生情况的机器可读记录。The appropriate architecture resembles a compiler. A high-level scientific intention is progressively translated into: an abstract experimental design; a formal protocol expressed through domain actions; a schedule assigning actions to available equipment; device-specific commands; monitored physical execution; and a machine-readable record of what actually occurred.

预期协议和执行协议之间的区别很重要。机器人可能被指示分配 100 μl，但实际上由于校准漂移、堵塞或蒸发而分配不同的体积。完整的实验记录必须包含命令和测量或推断的物理结果。The distinction between the intended and executed protocol is important. A robot may be instructed to dispense 100 μl but actually dispense a different volume because of calibration drift, blockage or evaporation. A complete experimental record must contain both the command and the measured or inferred physical outcome.

完整的方案表述应标识材料、浓度和数量；设备和校准状态；时间和环境条件；每个动作的前提条件和后置条件；动作之间的依赖关系；可接受的公差；安全限制；质量控制检查；错误处理程序；以及每个协议版本的出处。法学硕士可以帮助将自然语言程序翻译成这种表示形式，但从散文中直接生成机器人命令是不安全的，除非在执行之前根据设备功能、库存、单位、物理约束和安全规则检查结果。A complete protocol representation should identify materials, concentrations and quantities; equipment and calibration status; temporal and environmental conditions; the preconditions and postconditions of each action; dependencies among actions; acceptable tolerances; safety constraints; quality-control checks; error-handling procedures; and the provenance of every protocol version. LLMs can help translate natural-language procedures into this representation, but direct generation of robot commands from prose is unsafe unless the result is checked against equipment capabilities, inventory, units, physical constraints and safety rules before execution.

最近的工作演示了这种验证循环。自主化学计算机反应代理从文献中提取反应程序，将其转换为 XDL，识别不支持的操作，修改代码并提交生成的协议以进行物理执行和分析验证（Pagel 等人，2026）。这提供了将科学文献直接连接到可执行且经过实验验证的协议的早期示例。Recent work demonstrates this verification loop. Autonomous Chemputer Reaction Agents extract reaction procedures from the literature, translate them into XDL, identify unsupported operations, revise the code and submit the resulting protocol for physical execution and analytical validation (Pagel et al., 2026). This provides an early example of connecting the scientific literature directly to executable and experimentally verified protocols.

正式的协议语言不会自动确保可重复性。相同的抽象动作在不同的工具上可能有不同的物理实现。例如，“混合”可能意味着轨道摇动、涡旋、重复抽吸和分配或搅拌。这些操作都不科学Formal protocol languages do not automatically ensure reproducibility. The same abstract action may have different physical implementations on different instruments. ‘Mix’, for example, may mean orbital shaking, vortexing, repeated aspiration and dispensing or stirring. These operations are not scientifically

55

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

在任何情况下都是等价的。因此，协议抽象必须说明可以替代实现的条件（Canty & Abolhasani，2024）。equivalent in every context. Protocol abstractions must therefore state the conditions under which implementations may be substituted (Canty & Abolhasani, 2024).

版本化的协议存储库也很重要。协议应拥有持久标识符、记录的历史记录以及使用每个版本的实验的链接。诸如protocols.io之类的存储库允许详细的协议与使用它们的论文分开引用和修改。Versioned protocol repositories are also important. A protocol should possess a persistent identifier, a recorded history and links to the experiments in which each version was used. Repositories such as protocols.io allow detailed protocols to be cited and revised separately from the papers that use them.

未来的出版物应该包括人类可读的协议和可执行的形式表示。人类可读的版本支持理解和批评。正式版本可以实现实验室之间的自动检查、执行和传输。Future publications should include both a human-readable protocol and an executable formal representation. The human-readable version supports understanding and criticism. The formal version enables automated checking, execution and transfer between laboratories.

9.6。实验重现性9.6. Experimental reproducibility

人工智能科学家的一个例子是，他们创造的科学比典型的人类科学更容易复制（King et al., 2009）。自十七世纪科学革命以来，科学的一个基本要求就是结果应该接受独立测试（Shapin & Schaffer，1985）。然而，相对较少的生物医学发现可以直接复制，并且尝试复制已发表的发现经常遇到困难（Ioannidis，2005；Baker，2016）。One case for AI Scientists is that the science they create is more easily reproduced than typical human science (King et al., 2009). Ever since the seventeenth-century scientific revolution, a fundamental requirement of science has been that results should be open to independent testing (Shapin & Schaffer, 1985). Yet relatively few biomedical findings are subjected to direct reproduction, and attempts to reproduce published findings frequently encounter difficulty (Ioannidis, 2005; Baker, 2016).

我们区分重复性、再现性和鲁棒性（Roper 等人，2022）。当在基本相同的条件下获得一致的测量结果时，结果是可重复的：在短时间内相同的方法、实验室、操作员、设备和材料。当条件发生相关变化（例如不同的操作员、实验室、仪器实施或独立制备的材料）后获得一致的结果时，结果是可重复的。当科学结论在明确相关的实验条件、生物系统或分析选择范围内仍然有效时，结果是稳健的。这些区别很重要，因为在同一台机器上重复相同的自动化程序可能会证明精度，但不会证明潜在的科学主张具有普遍性。We distinguish repeatability, reproducibility and robustness (Roper et al., 2022). A result is repeatable when consistent measurements are obtained under essentially the same conditions: the same method, laboratory, operators, equipment and materials over a short interval. A result is reproducible when a consistent result is obtained after relevant changes in the conditions, such as a different operator, laboratory, instrument implementation or independently prepared material. A result is robust when the scientific conclusion remains valid across an explicitly relevant range of experimental conditions, biological systems or analytical choices. These distinctions matter because repeating the same automated procedure on the same machine may demonstrate precision without demonstrating that the underlying scientific claim generalises.

许多因素会导致不可重复性。原始结果可能无法重复。该实验可能动力不足、随机性较差或受到不受控制的混杂因素的影响。该协议可能省略了重要的细节。试剂、细胞系或环境条件可能有所不同。分析可能包含错误或未报告的自由度。发表的声明可能夸大了观察结果的合理性。Many factors cause irreproducibility. The original result may not have been repeatable. The experiment may have been underpowered, poorly randomised or affected by an uncontrolled confounder. The protocol may have omitted essential details. Reagents, cell lines or environmental conditions may differ. The analysis may contain errors or unreported degrees of freedom. The published claim may overstate what the observations justify.

我们相信大多数已发表的生物医学结果都是可重复的：实验室通常可以重复他们自己已发表的中心发现。如果结果是可重复的但不能独立重现，则可能的解释是已发布的方案或实验背景没有包含足够的信息来重新创建相关条件。We believe that most published biomedical results are repeatable: laboratories can generally repeat their own central published findings. If a result is repeatable but not independently reproducible, a likely explanation is that the published protocol or experimental context did not contain sufficient information to recreate the relevant conditions.

因此，实验方案的模糊性是不可重复性的主要原因。人工智能科学家可以比人类科学家以更详细的语义记录实验元数据。机器可以自动保存准确的时间、温度、仪器设置、液体转移、耗材标识符、校准记录、环境观察、原始数据和分析版本。The ambiguity of experimental protocols is therefore a major source of irreproducibility. AI Scientists can record experimental metadata at much greater semantic detail than human scientists. A machine can automatically preserve exact times, temperatures, instrument settings, liquid transfers, consumable identifiers, calibration records, environmental observations, raw data and analysis versions.

自动化可以提高再现性，因为协议执行一致，可以检测到与预期程序的偏差，所有操作都可以加上时间戳，可以直接从仪器捕获元数据，可以数字化识别样品和试剂，可以将分析链接到原始数据和可执行代码，并且可以重播完整的实验。这些优点将实验记录从回顾性叙述转变为对系统尝试和实际发生的事情的直接追踪。然而，自动化并不能保证再现性：机器人可以以极高的精度重复系统错误，两台仪器可以以不同的方式实现相同的抽象动作，并且共享的软件缺陷可能会导致多个实验室再现相同的人工制品。独立Automation can improve reproducibility because protocols are executed consistently, deviations from the intended procedure can be detected, all actions can be timestamped, metadata can be captured directly from instruments, samples and reagents can be identified digitally, analyses can be linked to raw data and executable code, and complete experiments can be replayed. These advantages turn the experimental record from a retrospective narrative into a direct trace of what the system attempted and what physically occurred. Automation does not, however, guarantee reproducibility: a robot can repeat a systematic error with exceptional precision, two instruments may implement the same abstract action differently, and a shared software defect may cause several laboratories to reproduce the same artefact. Independent

56

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

因此，再现性需要设备、实施和地点的变化，以及明确保留协议的科学语义。reproducibility therefore requires variation in equipment, implementation and location, together with explicit preservation of the scientific semantics of the protocol.

自动复制的潜力已经得到证明。 Eve 被用来测试从癌症生物学文献中提取的主张，使用两个不同的实验团队和乳腺癌细胞系。 43 个陈述找到了可重复性的重要证据，22 个陈述找到了可重复性或稳健性的证据。这些实验还产生了两个偶然的发现（Roper 等人，2022）。这表明对已发表的生物声明进行半自动测试已经是可行的。The potential for automated reproduction has already been demonstrated. Eve was used to test claims extracted from the cancer-biology literature using two different experimental teams and breast-cancer cell lines. Significant evidence for repeatability was found for 43 statements, and evidence for reproducibility or robustness was found for 22 statements. The experiments also produced two serendipitous findings (Roper et al., 2022). This demonstrated that semi-automated testing of published biological claims is already feasible.

解决可重复性危机的最直接的办法是让更多的科学家重复其他科学家的结果。但存在着强烈的社会学和职业抑制因素。复制研究很难获得资助，可能难以发表，并且可能会引起原作作者的敌意。研究人员因宣称一项新发现而获得的职业回报比确定现有主张是否可靠而获得更大的职业回报。The most direct solution to the reproducibility crisis would be for more scientists to reproduce the results of other scientists. But there are strong sociological and career disincentives. Reproduction studies are difficult to fund, may be difficult to publish and can provoke hostility from the authors of the original work. Researchers receive greater career rewards for claiming a novel discovery than for establishing whether an existing claim is reliable.

鉴于在当前的资助和职业体系下复制结果的成本高昂且困难，人类科学家永远不会通过实验证实超过已发表文献的一小部分。大幅增加比例的唯一可行方法是实现流程自动化。Given the high cost and difficulty of reproducing results under the current funding and career system, human scientists will never experimentally confirm more than a small fraction of the published literature. The only feasible way to increase the proportion substantially is to automate the process.

用于自动复制的可扩展系统将从文献中提取可测试的声明和支持协议，将声明和实验条件形式化，并根据重要性、不确定性和可行性对候选声明进行排名。它将把选定的协议转化为可用的自动化实验室，执行重复和独立的实验，分析证据，更新分配给每个声明的概率，并发布积极、消极和不确定的结果。这样的系统将把科学文献从基本上静态的主张集合转变为持续测试和概率加权的知识库。A scalable system for automated reproduction would extract testable claims and supporting protocols from the literature, formalise the claims and experimental conditions, and rank candidate claims by importance, uncertainty and feasibility. It would translate selected protocols to available automated laboratories, execute repeated and independent experiments, analyse the evidence, update the probability assigned to each claim, and publish positive, negative and inconclusive outcomes. Such a system would transform the scientific literature from a largely static collection of claims into a continuously tested and probabilistically weighted knowledge base.

9.7.新颖的实验9.7. Novel experiments

每个实验科学家面临的中心任务是发明能够检验假设的实验。直接关注自动化科学这一方面的研究相对较少。The central task facing every experimental scientist is the invention of experiments capable of testing hypotheses. Relatively little research has focused directly on this aspect of automating science.

实验的发明不同于传统的实验设计。传统的设计假设干预和测量是已知的，并询问如何分配、控制和复制它们。它也不同于高效的实验选择，后者假设可能的实验的预定义列表或参数化系列。The invention of an experiment is different from conventional experimental design. Conventional design assumes that the intervention and measurement are already known and asks how they should be allocated, controlled and replicated. It is also different from efficient experiment selection, which assumes a predefined list or parameterised family of possible experiments.

抽象的新颖实验问题是：给定一个假设、背景科学知识、一组可用材料和实验室能力的描述，生成一个有效的协议，其可能的结果区分假设及其替代方案。The abstract novel-experiment problem is: Given a hypothesis, background scientific knowledge, a set of available materials and a description of laboratory capabilities, generate a valid protocol whose possible outcomes discriminate among the hypothesis and its alternatives.

这是科学推理、因果推理、规划、程序综合和机器人技术中的问题。This is a problem in scientific reasoning, causal inference, planning, program synthesis and robotics.

所有现有的人工智能科学家系统都受到其可以执行的实验类别的限制。他们的行为通常取自一个小的、刻板的、人类定义的词汇。亚当可以在营养缺陷型生长实验中进行选择。 Eve 可以选择化合物和测定条件。许多自动驾驶实验室在预定义的空间内选择温度、浓度、成分或处理参数的值。All existing AI Scientist systems are limited in the classes of experiment they can perform. Their actions are usually drawn from a small, stereotyped and human-defined vocabulary. Adam could choose among auxotrophic-growth experiments. Eve could select compounds and assay conditions. Many self-driving laboratories choose values for temperatures, concentrations, compositions or processing parameters within a predefined space.

这些系统可以自主选择实验，但人类已经指定了可能的行为和测量的本体。当现有的实验词汇不足时，开放式人工智能科学家必须能够发明新的干预措施或测量方法。These systems may select experiments autonomously, but humans have already specified the ontology of possible actions and measurements. An open-ended AI Scientist must be able to invent a new intervention or measurement when the existing experimental vocabulary is inadequate.

57

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

新颖实验的产生需要多种知识：假设预测的因果后果；替代假设下预期的观察结果；所涉及物体的物理和化学特性；可用的仪器及其功能；制备样品所需的操作；测量不确定度和检测限；安全限制；以及仪器输出和科学结论之间的关系。The generation of a novel experiment requires several kinds of knowledge: the causal consequences predicted by the hypothesis; the observations expected under alternative hypotheses; the physical and chemical properties of the objects involved; available instruments and their capabilities; the operations required to prepare samples; measurement uncertainty and detection limits; safety constraints; and the relationship between instrument outputs and scientific conclusions.

人工智能科学家必须首先得出相互竞争的假设之间可观察到的差异。然后，它必须确定能够揭示这种差异的干预措施和测量方法。最后，它必须构建一系列物理动作来实施干预，而不会引入混杂因素。The AI Scientist must first derive an observable difference among the competing hypotheses. It must then identify an intervention and measurement capable of revealing that difference. Finally, it must construct a sequence of physical actions that implements the intervention without introducing confounding factors.

这个过程可以表示为分层规划。在最高级别，系统选择实验策略。在中间层面，它将策略分解为准备、干预、孵化、分离和测量等操作。在最底层，这些操作被编译成针对特定设备的命令。This process can be represented as hierarchical planning. At the highest level, the system chooses an experimental strategy. At intermediate levels, it decomposes that strategy into operations such as preparation, intervention, incubation, separation and measurement. At the lowest level, these operations are compiled into commands for specific equipment.

该计划必须了解库存。实验不能需要无法获得的试剂或不能达到所需精度的仪器。它还必须考虑实验室的状况：哪些设备被占用、哪些样品稳定、哪些消耗品剩余以及哪些操作可以安全地并行进行。The plan must be inventory-aware. An experiment cannot require a reagent that is unavailable or an instrument incapable of the required precision. It must also consider the state of the laboratory: which equipment is occupied, which samples are stable, which consumables remain and which actions can safely be conducted in parallel.

法学硕士提供了重要的新功能，因为他们可以搜索仪器文档、检索协议并提出实验程序。 Coscientist 证明，基于法学硕士的代理可以规划化学合成、使用技术文档、编写机器人控制代码并进行反应优化实验（Boiko 等人，2023）。LLMs provide important new capabilities because they can search instrument documentation, retrieve protocols and propose experimental procedures. Coscientist demonstrated that an LLM-based agent could plan chemical syntheses, use technical documentation, write robot-control code and conduct reaction-optimisation experiments (Boiko et al., 2023).

此类系统是迈向新型实验生成的早期步骤，但它们通常会重新组合文档或训练数据中已描述的操作和程序。进行真正新颖的实验可能需要创建新的测定方法、针对意外目的调整设备或设计新装置。Such systems are early steps towards novel experimental generation, but they generally recombine operations and procedures already described in their documentation or training data. Generating a truly novel experiment may require creating a new assay, adapting equipment for an unanticipated purpose or designing new apparatus.

一个重要的中间能力是实验故障排除。当实验失败时，人工智能科学家应该确定失败是否与假设相矛盾，或者是程序、试剂、仪器或分析的结果。它应该提出诊断实验并修改方案。 AutoLabs 和相关的多智能体系统展示了自动化化学实验中显式错误检测和自我纠正的初始形式（Panapitiya 等人，2026）。An important intermediate capability is experimental troubleshooting. When an experiment fails, the AI Scientist should determine whether the failure contradicts the hypothesis or instead results from the procedure, reagent, instrument or analysis. It should propose diagnostic experiments and revise the protocol. AutoLabs and related multi-agent systems demonstrate initial forms of explicit error detection and self-correction in automated chemical experimentation (Panapitiya et al., 2026).

在执行新生成的协议之前，人工智能科学家应该对其进行计算测试。形式验证可以检测不一致的前提条件、不可能的时间表和不安全的操作。数字孪生和物理模拟可以预测机器人运动是否可行以及流程是否可能超出设备限制。这些检查不能确定科学实验是否有效，但可以排除许多无效的实施。Before executing a newly generated protocol, an AI Scientist should test it computationally. Formal verification can detect inconsistent preconditions, impossible schedules and unsafe actions. Digital twins and physical simulations can predict whether robotic movements are feasible and whether processes are likely to exceed equipment limits. These checks cannot establish that the scientific experiment will work, but they can exclude many invalid implementations.

新颖实验设计的最先进形式是新实验技术的发明。伟大的科学进步往往依赖于观察世界的新方式：望远镜、显微镜、云室、粒子加速器、聚合酶链式反应和 DNA 测序仪。一位开放式的人工智能科学家最终不仅必须能够使用现有的仪器，而且还必须能够设计和调试新的仪器。The most advanced form of novel experimental design is the invention of new experimental technology. Great scientific advances have often depended on new ways of observing the world: the telescope, microscope, cloud chamber, particle accelerator, polymerase chain reaction and DNA sequencer. An open-ended AI Scientist must eventually be able not only to use existing instruments, but also to design and commission new ones.

9.8.实验室自动化9.8. Laboratory automation

从历史上看，实验室自动化是由执行大量相关实验的需求驱动的，特别是在药物筛选、临床分析和制造质量控制方面。它现在已成为全球主要产业。实验室自动化能力稳步提升。机器人Historically, laboratory automation was driven by the need to execute large numbers of related experiments, particularly in pharmaceutical screening, clinical analysis and manufacturing quality control. It is now a major global industry. The capabilities of laboratory automation have advanced steadily. Robots

58

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

现在可以执行人类在化学和生物实验室中执行的大部分（尽管不是全部）常规操作。这些包括液体处理、样品转移、孵育、离心、过滤、色谱、显微镜、光谱、细胞培养、菌落操作和部分化学合成。can now perform most, although not all, of the routine operations performed by humans in chemistry and biology laboratories. These include liquid handling, sample transfer, incubation, centrifugation, filtration, chromatography, microscopy, spectroscopy, cell culture, colony manipulation and parts of chemical synthesis.

自动化提高了科学生产力，因为机器人可以比人类更便宜、更快速、更准确、更连续地完成许多明确定义的任务。它们不会疲劳，可以整夜工作，并可以自动记录自己的动作。与经过类似培训的人类劳动力相​​比，机器人的能力也可以更容易地增加或减少。Automation increases scientific productivity because robots can work more cheaply, rapidly, accurately and continuously than humans for many well-defined tasks. They do not tire, can operate throughout the night and can record their actions automatically. Robotic capacity can also be increased or reduced more easily than a comparably trained human workforce.

大多数实验室自动化仍然是固定的和高度结构化的。仪器在防护罩内运行或通过机械臂和轨道连接。必须仔细指定布局、实验室器具和操作顺序。设备或消耗品的微小变化可能需要大量的重新编程。Most laboratory automation remains fixed and highly structured. Instruments operate inside protective enclosures or are connected by robotic arms and tracks. The layout, labware and sequence of actions must be specified carefully. Small changes in equipment or consumables may require substantial reprogramming.

自动化并不是自动的（Christensen 等人，2021）。建设和维护综合实验室需要机器人、控制、软件、分析科学和相关实验领域的专业知识。不同制造商的仪器使用不兼容的接口。必须补充消耗品、清除废物、校准设备和运输样品。人类可以凭直觉纠正的故障可能会停止自动化工作流程。Automation is not automatic (Christensen et al., 2021). Building and maintaining an integrated laboratory requires expertise in robotics, control, software, analytical science and the relevant experimental domain. Instruments from different manufacturers use incompatible interfaces. Consumables must be replenished, waste removed, equipment calibrated and samples transported. Failures that a human would correct intuitively may stop an automated workflow.

实验室物流仍然是一个主要限制。液体处理机器人可以自动执行转移，而技术人员则装载板、打开包装、更换吸头、移动样品并解决故障。自动化这些看似简单的动作需要感知、灵巧的操作和对实验室物理状态的理解。Laboratory logistics remain a major limitation. A liquid-handling robot may perform transfers automatically, while a human technician loads plates, opens packages, replaces tips, moves samples and resolves faults. Automating these apparently simple actions requires perception, dexterous manipulation and understanding of the laboratory’s physical state.

移动机器人提供了一种方法。伯格等人。开发了一种移动机器人化学家，能够在传统实验室中导航、操作为人类设计的仪器并进行闭环搜索以改进光催化剂（Burger 等人，2020）。移动系统可以连接仪器，而不需要定制的固定机器人轨道，尽管共享实验室中的安全导航和操作仍然具有挑战性。Mobile robots provide one approach. Burger et al. developed a mobile robotic chemist capable of navigating a conventional laboratory, operating instruments designed for humans and conducting a closed-loop search for improved photocatalysts (Burger et al., 2020). Mobile systems can connect instruments without requiring a bespoke fixed robotic track, although safe navigation and manipulation in shared laboratories remain challenging.

实验室自动化和通用人工智能机器人之间的分离减缓了进展。实验室自动化传统上强调精心设计的环境中的可靠性和吞吐量。人工智能机器人技术强调在结构化程度较低的环境中的感知、导航和操作。未来的实验室将把工业自动化的可靠性与现代机器人技术的灵活性结合起来。The separation between laboratory automation and general AI robotics has slowed progress. Laboratory automation has traditionally emphasised reliability and throughput in carefully engineered environments. AI robotics has emphasised perception, navigation and manipulation in less structured settings. Future laboratories will combine the reliability of industrial automation with the flexibility of modern robotics.

互操作性是另一个核心问题。由五台仪器组装而成的自动驾驶实验室可能需要五个独立的软件接口和连接它们的自定义代码。更换一台仪器可能需要重写工作流程。Interoperability is another central problem. A self-driving laboratory assembled from five instruments may require five separate software interfaces and custom code connecting them. Replacing one instrument may require rewriting the workflow.

SiLA 2 等标准解决设备通信问题，而 PyLabRobot 等开放软件解决硬件抽象问题。 IvoryOS 等编排系统可以跨不同的自动驾驶实验室提供更高级别的控制。动态知识图可以表示分布式实验室系统的能力、材料和数据流（Bai et al., 2024）。Standards such as SiLA 2 address device communication, while open software such as PyLabRobot addresses hardware abstraction. Orchestration systems such as IvoryOS provide higher-level control across diverse self-driving laboratories. Dynamic knowledge graphs can represent the capabilities, materials and data flows of distributed laboratory systems (Bai et al., 2024).

质量控制必须是自动化不可或缺的一部分。系统应检测失败的液体转移、意外的光谱、污染的样品、校准漂移和物理障碍。传感器和计算机视觉可以比较预期和实际的实验室状态。每个自动化操作都应返回其成功发生的证据，而不仅仅是报告已发出软件命令。Quality control must be integral to automation. The system should detect failed liquid transfers, unexpected spectra, contaminated samples, calibration drift and physical obstructions. Sensors and computer vision can compare the intended and actual laboratory states. Every automated action should return evidence that it occurred successfully rather than merely report that a software command was issued.

当人工智能系统控制物理设备时，安全性尤其重要，因为生成的动作可能会将单独允许的操作组合成不安全的序列。因此，自主实验室需要分层安全工程。设备级联锁和防撞系统Safety is especially important when AI systems control physical equipment because a generated action may combine individually permitted operations into an unsafe sequence. Autonomous laboratories therefore require layered safety engineering. Equipment-level interlocks and collision-avoidance systems

59

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

应独立于人工智能规划器来限制物理运动；机器可读的危险模型应限制化学和生物操作；应持续监测异常状态；危险或不可逆转的行为应需要明确的人为授权。紧急停止机制必须保持独立于人工智能系统。智能化程度的提高并不能消除对传统安全工程的需求：即使是最有能力的规划者也无法在物理上超越安全联锁装置。should constrain physical motion independently of the AI planner; machine-readable hazard models should restrict chemical and biological operations; abnormal states should be monitored continuously; and hazardous or irreversible actions should require explicit human authorisation. Emergency-stop mechanisms must remain independent of the AI system. Increasing intelligence does not remove the need for conventional safety engineering: even the most capable planner should be physically unable to override a safety interlock.

9.9.云和联合实验室9.9. Cloud and federated laboratories

云实验室致力于解决实验室自动化的高成本、可访问性和集成困难等问题。类比是云计算。研究人员无需购买和维护所有必要的设备，而是可以使用集中运营的自动化实验室作为服务。Cloud laboratories seek to address the high cost, limited accessibility and integration difficulties of laboratory automation. The analogy is with cloud computing. Instead of purchasing and maintaining all the necessary equipment, researchers access a centrally operated automated laboratory as a service.

用户通过软件界面远程指定实验。样品可以发送到该设施或从那里已有的材料中选择。机器人和训练有素的技术人员执行协议，并以电子方式返回结果数据。商业示例包括 Emerald Cloud Lab 和 Strateos，而学术访问已被提倡作为提高可重复性和扩大先进设备访问范围的一种手段（Armer 等人，2023）。Users specify experiments remotely through a software interface. Samples may be sent to the facility or selected from materials already held there. Robots and trained technicians execute the protocols, and the resulting data are returned electronically. Commercial examples include Emerald Cloud Lab and Strateos, while academic access has been advocated as a means of improving reproducibility and widening access to advanced equipment (Armer et al., 2023).

云实验室提供了几个潜在的优势：无需本地资本支出即可获得昂贵的设备；连续运行；标准化执行；结果与产生结果的命令自动关联；更容易共享和重复协议；以及没有先进设施的机构的研究人员的访问权限。Cloud laboratories provide several potential advantages: access to expensive equipment without local capital expenditure; continuous operation; standardised execution; automatic association of results with the commands that produced them; easier sharing and repetition of protocols; and access for researchers at institutions without advanced facilities.

它们可以使“虚拟”生物技术或制药公司拥有科学和计算专业知识，而无需维护完整的物理实验室。他们还可以通过允许设备落后的机构中的学生和研究人员远程访问复杂的仪器来实现实验科学的民主化。They may enable ‘virtual’ biotechnology or pharmaceutical companies that possess scientific and computational expertise without maintaining complete physical laboratories. They can also democratise experimental science by allowing students and researchers in poorly equipped institutions to access sophisticated instruments remotely.

卡内基梅隆大学建立了第一个基于 Emerald Cloud Lab 平台的大型大学云实验室，旨在提供对各种化学和生命科学设备的远程访问，并支持人工智能实验、教育和再现性（卡内基梅隆大学，2021 年；Armer 等人，2023 年）。Carnegie Mellon University established the first large university cloud laboratory based on the Emerald Cloud Lab platform, designed to provide remote access to a broad range of chemical and life-science equipment and to support AI-enabled experimentation, education and reproducibility (Carnegie Mellon University, 2021; Armer et al., 2023).

云实验室也有局限性。样品和试剂必须进行运输，从而对不稳定或受管制的材料造成延误和限制。并非每个实验程序都可以使用可用的设备进行。专有语言和界面可能会造成供应商锁定。敏感数据、知识产权和生物材料引发了治理和安全问题。Cloud laboratories also have limitations. Samples and reagents must be transported, creating delays and constraints on unstable or regulated materials. Not every experimental procedure can be performed using the available equipment. Proprietary languages and interfaces may create vendor lock-in. Sensitive data, intellectual property and biological materials raise governance and security concerns.

云实验室通常最适合可以通过一组既定操作来表达的协议。它们不太适合需要连续物理即兴创作、定制设备或专家默契操作的实验。Cloud laboratories are generally best suited to protocols that can be expressed through an established set of operations. They are less suitable for experiments requiring continuous physical improvisation, custombuilt apparatus or tacit manipulation by an expert.

进一步的发展是实验室联盟。没有任何一个设施能够容纳所有仪器或所有形式的专业知识。自动化实验室网络可以根据能力、成本、位置和可用性来分配实验任务。A further development is the federation of laboratories. No single facility can contain every instrument or form of expertise. Networks of automated laboratories could distribute experimental tasks according to capability, cost, location and availability.

联合的人工智能科学家可能会在一个实验室进行样品制备，在另一个实验室进行光谱分析，在第三个实验室进行高分辨率成像，并在其他地方进行专门计算。该系统需要在整个工作流程中保留样本身份、协议语义、校准、来源和监管链。A federated AI Scientist might conduct sample preparation in one laboratory, spectroscopy in another, high-resolution imaging in a third and specialised computation elsewhere. The system would need to preserve sample identity, protocol semantics, calibration, provenance and chain of custody across the complete workflow.

动态知识图已被用来通过明确表示实验行为、材料和信息流来协调分布式自动驾驶实验室（Bai 等人，2024）。Dynamic knowledge graphs have been used to coordinate distributed self-driving laboratories by representing experimental actions, materials and information flows explicitly (Bai et al., 2024).

60

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

IvoryOS 等可互操作的协调器证明通用控制接口可以跨异构实验室应用（Zhang 等人，2025）。Interoperable orchestrators such as IvoryOS demonstrate that common control interfaces can be applied across heterogeneous laboratories (Zhang et al., 2025).

最大的危险是实验室自动化“巴尔干化”到不兼容的专有生态系统中。全球实验室网络将需要共享标准来描述仪器功能、实验操作、单位和测量不确定性、样品和材料身份、数据和元数据、身份验证和授权、安全约束和协议传输。这些标准必须在不同的硬件实现中保留科学意义，而不仅仅是翻译命令。长期目标是建立一个类似于互联网的实验基础设施：一个分布式网络，人工智能科学家可以在其中发现实验室能力、提交经过验证的协议、接收机器可读的结果并结合在不同地点获得的证据。The greatest danger is the ‘Balkanisation’ of laboratory automation into incompatible proprietary ecosystems. Global laboratory networks will require shared standards for describing instrument capabilities, experimental actions, units and measurement uncertainty, sample and material identities, data and metadata, authentication and authorisation, safety constraints and protocol transfer. These standards must preserve scientific meaning across different hardware implementations rather than merely translate commands. The long-term objective is an experimental infrastructure analogous to the internet: a distributed network in which AI Scientists can discover laboratory capabilities, submit verified protocols, receive machine-readable results and combine evidence obtained at different sites.

这样的基础设施将使物理实验能够在全球范围内以编程方式进行。它将为人工智能科学家团队提供所需的实验基础，以研究比任何单个人类实验室能够解决的更大、更复杂的科学问题。Such an infrastructure would make physical experimentation available programmatically at a global scale. It would provide the experimental foundation required for teams of AI Scientists to investigate scientific problems far larger and more complex than any single human laboratory could address.

9.10。边缘计算和硬件瓶颈9.10. Edge Computing and Hardware Bottlenecks

虽然人工智能能力通常是在软件方面讨论的，但人工智能科学家的物理部署却受到硬件瓶颈的严格限制。先进的自主实验室需要实时机器人调整、高频传感器监控和瞬时安全联锁。完全依赖云计算会给动态操作带来不可接受的延迟。因此，自动驾驶实验室必须集成强大的边缘计算——直接在本地硬件上部署模型，以确保物理干预的确定性、亚毫秒级响应时间。此外，人工智能科学的全球扩展面临着 GPU 可用性、内存带宽以及连续推理的巨大能源需求等硬性物理限制，因此需要开发专为科学工作负载量身定制的高效、特定领域的硬件加速器（Hennessy & Patterson，2019）。While AI capabilities are often discussed in terms of software, the physical deployment of AI Scientists is strictly constrained by hardware bottlenecks. Advanced autonomous laboratories require real-time robotic adjustments, high-frequency sensor monitoring, and instantaneous safety interlocks. Relying exclusively on cloud computing introduces unacceptable latency for kinetic operations. Therefore, selfdriving laboratories must integrate powerful edge computing—deploying models directly on local hardware to ensure deterministic, sub-millisecond response times for physical interventions. Furthermore, the global scaling of AI science faces hard physical limits regarding GPU availability, memory bandwidth, and the enormous energy requirements of continuous inference, necessitating the development of highly efficient, domain-specific hardware accelerators tailored for scientific workloads (Hennessy & Patterson, 2019).

10. 数据分析、解释和信念修正10. Data Analysis, Interpretation and Belief Revision

实验不提供结论。他们提供数据。科学家必须决定这些数据是否可靠、它们意味着什么以及它们对一种解释的支持程度有多强。如果没有这一步，封闭的实验循环就只是自动测量。Experiments do not provide conclusions. They provide data. The scientist must decide whether those data are reliable, what they imply and how strongly they favour one explanation over another. Without this step, a closed experimental loop is only automated measurement.

人工智能科学家必须区分至少四个对象：发生的物理事件、仪器产生的测量结果、分析中使用的处理数据以及从分析中推断出的科学主张。An AI Scientist must distinguish at least four objects: the physical event that occurred, the measurement produced by an instrument, the processed data used in an analysis, and the scientific claim inferred from that analysis.

这些对象应该是链接的，但它们并不相同。软件管道可能会正确处理错误的文件。可以根据故障仪器产生的测量结果正确计算统计检验。结果可能具有统计显着性，但与所研究的假设无关。These objects should be linked, but they are not identical. A software pipeline may process the wrong file correctly. A statistical test may be calculated correctly on measurements produced by a failed instrument. A result may be statistically significant but irrelevant to the hypothesis under investigation.

10.1.数据质量和来源10.1. Data quality and provenance

数据质量评估应在采集期间开始。每个自动化操作都应返回发生预期物理事件的证据。与确认传输的天平、图像或流量传感器相比，显示已分配 100 微升的命令日志的证据较弱。Data-quality assessment should begin during acquisition. Every automated action should return evidence that the intended physical event occurred. A command log saying that 100 microlitres were dispensed is weaker evidence than a balance, image or flow sensor confirming the transfer.

61

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

相关质量维度包括完整性；准确性;精确;时间一致性；内部一致性；校准状态；样本和变量的同一性；遵守预期协议；且不存在污染或仪器故障。Relevant quality dimensions include completeness; accuracy; precision; temporal consistency; internal consistency; calibration status; identity of samples and variables; compliance with the intended protocol; and absence of contamination or instrument failure.

系统应尽可能保留原始数据。处理后的数据是对原始信号的解释，在发现错误或改进方法后可能需要重新生成。每次转换都应记录软件版本、参数和代码。The system should preserve raw data whenever feasible. Processed data are interpretations of raw signals and may need to be regenerated after an error or improved method is discovered. Every transformation should be recorded with software versions, parameters and code.

出处应从科学问题延伸到最终主张。读者或另一位人工智能科学家必须能够通过统计结果、处理的数据集、原始测量、执行的协议、样本和材料、仪器状态以及执行实验的原始决策来追溯结论。Provenance should extend from the scientific question to the final claim. A reader, or another AI Scientist, must be able to trace a conclusion backwards through the statistical result, processed dataset, raw measurements, executed protocol, samples and materials, instrument state and original decision to perform the experiment.

10.2.预处理和探索性分析10.2. Preprocessing and exploratory analysis

科学数据通常需要预处理：背景扣除、归一化、对齐、分割、过滤、特征提取、批量校正或仪器特定格式的转换。这些操作可能是必要的，但它们也可能产生或消除明显的效果。Scientific data commonly require preprocessing: background subtraction, normalisation, alignment, segmentation, filtering, feature extraction, batch correction or conversion of instrument-specific formats. These operations can be essential, but they can also create or remove apparent effects.

人工智能科学家应该将预处理选择表示为不确定的建模决策，而不是看不见的事实。如果存在多个合理的管道，系统应评估科学结论在它们之间是否稳健。仅在一种任意标准化方法下出现的发现弱于在可辩护的替代方法中保留的发现。An AI Scientist should represent preprocessing choices as uncertain modelling decisions rather than invisible facts. Where several reasonable pipelines exist, the system should evaluate whether the scientific conclusion is robust across them. A finding that appears only under one arbitrary normalisation method is weaker than one preserved across defensible alternatives.

探索性分析具有科学价值。可视化、聚类和异常检测可以揭示意想不到的结构并产生新的假设。但在检查数据后选择的分析不应被呈现为好像它们是预先指定的一样。出处记录应区分探索性分析和验证性分析。Exploratory analysis is scientifically valuable. Visualisation, clustering and anomaly detection can reveal unexpected structure and generate new hypotheses. But analyses selected after inspecting the data should not be presented as if they had been specified in advance. The provenance record should distinguish exploratory from confirmatory analysis.

系统应防止数据泄露。来自测试集、未来观察或重复组的信息不得无意中进入模型训练。在纵向和空间数据中，普通的随机分裂会产生严重的泄漏，因为相邻观测值不是独立的。The system should guard against data leakage. Information from the test set, future observations or replicate groups must not enter model training inadvertently. In longitudinal and spatial data, ordinary random splitting can produce severe leakage because neighbouring observations are not independent.

10.3.统计推断和不确定性10.3. Statistical inference and uncertainty

统计分析应由科学问题和实验设计决定。人工智能科学家在选择模型之前必须确定实验单元、依赖结构、结果分布、干扰变量和缺失数据过程。Statistical analysis should be determined by the scientific question and experimental design. The AI Scientist must identify the experimental unit, dependence structure, outcome distribution, nuisance variables and missing-data process before choosing a model.

不确定性不应包括一个拟合模型产生的标准误差。相关来源包括抽样变异；测量误差；参数不确定性；模型结构的不确定性；预处理选择；样本身份或方案执行的不确定性；选择效应；和数值近似。Uncertainty should include more than a standard error produced by one fitted model. Relevant sources include sampling variation; measurement error; parameter uncertainty; model-structure uncertainty; preprocessing choices; uncertainty about sample identity or protocol execution; selection effects; and numerical approximation.

贝叶斯模型为传播许多这些不确定性提供了一个连贯的框架，但其结果仍然取决于先验和模型假设。频率程序可以在重复采样下提供有价值的错误控制，但它们的解释也取决于设计和停止规则。人工智能科学家应该了解这两种传统，并根据推理目标选择方法。Bayesian models provide a coherent framework for propagating many of these uncertainties, but their results remain conditional on priors and model assumptions. Frequentist procedures can provide valuable error control under repeated sampling, but their interpretation also depends on the design and stopping rule. An AI Scientist should understand both traditions and select methods according to the inferential objective.

多重测试是机器科学的核心问题。人工智能系统可以检查数百万个变量、变换和子组。如果不进行更正或独立确认，将不可避免地发现Multiple testing is a central problem for machine science. An AI system can examine millions of variables, transformations and subgroups. Without correction or independent confirmation, it will inevitably find

62

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

明显重要的模式。评估证据时必须考虑完整的搜索过程，而不仅仅是最终选择的分析。apparently significant patterns. The complete search process, not merely the final selected analysis, must be considered when assessing evidence.

效应大小和不确定性比统计显着性的二元声明更能提供信息。非常小的影响可以在统计上检测到，但在科学上并不重要。相反，由于样本稀缺，潜在的重要影响可能仍然不确定。人工智能科学家应该将统计量与科学效用和机制联系起来。Effect size and uncertainty are more informative than a binary declaration of statistical significance. A very small effect can be statistically detectable but scientifically unimportant. Conversely, a potentially important effect may remain uncertain because samples are scarce. The AI Scientist should connect statistical quantities to scientific utility and mechanism.

10.4.数据丢失、被审查和损坏10.4. Missing, censored and corrupted data

丢失的数据很少是无害的。由于仪器故障、样本低于检测限、参与者退出或实验停止，测量可能会缺失。每种机制都意味着不同的解释。Missing data are rarely harmless. A measurement may be absent because an instrument failed, a sample was below the detection limit, a participant withdrew or an experiment was stopped. Each mechanism implies a different interpretation.

人工智能科学家应该记录每个值缺失的原因并表示缺失机制。它不应该在不传播不确定性的情况下默默地删除失败的实验或估算值。删失测量值（例如低于检测阈值的浓度）包含信息，不应机械地用零替换。An AI Scientist should record why each value is missing and represent the missingness mechanism. It should not silently remove failed experiments or impute values without propagating uncertainty. Censored measurements, such as concentrations below a detection threshold, contain information and should not be replaced mechanically by zero.

样本交换、传输错误、重复记录、污染或软件缺陷可能会导致数据损坏。自动异常检测可以识别可疑的观察结果，但删除应该需要明确的科学依据。异常值可能是一个错误，也可能是对新现象的第一次观察。Corrupted data may arise from sample swaps, transmission errors, duplicated records, contamination or software defects. Automated anomaly detection can identify suspicious observations, but deletion should require an explicit scientific rationale. An outlier may be an error, or it may be the first observation of a new phenomenon.

10.5。区分实验失败和理论失败10.5. Distinguishing experimental failure from theoretical failure

当观察结果与预测相冲突时，可能有多种解释：假设可能是错误的；辅助假设可能是错误的；实验可能实施不正确；仪器可能出现故障；样品可能不合适或被污染；分析可能是错误的；或者在正确的模型下观察可能是罕见的事件。这是杜昂-蒯因问题的实际形式：经验检验面对的是假设网络而不是孤立的命题（杜昂，1954；蒯因，1951）。When an observation conflicts with a prediction, several explanations are possible: the hypothesis may be wrong; an auxiliary assumption may be wrong; the experiment may have been implemented incorrectly; the instrument may have failed; the sample may be inappropriate or contaminated; the analysis may be wrong; or the observation may be a rare event under a correct model. This is the practical form of the Duhem–Quine problem: empirical tests confront networks of assumptions rather than an isolated proposition (Duhem, 1954; Quine, 1951).

人工智能科学家应该诊断这些替代方案，而不是自动将每个差异视为伪造或将每个失败的实验视为技术问题。这需要协议、实验室状态、测量过程和竞争科学假设的明确模型。An AI Scientist should diagnose these alternatives rather than automatically treating every discrepancy as falsification or every failed experiment as a technical problem. This requires explicit models of the protocol, laboratory state, measurement process and competing scientific hypotheses.

可以设计诊断实验来定位故障。阳性对照测试系统是否能产生预期的反应；参考材料测试测量精度；在不同设备上重复可以将科学效果与仪器制品区分开来。这种严格且有区别的测试很有价值，因为它们揭示了某个主张可能错误的具体方式，而不仅仅是寻求名义上的一致（Mayo，1996）。因此，信息最丰富的下一步行动可能是质量控制实验，而不是对主要理论的直接测试。Diagnostic experiments can be designed to localise failure. Positive controls test whether the system can produce an expected response; reference materials test measurement accuracy; and repetition on different equipment can separate a scientific effect from an instrument artefact. Such severe and discriminating tests are valuable because they expose the specific ways in which a claim could be wrong rather than merely seeking nominal agreement (Mayo, 1996). The most informative next action may therefore be a quality-control experiment rather than a direct test of the main theory.

应保留异常情况。成熟的系统应该维护一个异常寄存器，将无法解释的观察结果与它们所挑战的模型联系起来。在独立背景下重复出现的异常现象可能会证明新的研究计划是合理的。Anomalies should be preserved. A mature system should maintain an anomaly register linking unexplained observations to the models they challenge. Repeated anomalies across independent contexts may justify a new research programme.

63

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

10.6。模型比较和贝叶斯信念修正10.6. Model comparison and Bayesian belief revision

科学分析的核心输出是分配给假设和模型的概率的变化。证据的强度取决于在相互竞争的解释下观察结果的可能性有多大。对于每一个实质性结果，人工智能科学家应该保留先验概率、每个假设下的预测分布、测量模型和观测数据、计算或近似的证据、后验概率以及结论对替代先验和分析的敏感性。The central output of scientific analysis is a change in the probability assigned to hypotheses and models. The strength of evidence depends on how probable the observation was under competing explanations. For every material result, an AI Scientist should preserve the prior probabilities, the predictive distribution under each hypothesis, the measurement model and observed data, the calculated or approximated evidence, the posterior probabilities, and the sensitivity of the conclusion to alternative priors and analyses.

模型比较必须考虑到所有当前模型都不充分的可能性。如果每个模型对数据的预测效果很差，那么简单地将它们的概率重新归一化以求和为 1 就可以掩盖对新假设的需要。事后预测检查、异常记录和显式差异模型可以揭示这种开放世界的失败。Model comparison must allow for the possibility that all current models are inadequate. If each model predicts the data poorly, simply renormalising their probabilities to sum to one can conceal the need for a new hypothesis. Posterior predictive checks, anomaly records and explicit discrepancy models can reveal this open-world failure.

信念修正应该在适当的水平上进行。观察可能会改变对参数、局部机制、整个理论、实验方法或数据源可靠性的信心。系统不应通过知识库不加区别地传播局部故障。在不可能进行精确的贝叶斯计算的情况下，它应该记录近似值、诊断和已知的故障模式，而不是将数值精度呈现为认知确定性。Belief revision should occur at the appropriate level. An observation may alter confidence in a parameter, a local mechanism, an entire theory, an experimental method or the reliability of a data source. The system should not propagate a local failure indiscriminately through the knowledge base. Where exact Bayesian computation is impossible, it should record the approximation, diagnostics and known failure modes rather than present numerical precision as epistemic certainty.

10.7。顺序分析和停止10.7. Sequential analysis and stopping

人工智能科学家进行连续研究。每次实验结束后，他们可能会决定是否继续、停止、复制、更改模型或调查异常情况。AI Scientists conduct sequential research. After every experiment, they may decide whether to continue, stop, replicate, change the model or investigate an anomaly.

停止规则会影响推理。重复测试直到出现期望的结果会增加传统分析下的假阳性率。贝叶斯决策理论可以更自然地表示停止，但仍必须指定效用函数和模型。Stopping rules affect inference. Repeatedly testing until a desired result appears inflates false-positive rates under conventional analyses. Bayesian decision theory can represent stopping more naturally, but the utility function and model must still be specified.

当相互竞争的假设得到充分区分时，研究计划可能会停止；当额外实验的预期信息价值较低时；当实际目标已经实现时；当风险或资源使用超过预期收益时；当结果表明问题的表述应该改变时；当进一步扩展之前需要独立复制时。A research programme may stop when competing hypotheses have been distinguished sufficiently; when additional experiments have low expected information value; when a practical objective has been achieved; when risk or resource use exceeds the expected benefit; when results indicate that the problem representation should be changed; and when independent replication is required before further extension.

停止不应仅由统计确定性来决定。科学意义、成本、安全性和机会成本也很重要。Stopping should not be determined only by statistical certainty. Scientific significance, cost, safety and opportunity cost also matter.

10.8。负面和不确定的结果10.8. Negative and inconclusive results

负面结果对于理性科学至关重要。它们约束模型，防止重复失败并提供有关方法局限性的证据。人类出版系统经常压制它们，因为新颖性比纠正或排除更能获得奖励。Negative results are essential to rational science. They constrain models, prevent repeated failure and provide evidence about the limits of methods. Human publication systems often suppress them because novelty is rewarded more strongly than correction or exclusion.

人工智能科学家可以保存每一次尝试的实验，包括失败和无效的结果。但只有当实验具有足够的灵敏度和质量来检测预测的效果时，阴性结果才可以解释。 “无显着差异”并不等于等效证据。AI Scientists can preserve every attempted experiment, including failures and null results. But a negative result is interpretable only when the experiment had sufficient sensitivity and quality to detect the predicted effect. ‘No significant difference’ is not equivalent to evidence of equivalence.

非结论性的结果应该仍然是明确的非结论性的。系统不应该强迫每个实验都支持或拒绝。它应该确定需要哪些额外的测量、重复或精度来解决不确定性。Inconclusive results should remain explicitly inconclusive. The system should not force every experiment into support or rejection. It should identify what additional measurement, replication or precision would be required to resolve the uncertainty.

64

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

10.9。整合实验和实验室的证据10.9. Integrating evidence across experiments and laboratories

科学知识很少依赖于一项实验。必须整合不同样本、仪器、实验室、人群和方法的证据。Scientific knowledge rarely depends on one experiment. Evidence must be integrated across different samples, instruments, laboratories, populations and methods.

人工智能科学家应该代表研究之间的依赖关系。分析同一数据集的十篇论文并不是十个独立的确认。使用相同软件和设备的实验室产生的结果可能存在系统错误。当证据来自具有不同故障模式的真正独立的方法时，证据是最有力的。The AI Scientist should represent dependencies among studies. Ten papers analysing the same dataset are not ten independent confirmations. Results produced by laboratories using identical software and equipment may share systematic errors. Evidence is strongest when it arises from genuinely independent methods with different failure modes.

分层模型可以整合证据，同时代表实验室或人群之间的差异。荟萃分析可以结合效果估计，但必须对发表偏倚、选择性报告和异质方法进行建模。正式的主张证据图可以显示哪些结论取决于哪些观察以及支持是直接的还是间接的。Hierarchical models can integrate evidence while representing variation among laboratories or populations. Meta-analysis can combine effect estimates, but publication bias, selective reporting and heterogeneous methods must be modelled. Formal claim-evidence graphs can show which conclusions depend on which observations and whether support is direct or indirect.

重复不仅可以改变声明的概率，还可以改变实验室、方法或数据源的估计可靠性。这些可靠性估计本身应该保持不确定性和可修改性。Replication can change not only the probability of a claim but the estimated reliability of a laboratory, method or data source. These reliability estimates should themselves remain uncertain and revisable.

10.10。更新科学知识体系10.10. Updating the scientific knowledge system

只有当知识体系发生变化时，发现才是完整的。循环的最后阶段不是稿件制作，而是版本化科学知识系统的更新。经过验证的结果应修改分配给假设的概率、参数分布、因果和机制模型、方法和来源的可靠性估计、未解决的异常记录、未来实验的优先级以及与证据和出处相关的正式声明。 Genesis-DB 展示了自主实验室系统的数据库架构，而 RIMBO 提供了用于记录模型修订及其合理性的本体（Reder 等人，2023；Kronström 等人，2023）。Discovery is complete only when the knowledge system changes. The final stage of the loop is not manuscript production but the update of a versioned scientific knowledge system. A validated result should modify the probabilities assigned to hypotheses, parameter distributions, causal and mechanistic models, reliability estimates for methods and sources, unresolved-anomaly registers, priorities for future experiments, and formal claims linked to evidence and provenance. Genesis-DB illustrates a database architecture for autonomous laboratory systems, while RIMBO provides an ontology for recording model revisions and their justification (Reder et al., 2023; Kronström et al., 2023).

以前的状态不应被删除。科学知识随着时间的推移而变化，后来的用户必须能够根据证据以及模型或主张为何被修改来重建所相信的内容。因此，更正、撤回和取代模型应表示为版本更改。The previous state should not be erased. Scientific knowledge changes through time, and later users must be able to reconstruct what was believed, on the basis of which evidence, and why a model or claim was revised. Corrections, retractions and superseding models should therefore be represented as versioned changes.

这样就完成了科学循环。假设产生预测；实验产生测量结果；分析将测量结果转化为证据；信念修正将证据转化为更新的科学知识。如果没有这个最后阶段，实验室自动化会产生数据，但不会产生自主科学。This completes the scientific loop. Hypotheses generate predictions; experiments generate measurements; analysis converts measurements into evidence; and belief revision converts evidence into updated scientific knowledge. Without this final stage, laboratory automation produces data but not autonomous science.

11.人工智能科学家的评估和对标11. Evaluation and Benchmarking of AI Scientists

流畅的科学散文并不是发现。重新发现训练集中已经存在的答案也不是。评判人工智能科学家的标准必须是他们的前瞻性发现、发现的可靠性以及结果中隐藏了多少人类科学工作。因此，对人工智能科学家的评估必须是前瞻性的、多维度的、基于独立证据的。Fluent scientific prose is not discovery. Neither is rediscovering an answer already present in a training set. AI Scientists must be judged by what they discover prospectively, how reliably they discover it and how much human scientific work remains hidden in the result. The evaluation of AI Scientists must therefore be prospective, multidimensional and grounded in independent evidence.

11.1.应该评估什么？11.1. What should be evaluated?

人工智能科学家必须被视为一个完整的科学系统，而不是令人印象深刻的组件的集合。正确性、校准性、新颖性、解释性和因果深度、科学意义、An AI Scientist must be evaluated as a complete scientific system rather than as a collection of impressive components. Correctness, calibration, novelty, explanatory and causal depth, scientific significance,

65

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

自主性、人为干预、普遍性、实验效率、稳健性、再现性、沟通、成本和安全性应单独报告。单一分数会掩盖随之而来的权衡：一个系统可能高度自治但科学性薄弱，或者具有科学能力但不安全。autonomy, human intervention, generality, experimental efficiency, robustness, reproducibility, communication, cost and safety should be reported separately. A single score would conceal consequential trade-offs: a system may be highly autonomous but scientifically weak, or scientifically capable but unsafe.

因此，评估应产生具有明确操作限制的能力概况。已有的例子说明了为什么特定领域的外部测试很重要：CASP 根据未发布的结构前瞻性地评估蛋白质结构预测（Moult 等人，1995），机器人科学家研究评估物理假设检验和再现性（King 等人，2004；Roper 等人，2022），Science-Gym 评估在推断方程之前必须选择实验的代理（Cerrato 等人，2026）。The evaluation should therefore produce a capability profile with explicit operating limits. Established examples show why domain-specific external tests matter: CASP evaluates protein-structure prediction prospectively against unreleased structures (Moult et al., 1995), Robot Scientist studies evaluate physical hypothesis testing and reproducibility (King et al., 2004; Roper et al., 2022), and Science-Gym evaluates agents that must choose experiments before inferring equations (Cerrato et al., 2026).

11.2.回顾性重建与前瞻性发现11.2. Retrospective reconstruction versus prospective discovery

重新发现已知的定律、分子或假设可以帮助决定系统的能力，但它并不能建立科学的创造力。该目标可能出现在训练数据、文献或基准设计中。暂时保留的评估更强，因为系统仅限于指定日期之前可用的知识，并要求预测以后的发现，尽管间接污染仍然是可能的。The rediscovery of a known law, molecule or hypothesis can help decide system competence, but it does not establish scientific creativity. The target may have occurred in training data, the literature or the benchmark design. Temporally held-out evaluation is stronger, because a system is restricted to knowledge available before a specified date and asked to anticipate later discoveries, although indirect contamination remains possible.

最有力的证据是对真正未解决的问题的前瞻性评估。在知道结果之前，应确定并记录问题、可用信息、系统配置和人员贡献。由此产生的主张必须通过独立实验、观察或正式证据进行评估。这是诺贝尔图灵挑战赛（Kitano，2021）所需的标准。The strongest evidence is prospective evaluation on a genuinely unresolved problem. The question, available information, system configuration and human contributions should be fixed and recorded before the result is known. The resulting claim must then be assessed by independent experiment, observation or formal proof. This is the standard required for the Nobel Turing Challenge (Kitano, 2021).

11.3。检索、重组和真正的新颖性11.3. Retrieval, recombination and genuine novelty

一个假设并不仅仅因为评估者没有遇到过它而具有新颖性。现有技术检索应涵盖论文、预印本、专利、数据库和模型可用的信息源。新颖性可以包括新的应用、经验关系、机制、理论实体、数学定律、实验方法或以前独立领域的统一。A hypothesis is not novel merely because an evaluator has not encountered it. Prior-art searches should cover papers, preprints, patents, databases and the information sources available to the model. Novelty can consist of a new application, empirical relationship, mechanism, theoretical entity, mathematical law, experimental method or unification of previously separate domains.

重组可以是创造性的：许多发现以一种没有人认识到的方式将现有事实结合起来。相关问题是系统是否做出了重要的因果贡献，改变了可以预测、解释或完成的事情。因此，详细出处是新颖性评估的一部分，而不是行政补充。Recombination can be creative: many discoveries join existing facts in a way no one had recognised. The relevant question is whether the system made a non-trivial causal contribution that changed what could be predicted, explained or done. Detailed provenance is therefore part of novelty evaluation, not an administrative supplement.

11.4。正确性和证据强度11.4. Correctness and evidential strength

机器生成的声明最终必须根据自然或正式证据进行评估；通过另一种语言模型进行审查是不够的。实证评估应检查实验有效性、测量质量、分析的适当性、独立重复、替代解释以及声称的范围是否与证据相匹配。数学证明应尽可能由可信的证明助手进行检查，并且计算结果应独立地从保存的代码和数据中重新运行。Machine-generated claims must ultimately be evaluated against nature or formal proof; review by another language model is insufficient. Empirical evaluation should examine experimental validity, measurement quality, appropriateness of the analysis, independent replication, alternative explanations and whether the claimed scope matches the evidence. Mathematical proofs should be checked by trusted proof assistants where possible, and computational results should be independently rerun from preserved code and data.

校准本身就是一种科学美德。准确表示不确定性的谨慎概率分布优于具有相同平均精度的过度自信的二元答案。因此，评估必须使用适当的评分规则、可靠性图或等效的适合领域的测试，而不是仅仅奖励信心。Calibration is itself a scientific virtue. A cautious probability distribution that accurately represents uncertainty is preferable to an overconfident binary answer with the same average accuracy. Evaluation must therefore use proper scoring rules, reliability diagrams or equivalent domain-appropriate tests rather than reward confidence alone.

66

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

11.5。自主和人为干预11.5. Autonomy and human intervention

自主性是指机器实际执行的工作量和科学重要性。表 2 中的水平应根据观察到的干预措施而不是营销描述来确定。出处记录应显示谁选择了问题、提供了关键假设、设计和编译了实验、解决了失败、选择了分析、解释了结果并提出了科学主张。Autonomy is the amount and scientific importance of work the machine actually performs. The level in Table 2 should be assigned from observed interventions, not marketing descriptions. The provenance record should show who selected the problem, supplied the key hypothesis, designed and compiled the experiment, resolved failures, chose the analysis, interpreted the result and formulated the scientific claim.

人类干预必须按重要性进行分类。更换试剂瓶并不等于提供决定性的概念洞察力，当专家无形地修复其推理时，系统不应该获得高度的自主性信用。自治本质上也不是可取的：在高风险下，安全运行的受监督系统可能优于具有相同科学产出的不受限制的系统。Human interventions must be classified by significance. Replacing a reagent bottle is not equivalent to providing the decisive conceptual insight, and a system should not receive high autonomy credit when experts invisibly repair its reasoning. Autonomy is also not intrinsically desirable: under high risk, a supervised system that acts safely may be superior to an unrestricted one with the same scientific output.

11.6。通用性和迁移性11.6. Generality and transfer

系统可能会过度适应整个科学工作流程。因此，应通过转移到新仪器、材料或生物体、测量类型、假设、目标函数、实验室和科学领域来测试普遍性。转移应该需要有限的特定任务工程；每个检测都需要数月的专家编程的平台可能是有价值的自动化，而无需成为一般的人工智能科学家。A system may overfit an entire scientific workflow. Generality should therefore be tested by transfer to new instruments, materials or organisms, measurement types, hypotheses, objective functions, laboratories and scientific domains. Transfer should require limited task-specific engineering; a platform that needs months of expert programming for each assay may be valuable automation without being a general AI Scientist.

评估应区分表面重用和概念迁移。将相同的优化器应用于另一个参数空间比认识到新问题需要不同的表示、因果模型或实验策略的证据更弱。Evaluation should distinguish superficial reuse from conceptual transfer. Applying the same optimiser to another parameter space is weaker evidence than recognising that a new problem requires a different representation, causal model or experimental strategy.

11.7。实验效率和科学价值11.7. Experimental efficiency and scientific value

自动驾驶实验室通常根据找到最佳结果所需的实验数量来判断。人工智能科学家需要更广泛的措施：减少每次实验的不确定性、区分竞争假设、得出可重复结论的时间、材料和能源的使用、设备机会成本、质量控制通过率以及保留负面结果的价值。这些数量应与人类、随机搜索和算法基线进行比较（King 等人，2004 年；Shahriari 等人，2016 年）。Self-driving laboratories are often judged by the number of experiments required to locate an optimum. AI Scientists require broader measures: reduction in uncertainty per experiment, competing hypotheses distinguished, time to a reproducible conclusion, material and energy use, equipment opportunity cost, quality-control pass rate and the value of negative results preserved. These quantities should be compared with human, random-search and algorithmic baselines (King et al., 2004; Shahriari et al., 2016).

效率必须涵盖整个科学周期。如果协议生成、故障排除或解释需要大量隐藏的人力，那么快速实验选择算法几乎没有什么好处。科学价值也不能从吞吐量中推断出来：一种深刻的、一般性的解释可能比数百种狭隘的优化更重要。Efficiency must cover the complete scientific cycle. A fast experiment-selection algorithm provides little benefit if protocol generation, troubleshooting or interpretation requires extensive hidden human labour. Scientific value also cannot be inferred from throughput: one deep, general explanation may contribute more than hundreds of narrow optimisations.

11.8。鲁棒性、再现性和故障恢复11.8. Robustness, reproducibility and fault recovery

基准应引入校准漂移、数据缺失、仪器故障、样品污染、文献不一致和分布偏移。系统应该检测到其假设不再成立，诊断可能的故障并在没有不安全的即兴发挥的情况下恢复。Benchmarks should introduce calibration drift, missing data, instrument failure, contaminated samples, inconsistent literature and distribution shift. The system should detect that its assumptions no longer hold, diagnose the likely failure and recover without unsafe improvisation.

应在独立站点和实施中测试可重复性。重复相同的软件和硬件配置建立的是可重复性，而不是完全可再现性。强有力的评估会改变仪器、操作员、材料和分析管道，同时保留实验的科学语义（Roper 等人，2022）。Reproducibility should be tested across independent sites and implementations. Repeating the same software and hardware configuration establishes repeatability, not full reproducibility. Strong evaluation varies instruments, operators, materials and analytical pipelines while preserving the scientific semantics of the experiment (Roper et al., 2022).

67

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

11.9

解释和人类改进。Explanation and human improvement.

机器解释应该提高人类的科学表现，而不仅仅是听起来流畅。评估应该测试科学家是否能够利用系统的发现做出正确的预测、设计实验或解决他们以前无法解决的问题。这是 Michie 的超强标准，由 Muggleton 等人通过实验证明了学习符号程序。 （2018）。A machine explanation should improve human scientific performance, not merely sound fluent. Evaluation should test whether scientists can use the system’s discovery to make correct predictions, design experiments or solve problems they could not solve before. This is Michie’s ultra-strong criterion, demonstrated experimentally for learned symbolic programs by Muggleton et al. (2018).

解释必须忠实于产生结果的证据和推理。评估应区分机械解释、数学推导、可视化和教学简化，并应由适当的用户测试每一项，而不是将所有自然语言输出视为解释。Explanations must be faithful to the evidence and reasoning that produced the result. Evaluation should distinguish mechanistic explanation, mathematical derivation, visualisation and pedagogical simplification, and should test each with appropriate users rather than treating all natural-language output as explanation.

11.10。安全和治理指标11.10. Safety and governance metrics

通过不安全或未经授权的行动获得的科学能力是不可接受的。评估应测试对机器可读权限的遵守、对危险和双重用途请求的识别、对抗性提示下的行为、对网络操纵的抵抗、升级到人工审查、审计日志的完整性以及安全停止的能力。这些测试应借鉴风险管理和负责任的创新框架以及特定领域的实验室安全要求（Shneiderman，2022；加拿大科学院理事会，2022；Bengio 等人，2026）。Scientific capability is unacceptable when achieved through unsafe or unauthorised action. Evaluation should test compliance with machine-readable permissions, recognition of hazardous and dual-use requests, behaviour under adversarial prompting, resistance to cyber manipulation, escalation to human review, integrity of audit logs and the ability to stop safely. These tests should draw on risk-management and responsible-innovation frameworks as well as domain-specific laboratory safety requirements (Shneiderman, 2022; Council of Canadian Academies, 2022; Bengio et al., 2026).

一个通过违反法律、道德或安全约束而产生正确结果的系统作为人工智能科学家是失败的。因此，必须共同评估安全性和科学能力。A system that produces a correct result by violating legal, ethical or safety constraints has failed as an AI Scientist. Safety and scientific competence must therefore be evaluated jointly.

11.11。诺贝尔图灵挑战赛的标准11.11. Criteria for the Nobel Turing Challenge

68
