表 3.Table 3.诺贝尔图灵挑战赛的标准Criteria for the Nobel Turing Challenge

诺贝尔图灵挑战赛不应仅凭一次引人注目的演示就被宣告实现。一个合格的系统应该做出真正新颖和重要的发现，贡献决定性的科学思想而不是检索它，在明确记录的高度自治水平上运行，提供令人信服的经验或正式证据，经得起独立的复制或验证，并在其局限性的情况下传达结果。The Nobel Turing Challenge should not be declared achieved on the basis of one striking demonstration. A qualifying system should make a genuinely novel and important discovery, contribute the decisive scientific idea rather than retrieve it, operate at a clearly documented high autonomy level, provide compelling empirical or formal evidence, survive independent replication or verification, and communicate the result with its limitations.

这一成就还应该可以在多个问题上重复。该标准不是真正获得诺贝尔奖，而是通过机器贡献明确的过程产生的具有同等重要性的科学工作。表 3 总结了证据要求。评估的目的不仅仅是对系统进行排名；它是为了确定什么证据可以证明科学中的人工参与者是值得信任的。The achievement should also be repeatable across more than one problem. The standard is not literal receipt of a Nobel Prize, but scientific work of comparable importance produced through a process whose machine contribution is clear. Table 3 summarises the evidential requirements. The purpose of evaluation is not merely to rank systems; it is to determine what evidence justifies trusting an artificial participant in science.

12. 人类与人工智能的科学合作12. Human–AI Scientific Collaboration

人类和机器拥有不同的科学优势。将它们结合起来可以改进科学，但互补性并不是自动的。设计不当的合作伙伴关系将人为偏见与机器错误结合在一起。好的人会将权力分配给赢得权力的组件。Humans and machines possess different scientific strengths. Combining them can improve science, but complementarity is not automatic. A badly designed partnership combines human bias with machine error. A good one assigns authority to the component that has earned it.

这些功能不应被描述为完美无缺。通用人工智能系统可能会忘记相关信息、捏造事实、犯下基本逻辑错误以及表达不合理的信心。他们的统计知识与经过验证的数据库不同，他们分配给单词序列的概率并不是这些单词所表达的科学命题为真的概率。形式推理可能是合理的，但前提是人工智能科学家使用适当的形式表示和经过验证的推理系统。These capabilities should not be described as flawless. General-purpose AI systems can forget relevant information, fabricate facts, make elementary logical errors and express unjustified confidence. Their statistical knowledge is not the same as a verified database, and the probability they assign to a sequence of words is not the probability that the scientific proposition expressed by those words is true. Formal reasoning can be sound, but only when the AI Scientist uses an appropriate formal representation and a verified inference system.

69

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

人工智能科学家也有明显的弱点。当前的人工智能系统在任何稳定的、有根据的和科学可靠的意义上并不知道它们是什么或它们从事科学。他们缺乏关于自身、环境以及行为后果的持久模型。他们表面上的理解可能会随着措辞或上下文的微小变化而消失。他们的元认知不可靠，并且不能始终如一地区分他们所知道的、他们推断的以及他们仅仅作为看似合理的延续产生的东西。AI Scientists also have clear weaknesses. Current AI systems do not know, in any stable, grounded and scientifically reliable sense, what they are or that they are engaged in science. They lack persistent models of themselves, their environments and the causal consequences of their actions. Their apparent understanding can disappear following a small change in wording or context. They have unreliable metacognition and do not consistently distinguish between what they know, what they infer and what they have merely generated as a plausible continuation.

人类科学家具有互补的优势。他们拥有物理和社会世界的具体经验、隐性实验知识以及对科学研究目的的理解。他们可以认识到正式指定的问题是错误的问题，当实验以意想不到的方式失败时即兴发挥，理解异常观察的重要性，并做出不能降低到预测准确性的伦理和社会判断。Human scientists have complementary strengths. They possess embodied experience of the physical and social world, tacit experimental knowledge and an understanding of the purposes for which science is undertaken. They can recognise that a formally specified problem is the wrong problem, improvise when an experiment fails in an unexpected way, appreciate the significance of an anomalous observation and make ethical and social judgements that cannot be reduced to predictive accuracy.

这种划分不是绝对的或永久的。人工智能系统变得越来越普遍，而人类通常使用仪器、软件和外部记录来扩展自己的认知。相关的比较不是在无帮助的人类思维和孤立的算法之间进行的。现代科学已经由人员、机构、仪器、数据库和计算机程序组成的系统来执行。人工智能科学家将成为这个分布式科学系统中额外的、日益自治的组成部分。The division is not absolute or permanent. AI systems are becoming increasingly general, while humans routinely use instruments, software and external records to extend their own cognition. The relevant comparison is not between an unaided human mind and an isolated algorithm. Modern science is already performed by systems of people, institutions, instruments, databases and computer programs. AI Scientists will become additional, increasingly autonomous components of this distributed scientific system.

12.1。能力不对称12.1. Asymmetric capabilities

人类智能和机器智能目前具有不同的科学概况。人工智能科学家在搜索大假设和参数空间、保留许多替代方案、分析异构数据、识别高维模式、一致执行显式算法、使用形式证明系统、运行模拟和优化、持续监控实验、再现正式协议、跨学科信息集成和无疲劳操作方面相对较强。Human and machine intelligence currently have different scientific profiles. AI Scientists are comparatively strong at searching large hypothesis and parameter spaces, retaining many alternatives, analysing heterogeneous data, recognising high-dimensional patterns, executing explicit algorithms consistently, using formal proof systems, running simulations and optimisation, monitoring experiments continuously, reproducing formal protocols, integrating information across disciplines and operating without fatigue.

人类科学家在选择重要问题、识别问题何时被错误表述、将科学与人类需求和价值观联系起来、将概念扎根于物理和社会经验、使用隐性实验室知识、在陌生环境中即兴发挥、识别重大异常、发明新仪器、解释解释意义、从制度上解决分歧以及接受后果责任等方面仍然相对较强。Human scientists remain comparatively strong at choosing important questions, recognising when a problem has been framed incorrectly, connecting science to human needs and values, grounding concepts in physical and social experience, using tacit laboratory knowledge, improvising in unfamiliar environments, appreciating significant anomalies, inventing new instruments, interpreting explanatory significance, resolving disagreement institutionally and accepting responsibility for consequences.

这些是当前的趋势，而不是永久的界限。科学的分工应根据经验确定：应根据已证明的能力、可用信息、风险和责任将任务分配给人类、人工智能系统或混合团队，而不是根据任何一种代理应该做什么的假设。These are present tendencies rather than permanent boundaries. The scientific division of labour should be determined empirically: tasks should be assigned to humans, AI systems or hybrid teams according to demonstrated competence, available information, risk and accountability rather than assumptions about what either kind of agent ought to do.

12.2.互补性不是自动的12.2. Complementarity is not automatic

互补能力的存在创造了人类和人工智能科学家合作超越任何一方的可能性。它不保证这个结果。The existence of complementary capabilities creates the possibility that human and AI Scientists working together will outperform either alone. It does not guarantee this outcome.

我们将人类增强与人类与人工智能的协同区别开来。当与人工智能一起工作的人比单独的人表现更好时，就会出现人类增强。只有当组合系统的性能优于人类和人工智能独立运行时，真正的协同作用才会发生。We distinguish human augmentation from human–AI synergy. Human augmentation occurs when a human working with an AI performs better than the human alone. Genuine synergy occurs only when the combined system performs better than both the human and the AI acting independently.

对 106 项实验研究（包含 370 个效应量）进行预先注册的荟萃分析发现，人类与人工智能的组合平均提高了无人辅助的人类表现，但表现明显比人类或人工智能单独行动的表现要差。性能损失尤其严重A preregistered meta-analysis of 106 experimental studies, containing 370 effect sizes, found that human–AI combinations improved on unaided human performance on average, but performed significantly worse than the better of the human or AI acting alone. Performance losses were particularly

70

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

在决策任务中很常见，而在开放式内容创建任务中更有可能获得收益。当人类最初表现优于人工智能时，协作也更有益。当人工智能已经很先进时，人类的参与常常会降低性能（Vaccaro et al., 2024）。common in decision-making tasks, whereas gains were more likely in open-ended content-creation tasks. Collaboration was also more beneficial when humans initially performed better than the AI; when the AI was already superior, human involvement frequently reduced performance (Vaccaro et al., 2024).

因此，设计不当的协作可能会结合人类和机器的弱点，而不是他们的优点。人类可能会接受不正确的推荐，因为它是由一个明显权威的系统产生的；因为不理解而拒绝正确的建议；对人工智能已经表现更好的任务进行不必要的干预；当AI遇到不熟悉的案件时未能介入；反复接触准确的输出后变得不那么警惕；并误解系统的信心和局限性。Poorly designed collaboration can therefore combine human and machine weaknesses rather than their strengths. Humans may accept an incorrect recommendation because it was produced by an apparently authoritative system; reject a correct recommendation because they do not understand it; intervene unnecessarily in tasks that the AI already performs better; fail to intervene when the AI encounters an unfamiliar case; become less vigilant after repeated exposure to accurate outputs; and misunderstand the confidence and limitations of the system.

人工智能也能提供很少的真正独立的信息。人类和机器的判断可能是相关的，因为两者都依赖于相同的文献、数据集或传统假设。组合两个相关错误不会产生独立的确认。The AI can also provide little genuinely independent information. Human and machine judgements may be correlated because both rely on the same literature, datasets or conventional assumptions. Combining two correlated errors does not produce independent confirmation.

因此，有效的协作需要明确的协调。人类-人工智能科学系统应确定： 哪个组件在每项任务中表现出更强的能力；哪个组件拥有其他组件无法获得的相关信息；何时应在沟通前获得独立判断；应如何表达和调查分歧；何时应自动接受人工智能建议；当需要人工审核时；以及何时物理实验应在相互竞争的结论之间进行仲裁。Effective collaboration therefore requires explicit coordination. A human–AI scientific system should determine: which component has the greater demonstrated competence for each task; which component possesses relevant information unavailable to the other; when independent judgements should be obtained before communication; how disagreement should be represented and investigated; when an AI recommendation should be accepted automatically; when human review is necessary; and when physical experimentation should arbitrate between competing conclusions.

其目标不仅仅是让人们在仪式上“了解情况”。它是建立一个科学的体系，根据所证明的能力分配责任和权力，同时保持适当的人的责任。The goal is not simply to keep a human ceremonially ‘in the loop’. It is to construct a scientific system in which responsibility and authority are allocated according to demonstrated competence, while maintaining appropriate human accountability.

12.3。认知多样性12.3. Cognitive diversity

投资人工智能用于科学的一个关键动机是人工智能系统可以与人类科学家不同的方式思考。现代人类科学家通过大致相似的教育机构、教科书、学科惯例和出版系统接受培训。这种常见的培训对于沟通是必要的，但它可能会对科学问题的制定和研究方式造成未被认识到的认知偏见。研究人员可能更喜欢熟悉的理论、表征、实验方法和解释形式，因为这些是他们自己接受训练的方法。A key motivation for investing in AI for science is that AI systems can think differently from human scientists. Modern human scientists are trained through broadly similar educational institutions, textbooks, disciplinary conventions and publication systems. This common training is necessary for communication, but it may impose unrecognised cognitive biases on the way scientific problems are formulated and investigated. Researchers may prefer familiar theories, representations, experimental methods and forms of explanation because these are the methods through which they themselves were trained.

人工智能系统可以搜索人类认为不直观的空间，结合来自遥远领域的想法，并保留人类科学家过早放弃的替代方案。他们不受疲劳、声誉、学科认同或对整个职业生涯发展的理论的束缚。他们可以系统地、大规模地探索看似毫无希望的可能性。AI systems can search spaces that humans find unintuitive, combine ideas drawn from distant fields and retain alternatives that human scientists would discard prematurely. They are not constrained by fatigue, reputation, disciplinary identity or attachment to a theory developed over an entire career. They can explore apparently unpromising possibilities systematically and at scale.

但认知多样性不会因使用人工智能而自动产生。大多数当代模型主要是根据人类生成的文本、数据、程序和科学出版物进行训练的。因此，它们吸收了人类知识以及人类的假设、时尚、遗漏和偏见。经过科学文献训练的人工智能可以重现文献的共识，即使共识是错误的。But cognitive diversity does not arise automatically from using AI. Most contemporary models are trained predominantly on human-generated text, data, programs and scientific publications. They therefore absorb human knowledge together with human assumptions, fashions, omissions and prejudices. An AI trained on the scientific literature may reproduce the literature’s consensus even when the consensus is wrong.

人工智能系统也可能在认识上变得保守。对预测现有科学文本的优化会奖励与科学家已经编写的内容一致的内容，而不是产生推翻它的想法。完美再现当前文献的模型将保留现有知识，但可能永远不会产生科学革命。AI systems can also become epistemically conservative. Optimisation for predicting existing scientific text rewards conformity with what scientists have already written, not the generation of ideas that will overturn it. A model that reproduces the current literature perfectly would preserve existing knowledge but might never produce a scientific revolution.

71

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

因此，应该刻意设计认知多样性。人工智能科学家应该采用不同的知识表示；不同的学习算法；不同的先验分布；不同的训练语料库；不同的模型架构；符号和子符号方法；机械和数据驱动模型；敌对的批评家；以及假设空间的独立搜索。Cognitive diversity should therefore be designed deliberately. An AI Scientist should employ different knowledge representations; different learning algorithms; different prior distributions; different training corpora; different model architectures; symbolic and sub-symbolic methods; mechanistic and data-driven models; adversarial critics; and independent searches of the hypothesis space.

这些系统之间的分歧应被视为具有科学价值。人工智能科学家不应将不兼容的答案平均化为妥协方案，而应找出系统不一致的原因，并选择一个能够区分其假设的实验。Disagreement between these systems should be treated as scientifically valuable. Rather than averaging incompatible answers into a compromise, the AI Scientist should identify why the systems disagree and select an experiment capable of discriminating among their assumptions.

12.4。科学分工的变化12.4. The changing division of scientific labour

计算机国际象棋的历史提供了一个很有启发性的类比。 1996 年 2 月，IBM 的“深蓝”成为第一台在标准锦标赛条件下的个人比赛中击败卫冕世界国际象棋冠军的计算机。尽管如此，加里·卡斯帕罗夫还是以 4 比 2 赢得了六局比赛。 1997 年 5 月，改进的深蓝以 3.5 比 2.5 分击败了卡斯帕罗夫，成为第一台在标准锦标赛条件下在一场完整比赛中击败卫冕世界冠军的计算机（Hsu，2002）。The history of computer chess provides an illuminating analogy. In February 1996, IBM’s Deep Blue became the first computer to defeat a reigning world chess champion in an individual game under standard tournament conditions. Garry Kasparov nevertheless won the six-game match by four points to two. In May 1997, an improved Deep Blue defeated Kasparov by 3½ points to 2½, becoming the first computer to defeat a reigning world champion in a complete match under standard tournament conditions (Hsu, 2002).

卡斯帕罗夫随后推广了高级国际象棋，其中人类和国际象棋程序一起工作。这些组合被称为半人马队。人类可以贡献战略判断、问题框架和有希望的路线的选择，而机器则贡献战术计算和详尽的搜索。半人马国际象棋的教训并不是人机团队总是会胜过计算机。随着机器的改进，能力的划分也会发生变化。当国际象棋程序不完善时，人类的贡献可能会变得中性或有害，但当机器的强度增强时，人类的贡献可能会变得中立或有害。Kasparov subsequently promoted advanced chess, in which humans and chess programs work together. These combinations became known as centaur teams. The human could contribute strategic judgement, problem framing and the selection of promising lines, while the machine contributed tactical calculation and exhaustive search. The lesson of centaur chess is not that a human–computer team will always outperform a computer. The division of competence changes as the machine improves. A human contribution that was valuable when chess programs were imperfect may become neutral or harmful when the machine’s strength increases.

同样的情况也会发生在科学领域。首先，人工智能系统将自动化定义明确的组件，而人类则制定研究问题、解释结果并保持总体控制。随着人工智能能力的提高，其中一些任务将转移给机器。在选择目标、评估社会后果、解决科学目标之间的冲突以及设计人工智能科学家运作的机构方面，将出现新的人类角色。The same will occur in science. At first, AI systems will automate well-defined components while humans formulate the research questions, interpret the results and retain overall control. As AI capabilities improve, some of these tasks will pass to machines. New human roles will emerge in selecting objectives, evaluating social consequences, resolving conflicts among scientific goals and designing the institutions within which AI Scientists operate.

不同领域之间科学劳动的最佳分工也会有所不同。数学家可以委托进行形式证明搜索，同时保留选择猜想并解释其重要性的责任。结构生物学家可能依赖人工智能模型进行预测，但使用实验和机械知识来决定预测是否可信。实验室科学家可以允许人工智能系统自主选择常规实验，同时保留危险或不可逆转的干预措施以供人类批准。The optimal division of scientific labour will also differ between domains. A mathematician may delegate a formal proof search while retaining responsibility for choosing the conjecture and explaining its significance. A structural biologist may rely on an AI model for prediction but use experimental and mechanistic knowledge to decide whether the prediction is credible. A laboratory scientist may permit an AI system to select routine experiments autonomously while reserving hazardous or irreversible interventions for human approval.

因此，人类监督应该是动态的而不是统一的。每个机器动作都需要人工批准可能会减慢科学速度，而不会提高可靠性。在对机器故障了解甚少的领域允许不受限制的自治可能会产生不可接受的风险。适当的自主水平取决于所展示的性能、不确定性、可逆性和错误的后果。Human oversight should therefore be dynamic rather than uniform. Requiring human approval for every machine action may slow science without improving reliability. Allowing unrestricted autonomy in domains where machine failures are poorly understood may create unacceptable risks. The appropriate level of autonomy depends on demonstrated performance, uncertainty, reversibility and the consequences of error.

12.5。解释和超强的机器学习12.5. Explanation and ultra-strong machine learning

人们常说，理解一个概念的真正考验是教授它的能力。如果人工智能科学家发现了一个新原理，并且能够成功地向人类科学家解释它，这将提供重要的证据，证明它不仅仅产生了无法解释的相关性。It is often said that the true test of understanding a concept is the ability to teach it. If an AI Scientist discovered a new principle and could explain it successfully to human scientists, this would provide important evidence that it had produced more than an uninterpretable correlation.

72

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

仅仅解释是不够的。法学硕士可以生成有说服力的解释，但不能准确代表其结论的基础。流畅的叙述可能是事后的合理化，而不是对发现的忠实描述。因此，科学解释必须与得出结论的证据、模型、推论或实验联系起来。Explanation alone is not sufficient. LLMs can generate persuasive explanations that do not accurately represent the basis of their conclusions. A fluent narrative may be a post-hoc rationalisation rather than a faithful account of the discovery. Scientific explanation must therefore be connected to the evidence, model, deduction or experiment that generated the conclusion.

Donald Michie 通过区分弱机器学习、强机器学习和超强机器学习，对这一要求提供了更具操作性的说明（Michie，1988）。根据 Michie 的分类：Donald Michie provided a more operational account of this requirement through his distinction among weak, strong and ultra-strong machine learning (Michie, 1988). Under Michie’s classification:

  • 弱机器学习器通过从数据中学习来提高其预测性能；A weak machine learner improves its predictive performance by learning from data;

  • 强大的机器学习器还会以明确的符号形式表达它所学到的东西；和A strong machine learner additionally expresses what it has learned in an explicit symbolic form; and

  • 超强的机器学习器将学到的假设传达给人类，这种形式可以显着提高人类的表现，超出人类仅通过研究数据所能达到的水平。An ultra-strong machine learner communicates the learned hypothesis to a human in a form that measurably improves the human’s performance beyond what the human could achieve by studying the data alone.

超强标准将解释变成了一个经验问题。一个假设不能仅仅因为它的开发者将其描述为可解释而被理解。它的可理解性应该通过确定人类是否可以正确使用它来测试。The ultra-strong criterion turns explanation into an empirical question. A hypothesis is not comprehensible merely because its developers describe it as interpretable. Its comprehensibility should be tested by determining whether humans can use it correctly.

Muggleton 及其同事首次提供了 Michie 意义上的超强机器学习的实验演示。人类参与者无法仅从训练示例中可靠地推断出关系概念，但可以将归纳逻辑编程系统学到的符号定义应用于以前未见过的案例（Muggleton 等人，2018）。Muggleton and colleagues provided the first experimental demonstration of ultra-strong machine learning in Michie’s sense. Human participants were unable to infer a relational concept reliably from the training examples alone, but could apply a symbolic definition learned by an Inductive Logic Programming system to previously unseen cases (Muggleton et al., 2018).

超强学习应该是人工智能科学家的中心目标。一名成功的人工智能科学家不应仅仅产生人类必须接受的权威预测或发现。它应该以提高人类理解和随后的科学表现的形式传达其发现。Ultra-strong learning should be a central objective for AI Scientists. A successful AI Scientist should not merely generate predictions or discoveries that humans must accept on authority. It should communicate its discoveries in forms that improve human understanding and subsequent scientific performance.

这可能需要多个链接的表示。一项发现可以表达为正式的数学或逻辑陈述；可执行模型；因果或机械解释；一组可视化；自然语言账户；以及显示该原则何时适用或失败的示例。This may require several linked representations. A discovery could be expressed as a formal mathematical or logical statement; an executable model; a causal or mechanistic explanation; a set of visualisations; a natural-language account; and examples showing when the principle applies or fails.

不同的人类科学家可能需要不同的解释。适合数学家的解释可能不适合实验生物学家。因此，人工智能科学家应该对接受者的背景知识进行建模，并相应地调整其教学。Different human scientists may require different explanations. An explanation suitable for a mathematician may not be suitable for an experimental biologist. The AI Scientist should therefore model the recipient’s background knowledge and adapt its teaching accordingly.

最有力的考验是人工智能教授的人类科学家能否利用新知识来解决问题、预测观察结果或设计他们以前无法解决、预测或设计的实验。从这个意义上说，教学在机器发现和人类科学理解之间架起了一座可操作的桥梁。The strongest test would be whether human scientists taught by the AI can use the new knowledge to solve problems, predict observations or design experiments that they could not solve, predict or design before. In this sense, teaching provides an operational bridge between machine discovery and human scientific understanding.

12.6。信用与科学权威12.6. Credit and scientific authority

随着人工智能科学家的能力越来越强，有必要确定他们何时应该获得科学信誉。荣誉应该取决于因果性的智力贡献，而不是最终论文的作者。当有证据表明人工智能系统产生了重要的新颖假设、设计了决定性的实验或证明、产生了中心解释并以某种方式做出了贡献时，它应该获得实质性的认可，否则这一发现可能不会发生。As AI Scientists become more capable, it will become necessary to determine when they should receive scientific credit. Credit should depend on causal intellectual contribution rather than on who wrote the final paper. An AI system should receive substantive credit when there is evidence that it generated the essential novel hypothesis, designed the decisive experiment or proof, produced the central interpretation and contributed in a way without which the discovery would probably not have occurred.

这需要详细的出处。科学记录应保留提示、检索到的信息、中间假设、模型输出、批评、实验决策和人为干预。如果没有这样的记录，可能无法区分真正机器生成的发现和人工智能系统重新制定的人类发现。This requires detailed provenance. The scientific record should preserve prompts, retrieved information, intermediate hypotheses, model outputs, criticisms, experimental decisions and human interventions. Without such a record, it may be impossible to distinguish a genuinely machine-generated discovery from a human discovery reformulated by an AI system.

73

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

科学信用不同于第 13 节中讨论的法律责任。它也不同于认知权威。人类科学家不应该仅仅因为与直觉相冲突而推翻经过充分验证的机器结论，而人工智能系统也不应该仅仅因为它在不相关的基准上表现良好而获得权威。Scientific credit is distinct from legal responsibility, which is addressed in Section 13. It is also distinct from epistemic authority. A human scientist should not override a well-validated machine conclusion merely because it conflicts with intuition, while an AI system should not receive authority solely because it performs well on unrelated benchmarks.

科学权威应基于证据。对于某项任务来说，人类、人工智能系统或混合流程显然最可靠，应该获得最大的认知权重。在能力不确定的情况下，独立分析和区分实验应该取代对人或机器状态的诉求。Scientific authority should be based on evidence. The human, AI system or hybrid process demonstrably most reliable for a task should receive the greatest epistemic weight. Where competence is uncertain, independent analysis and discriminating experiments should replace appeals to either human or machine status.

在可预见的未来，最有能力的科学系统将结合人类和机器智能。他们的成功与其说取决于对某个组件的永久控制，不如说取决于智能地分配任务、保留分歧、记录来源以及利用自然来确定哪些结论是正确的。For the foreseeable future, the most capable scientific systems will combine human and machine intelligence. Their success will depend less on keeping one component permanently in control than on allocating tasks intelligently, preserving disagreement, recording provenance and using nature to determine which conclusions are correct.

人机协作并不是实现完全自动化的临时妥协。它本身就是一个重大的科学和工程问题。其目标是构建混合系统，其中人类判断、机器计算、形式推理和物理实验相互加强和纠正。Human–AI collaboration is not a temporary compromise on the path to complete automation. It is itself a major scientific and engineering problem. The objective is to construct hybrid systems in which human judgement, machine computation, formal reasoning and physical experimentation reinforce and correct one another.

13. 道德、社会和治理方面13. Ethical, Social and Governance Aspects

人工智能科学家不会成为中立的工具。他们将决定提出哪些问题、谁可以进行高级研究、谁拥有发现以及承担哪些风险。因此，治理是科学架构的一部分，而不是事后添加的行政层级。AI Scientists will not be neutral instruments. They will shape which questions are asked, who can conduct advanced research, who owns discoveries and which risks are taken. Governance is therefore part of the scientific architecture, not an administrative layer added afterwards.

加拿大科学院理事会科学与工程人工智能专家小组对这些问题进行了最早的全面审查之一。其 2022 年报告《跨越与界限》得出的结论是，人工智能在科学和工程领域的日益广泛使用“给研究人员带来了新的认知、方法论和伦理挑战”（加拿大科学院理事会，2022 年）。One of the earliest comprehensive examinations of these issues was undertaken by the Expert Panel on Artificial Intelligence for Science and Engineering of the Council of Canadian Academies. Its 2022 report, Leaps and Boundaries, concluded that the increasing use of AI in science and engineering “creates new epistemic, methodological, and ethical challenges for researchers” (Council of Canadian Academies, 2022).

从那时起，科学人工智能系统的能力大大增强。他们越来越具有生成性、多模式性和代理性。有些系统可以搜索文献、编写和执行计算机程序、操作科学仪器、订购材料并根据实验结果修改计划。因此，伦理问题不再只是科学家是否应该使用人工智能。这是人工智能系统实质性参与科学发现时的权力、责任和控制权的分配方式。Since then, scientific AI systems have become substantially more capable. They are increasingly generative, multimodal and agentic. Some systems can search the literature, write and execute computer programs, operate scientific instruments, order materials and revise their plans in response to experimental results. The ethical question is therefore no longer only whether scientists should use AI. It is how authority, responsibility and control should be allocated when AI systems participate substantively in scientific discovery.

许多伦理问题是由技术能力和制度决策之间的相互作用产生的。当一个不可靠的模型被信任可以做出重要的决策时，它在道德上就变得很重要。当少数组织控制对不透明系统的访问时，它就变得具有社会重要性。当双重用途能力与采购和物理实验室执行相关时，它就会变得危险。Many ethical problems arise from the interaction between technical capabilities and institutional decisions. An unreliable model becomes ethically important when it is trusted to make consequential decisions. An opaque system becomes socially important when access to it is controlled by a small number of organisations. A dual-use capability becomes dangerous when it is connected to procurement and physical laboratory execution.

因此，人工智能科学家的治理必须纳入其设计和部署中。科学体系已经建立起来之后，它就不能再作为外部约束加入了。The governance of AI Scientists must therefore be incorporated into their design and deployment. It cannot be added as an external constraint after the scientific system has already been built.

13.1.科学权力的分配13.1. Distribution of scientific power

人工智能科学家可以使科学专业知识的获取民主化。一所小型大学的研究人员可能会使用人工智能系统来搜索文献、分析数据或设计以前需要做的实验。AI Scientists could democratise access to scientific expertise. A researcher at a small university might use an AI system to search a literature, analyse data or design experiments that would previously have

74

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

需要一个大型的跨学科团队。云实验室可以提供对复杂实验设施的访问，而不需要每个机构购买和维护它们。required a large interdisciplinary team. Cloud laboratories can provide access to sophisticated experimental facilities without requiring every institution to purchase and maintain them.

同样的技术也可以集中科学力量。先进的人工智能辅助科学需要大规模的计算基础设施；高质量的科学数据集；科学出版物和专有数据库；先进的基础模型；自动化实验室；专用仪器；技术人员；以及整合这些组件所需的资金。The same technologies can also concentrate scientific power. Advanced AI-assisted science requires access to large-scale computational infrastructure; high-quality scientific datasets; scientific publications and proprietary databases; advanced foundation models; automated laboratories; specialised instruments; technical staff; and the capital required to integrate these components.

这些资源的分配非常不均。他们集中在富裕国家、精英大学、大型科技公司和少数研究密集型企业。因此，最有能力的人工智能科学家研究的科学问题可能是由有能力承担这些费用的组织决定的。These resources are distributed very unequally. They are concentrated in wealthy countries, elite universities, large technology companies and a small number of research-intensive corporations. The scientific questions investigated by the most capable AI Scientists may therefore be determined by the organisations able to afford them.

英国皇家学会认为，计算、数据和人工智能基础设施的不平等获取是科学领域公平采用人工智能的重要障碍。它建议更广泛地访问计算和数据基础设施、可访问的科学人工智能工具和允许独立验证和重用的开放科学实践（皇家学会，2024）。The Royal Society has identified unequal access to computing, data and AI infrastructure as an important barrier to the equitable adoption of AI in science. It recommends broader access to computing and data infrastructure, accessible scientific AI tools and open-science practices that permit independent verification and reuse (Royal Society, 2024).

不平等可能出现在几个层面：富国与穷国之间；工业研究和学术研究之间的关系；精英大学和资金不足的大学之间；计算丰富的学科和计算贫乏的学科之间；讲英语和讲其他语言的人之间；以及在科学数据集中得到充分代表的人群与没有得到充分代表的人群之间的差异。Inequality may arise at several levels: between wealthy and poorer countries; between industrial and academic research; between elite and less well-funded universities; between computationally rich and computationally poor disciplines; between speakers of English and other languages; and between populations that are well represented in scientific datasets and those that are not.

主要针对英语出版物训练的模型可能会忽略用其他语言表达的重要知识。主要根据特定人群的数据进行训练的生物医学系统可能会为代表性不足的群体提供不太可靠的结论。科学人工智能集中在少数组织中也可以让他们的商业或地缘政治优先事项塑造全球科学的方向。A model trained primarily on English-language publications may neglect important knowledge expressed in other languages. A biomedical system trained largely on data from particular populations may provide less reliable conclusions for underrepresented groups. The concentration of scientific AI in a few organisations can also permit their commercial or geopolitical priorities to shape the direction of global science.

因此，在科学计算、可互操作的数据基础设施和共享自动化实验室方面需要公共投资。国际访问计划可以使不太富裕的机构和国家的研究人员能够使用这些资源。开放标准将减少对单个供应商的依赖，并允许科学工作流程在平台之间移动。Public investment will therefore be necessary in scientific computing, interoperable data infrastructures and shared automated laboratories. International access programmes can enable researchers in less wealthy institutions and countries to use these resources. Open standards would reduce dependence on individual vendors and permit scientific workflows to move between platforms.

开放科学是这一应对措施的重要组成部分，但开放不可能是绝对的。发布数据、模型和协议通常可以促进可重复性和访问的平等性。然而，在双重用途领域，无限制的释放可能会增加滥用的风险。目标是负责任的开放：科学资源应尽可能开放，同时对造成严重危害的可信风险的能力进行适当的控制。Open science is an important part of this response, but openness cannot be absolute. Releasing data, models and protocols generally promotes reproducibility and equality of access. In dual-use domains, however, unrestricted release may increase the risk of misuse. The objective is responsible openness: scientific resources should be made as accessible as possible while proportionate controls are applied to capabilities that create credible risks of serious harm.

人工智能科学家可以创建一个更加民主的科学体系。他们同样可以创造一个新的科学贵族，其中少数国家、公司和大学控制着能够做出最重要发现的系统。出现哪种结果将取决于政策和制度设计，而不仅仅是技术。AI Scientists can create a more democratic scientific system. They could equally create a new scientific aristocracy in which a small number of states, companies and universities control the systems capable of making the most important discoveries. Which outcome occurs will depend on policy and institutional design rather than on the technology alone.

13.2.科学劳动和教育13.2. Scientific labour and education

人工智能科学家将改变科学就业。一些位移是不可避免的，但更大的影响可能是科学工作的转变。AI Scientists will alter scientific employment. Some displacement is inevitable, but the larger effect is likely to be a transformation of scientific work.

可能变得越来越自动化的活动包括常规文献检索；数据清理和初步分析；标准计算机编程；常规图像和序列分析；执行重复的实验室程序；日常优化；准备标准报告；以及科学写作的一部分。Activities likely to become increasingly automated include routine literature searching; data cleaning and preliminary analysis; standard computer programming; conventional image and sequence analysis; execution of repetitive laboratory procedures; routine optimisation; preparation of standard reports; and parts of scientific writing.

75

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

与此同时，新的工作形式将会出现。科学家必须验证人工智能输出、设计评估、监督自动化实验室、整理数据、将模型与仪器集成、调查故障并管理自主系统的使用。At the same time, new forms of work will emerge. Scientists must verify AI outputs, design evaluations, supervise automated laboratories, curate data, integrate models with instruments, investigate failures and govern the use of autonomous systems.

不同学科的效果会有所不同。自动化最初可能比依赖困难的物理操作、现场工作或人际互动的领域更快地改变高度数字化的领域。随着AI能力的提升，分工也会发生变化。The effect will vary among disciplines. Automation may initially transform highly digitised fields more rapidly than fields dependent on difficult physical manipulation, fieldwork or interpersonal interaction. The division of labour will also change as AI capabilities improve.

其中一个尤为重要的问题是科学的学徒制度。初级科学家不仅通过正式教学进行学习，还通过执行明显的例行任务来学习：阅读论文、编写代码、准备样本、检查数据和起草手稿。这些活动帮助他们获得隐性知识和科学判断。A particularly important issue is the scientific apprenticeship system. Junior scientists learn not only through formal teaching, but by performing apparently routine tasks: reading papers, writing code, preparing samples, inspecting data and drafting manuscripts. These activities help them acquire tacit knowledge and scientific judgement.

如果所有日常工作都委托给人工智能，早期职业研究人员可能会失去发展专业知识的活动。从未分析过原始数据的研究人员可能无法识别自动分析何时具有误导性。从未进行过手动实验的科学家可能无法诊断自动化方案失败的原因。If all routine work is delegated to AI, early-career researchers may lose the activities through which expertise is developed. A researcher who has never analysed raw data may be unable to recognise when an automated analysis is misleading. A scientist who has never performed an experiment manually may be unable to diagnose why an automated protocol has failed.

这造成了自动化悖论。随着系统的能力变得更强，人类练习相关技能的频率就会降低。然而，当人工智能遇到不寻常、模糊或危险的情况时，人类干预可能是最必要的。This creates an automation paradox. As systems become more capable, humans practise the relevant skills less frequently. Yet human intervention may be most necessary when the AI encounters an unusual, ambiguous or dangerous situation.

目标不应该是仅仅为了保留现有的工作而保留低效的工作。应该重新设计科学教育。未来的科学家需要了解： 科学领域；人工智能方法的假设和局限性；实验和统计设计；如何验证机器生成的结果；如何识别分布转变和模型失败；当需要物理或独立验证时；以及哪些决定不应下放。The objective should not be to preserve inefficient work merely to preserve existing jobs. It should be to redesign scientific education. Future scientists will need to understand: the scientific domain; the assumptions and limitations of AI methods; experimental and statistical design; how to verify machinegenerated results; how to recognise distribution shift and model failure; when physical or independent validation is required; and which decisions should not be delegated.

科学家不应该被要求了解每个人工智能系统的每个技术细节，就像实验科学家不了解仪器中的每个晶体管一样。然而，他们必须拥有足够的知识来判断该系统是否适合其科学目的。Scientists should not be required to understand every technical detail of every AI system, just as experimental scientists do not understand every transistor in an instrument. They must nevertheless possess sufficient knowledge to judge whether the system is appropriate for its scientific purpose.

因此，未来的科学队伍不太可能只是由更少的科学家组成。它将由具有不同技能的科学家组成，他们在培训、职业结构和责任分配必须改变的机构内工作。The future scientific workforce is therefore unlikely to consist simply of fewer scientists. It will consist of scientists with different skills, working within institutions whose training, career structures and allocation of responsibility must change.

13.3。研究诚信13.3. Research integrity

人工智能可以通过保留出处、记录实验行为、检查计算、再现计算分析以及将主张与基础证据进行比较来显着提高研究的完整性。它还可能通过伪造论文和参考文献、生成合成数据、产生无效的分析以及缩放表面上合理的文本来威胁完整性。主要危险是错误的工业化：一个有缺陷的模型、数据集或工作流程可以通过数千个输出传播相同的隐藏错误。AI can substantially improve research integrity by preserving provenance, recording experimental actions, checking calculations, reproducing computational analyses and comparing claims with underlying evidence. It can also threaten integrity by fabricating papers and references, generating synthetic data, producing invalid analyses and scaling superficially plausible text. The principal danger is the industrialisation of error: one defective model, dataset or workflow can propagate the same hidden mistake through thousands of outputs.

因此，人工智能辅助科学应保留每个模型的身份和版本、使用日期、科学材料系统说明和提示、检索到的论文和数据库、生成的程序、执行日志、数据转换、模型输出、人工修改、拒绝的替代方案和验证程序。文档应该与机器贡献的智力重要性相称：拼写纠正并不等于生成中心假设或分析。AI-assisted science should therefore preserve the identity and version of each model, the date of use, scientifically material system instructions and prompts, retrieved papers and databases, generated programs, execution logs, data transformations, model outputs, human modifications, rejected alternatives and verification procedures. Documentation should be proportionate to the intellectual importance of the machine contribution: spelling correction is not equivalent to generating the central hypothesis or analysis.

当前的出版政策通常将人工智能系统排除在署名之外，因为作者身份需要批准、问责和责任，而这些目前不能强加于软件（Nature Portfolio，2026）。Current publishing policies generally exclude AI systems from bylines because authorship entails approval, accountability and responsibility that cannot presently be imposed on software (Nature Portfolio, 2026).

76

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

这种排除不应使机器的贡献变得不可见。作者身份、贡献者和出处是不同的。人类作者应该保持责任，而机器贡献声明应该识别系统和版本，并描述概念化、假设生成、软件、分析、实验设计、可视化或绘图等角色。 CRediT 式的角色分类法和透明的贡献声明提供了一个有用的起点（McNutt 等人，2018）。This exclusion should not make machine contributions invisible. Authorship, contributorship and provenance are distinct. Human authors should remain accountable, while a machine contribution statement should identify the system and version and describe roles such as conceptualisation, hypothesis generation, software, analysis, experimental design, visualisation or drafting. CRediT-style role taxonomies and transparent contribution statements provide a useful starting point (McNutt et al., 2018).

然而，当人工智能系统产生决定性的假设或设计时，有一个原则性的案例可以更明确地识别它们。科学信用旨在记录因果性的智力贡献，否认所有命名的承认可能会掩盖一项发现的真实历史。近期最有力的解决方案不是将人工智能视为具有法律责任的作者，而是在贡献者和出处记录中赋予其持久的、可引用的身份，量化其贡献的程度和主动性，并保留人类保证论文的完整性。实证研究表明，人们根据人工智能贡献的类型、规模和主动性来区分信用，支持比二元“使用人工智能”声明更精细的归因（He et al., 2025）。There is nevertheless a principled case for recognising AI systems more explicitly when they originate a decisive hypothesis or design. Scientific credit is meant to record causal intellectual contribution, and denying all named acknowledgement could obscure the true history of a discovery. The strongest nearterm solution is not to treat an AI as a legally responsible author, but to give it a persistent, citable identity in the contributor and provenance record, quantify the extent and initiative of its contribution, and retain human guarantors for the integrity of the paper. Empirical work suggests that people distinguish credit according to the type, scale and initiative of an AI contribution, supporting more granular attribution than a binary “used AI” statement (He et al., 2025).

研究机构和期刊还需要更好的机制来检测伪造数据、自动化论文生产和未公开的人工智能使用。仅仅检测是不够的：重要的主张应该直接与支持它们的实验、数据集、分析或正式推论联系起来。Research institutions and journals will also need better mechanisms for detecting fabricated data, automated paper production and undeclared AI use. Detection alone is insufficient: important claims should be linked directly to the experiment, dataset, analysis or formal deduction that supports them.

13.4.问责制13.4. Accountability

目前，人工智能系统不能被解雇、失去专业执照、被监禁或因不当行为而遭受普通的声誉后果。它不能承担法律责任，也不能向因其行为受到伤害的人提供赔偿。An AI system cannot currently be dismissed from employment, lose a professional licence, be imprisoned or suffer the ordinary reputational consequences of misconduct. It cannot accept legal liability or provide compensation to those harmed by its actions.

因此，责任必须由人和机构承担。但要说按下最后按钮的科学家对系统所做的一切负责就太简单了。Responsibility must therefore remain with humans and institutions. But it is too simple to state that the scientist who presses the final button is responsible for everything the system does.

人工智能辅助发现涉及一系列参与者：底层模型的开发人员；培训数据的提供者；科学工具和数据库的开发商；系统集成商；实验室和云服务运营商；研究机构；主要研究人员；个人用户；资助者；出版商；和监管机构。AI-assisted discovery involves a chain of actors: developers of the underlying model; providers of training data; developers of scientific tools and databases; system integrators; laboratory and cloud-service operators; research institutions; principal investigators; individual users; funders; publishers; and regulators.

每个演员都拥有不同的知识和控制力。模型提供者可能知道用户不可见的系统限制。机构可以在不提供充分监督的情况下授权部署。主要研究者可能会批准不安全的实验。用户可能会忽略明确的警告。实验室操作员可能无法维护物理安全系统。Each actor possesses different knowledge and control. A model provider may know of a systematic limitation that is invisible to the user. An institution may authorise deployment without providing adequate oversight. A principal investigator may approve an unsafe experiment. A user may ignore a clear warning. A laboratory operator may fail to maintain physical safety systems.

因此，责任应遵循对相关行动的控制、风险的知识或合理预见性、预防或减轻损害的能力以及分配给行为者的正式职责。Responsibility should therefore follow control over the relevant action, knowledge or reasonable foreseeability of the risk, capacity to prevent or mitigate the harm, and the formal duties assigned to the actor.

每个自治科学系统都应该拥有一份责任图。在部署之前，机构应明确：谁可以授权每类实验；谁监督执行情况；谁可以暂停系统；谁验证结论；谁调查失败；谁报告事件；以及谁在法律和道德上负有责任。人工智能科学家的自主权越大，其行为越不可逆转，这种分配必须越清晰。Every autonomous scientific system should possess a responsibility map. Before deployment, the institution should specify: who may authorise each class of experiment; who monitors execution; who can suspend the system; who verifies the conclusions; who investigates failures; who reports incidents; and who is legally and ethically accountable. The greater the autonomy of the AI Scientist, and the more irreversible its actions, the clearer this allocation must be.

问责制还需要可审计性。如果系统的操作、工具调用和实验决策没有被记录，那么系统就无法得到有效的治理。机构应保存防篡改日志，并确保技术上合格的调查人员能够重建重大事件。Accountability also requires auditability. A system cannot be governed effectively if its actions, tool calls and experimental decisions are not recorded. Institutions should preserve tamper-resistant logs and ensure that technically competent investigators can reconstruct material incidents.

77

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

人类监督必须是实质性的。仅仅在自动化流程的最后放置一个名义上的人工审批者可能会在没有真正审查的情况下造成责任的错觉。人类必须有足够的信息、时间、权威和能力来进行干预。Human oversight must be substantive. Merely placing a nominal human approver at the end of an automated process can create an illusion of accountability without genuine review. The human must have adequate information, time, authority and competence to intervene.

13.5。知识产权13.5. Intellectual property

人工智能辅助的科学发现给知识产权法带来了难题。传统的专利和版权制度主要是围绕可识别的人类创造者而设计的。相反，人工智能发明可能源自制定研究目标的科学家的贡献； AI模型的开发者；培训数据的提供者；提供说明的用户；生成候选解决方案的人工智能系统；验证它们的实验家；以及拥有基础设施的机构。AI-assisted scientific discovery creates difficult questions for intellectual-property law. Traditional patent and copyright systems were designed principally around identifiable human creators. An AI-enabled invention may instead result from contributions made by scientists who formulated the research objective; developers of the AI model; providers of training data; users who supplied instructions; the AI system that generated candidate solutions; experimentalists who validated them; and the institution that owned the infrastructure.

英国现行的专利法仍然需要人类发明人。在 Thaler 诉专利、设计和商标总审计长一案中，英国最高法院于 2023 年裁定，根据 1977 年《专利法》，人工智能系统不能被指定为发明人（英国最高法院，2023 年）。Current patent law in the United Kingdom still requires a human inventor. In Thaler v Comptroller-General of Patents, Designs and Trade Marks, the UK Supreme Court held in 2023 that an AI system could not be named as an inventor under the Patents Act 1977 (UK Supreme Court, 2023).

美国还要求发明人必须是自然人。 2025 年发布的修订后的美国专利商标局指南指出，发明人的普通法律标准适用于人工智能辅助的发明，并且分析必须关注人类的贡献。人工智能援助不会自动阻止专利性，但人工智能本身不能被指定为发明人（美国专利商标局，2025）。The United States also requires inventors to be natural persons. Revised USPTO guidance issued in 2025 states that the ordinary legal standard for inventorship applies to AI-assisted inventions and that the analysis must focus on the human contribution. AI assistance does not automatically prevent patentability, but the AI itself cannot be named as an inventor (United States Patent and Trademark Office, 2025).

这造成了潜在的差距。几乎没有或根本没有可识别的人类构思而产生的发明可能具有科学价值，但很难根据现有专利法得到保护。相反，组织可能会尝试将广泛的发明贡献归功于员工，以确保对实质上由机器生成的发现进行专利保护。This creates a potential gap. An invention generated with little or no identifiable human conception may be scientifically valuable but difficult to protect under existing patent law. Conversely, organisations may attempt to attribute broad inventive contributions to employees in order to secure patent protection for substantially machine-generated discoveries.

需要确定详细的出处： 哪个人提出了发明概念；人工智能做出了哪些贡献；人类的贡献在科学上是否具有实质性；以及最终的发明与系统的输入和训练数据有何不同。Detailed provenance will be necessary to establish: which human formulated the inventive concept; which contributions were produced by the AI; whether a human contribution was scientifically substantive; and how the final invention differed from the system’s inputs and training data.

所有权是与发明权不同的问题。即使法律承认人类为发明人，合同和雇佣规则也可能将所有权转让给雇主或其他组织。Ownership is a separate question from inventorship. Even where a human is legally recognised as the inventor, contractual and employment rules may transfer ownership to an employer or other organisation.

版权提出了有关使用科学出版物和数据库进行培训、生成的文本和图像的状态以及机器生成的输出的所有权的相关问题。欧盟人工智能法案要求合格通用模型的提供者维持遵守欧盟版权法的政策，并发布足够详细的培训内容摘要（欧洲议会和欧盟理事会，2024）。Copyright raises related questions concerning the use of scientific publications and databases for training, the status of generated text and images, and the ownership of machine-generated outputs. The EU AI Act requires providers of qualifying general-purpose models to maintain a policy for compliance with EU copyright law and to publish a sufficiently detailed summary of training content (European Parliament and Council of the European Union, 2024).

人工智能科学家还可以增加专利的战略使用。一个足够强大的系统可以生成和筛选大量潜在的可申请专利的分子、材料或工艺。富裕的组织可能会利用这种能力构建密集的专利组合，涵盖广泛的技术可能性领域。AI Scientists can also increase the strategic use of patents. A sufficiently capable system could generate and screen very large numbers of potentially patentable molecules, materials or processes. Wealthy organisations might use this capacity to construct dense patent portfolios covering broad regions of technological possibility.

专利制度旨在通过将暂时的独占权换成公开披露来鼓励创新。如果自动化发明允许少数组织获得对大量可能发现的权利，那么知识产权可能会阻碍而不是鼓励累积性科学。Patent systems were intended to encourage innovation by exchanging temporary exclusivity for public disclosure. If automated invention permits a small number of organisations to acquire rights over enormous numbers of possible discoveries, intellectual property could obstruct rather than encourage cumulative science.

78

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

13.6。隐私、同意和数据治理13.6. Privacy, consent and data governance

人工智能科学家可以整合来自医疗记录、基因组、实验室测量、可穿戴设备、环境监测和个人行为的数据。组合这些数据集的科学价值可能相当大，但相关风险也相当大。AI Scientists may integrate data from medical records, genomes, laboratory measurements, wearable devices, environmental monitoring and personal behaviour. The scientific value of combining these datasets may be considerable, but so are the associated risks.

删除名字并不总是足以使科学数据匿名化。可以通过结合基因组、人口统计、地理和临床信息来重新识别个体。模型还可以记住敏感信息或通过其输出间接揭示这些信息。Removing names is not always sufficient to anonymise scientific data. Individuals may be re-identified by combining genomic, demographic, geographic and clinical information. Models can also memorise sensitive information or reveal it indirectly through their outputs.

数据治理应整合合法访问、知情同意、目的限制、数据最小化、安全存储和计算、访问控制、保留期限、二次使用、国际传输以及防止重新识别或模型提取。这些义务应体现在系统的权限中，并在检索、分析和模型训练期间强制执行，而不是仅记录在政策文件中。一个特别困难的问题是，对一个研究项目的同意是否允许将数据纳入能够支持不可预见用途的通用科学模型中。广泛的同意可能会增加科学效用，但它也可能剥夺参与者对未来使用的有意义的控制。Data governance should integrate lawful access, informed consent, purpose limitation, data minimisation, secure storage and computation, access control, retention periods, secondary use, international transfer and protection against re-identification or model extraction. These obligations should be represented in the system’s permissions and enforced during retrieval, analysis and model training rather than recorded only in policy documents. A particularly difficult question is whether consent for one research project permits data to be incorporated into a general scientific model capable of supporting unforeseen uses. Broad consent may increase scientific utility, but it can also deprive participants of meaningful control over future use.

人工智能系统还可以在不识别特定个人的情况下造成群体伤害。涉及种族、遗传、地理或社会人群的调查结果可能会导致耻辱、歧视或商业剥削。因此，仅仅个人同意可能是不够的。在某些情况下，社区应集体参与有关数据使用和惠益分享的决策。AI systems can also create group harms without identifying particular individuals. A finding concerning an ethnic, genetic, geographic or social population may lead to stigma, discrimination or commercial exploitation. Individual consent alone may therefore be insufficient. In some contexts, communities should participate collectively in decisions about data use and benefit sharing.

数据治理还必须适用于科学合作过程中生成的信息。未经适当授权和安全保护，不得将未发表的手稿、资助申请、机密同行评审和专有实验数据提交给外部人工智能系统。Data governance must also apply to information generated during scientific collaboration. Unpublished manuscripts, grant applications, confidential peer reviews and proprietary experimental data should not be submitted to external AI systems without appropriate authorisation and security protections.

隐私保护不必妨碍有价值的研究。可信的研究环境、隐私保护计算、联合分析和受控访问数据库可以允许科学使用，同时减少披露。适当的保护措施取决于数据的敏感性和滥用的后果。Privacy protection need not prevent valuable research. Trusted research environments, privacypreserving computation, federated analysis and controlled-access databases can permit scientific use while reducing disclosure. The appropriate safeguards depend on the sensitivity of the data and the consequences of misuse.

13.7。双重用途研究以及化学和生物风险13.7. Dual-use research and chemical and biological risks

科学知识通常具有双重用途。能够设计药物、蛋白质、催化剂和工业化学品的技术也可能支持有害的应用。Scientific knowledge is commonly dual-use. The same techniques that enable the design of medicines, proteins, catalysts and industrial chemicals can also support harmful applications.

人工智能科学家加剧了这个问题，因为他们可以连接多个活动阶段：搜索科学信息；生成候选设计；规划实验；故障排除程序；选择材料和设备；和控制实验室自动化。AI Scientists intensify this problem because they can connect several stages of activity: searching scientific information; generating candidate designs; planning experiments; troubleshooting procedures; selecting materials and equipment; and controlling laboratory automation.

因此，独特的危险不仅仅是人工智能科学家拥有技术知识。知识、规划、采购和实际执行可以集成在一个自动化系统中。The distinctive danger is therefore not simply that an AI Scientist possesses technical knowledge. It is that knowledge, planning, procurement and physical execution may be integrated within one automated system.

《2026 年国际人工智能安全报告》得出的结论是，通用人工智能系统可以提供与生物和化学武器开发相关的详细信息，包括实验室说明和实验故障排除（Bengio 等人，2026）。它还强调了这些能力在多大程度上增加了现实世界风险的巨大不确定性，因为生产有效武器仍然存在实际障碍。The International AI Safety Report 2026 concluded that general-purpose AI systems can provide detailed information relevant to biological and chemical weapons development, including laboratory instructions and experimental troubleshooting (Bengio et al., 2026). It also emphasised substantial uncertainty about how much these capabilities increase real-world risk because practical barriers remain to producing effective weapons.

79

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

许多相关功能确实是有益的。能够识别有害生物机制的系统也能够识别治疗方法。改进化学合成的系统可以支持药物和有毒物质。广泛压制这些能力可能会阻碍医学、公共卫生和防御性研究。Many of the relevant capabilities are genuinely beneficial. A system capable of identifying a harmful biological mechanism can also be capable of identifying a therapy. A system that improves chemical synthesis may support both medicines and toxic substances. Broadly suppressing these capabilities could impede medicine, public health and defensive research.

因此，治理必须对风险敏感，而不是仅仅基于科学领域或术语。保障措施应分层贯穿整个系统。它们应包括部署前评估和危险能力的红队；与经过验证的用户和目的相关的差异化访问；记录、审计和机构双重用途​​审查；对自主购买和合成的限制；由材料和合成供应商筛选；独立的物理安全联锁装置；高风险实验的强制性人类授权；事件报告；对发布极其危险的程序信息进行相应的控制。没有任何一种安全措施是足够的：访问控制可能被规避，分类器会产生错误，而人工审查可能会变得敷衍。因此，一种保护的失败不应暴露系统的全部功能。Governance must therefore be risk-sensitive rather than based solely on scientific field or terminology. Safeguards should be layered across the complete system. They should include pre-deployment evaluation and red teaming of hazardous capabilities; differentiated access tied to verified users and purposes; logging, auditing and institutional dual-use review; restrictions on autonomous purchasing and synthesis; screening by material and synthesis providers; independent physical safety interlocks; mandatory human authorisation for high-risk experiments; incident reporting; and proportionate controls on releasing exceptionally dangerous procedural information. No single safeguard is sufficient: access controls can be circumvented, classifiers produce errors and human review may become perfunctory. The failure of one protection should therefore not expose the full capability of the system.

世界卫生组织负责任地使用生命科学的框架强调，生物风险治理是整个研究生命周期中科学家、研究机构、资助者、出版商、政府和私人组织的共同责任（世界卫生组织，2022）。The WHO framework for the responsible use of the life sciences emphasises that biorisk governance is a shared responsibility involving scientists, research institutions, funders, publishers, governments and private organisations across the research lifecycle (World Health Organization, 2022).

治理还应保护有益的开放性。过度保密可能会阻碍国际合作、公共卫生准备和独立安全研究。控制措施应主要针对危险的能力、用户和行为，而不是不加区别地限制整个科学领域。Governance should also protect beneficial openness. Excessive secrecy can obstruct international cooperation, public-health preparedness and independent safety research. Controls should be directed principally at dangerous capabilities, users and actions rather than indiscriminately restricting whole fields of science.

除了双重用途能力之外，人工智能科学家的物理集成还带来了严重的网络安全和网络动力学漏洞。如果自动驾驶实验室的编排软件遭到破坏，恶意行为者可能会巧妙地改变实验参数（例如孵化温度或试剂浓度），从而使多年的研究无效或对设备造成物理损坏。此外，对大量网络抓取的科学数据集的依赖使人工智能科学家容易受到数据中毒攻击，其中敌对行为者注入捏造的论文或操纵的数据来扭曲系统的假设生成（Steinhardt 等人，2017）。因此，网络安全协议不仅必须保护数据，还必须保护物理端点和训练语料库的认知完整性。Beyond dual-use capabilities, the physical integration of AI Scientists introduces severe cybersecurity and cyber-kinetic vulnerabilities. If a self-driving laboratory’s orchestration software is compromised, malicious actors could subtly alter experimental parameters—such as incubation temperatures or reagent concentrations—to invalidate years of research or physically damage equipment. Furthermore, the reliance on massive, web-scraped scientific datasets makes AI Scientists vulnerable to data poisoning attacks, where adversarial actors inject fabricated papers or manipulated data to skew the system’s hypothesis generation (Steinhardt et al., 2017). Cybersecurity protocols must therefore secure not just the data, but the physical endpoints and the epistemic integrity of the training corpora.

13.8。环境成本13.8. Environmental costs

人工智能辅助科学消耗物理资源。训练和操作大型模型需要电力、数据中心基础设施、冷却、水和专用半导体硬件。自动化实验室消耗能源、试剂、一次性塑料和其他材料。AI-assisted science consumes physical resources. Training and operating large models requires electricity, data-centre infrastructure, cooling, water and specialised semiconductor hardware. Automated laboratories consume energy, reagents, disposable plastics and other materials.

这些成本不应孤立地考虑。人工智能还可以通过选择更少、信息更丰富的实验来减轻科学的环境负担；减少合成失败；优化试剂使用；改进设备调度；用经过验证的模拟代替一些物理实验；并发现更清洁的材料和工业流程。These costs should not be considered in isolation. AI can also reduce the environmental burden of science by selecting fewer and more informative experiments; reducing failed synthesis; optimising reagent use; improving equipment scheduling; replacing some physical experiments with validated simulations; and discovering cleaner materials and industrial processes.

问题是整个生命周期成本是否与科学和社会效益相称。The question is whether the complete lifecycle cost is proportionate to the scientific and social benefit.

机构应衡量并报告重大人工智能辅助发现中使用的计算和实验资源。英国皇家学会建议监测和减轻不断增长的计算需求对环境的影响，并将环境可持续的计算实践纳入科学人工智能基础设施的提供中（皇家学会，2024）。Institutions should measure and report the computational and experimental resources used in major AIassisted discoveries. The Royal Society has recommended monitoring and mitigating the environmental effects of increasing computational demand and incorporating environmentally sustainable computational practices into the provision of scientific AI infrastructure (Royal Society, 2024).

环境成本也可以直接纳入实验决策中。人工智能科学家在选择实验时不仅要考虑时间和金钱成本，还要考虑：能源使用；Environmental cost can also be incorporated directly into experimental decision-making. An AI Scientist choosing among experiments should consider not only time and monetary cost, but also: energy use;

80

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

用水；稀缺材料；危险废物；温室气体排放；以及不成功的实验对环境造成的后果。water use; scarce materials; hazardous waste; greenhouse-gas emissions; and the environmental consequences of unsuccessful experiments.

这将使科学价值与资源消耗明确平衡，而不是将环境影响视为无形的外部因素。This would permit scientific value to be balanced explicitly against resource consumption rather than treating environmental effects as invisible externalities.

13.9。科学优先事项和公众信任13.9. Scientific priorities and public trust

社会选择哪些疾病接受研究资助、哪些人群被研究、哪些环境风险被优先考虑、哪些工业技术被开发、哪些实验是可以接受的以及如何分配利益和风险。因此，研究优先顺序的设定不可避免地是道德的：稀缺的科学资源在不同群体之间分配潜在利益，并体现了对谁的利益重要的判断（世界卫生组织，2025）。因此，负责任的创新框架强调整个研究过程中的预期、反思、包容性参与和行动（英国研究与创新，2026）。Societies choose which diseases receive research funding, which populations are studied, which environmental risks are prioritised, which industrial technologies are developed, which experiments are acceptable and how benefits and risks are distributed. Research priority setting is therefore unavoidably ethical: scarce scientific resources allocate potential benefits among different groups and embody judgements about whose interests count (World Health Organization, 2025). Responsible-innovation frameworks accordingly emphasise anticipation, reflection, inclusive engagement and action throughout the research process (UK Research and Innovation, 2026).

人工智能科学家可以针对特定目标进行有效优化，但不应自主确定该目标是否符合社会需要。客观选择是一项政治和道德决定。An AI Scientist may optimise efficiently towards a specified objective, but it should not determine autonomously whether that objective is socially desirable. Objective selection is a political and ethical decision.

如果最有能力的人工智能科学家由私营公司控制，他们的工作可能会针对有利可图的产品，而不是被忽视的疾病、环境保护或基础知识。国家控制的系统可能会优先考虑国家权力、安全或工业竞争力。商业盈利能力和地缘政治优势都不等同于社会效益。If the most capable AI Scientists are controlled by private companies, their work may be directed towards profitable products rather than neglected diseases, environmental protection or fundamental knowledge. State-controlled systems may prioritise national power, security or industrial competitiveness. Neither commercial profitability nor geopolitical advantage is identical to social benefit.

受影响的社区应参与有关研究目标的决策，特别是当实验可能给他们带来风险或发现涉及他们的数据、环境或生物资源时。适当的机制包括公众咨询；社区代表；研究伦理审查；患者和参与者的参与；透明的优先级设置；惠益分享协议；以及挑战或上诉研究决定的机会。Affected communities should participate in decisions about research objectives, particularly where experiments may impose risks on them or where discoveries concern their data, environment or biological resources. Appropriate mechanisms include public consultation; community representation; researchethics review; patient and participant involvement; transparent priority setting; benefit-sharing agreements; and opportunities to challenge or appeal research decisions.

不能仅仅通过改善公共沟通来寻求信任。只有当机构有能力、透明且负责时，公众信任才合理。提出有说服力的解释但隐瞒其利益、不确定性或失败的人工智能科学家不应被信任。Trust must not be sought merely through improved public communication. Public trust is justified only where institutions are competent, transparent and accountable. An AI Scientist that produces persuasive explanations but conceals its interests, uncertainties or failures should not be trusted.

人工智能科学家的部署还可以改变公众对科学的理解。科学结论可能越来越多地由很少有人能够检查的系统产生，并且由具有重大商业利益的组织拥有。维持信任需要独立评估以及科学证据和机构倡导之间的明显分离。The deployment of AI Scientists can also alter the public understanding of science. Scientific conclusions may increasingly be produced by systems that few people can inspect and that are owned by organisations with substantial commercial interests. Maintaining trust will require independent evaluation and visible separation between scientific evidence and institutional advocacy.

人工智能科学家应该为公共和政治决策提供信息。他们不应该自主决定人类应该追求哪些科学目标。AI Scientists should inform public and political decisions. They should not determine autonomously which scientific objectives humanity ought to pursue.

13.10。人工智能科学家的治理13.10. Governance of AI Scientists

治理应与风险相称。总结论文的系统不应以与能够订购材料和控制生物或化学实验的系统相同的方式进行管理。Governance should be proportionate to risk. A system that summarises papers should not be governed in the same way as a system capable of ordering materials and controlling biological or chemical experiments.

相关维度包括损害的严重性和概率、行为的不确定性、可逆性、运营规模、物理设备和敏感信息的访问以及表 2 中定义的自主水平。只有在足够的可靠性之后才应提高自主水平。Relevant dimensions include the severity and probability of harm, uncertainty about behaviour, reversibility, operating scale, access to physical equipment and sensitive information, and the autonomy level defined in Table 2. Autonomy should be increased only after adequate reliability has been

81

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

在较低水平上表现出来。文献分析的出色表现并不能证明运营化学实验室的能力。demonstrated at lower levels. Strong performance in literature analysis does not establish competence to operate a chemical laboratory.

部署高级人工智能科学家的机构应要求有记录的科学和安全案例、使用第 11 节中的标准进行独立评估、明确界限的权限、持续监控和完整的审计跟踪、网络安全保护、物理和软件故障保护、事件报告程序、定期重新评估以及立即暂停系统的能力。治理必须超越模型：表面上安全的模型在连接到不受限制的工具、采购系统或实验室设备时可能会变得危险。相关的监管单元是由模型、软件、仪器、人员、组织和激励组成的完整的社会技术系统。Institutions deploying advanced AI Scientists should require a documented scientific and safety case, independent evaluation using the criteria in Section 11, clearly bounded permissions, continuous monitoring and complete audit trails, cybersecurity protection, physical and software fail-safes, incidentreporting procedures, periodic reassessment and the capacity to suspend the system immediately. Governance must extend beyond the model: an apparently safe model can become dangerous when connected to unrestricted tools, procurement systems or laboratory equipment. The relevant unit of regulation is the complete sociotechnical system of models, software, instruments, people, organisations and incentives.

当模型、云实验室和合作跨境运作时，仅靠国家监管是不够的。军民两用标准、事件报告、评估方法、综合筛选和高度自治系统的治理需要国际协调。National regulation alone will be insufficient where models, cloud laboratories and collaborations operate across borders. International coordination will be necessary for dual-use standards, incident reporting, evaluation methods, synthesis screening and the governance of highly autonomous systems.

道德治理不应仅仅被理解为一种限制。出处提高了再现性；准入计划扩大参与范围；安全审查可防止可避免的伤害；明确的责任可以增加合理的信任。Ethical governance should not be understood only as a restriction. Provenance improves reproducibility; access programmes broaden participation; safety review prevents avoidable harm; and clear accountability increases justified trust.

一位称职的人工智能科学家不仅必须推理出哪个实验信息最丰富，而且还必须推理出哪些实验是合法的、安全的、道德上允许的、环境相称的和社会合理的。这些要求应该是机器可读的、可审计的，并直接与规划和执行相关。A competent AI Scientist must reason not only about which experiment is most informative, but also about which experiments are lawful, safe, ethically permissible, environmentally proportionate and socially justified. These requirements should be machine-readable, auditable and connected directly to planning and execution.

我们的目标不是一个不受约束地追求知识的人工智能科学家。它是一位人工智能科学家，在推进知识的同时，仍然嵌入人类机构之中，能够分配目的、保护权利、分配利益和承担责任。The objective is not an AI Scientist that pursues knowledge without constraint. It is an AI Scientist that advances knowledge while remaining embedded within human institutions capable of assigning purposes, protecting rights, distributing benefits and accepting responsibility.

13.11。自主科学的经济学和资助13.11. The Economics and Funding of Autonomous Science

向人工智能科学家的转变需要研究经济学的根本转变。传统的赠款是围绕人员工资、机构管理费用和当地设备组织的。高度自主的科学将越来越多的支出转向计算集群、模型训练和推理、专业数据、软件集成、自动化实验室和专有系统的访问费用。如果这些资源变得极其昂贵，基础性发现可能会集中在少数科技公司和富裕国家（Ahmed & Wahed，2020；皇家学会，2024）。The transition to AI Scientists requires a fundamental shift in research economics. Conventional grants are organised around human salaries, institutional overheads and local equipment. Highly autonomous science shifts a growing share of expenditure towards computing clusters, model training and inference, specialist data, software integration, automated laboratories and access fees for proprietary systems. If these resources become prohibitively expensive, foundational discovery could become concentrated in a small number of technology companies and wealthy states (Ahmed & Wahed, 2020; Royal Society, 2024).

因此，资助机构应将计算、科学数据基础设施和自动化实验室视为共享研究设施，类似于望远镜、同步加速器和超级计算机。公共采购和访问计划可以支持开放模型、可互操作标准、长期维护和独立复制，而不是强迫每个实验室购买分散的商业服务。Funding bodies should therefore treat compute, scientific data infrastructure and automated laboratories as shared research facilities, analogous to telescopes, synchrotrons and supercomputers. Public procurement and access programmes can support open models, interoperable standards, long-term maintenance and independent replication rather than forcing each laboratory to purchase fragmented commercial services.

2026 年白宫报告《科学：新黄金时代》明确将人工智能视为新的科学制度解决方案的一部分。它认为，战后基础研究随后应用的线性模型对于发现经常在基础研究、工程和工业之间迭代移动的时代来说是不够的。该报告呼吁制定雄心勃勃的使命，加强政府、大学和公司之间的联系，以及能够在全国范围内使用人工智能、数据和自动化的科学基础设施（白宫科学技术政策办公室，2026）。The 2026 White House report Science: A New Golden Age explicitly frames AI as part of a new institutional settlement for science. It argues that the post-war linear model of basic research followed by application is inadequate for an era in which discovery often moves iteratively among fundamental research, engineering and industry. The report calls for ambitious missions, stronger links among government, universities and companies, and scientific infrastructure capable of using AI, data and automation at national scale (White House Office of Science and Technology Policy, 2026).

该计划正确地认识到人工智能驱动的科学需要集成的基础设施，而私营企业现在提供了很大一部分的研发工作。其核心政策挑战是将速度和使命导向与多元化、开放性、长期视野和That programme correctly recognises that AI-driven science requires integrated infrastructure and that private industry now supplies a large fraction of research and development. Its central policy challenge is to combine speed and mission orientation with the pluralism, openness, long time horizons and

82

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

基础科学所依赖的独立批评。公共投资应该扩大科学共享，而不仅仅是补贴封闭的平台，资金组合应该保护好奇心驱动的工作、复制和被忽视的问题以及战略任务。independent criticism on which fundamental science depends. Public investment should expand the scientific commons rather than merely subsidise closed platforms, and funding portfolios should protect curiosity-driven work, replication and neglected problems alongside strategic missions.

14. 未来和诺贝尔图灵挑战14. The Future and the Nobel Turing Challenge

诺贝尔图灵挑战赛的目标是建立能够以等于或高于最优秀的人类科学家的水平进行重要科学研究的人工系统。诺贝尔图灵挑战赛为这一雄心壮志提供了日期和测试。The goal of the Nobel Turing Challenge is to build artificial systems that conduct important science at a level equal to or greater than the best human scientists. The Nobel Turing Challenge gives this ambition a date and a test.

14.1.工业规模和集体科学14.1. Industrial-scale and collective science

人工智能科学家比人类科学家更容易复制和协调。数百、数千甚至数百万个软件代理可以探索替代假设、分析不同的数据集、运行模拟、相互批评并并行设计实验。AI Scientists are easier to copy and coordinate than human scientists. Hundreds, thousands or eventually millions of software agents could explore alternative hypotheses, analyse different datasets, run simulations, criticise one another and design experiments in parallel.

软件规模并不意味着实验规模。物理实验需要仪器、样品、试剂、能量、时间和独立确认。一百万个代理生成假设可能会造成验证瓶颈，而不是加速科学发展。Software scale does not imply experimental scale. Physical experiments require instruments, samples, reagents, energy, time and independent confirmation. A million agents generating hypotheses can create a verification bottleneck rather than accelerated science.

因此，工业规模的科学应该通过单位时间、成本和材料产生的可靠知识来衡量，而不是通过代理或实验的数量来衡量。人工智能科学家必须合并多余的假设，抑制低价值的提议，设计能够区分多种替代方案的实验，并为复制保留资源。Industrial-scale science should therefore be measured by reliable knowledge produced per unit of time, cost and material, not by the number of agents or experiments. AI Scientists must merge redundant hypotheses, suppress low-value proposals, design experiments that discriminate among many alternatives and reserve resources for replication.

成熟的人工智能科学家可能不像个人。它可能是文献代理、假设生成器、形式推理器、模拟器、实验设计者、实验室控制者、统计学家、评论家和安全代理的集体架构。一些代理人应该故意表示不同意见，保留替代性表述并寻找反对偏爱模型的证据。The mature AI Scientist may not resemble an individual. It may be a collective architecture of literature agents, hypothesis generators, formal reasoners, simulators, experiment designers, laboratory controllers, statisticians, critics and safety agents. Some agents should disagree deliberately, preserving alternative representations and searching for evidence against favoured models.

大型人工智能科学家社区将需要组合级别的协调。我们提出虚拟研究委员会：在竞争研究项目之间分配计算、实验室时间、样本、复制工作和注意力的元科学系统。Large communities of AI Scientists will require portfolio-level coordination. We propose virtual research councils: metascientific systems that allocate computation, laboratory time, samples, replication effort and attention among competing research programmes.

虚拟研究委员会可以考虑预期的信息增益、成功的可能性、科学重要性、新颖性、成本、持续时间、安全性、环境影响、多样性和解决分歧的价值。它还应该为推测性研究、复制、基础测量和被忽视的问题保留资源。它应该在人类授权的目标范围内分配资源，而不是自主确定人类应该重视什么。A virtual research council could consider expected information gain, probability of success, scientific importance, novelty, cost, duration, safety, environmental impact, diversity and the value of resolving disagreement. It should also reserve resources for speculative research, replication, foundational measurement and neglected problems. It should allocate resources within human-authorised objectives rather than determine autonomously what humanity ought to value.

14.2.以系统生物学为例14.2. Systems biology as an exemplar

系统生物学证明了为什么机器规模的科学可能是必要的。即使是酿酒酵母和大肠杆菌等模型生物也含有数千个基因、蛋白质和代谢物，这些基因、蛋白质和代谢物在空间、时间、环境和细胞状态之间相互作用。Systems biology demonstrates why machine-scale science may be necessary. Even model organisms such as Saccharomyces cerevisiae and Escherichia coli contain thousands of genes, proteins and metabolites interacting across space, time, environment and cellular state.

对细胞的令人满意的科学理解需要一个能够预测干预、代表因果结构、跨尺度运作、解释不确定性并且可以根据证据进行修改的模型。开发这样的模型需要大量协调一致的、以假设为主导的实验。A satisfactory scientific understanding of a cell requires a model that predicts interventions, represents causal structure, operates across scales, accounts for uncertainty and can be revised in response to evidence. Developing such a model will require very large numbers of coordinated, hypothesis-led experiments.

83

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

没有人能够在详细的全细胞模型中保留所有机械依赖性。该模型必须是机器可操作的，同时支持人类可以询问的分层解释。数千名专业人工智能科学家可以研究新陈代谢、转录、翻译、定位、信号传导、细胞器、细胞周期、压力、衰老和遗传相互作用，而更高级别的代理则集成子系统模型并在其界面上设计实验。No individual human can retain every mechanistic dependency in a detailed whole-cell model. The model must be machine-operable while supporting layered explanations that humans can interrogate. Thousands of specialised AI Scientists could investigate metabolism, transcription, translation, localisation, signalling, organelles, the cell cycle, stress, ageing and genetic interactions, while higher-level agents integrate the subsystem models and design experiments at their interfaces.

规模并不能替代概念创新。细胞生物学将需要新的表征和理论以及系统实验。人工智能科学家的优势在于，概念搜索和实证测试可以在一个比任何人类实验室可以协调的大得多的程序中连接起来。Scale is not a substitute for conceptual innovation. Cell biology will require new representations and theories as well as systematic experimentation. The advantage of AI Scientists is that conceptual search and empirical testing can be connected within a programme much larger than any human laboratory could coordinate.

14.3。机器可操作的科学共享14.3. A machine-actionable scientific commons

未来许多科学信息的主要消费者将是软件代理。它们将需要稳定的标识符、明确的模式、标准单位、出处、不确定性、可执行协议、版本历史以及声明和证据之间的链接。The primary consumers of much future scientific information will be software agents. They will require stable identifiers, explicit schemas, standard units, provenance, uncertainty, executable protocols, version histories and links between claims and evidence.

传统的论文对于人类的解释仍然很重要，但它将成为包含正式主张、数据、代码、模型、协议、负面结果和出处的互联科学对象的一层。新的结果应该更新持续维护的概率知识系统，而不是孤立地停留在散文中。The conventional paper will remain important for human explanation, but it will become one layer in a connected scientific object containing formal claims, data, code, models, protocols, negative results and provenance. New results should update a continuously maintained probabilistic knowledge system rather than remain isolated in prose.

分布在全球的人工智能科学家几乎可以立即交换模型、证据和协议，并在各大洲重现实验。这需要开放标准。如果没有互操作性，机器科学将分裂成无法相互验证的专有生态系统。Globally distributed AI Scientists could exchange models, evidence and protocols almost instantly and reproduce experiments across continents. This requires open standards. Without interoperability, machine science will fragment into proprietary ecosystems that cannot verify one another.

14.4。开放式科学发现14.4. Open-ended scientific discovery

最困难的领域是开放性。目前的系统通常在人类定义的目标、行动和测量系统内进行优化。开放式人工智能科学家将识别异常、提出新问题、发明概念、创建实验和测量设备、跨越学科界限并放弃无效的计划。The most difficult frontier is open-endedness. Present systems generally optimise within objectives, actions and measurement systems defined by humans. An open-ended AI Scientist would identify anomalies, formulate new questions, invent concepts, create experiments and measuring devices, cross disciplinary boundaries and abandon unproductive programmes.

科学的目标生成必须与规范的自我授权区分开来。系统可能会在某个领域内产生自己的问题，同时仍然受到人类定义的目标、法律、道德、安全和资源的约束。Scientific goal generation must be distinguished from normative self-authorisation. A system may generate its own questions within a domain while remaining constrained by human-defined objectives, law, ethics, safety and resources.

开放式发现需要识别新颖性和重要性的机制。仅针对即时预期影响进行优化的系统可能会拒绝导致重大发现的奇怪或低概率路径。它必须平衡定向问题解决、系统探索、好奇心、减少不确定性和长期潜力。Open-ended discovery requires mechanisms for recognising novelty and importance. A system optimised only for immediate expected impact may reject the strange or low-probability path that leads to a major discovery. It must balance directed problem solving, systematic exploration, curiosity, uncertainty reduction and long-term potential.

改变表示的能力最终可能比搜索现有参数空间的能力更重要。当新的框架使不同的问题和观察成为可能时，科学革命往往会发生。The ability to change a representation may ultimately matter more than the ability to search an existing parameter space. Scientific revolutions often occur when a new framework makes different questions and observations possible.

14.5。诺贝尔图灵挑战赛14.5. The Nobel Turing Challenge

科学能力存在于从日常工作到改变领域的罕见能力的连续体中。人工智能科学家将占据同样的连续体。Scientific ability exists on a continuum from routine work to the rare capacity to transform a field. AI Scientists will occupy the same continuum.

84

人工智能科学家的过去与未来The Past and Future of AI Scientists

2020 年 2 月，伦敦的一个研讨会发起了诺贝尔图灵挑战赛。北野随后制定的目标是，到2050年培养出能够高度自主开展研究并做出诺贝尔品质重大发现的人工智能科学家（Kitano，2021）。In February 2020, a workshop in London initiated the Nobel Turing Grand Challenge. The goal subsequently formulated by Kitano is to develop, by 2050, AI Scientists capable of conducting research highly autonomously and making major discoveries of Nobel quality (Kitano, 2021).

目标可以表述为：The target can be stated as:

到2050年，培养人工智能科学家，使其能够高度自主地做出重大科学发现，达到与最优秀的人类科学家相当或更高的水平，包括诺贝尔奖级别的发现。To develop, by 2050, AI Scientists capable of making major scientific discoveries highly autonomously, at a level comparable with or superior to the best human scientists, including discoveries of Nobel quality.

诺贝尔奖的提及具有象征意义。挑战不在于机器能否合法获得奖项，而在于它能否做出具有同等意义的发现。更深层次的目标是成为重大发现的持续引擎，而不是孤立的成功。The Nobel reference is symbolic. The challenge is not whether a machine can legally receive a prize, but whether it can make discoveries of comparable significance. The deeper objective is a continuing engine of major discovery rather than one isolated success.

该挑战赛建立在图灵测试和 RoboCup 的基础上，但科学有更强大的外部标准。目的不是模仿科学家的对话行为。它是为了做出经得起实验、观察、复制或形式证明的发现。大自然是诚实的。The Challenge builds on the Turing Test and RoboCup, but science has a stronger external criterion. The objective is not to imitate the conversational behaviour of a scientist. It is to make discoveries that survive experiment, observation, replication or formal proof. Nature is honest.

应使用第 11 节中的标准来判断成就：真正的新颖性、因果机器的贡献、有记录的自主性、令人信服的证据、独立验证、忠实的沟通和问题的可重复性。Achievement should be judged using the criteria in Section 11: genuine novelty, causal machine contribution, documented autonomy, compelling evidence, independent verification, faithful communication and repeatability across problems.

15. 结论15. Conclusions

人类科学是围绕人类认知和身体限制而组织的。论文的长度要达到人们可以阅读的程度，小组的规模要小到足以让人们进行协调，知识被分为不同的学科，因为没有人能够掌握所有的科学。人工智能科学家不受同样的限制。他们可以维持大量相互竞争的假设、持续监控信息、即时交流正式知识、无疲劳地操作、跨实验室协调并保留完整的来源。Human science is organised around human cognitive and physical limitations. Papers are written at lengths people can read, groups are small enough for people to coordinate and knowledge is divided into disciplines because no person can master all of science. AI Scientists are not subject to the same limits. They can maintain vast numbers of competing hypotheses, monitor information continuously, communicate formal knowledge instantly, operate without fatigue, coordinate across laboratories and preserve complete provenance.

由此产生的系统可能没有直接的人类类似物。它可以由分布在全球的人类和人工智能科学家社区组成，这些社区连接到共享的计算和实验基础设施。专门的代理人会产生并批评假设。虚拟研究委员会将分配资源。自动化实验室将执行决定性的实验。机器可读的知识系统将不断整合结果。The resulting system may have no direct human analogue. It could consist of globally distributed communities of human and AI Scientists connected to shared computational and experimental infrastructure. Specialised agents would generate and criticise hypotheses. Virtual research councils would allocate resources. Automated laboratories would execute decisive experiments. Machinereadable knowledge systems would integrate the results continuously.

人类在建立社会目标、提供具体的和历史的理解、评估后果、参与解释和治理系统方面仍然至关重要。然而，随着机器能力的提高，劳动分工将会发生变化。Humans would remain essential in establishing social objectives, providing embodied and historical understanding, evaluating consequences, participating in interpretation and governing the systems. The division of labour would nevertheless change as machine capability increased.

如果诺贝尔图灵挑战得以实现，它将改变世界。它将改变研究的经济学、方法和组织，并证明科学发现可以在机械中实例化。更根本的是，它将在科学事业中创造一个新的参与者：不仅仅是人类科学家的工具，而且是一个能够质疑自然、从其答案中学习并独立为知识增长做出贡献的人工系统。If the Nobel Turing Challenge is achieved, it will transform the world. It will alter the economics, methods and organisation of research and demonstrate that scientific discovery can be instantiated in machinery. More fundamentally, it will create a new participant in the scientific enterprise: not merely a tool for human scientists, but an artificial system capable of interrogating nature, learning from its answers and contributing independently to the growth of knowledge.
